    private void Start()
    {
        DiceRoller.Rolled +=
            HandleLoggedDiceRoll;

        factionRules =
            new FactionRuleSystem(this);

        aeldariRules =
            new AeldariRulesSystem(this);

        battleSetupMode = true;
        armyImportMode = false;
        deploymentMode = false;
        activeFaction = "";

        status =
            "Choose a battle size and battlefield before loading armies.";
    }

    private void EnsureTraditionalDiceTray()
    {
        if (traditionalDiceTray != null)
            return;

        traditionalDiceTray =
            GetComponent<TraditionalDiceTray3D>();

        if (traditionalDiceTray == null)
        {
            traditionalDiceTray =
                gameObject.AddComponent<
                    TraditionalDiceTray3D
                >();
        }

        traditionalDiceTray.Initialize(this);
    }

    private void OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
        int suggestedDice)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode)
            return;

        EnsureTraditionalDiceTray();

        traditionalDiceTray
            .SetRequestedDiceCount(
                Mathf.Max(
                    1,
                    suggestedDice
                )
            );

        showDiceTray = true;
    }

    private bool TraditionalStateResolutionPending()
    {
        return
            !IsXcomMode &&
            (traditionalFirstTurnPending ||
             traditionalNumericPromptPending ||
             traditionalAdvancePending ||
             traditionalChargePending ||
             traditionalBattleShockPending ||
             traditionalBattleShockQueue.Count > 0 ||
             traditionalReanimationPending ||
             traditionalRuleAlertPending);
    }

    private void OpenTraditionalNumericPrompt(
        string title,
        string text,
        int minimum,
        int maximum,
        int initial,
        int suggestedDice,
        System.Action<int> apply,
        System.Action cancel = null)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode)
            return;

        traditionalNumericPromptPending = true;
        traditionalNumericTitle =
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                title)
            ? "TRADITIONAL RESULT"
            : title;
        traditionalNumericText =
            text ?? "";
        traditionalNumericMin =
            minimum;
        traditionalNumericMax =
            Mathf.Max(
                minimum,
                maximum
            );
        traditionalNumericValue =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                initial,
                traditionalNumericMin,
                traditionalNumericMax
            );
        traditionalNumericApply = apply;
        traditionalNumericCancel = cancel;

        OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
            suggestedDice
        );

        status =
            traditionalNumericTitle +
            ": resolve the dice yourself, then enter the final value.";
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalNumericPrompt()
    {
        if (!traditionalNumericPromptPending)
            return;

        int value =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                traditionalNumericValue,
                traditionalNumericMin,
                traditionalNumericMax
            );

        string title =
            traditionalNumericTitle;

        System.Action<int> apply =
            traditionalNumericApply;

        traditionalNumericPromptPending = false;
        traditionalNumericTitle = "";
        traditionalNumericText = "";
        traditionalNumericApply = null;
        traditionalNumericCancel = null;

        AppendBattleLog(
            "MANUAL RESULT",
            title,
            "Player entered final value " +
            value +
            ". Warboard did not inspect the physical dice."
        );

        if (apply != null)
            apply(value);
    }

    private void CancelTraditionalNumericPrompt()
    {
        if (!traditionalNumericPromptPending)
            return;

        System.Action cancel =
            traditionalNumericCancel;

        traditionalNumericPromptPending = false;
        traditionalNumericTitle = "";
        traditionalNumericText = "";
        traditionalNumericApply = null;
        traditionalNumericCancel = null;

        if (cancel != null)
            cancel();

        status =
            "Traditional manual result prompt cancelled.";
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        DiceRoller.Rolled -=
            HandleLoggedDiceRoll;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (battleSetupMode ||
            missionSetupMode ||
            battleOver)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (armyImportMode)
        {
            HandleCamera();
            return;
        }

        if (Time.unscaledTime >=
            nextBattlefieldVisualRefreshTime)
        {
            nextBattlefieldVisualRefreshTime =
                Time.unscaledTime +
                0.12f;

            RefreshObjectiveDisplays();
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.E))
        {
            if (TraditionalStateResolutionPending())
            {
                status =
                    "Finish the current Traditional tabletop state prompt first.";
            }
            else if (interactiveAttack != null &&
                (!interactiveAttack.IsComplete ||
                 interactiveAttack
                    .HasPendingCasualtyChoices))
            {
                status =
                    interactiveAttack
                        .HasPendingCasualtyChoices
                    ? "Finish casualty selection first."
                    : "Finish the current dice sequence first.";
            }
            else if (showRuleChoiceWindow ||
                     specialMoveSquad != null ||
                     specialShootUnit != null ||
                     endPhaseFlowRunning)
            {
                status =
                    "Resolve the current rules/reaction window first.";
            }
            else if (showStratagemReaction)
            {
                status =
                    "Resolve the stratagem reaction window first.";
            }
            else if (deploymentMode)
            {
                status =
                    "Deployment is active. Place the current unit or send it to Reserves.";
            }
            else
            {
                NextPhase();
            }
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
        {
            if (missionActionTargetingDefinition !=
                    null)
            {
                missionActionTargetingUnit = null;
                missionActionTargetingDefinition =
                    null;

                status =
                    "Mission action targeting cancelled.";
            }
            else if (interactiveAttack != null &&
                (!interactiveAttack.IsComplete ||
                 interactiveAttack
                    .HasPendingCasualtyChoices))
            {
                status =
                    interactiveAttack
                        .HasPendingCasualtyChoices
                    ? "Casualty selection must be completed."
                    : (IsXcomMode
                        ? "Resolve the current XCOM decision."
                        : "The attack must be resolved; use the dice popup controls.");
            }
            else if (showRuleChoiceWindow ||
                     specialMoveSquad != null ||
                     specialShootUnit != null)
            {
                status =
                    "Resolve or skip the current rules window first.";
            }
            else if (showStratagemReaction)
            {
                KeepPendingChargeRoll();
            }
            else if (showStratagemMenu)
            {
                showStratagemMenu = false;
            }
            else if (showBattleLog)
            {
                showBattleLog = false;
            }
            else if (showMissionPanel)
            {
                showMissionPanel = false;
            }
            else if (showDatasheet)
            {
                CloseDatasheet();
            }
            else
            {
                ClearSelection();
            }
        }

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0) &&
            (interactiveAttack == null ||
             interactiveAttack
                .HasPendingCasualtyChoices) &&
            !showDatasheet &&
            !showRuleChoiceWindow &&
            !showStratagemReaction &&
            !showStratagemMenu)
        {
            HandleClick(Input.mousePosition);
        }

        HandleCamera();
        UpdateMovementCursorMeasure();
    }

    private void ConfigureBattle(
        string sizeName,
        int points,
        float width,
        float depth,
        float deploymentWidth,
        string missionName)
    {
        battleSizeName =
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                sizeName)
            ? "Custom"
            : sizeName;

        battlePoints =
            Mathf.Max(
                1,
                points
            );

        BoardWidth =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                width,
                24f,
                120f
            );

        BoardDepth =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                depth,
                24f,
                120f
            );

        DeploymentZoneWidth =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                deploymentWidth,
                4f,
                Mathf.Max(
                    4f,
                    BoardWidth *
                        0.40f
                )
            );

        missionPresetName =
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                missionName)
            ? "Custom"
            : missionName;

        BuildWorld();
        CreateMoveRing();

        battleSetupMode = false;
        armyImportMode = true;

        AppendBattleLog(
            "SETUP",
            "Resolution mode",
            IsXcomMode
            ? "XCOM / AUTOMATIC selected. Routine dice resolve automatically, but every roll remains available in the Battle Log."
            : "TRADITIONAL / MANUAL selected. Players roll and interpret every die themselves; Warboard records only the state/results they explicitly enter."
        );

        status =
            battleSizeName +
            " (" +
            battlePoints +
            " pts) • " +
            missionPresetName +
            " • battlefield " +
            BoardWidth.ToString("0.#") +
            " x " +
            BoardDepth.ToString("0.#") +
            " • deployment depth " +
            DeploymentZoneWidth.ToString("0.#") +
            ". Load both armies.";
    }

    private bool TryParseCustomBattle(
        out int points,
        out float width,
        out float depth,
        out float deployment)
    {
        points = 0;
        width = 0f;
        depth = 0f;
        deployment = 0f;

        bool valid =
            int.TryParse(
                customPointsText,
                out points
            ) &&
            float.TryParse(
                customWidthText,
                out width
            ) &&
            float.TryParse(
                customDepthText,
                out depth
            ) &&
            float.TryParse(
                customDeploymentText,
                out deployment
            );

        if (!valid)
            return false;

        return
            points > 0 &&
            width >= 24f &&
            depth >= 24f &&
            deployment >= 4f &&
            deployment <
                width * 0.5f;
    }

    private void DrawBattleSetupPanel()
    {
        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                780f,
                Screen.width -
                    40f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                580f,
                Screen.height -
                    40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width -
                 width) *
                    0.5f,
                (Screen.height -
                 height) *
                    0.5f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.042f,
                1f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 22;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle sub =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        sub.fontSize = 13;
        sub.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 16f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                30f
            ),
            "WARBOARD — BATTLE SETUP",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 54f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                36f
            ),
            "Choose how Warboard resolves dice, then choose battle size. Tactical decisions remain player-controlled in both modes.",
            sub
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 96f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                24f
            ),
            "RESOLUTION MODE",
            heading
        );

        float modeWidth =
            (panel.width - 62f) *
            0.5f;

        Color oldColor =
            GUI.color;

        if (ResolutionMode ==
            WarboardResolutionMode.XcomAutomatic)
        {
            GUI.color =
                new Color(
                    0.72f,
                    1f,
                    0.72f,
                    1f
                );
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 128f,
                modeWidth,
                52f
            ),
            "XCOM / AUTOMATIC\nWarboard rolls; you make the decisions"))
        {
            ResolutionMode =
                WarboardResolutionMode.XcomAutomatic;

            showDiceTray = false;
        }

        GUI.color = oldColor;

        if (ResolutionMode ==
            WarboardResolutionMode.TraditionalManual)
        {
            GUI.color =
                new Color(
                    0.72f,
                    0.88f,
                    1f,
                    1f
                );
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 40f +
                    modeWidth,
                panel.y + 128f,
                modeWidth,
                52f
            ),
            "TRADITIONAL / MANUAL\nPlayer resolves visible combat dice"))
        {
            ResolutionMode =
                WarboardResolutionMode.TraditionalManual;
        }

        GUI.color = oldColor;

        float battleButtonWidth =
            (panel.width - 62f) *
            0.5f;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 196f,
                battleButtonWidth,
                50f
            ),
            "INCURSION\n1,000 pts • 60 x 44"))
        {
            ConfigureBattle(
                "Incursion",
                1000,
                60f,
                44f,
                10f,
                "Chapter Approved Mission"
            );

            return;
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 40f +
                    battleButtonWidth,
                panel.y + 196f,
                battleButtonWidth,
                50f
            ),
            "STRIKE FORCE\n2,000 pts • 60 x 44"))
        {
            ConfigureBattle(
                "Strike Force",
                2000,
                60f,
                44f,
                10f,
                "Chapter Approved Mission"
            );

            return;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 262f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                22f
            ),
            "CUSTOM / HOUSE BATTLEFIELD",
            heading
        );

        float labelX =
            panel.x + 24f;

        float valueX =
            panel.x + 190f;

        float rowY =
            panel.y + 300f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                labelX,
                rowY,
                150f,
                24f
            ),
            "Points:"
        );

        customPointsText =
            GUI.TextField(
                new Rect(
                    valueX,
                    rowY - 2f,
                    110f,
                    28f
                ),
                customPointsText,
                5
            );

        rowY += 38f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                labelX,
                rowY,
                150f,
                24f
            ),
            "Board X (inches):"
        );

        customWidthText =
            GUI.TextField(
                new Rect(
                    valueX,
                    rowY - 2f,
                    110f,
                    28f
                ),
                customWidthText,
                5
            );

        rowY += 38f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                labelX,
                rowY,
                150f,
                24f
            ),
            "Board Z (inches):"
        );

        customDepthText =
            GUI.TextField(
                new Rect(
                    valueX,
                    rowY - 2f,
                    110f,
                    28f
                ),
                customDepthText,
                5
            );

        rowY += 38f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                labelX,
                rowY,
                150f,
                24f
            ),
            "Deployment depth:"
        );

        customDeploymentText =
            GUI.TextField(
                new Rect(
                    valueX,
                    rowY - 2f,
                    110f,
                    28f
                ),
                customDeploymentText,
                5
            );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 336f,
                panel.y + 300f,
                panel.width - 360f,
                112f
            ),
            ResolutionMode ==
                WarboardResolutionMode.XcomAutomatic
            ? "XCOM mode hides routine combat dice and resolves them immediately. Optional choices, model placement, targets, weapons, casualties and reactions still stop for the player."
            : "Traditional mode is a digital tabletop: Warboard keeps phases, movement, missions, CP, Stratagem/effect state and measurements, while players roll and interpret every die themselves.",
            sub
        );

        int customPoints;
        float customWidth;
        float customDepth;
        float customDeployment;

        bool customValid =
            TryParseCustomBattle(
                out customPoints,
                out customWidth,
                out customDepth,
                out customDeployment
            );

        GUI.enabled =
            customValid;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    232f,
                panel.y +
                    panel.height -
                    62f,
                210f,
                38f
            ),
            "START CUSTOM BATTLE"))
        {
            ConfigureBattle(
                "Custom",
                customPoints,
                customWidth,
                customDepth,
                customDeployment,
                "Custom Battlefield"
            );

            return;
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y +
                    panel.height -
                    58f,
                panel.width - 275f,
                36f
            ),
            customValid
            ? "Selected mode: " +
              (IsXcomMode
                  ? "XCOM / AUTOMATIC"
                  : "TRADITIONAL / MANUAL")
            : "Custom values: points > 0; board dimensions ≥ 24; deployment depth ≥ 4 and less than half the X dimension.",
            sub
        );
    }


    private void BuildWorld()
    {
        RenderSettings.ambientLight =
            new Color(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.58f);

        gameCamera = Camera.main;

        if (gameCamera == null)
        {
            GameObject cameraObject =
                new GameObject("Main Camera");

            gameCamera =
                cameraObject.AddComponent<Camera>();

            cameraObject.tag = "MainCamera";
        }

        float cameraDistance =
            Mathf.Max(
                BoardWidth,
                BoardDepth
            );

        gameCamera.transform.position =
            new Vector3(
                0f,
                cameraDistance * 0.78f,
                -cameraDistance * 0.80f
            );

        gameCamera.transform.rotation =
            Quaternion.Euler(
                48f,
                0f,
                0f
            );

        gameCamera.clearFlags =
            CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;

        gameCamera.backgroundColor =
            new Color(0.08f, 0.09f, 0.12f);

        Light light = Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Light>();

        if (light == null)
        {
            GameObject lightObject =
                new GameObject("Directional Light");

            light =
                lightObject.AddComponent<Light>();
        }

        light.type = LightType.Directional;
        light.intensity = 1.25f;
        light.transform.rotation =
            Quaternion.Euler(55f, -35f, 0f);

        GameObject board =
            GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);

        board.name = "Board";
        board.transform.position =
            new Vector3(0f, -0.2f, 0f);

        board.transform.localScale =
            new Vector3(
                BoardWidth,
                0.4f,
                BoardDepth
            );

        board.AddComponent<BoardSurface>();

        SetObjectColor(
            board,
            new Color(0.19f, 0.22f, 0.19f)
        );

        // Terrain and mission objectives are generated after Force
        // Dispositions resolve the Chapter Approved mission matchup.

        GameObject worldUiObject =
            new GameObject(
                "Battlefield World UI"
            );

        battlefieldWorldUI =
            worldUiObject
                .AddComponent<
                    BattlefieldWorldUI
                >();

        battlefieldWorldUI.Initialize(
            this
        );
    }

    private void CreateTerrain(
        Vector3 position,
        Vector3 scale,
        TerrainTrait trait,
        Color color)
    {
        GameObject terrain =
            GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);

        terrain.name =
            "Terrain_" +
            trait.ToString();

        terrain.transform.position = position;
        terrain.transform.localScale = scale;

        TerrainFeature feature =
            terrain.AddComponent<TerrainFeature>();

        feature.Initialize(trait);

        SetObjectColor(
            terrain,
            color
        );
    }

    private void ClearMissionBattlefield()
    {
        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in objectives.ToList())
        {
            if (objective != null)
                Destroy(objective.gameObject);
        }

        objectives.Clear();

        TerrainFeature[] terrain =
            Object.FindObjectsByType<
                TerrainFeature
            >();

        foreach (TerrainFeature feature
            in terrain)
        {
            if (feature != null)
                Destroy(feature.gameObject);
        }

        foreach (string outlineName
            in new[]
            {
                "Player 1 Deployment",
                "Player 2 Deployment",
                "Mission Exclusion"
            })
        {
            GameObject outline =
                GameObject.Find(
                    outlineName
                );

            if (outline != null)
                Destroy(outline);
        }
    }

    private void ApplyMissionBattlefieldLayout(
        int layoutIndex)
    {
        ClearMissionBattlefield();

        missionLayoutIndex =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                layoutIndex,
                1,
                3
            );

        activeMissionBattlefield =
            MissionBattlefieldRegistry.Build(
                missionDispositionPlayerOne,
                missionDispositionPlayerTwo,
                missionLayoutIndex,
                BoardWidth,
                BoardDepth
            );

        foreach (MissionObjectiveSpec spec
            in activeMissionBattlefield
                .Objectives)
        {
            MissionObjectiveRole role =
                spec.Role;

            if (spec.Role ==
                MissionObjectiveRole.PlayerOneHome)
            {
                role =
                    missionAttackerIndex == 0
                    ? MissionObjectiveRole
                        .PlayerOneHome
                    : MissionObjectiveRole
                        .PlayerTwoHome;
            }
            else if (spec.Role ==
                     MissionObjectiveRole.PlayerTwoHome)
            {
                role =
                    missionAttackerIndex == 0
                    ? MissionObjectiveRole
                        .PlayerTwoHome
                    : MissionObjectiveRole
                        .PlayerOneHome;
            }

            CreateMissionObjective(
                spec.Position,
                role
            );
        }

        foreach (MissionTerrainSpec spec
            in activeMissionBattlefield
                .Terrain)
        {
            CreateMissionTerrainFeature(
                spec
            );
        }

        CreateDeploymentZoneOutlines();

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();
    }

    private void CreateMissionObjective(
        Vector3 position,
        MissionObjectiveRole role)
    {
        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(
                "Mission Objective"
            );

        ObjectiveController objective =
            go.AddComponent<
                ObjectiveController
            >();

        objective.Initialize(
            position,
            role
        );

        objectives.Add(
            objective
        );
    }

    private void CreateMissionTerrainFeature(
        MissionTerrainSpec spec)
    {
        if (spec == null)
            return;

        GameObject terrain =
            GameObject.CreatePrimitive(
                PrimitiveType.Cube
            );

        terrain.name =
            "MissionTerrain_" +
            spec.Id;

        terrain.transform.position =
            spec.Position;

        terrain.transform.localScale =
            spec.Size;

        TerrainFeature feature =
            terrain.AddComponent<
                TerrainFeature
            >();

        feature.Initialize(
            spec.Trait,
            spec.Id,
            spec.IsObjectiveArea
        );

        Color color =
            spec.Trait ==
                TerrainTrait.Blocking
            ? new Color(
                0.26f,
                0.26f,
                0.28f
            )
            : spec.Trait ==
                TerrainTrait.Cover
                ? new Color(
                    0.38f,
                    0.34f,
                    0.24f
                )
                : new Color(
                    0.22f,
                    0.34f,
                    0.22f
                );

        SetObjectColor(
            terrain,
            color
        );
    }

    private bool FactionUsesAttackerZone(
        string faction)
    {
        if (factions.Count < 2)
            return false;

        int factionIndex =
            faction == factions[0]
            ? 0
            : 1;

        return
            factionIndex ==
            missionAttackerIndex;
    }

    private MissionDeploymentZone
        DeploymentZoneForFaction(
            string faction)
    {
        if (activeMissionBattlefield == null)
            return null;

        return
            activeMissionBattlefield
                .ZoneForRole(
                    FactionUsesAttackerZone(
                        faction
                    )
                );
    }

    public bool MissionUnitWhollyWithinDeploymentZone(
        SquadController unit,
        string zoneFaction)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            activeMissionBattlefield == null ||
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(zoneFaction))
        {
            return false;
        }

        MissionDeploymentZone zone =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(zoneFaction);

        if (zone == null)
            return false;

        List<ModelToken> models =
            unit.JoinedLivingModelTokens();

        if (models.Count == 0)
            return false;

        return models.All(
            model =>
                model != null &&
                zone.ContainsBase(
                    model.transform.position,
                    model.BaseRadiusInches
                )
        );
    }

    public bool MissionUnitWhollyWithinOpponentDeploymentZone(
        SquadController unit,
        string faction)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return false;
        }

        string opponent =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                value =>
                    value != faction
            );

        return
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(opponent) &&
            MissionUnitWhollyWithinDeploymentZone(
                unit,
                opponent
            );
    }

    private void CreateDeploymentZoneOutlines()
    {
        if (activeMissionBattlefield == null ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return;
        }

        CreateDeploymentPolygonOutline(
            "Player 1 Deployment",
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                factions[0]
            ),
            new Color(
                0.75f,
                0.25f,
                0.85f,
                1f
            )
        );

        CreateDeploymentPolygonOutline(
            "Player 2 Deployment",
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                factions[1]
            ),
            new Color(
                0.20f,
                0.90f,
                0.50f,
                1f
            )
        );

        if (activeMissionBattlefield
                .AttackerZone != null &&
            activeMissionBattlefield
                .AttackerZone
                .HasCircularExclusion)
        {
            CreateCircularMissionOutline(
                "Mission Exclusion",
                activeMissionBattlefield
                    .AttackerZone
                    .ExclusionCentre,
                activeMissionBattlefield
                    .AttackerZone
                    .ExclusionRadius,
                new Color(
                    0.92f,
                    0.82f,
                    0.32f,
                    0.85f
                )
            );
        }
    }

    private void CreateDeploymentPolygonOutline(
        string name,
        MissionDeploymentZone zone,
        Color color)
    {
        if (zone == null ||
            zone.Polygon.Count < 3)
        {
            return;
        }

        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(name);

        LineRenderer line =
            go.AddComponent<
                LineRenderer
            >();

        line.loop = true;
        line.useWorldSpace = true;
        line.positionCount =
            zone.Polygon.Count;
        line.widthMultiplier = 0.10f;

        Shader shader =
            Shader.Find(
                "Sprites/Default"
            );

        if (shader != null)
            line.material =
                new Material(shader);

        line.startColor = color;
        line.endColor = color;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < zone.Polygon.Count;
             i++)
        {
            Vector2 point =
                zone.Polygon[i];

            line.SetPosition(
                i,
                new Vector3(
                    point.x,
                    0.055f,
                    point.y
                )
            );
        }
    }

    private void CreateCircularMissionOutline(
        string name,
        Vector2 centre,
        float radius,
        Color color)
    {
        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(name);

        LineRenderer line =
            go.AddComponent<
                LineRenderer
            >();

        line.loop = true;
        line.useWorldSpace = true;
        line.positionCount = 72;
        line.widthMultiplier = 0.07f;

        Shader shader =
            Shader.Find(
                "Sprites/Default"
            );

        if (shader != null)
            line.material =
                new Material(shader);

        line.startColor = color;
        line.endColor = color;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < line.positionCount;
             i++)
        {
            float angle =
                i /
                (float)line.positionCount *
                Mathf.PI *
                2f;

            line.SetPosition(
                i,
                new Vector3(
                    centre.x +
                        Mathf.Cos(angle) *
                        radius,
                    0.06f,
                    centre.y +
                        Mathf.Sin(angle) *
                        radius
                )
            );
        }
    }

    private void CreateMoveRing()
    {
        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(
                "ActionRangeGuide"
            );

        moveRing =
            go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();

        moveRing.loop = true;
        moveRing.useWorldSpace = true;
        moveRing.positionCount = 128;
        moveRing.widthMultiplier = 0.055f;

        Shader shader =
            Shader.Find(
                "Sprites/Default"
            );

        if (shader != null)
        {
            moveRing.material =
                new Material(shader);
        }

        SetActionRangeRingColor(
            new Color(
                0.15f,
                0.75f,
                1f,
                0.95f
            )
        );

        moveRing.enabled = false;

        CreateMovementCursorLine();
    }

    private void CreateMovementCursorLine()
    {
        if (movementCursorLine != null)
            return;

        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(
                "MovementCursorDistanceGuide"
            );

        movementCursorLine =
            go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();

        movementCursorLine.loop = false;
        movementCursorLine.useWorldSpace = true;
        movementCursorLine.positionCount = 2;
        movementCursorLine.widthMultiplier = 0.07f;
        movementCursorLine.numCapVertices = 5;

        Shader shader =
            Shader.Find(
                "Sprites/Default"
            );

        if (shader != null)
        {
            movementCursorLine.material =
                new Material(shader);
        }

        movementCursorLine.startColor =
            new Color(
                0.16f,
                0.82f,
                1f,
                0.98f
            );

        movementCursorLine.endColor =
            movementCursorLine.startColor;

        movementCursorLine.enabled = false;
    }

    private void SetMovementCursorLineColor(
        Color color)
    {
        if (movementCursorLine == null)
            return;

        movementCursorLine.startColor =
            color;

        movementCursorLine.endColor =
            color;
    }

    private void SetActionRangeRingColor(
        Color color)
    {
        if (moveRing == null)
            return;

        moveRing.startColor = color;
        moveRing.endColor = color;
    }

    private float SelectedModelRemainingMove()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            selectedModel == null)
        {
            return 0f;
        }

        float allowance =
            selectedSquad
                .GetMovementAllowanceFor(
                    selectedModel
                );

        float alreadyMoved =
            selectedModel
                .DistanceMovedFromTurnStart(
                    selectedModel
                        .transform.position
                );

        return
            Mathf.Max(
                0f,
                allowance -
                alreadyMoved
            );
    }

    private bool TryCursorPointOnBattlefield(
        out Vector3 point)
    {
        point = Vector3.zero;

        if (gameCamera == null)
            return false;

        Ray ray =
            gameCamera.ScreenPointToRay(
                Input.mousePosition
            );

        Plane boardPlane =
            new Plane(
                Vector3.up,
                new Vector3(
                    0f,
                    0.02f,
                    0f
                )
            );

        float enter;

        if (!boardPlane.Raycast(
                ray,
                out enter))
        {
            return false;
        }

        point =
            ray.GetPoint(
                enter
            );

        point.y = 0.075f;

        // Do not leave a ruler stretching far into empty world when the
        // pointer is over UI outside the physical battlefield.
        float margin = 2.0f;

        return
            Mathf.Abs(point.x) <=
                BoardWidth * 0.5f +
                margin &&
            Mathf.Abs(point.z) <=
                BoardDepth * 0.5f +
                margin;
    }

    private void HideMovementCursorMeasure()
    {
        movementCursorMeasureActive =
            false;

        movementCursorMeasureText = "";

        if (movementCursorLine != null)
            movementCursorLine.enabled = false;
    }

    private void ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
        Vector3 start,
        Vector3 end,
        string text,
        bool legal,
        Color normalColor)
    {
        if (movementCursorLine == null)
            CreateMovementCursorLine();

        start.y = 0.085f;
        end.y = 0.085f;

        movementCursorLine.enabled = true;

        movementCursorLine.SetPosition(
            0,
            start
        );

        movementCursorLine.SetPosition(
            1,
            end
        );

        movementCursorMeasureActive =
            true;

        movementCursorMeasureText =
            text ?? "";

        movementCursorMeasureLegal =
            legal;

        SetMovementCursorLineColor(
            legal
            ? normalColor
            : new Color(
                1f,
                0.22f,
                0.18f,
                1f
              )
        );
    }

    private void UpdateMovementCursorMeasure()
    {
        HideMovementCursorMeasure();

        bool rulerHeld =
            Input.GetKey(
                KeyCode.LeftAlt
            ) ||
            Input.GetKey(
                KeyCode.RightAlt
            );

        if (!rulerHeld)
            return;

        if (battleSetupMode ||
            armyImportMode ||
            missionSetupMode ||
            battleOver ||
            deploymentMode ||
            reservePlacementSquad != null ||
            showDatasheet ||
            showRuleChoiceWindow ||
            showStratagemReaction ||
            showStratagemMenu ||
            traditionalAttackPending ||
            interactiveAttack != null)
        {
            return;
        }

        Vector3 cursor;

        if (!TryCursorPointOnBattlefield(
                out cursor))
        {
            return;
        }

        // Special / faction movement uses the unit centre and its explicit
        // maximum distance.
        if (specialMoveSquad != null)
        {
            Vector3 start =
                specialMoveSquad
                    .CurrentCentre();

            float distance =
                HorizontalDistance(
                    start,
                    cursor
                );

            bool legal =
                InsideBoard(
                    cursor) &&
                distance <=
                    specialMoveMaxDistance +
                    0.001f;

            ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                start,
                cursor,
                distance.ToString("0.0") +
                "″ / " +
                specialMoveMaxDistance
                    .ToString("0.0") +
                "″  •  " +
                specialMoveLabel,
                legal,
                new Color(
                    0.74f,
                    0.44f,
                    1f,
                    0.98f
                )
            );

            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
            fightActivationUnit != null &&
            selectedModel != null &&
            ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                selectedModel))
        {
            Vector3 start =
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position;

            if (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn ||
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.Consolidate)
            {
                Vector3 stageStart;

                if (!fightStageStartPositions
                    .TryGetValue(
                        selectedModel,
                        out stageStart))
                {
                    stageStart = start;
                }

                float segment =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        start,
                        cursor
                    );

                float total =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        stageStart,
                        cursor
                    );

                float limit =
                    FightStageMoveLimit();

                string reason;

                bool legal =
                    FightStageDestinationLegal(
                        selectedModel,
                        cursor,
                        out reason
                    );

                ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                    start,
                    cursor,
                    segment.ToString("0.0") +
                    "″ segment  •  " +
                    total.ToString("0.0") +
                    "/" +
                    limit.ToString("0.0") +
                    "″ " +
                    (fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.PileIn
                        ? "pile-in"
                        : "consolidate"),
                    legal,
                    fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.PileIn
                    ? new Color(
                        0.66f,
                        0.32f,
                        1.0f,
                        0.98f
                      )
                    : new Color(
                        0.20f,
                        0.90f,
                        0.52f,
                        0.98f
                      )
                );

                return;
            }

            if (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.Attacks &&
                !fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                    .Contains(
                        selectedModel))
            {
                float distance =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        start,
                        cursor
                    );

                ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                    start,
                    cursor,
                    distance.ToString("0.0") +
                    "″  •  melee range " +
                    EngagementRange.ToString("0.#") +
                    "″",
                    distance <=
                        EngagementRange +
                        0.001f &&
                    InsideBoard(
                        cursor
                    ),
                    new Color(
                        1f,
                        0.32f,
                        0.18f,
                        0.98f
                    )
                );

                return;
            }
        }

        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            !selectedSquad.IsAlive ||
            selectedSquad.FactionId !=
                activeFaction)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            if (wholeSquadMoveMode)
            {
                Vector3 start =
                    selectedSquad
                        .CurrentCentre();

                Vector3 delta =
                    cursor -
                    start;

                delta.y = 0f;

                float distance =
                    HorizontalMagnitude(
                        delta
                    );

                bool legal =
                    InsideBoard(
                        cursor) &&
                    selectedSquad
                        .CanTranslateWithinNormalMove(
                            delta
                        );

                ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                    start,
                    cursor,
                    distance.ToString("0.0") +
                    "″  •  formation translation",
                    legal,
                    new Color(
                        0.16f,
                        0.82f,
                        1f,
                        0.98f
                    )
                );

                return;
            }

            if (selectedModel != null &&
                selectedModel.IsAlive)
            {
                Vector3 start =
                    selectedModel
                        .transform.position;

                Vector3 destination =
                    cursor;

                destination.y =
                    start.y;

                float segment =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        start,
                        destination
                    );

                float total =
                    selectedModel
                        .DistanceMovedFromTurnStart(
                            destination
                        );

                float allowance =
                    selectedSquad
                        .GetMovementAllowanceFor(
                            selectedModel
                        );

                bool legal =
                    total <=
                        allowance +
                        0.001f &&
                    InsideBoard(
                        destination
                    ) &&
                    CanPlaceModel(
                        selectedModel,
                        destination
                    );

                string text =
                    segment.ToString("0.0") +
                    "″";

                if (selectedModel
                    .DistanceMovedFromTurnStart(
                        start) >
                    0.01f)
                {
                    text +=
                        " segment  •  " +
                        total.ToString("0.0") +
                        "/" +
                        allowance.ToString("0.0") +
                        "″ total";
                }
                else
                {
                    text +=
                        " / " +
                        allowance.ToString("0.0") +
                        "″";
                }

                ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                    start,
                    cursor,
                    text,
                    legal,
                    new Color(
                        0.16f,
                        0.82f,
                        1f,
                        0.98f
                    )
                );

                return;
            }
        }

        // The Charge phase still uses the charge solver for placement, but
        // the selected model now acts as a live tabletop ruler while the
        // player is choosing / checking a target.
        if (phase == Phase.Charge &&
            selectedModel != null &&
            selectedModel.IsAlive &&
            SelectedUnitCanCharge())
        {
            Vector3 start =
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position;

            float distance =
                HorizontalDistance(
                    start,
                    cursor
                );

            bool legal =
                InsideBoard(
                    cursor) &&
                distance <=
                    12f +
                    0.001f;

            ShowMovementCursorMeasure(
                start,
                cursor,
                distance.ToString("0.0") +
                "″  •  max 12″",
                legal,
                new Color(
                    1f,
                    0.30f,
                    0.18f,
                    0.98f
                )
            );
        }
    }

    private float SelectedModelMaximumRangedRange()
    {
        if (selectedModel == null ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            return 0f;
        }

        if (missionSystem != null &&
            missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                selectedSquad) &&
            !selectedSquad.HasKeyword(
                "titanic"))
        {
            return 0f;
        }

        float best = 0f;

        foreach (WeaponData weapon
            in selectedModel.RangedWeapons)
        {
            if (weapon == null ||
                !selectedModel.CanUseWeapon(
                    weapon) ||
                !selectedModel
                    .CanFireRangedWeaponThisTurn(
                        weapon))
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced &&
                !selectedSquad
                    .JoinedActionController()
                    .SnapShootingActive &&
                !WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                    weapon,
                    "assault") &&
                !selectedSquad
                    .JoinedActionController()
                    .FactionSuddenStormActive &&
                !selectedSquad
                    .JoinedActionController()
                    .StarEnginesActive &&
                !(aeldariRules != null &&
                  aeldariRules
                    .VehicleRangedHasAssault(
                        selectedSquad
                    )))
            {
                continue;
            }

            best =
                Mathf.Max(
                    best,
                    weapon.range +
                    AeldariRangedRangeModifier(
                        selectedSquad,
                        weapon
                    )
                );
        }

        return best;
    }

    private bool SelectedUnitCanCharge()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            !selectedSquad.IsAlive ||
            selectedSquad.FactionId !=
                activeFaction)
        {
            return false;
        }

        bool canChargeAfterFallBack =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.CanChargeAfterFallBack(
                selectedSquad
            );

        bool canChargeAfterAdvance =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.CanChargeAfterAdvance(
                selectedSquad
            );

        if (selectedSquad.HasCharged ||
            (selectedSquad.HasFallenBack &&
             !canChargeAfterFallBack) ||
            (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced &&
             !canChargeAfterAdvance) ||
            (missionSystem != null &&
             missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                 selectedSquad)) ||
            IsEngaged(selectedSquad))
        {
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void RefreshMoveRing()
    {
        if (moveRing == null)
            return;

        moveRing.enabled = false;

        string rangeFaction =
            phase == Phase.Fight &&
            fightSequenceActive
            ? fightSelectionFaction
            : activeFaction;

        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            !selectedSquad.IsAlive ||
            selectedSquad.FactionId !=
                rangeFaction)
        {
            return;
        }

        Vector3 centre =
            Vector3.zero;

        float radius = 0f;

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            if (wholeSquadMoveMode)
            {
                centre =
                    selectedSquad
                        .CurrentCentre();

                radius =
                    selectedSquad.GetMove() +
                    selectedSquad
                        .BattleFocusMoveBonus +
                    (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced
                        ? selectedSquad
                            .AdvanceBonus
                        : 0);

                SetActionRangeRingColor(
                    new Color(
                        0.15f,
                        0.75f,
                        1f,
                        0.95f
                    )
                );
            }
            else if (selectedModel != null)
            {
                // Single-model movement is displayed from the actual model
                // that was clicked, using only its remaining movement.
                centre =
                    selectedModel
                        .transform.position;

                radius =
                    SelectedModelRemainingMove();

                SetActionRangeRingColor(
                    new Color(
                        0.15f,
                        0.75f,
                        1f,
                        0.95f
                    )
                );
            }
        }
        else if (phase == Phase.Shoot &&
                 selectedModel != null &&
                 !selectedSquad.HasShot)
        {
            centre =
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position;

            radius =
                SelectedModelMaximumRangedRange();

            SetActionRangeRingColor(
                new Color(
                    1.0f,
                    0.72f,
                    0.16f,
                    0.95f
                )
            );
        }
        else if (phase == Phase.Charge &&
                 selectedModel != null &&
                 SelectedUnitCanCharge())
        {
            centre =
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position;

            // A Charge roll is 2D6, so 12" is the maximum pre-roll threat
            // guide. Actual charge movement still uses the rolled value and
            // full unit/coherency/target legality.
            radius = 12f;

            SetActionRangeRingColor(
                new Color(
                    1.0f,
                    0.28f,
                    0.18f,
                    0.95f
                )
            );
        }
        else if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
                 selectedModel != null &&
                 fightActivationUnit != null &&
                 ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                     selectedModel))
        {
            centre =
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position;

            if (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn ||
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.Consolidate)
            {
                float limit =
                    FightStageMoveLimit();

                Vector3 start;

                if (!fightStageStartPositions
                    .TryGetValue(
                        selectedModel,
                        out start))
                {
                    start =
                        selectedModel
                            .transform.position;
                }

                float used =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        start,
                        selectedModel
                            .transform.position
                    );

                radius =
                    Mathf.Max(
                        0f,
                        limit - used
                    );

                SetActionRangeRingColor(
                    fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.PileIn
                    ? new Color(
                        0.66f,
                        0.32f,
                        1.0f,
                        0.95f
                      )
                    : new Color(
                        0.20f,
                        0.90f,
                        0.52f,
                        0.95f
                      )
                );
            }
            else if (fightActivationStage ==
                     FightActivationStage.Attacks &&
                     !fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                         .Contains(
                             selectedModel))
            {
                radius =
                    EngagementRange;

                SetActionRangeRingColor(
                    new Color(
                        1.0f,
                        0.32f,
                        0.18f,
                        0.95f
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        if (radius <= 0.001f)
            return;

        centre.y = 0.065f;

        moveRing.enabled = true;

        for (int i = 0;
             i <
             moveRing.positionCount;
             i++)
        {
            float angle =
                i /
                (float)
                moveRing.positionCount *
                Mathf.PI *
                2f;

            moveRing.SetPosition(
                i,
                centre +
                new Vector3(
                    Mathf.Cos(angle) *
                        radius,
                    0f,
                    Mathf.Sin(angle) *
                        radius
                )
            );
        }
    }

    private void LoadArmy(string resourcePath)
    {
        TextAsset asset =
            Resources.Load<TextAsset>(resourcePath);

        if (asset == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError(
                "Missing army JSON: Resources/" +
                resourcePath +
                ".json"
            );

            return;
        }

        ArmyData army =
            JsonUtility.FromJson<ArmyData>(asset.text);

        if (!factions.Contains(army.factionId))
            factions.Add(army.factionId);

        foreach (ArmyEntryData entry in army.units)
        {
            SpawnSquad(
                entry.unitResource,
                new Vector3(entry.x, 0f, entry.z)
            );
        }
    }

    private void SpawnSquad(string resourcePath, Vector3 position)
    {
        TextAsset asset =
            Resources.Load<TextAsset>(resourcePath);

        if (asset == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError(
                "Missing unit JSON: Resources/" +
                resourcePath +
                ".json"
            );

            return;
        }

        UnitData data =
            JsonUtility.FromJson<UnitData>(asset.text);

        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(data.displayName);

        go.transform.position = position;

        SquadController squad =
            go.AddComponent<SquadController>();

        squad.Initialize(data);
        squads.Add(squad);
    }


    private void SpawnSquad(
        UnitData data,
        Vector3 position)
    {
        if (data == null)
            return;

        GameObject go =
            new GameObject(
                data.displayName
            );

        go.transform.position =
            position;

        SquadController squad =
            go.AddComponent<SquadController>();

        squad.Initialize(data);
        squads.Add(squad);
    }

    private void LoadBuiltInTestArmies()
    {
        ClearLoadedArmies();

        LoadArmy(
            "Armies/aeldari_test"
        );

        LoadArmy(
            "Armies/necron_test"
        );

        playerOneRosterLabel =
            "Aeldari";

        playerTwoRosterLabel =
            "Necrons";

        playerOneLoaded = true;
        playerTwoLoaded = true;

        OpenMissionSetup();
    }

    private void ClearLoadedArmies()
    {
        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad != null)
                Destroy(squad.gameObject);
        }

        squads.Clear();
        factions.Clear();
        score.Clear();
        primaryScoreByRound.Clear();
        secondaryScoreByRound.Clear();

        playerOneLoaded = false;
        playerTwoLoaded = false;

        playerOneRosterLabel =
            "Player 1";

        playerTwoRosterLabel =
            "Player 2";
    }

    private void BeginYellowScribeLoad(
        int playerIndex)
    {
        if (loadingYellowScribe)
            return;

        string code =
            playerIndex == 0
            ? yellowCodePlayerOne
            : yellowCodePlayerTwo;

        code =
            code == null
            ? ""
            : code.Trim();

        if (code.Length != 8)
        {
            status =
                "YellowScribe codes should be exactly 8 characters.";
            return;
        }

        StartCoroutine(
            LoadYellowScribeRoster(
                code,
                playerIndex
            )
        );
    }

    private IEnumerator LoadYellowScribeRoster(
        string code,
        int playerIndex)
    {
        loadingYellowScribe = true;

        status =
            "Loading YellowScribe roster " +
            code +
            "...";

        string url =
            YellowScribeEndpoint +
            UnityWebRequest.EscapeURL(
                code
            );

        using (UnityWebRequest request =
            UnityWebRequest.Get(url))
        {
            request.timeout = 20;

            yield return request.SendWebRequest();

            if (request.result !=
                UnityWebRequest.Result.Success)
            {
                loadingYellowScribe = false;

                status =
                    "YellowScribe request failed: " +
                    request.error +
                    ". If the code was created more than about 10 minutes ago, export a fresh one.";

                yield break;
            }

            string json =
                request.downloadHandler.text;

            YellowScribeImportResult imported;

            try
            {
                imported =
                    YellowScribeImporter.Parse(
                        json,
                        playerIndex == 0
                        ? "Player 1"
                        : "Player 2"
                    );
            }
            catch (
                System.Exception exception)
            {
                loadingYellowScribe = false;

                status =
                    "YellowScribe import failed: " +
                    exception.Message;

                yield break;
            }

            RemovePlayerArmy(
                playerIndex
            );

            string gameFactionId =
                playerIndex == 0
                ? "Player 1"
                : "Player 2";

            if (!factions.Contains(
                gameFactionId))
            {
                if (playerIndex == 0)
                    factions.Insert(
                        0,
                        gameFactionId
                    );
                else
                    factions.Add(
                        gameFactionId
                    );
            }

            int index = 0;

            foreach (UnitData unit
                in imported.Units)
            {
                // All imported models are staged for deployment immediately,
                // so these are only harmless temporary root positions.
                float x =
                    playerIndex == 0
                    ? -11f
                    : 11f;

                float z =
                    -9f +
                    (index % 8) *
                    2.4f;

                SpawnSquad(
                    unit,
                    new Vector3(
                        x,
                        0f,
                        z
                    )
                );

                index++;
            }

            if (playerIndex == 0)
            {
                playerOneLoaded = true;
                playerOneRosterLabel =
                    imported.SourceFaction;
            }
            else
            {
                playerTwoLoaded = true;
                playerTwoRosterLabel =
                    imported.SourceFaction;
            }

            loadingYellowScribe = false;

            status =
                "Loaded " +
                imported.Units.Count +
                " unit(s) for " +
                (playerIndex == 0
                    ? "Player 1"
                    : "Player 2") +
                " from YellowScribe" +
                (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    imported.Edition)
                    ? ""
                    : " (" +
                      imported.Edition +
                      ")") +
                ". Imported weapon assignments: " +
                imported.ImportedRangedWeaponInstances +
                " ranged / " +
                imported.ImportedMeleeWeaponInstances +
                " melee. Multi-weapon combat is active.";
        }
    }

    private void RemovePlayerArmy(
        int playerIndex)
    {
        string faction =
            playerIndex == 0
            ? "Player 1"
            : "Player 2";

        List<SquadController> toRemove =
            squads
                .Where(
                    squad =>
                        squad.FactionId ==
                        faction
                )
                .ToList();

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in toRemove)
        {
            squads.Remove(squad);

            if (squad != null)
                Destroy(squad.gameObject);
        }

        factions.Remove(faction);

        if (playerIndex == 0)
            playerOneLoaded = false;
        else
            playerTwoLoaded = false;
    }

    private void OpenMissionSetup()
    {
        if (!playerOneLoaded ||
            !playerTwoLoaded)
        {
            status =
                "Load both players before mission setup.";
            return;
        }

        factions.Clear();
        factions.Add("Player 1");
        factions.Add("Player 2");

        if (squads.Any(
            squad =>
                squad.FactionId ==
                "Aeldari"))
        {
            factions.Clear();
            factions.Add("Aeldari");
            factions.Add("Necrons");
        }

        if (aeldariRules != null)
        {
            aeldariRules.Configure(
                squads,
                factions
            );
        }

        armyImportMode = false;
        missionSetupMode = true;
        deploymentMode = false;

        status =
            "Select both Force Dispositions, secondary modes and one of the three recommended-layout slots.";
    }

    private void FinishArmyLoading()
    {
        if (factions.Count < 2)
        {
            status =
                "Mission setup requires two loaded armies.";
            return;
        }

        score.Clear();
        primaryScoreByRound.Clear();
        secondaryScoreByRound.Clear();
        fixedSecondaryScoreByCard.Clear();
        commandPoints.Clear();

        foreach (string faction
            in factions)
        {
            score[faction] = 0;

            primaryScoreByRound[faction] =
                new Dictionary<int, int>();

            secondaryScoreByRound[faction] =
                new Dictionary<int, int>();

            fixedSecondaryScoreByCard[faction] =
                new Dictionary<string, int>();

            commandPoints[faction] = 0;
        }

        ApplyMissionBattlefieldLayout(
            missionLayoutIndex
        );

        missionSystem =
            new MissionSystem(
                this,
                squads,
                objectives,
                factions
            );

        missionSystem.Configure(
            missionDispositionPlayerOne,
            missionDispositionPlayerTwo,
            missionSecondaryPlayerOne,
            missionSecondaryPlayerTwo,
            missionLayoutIndex,
            missionAttackerIndex
        );

        factionRules.Configure(
            squads,
            factions
        );

        if (aeldariRules != null)
        {
            aeldariRules.Configure(
                squads,
                factions
            );

            foreach (string faction
                in factions)
            {
                aeldariRules.ApplyDetachmentKeywords(
                    faction,
                    squads
                );

                if (aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                        faction))
                {
                    AppendBattleLog(
                        "AELDARI",
                        aeldariRules.DetachmentName(
                            faction
                        ),
                        aeldariRules.RuleSummary(
                            faction
                        )
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            squad.StageForDeployment();
        }

        missionSetupMode = false;
        deploymentMode = true;
        battleOver = false;
        battleSummary = "";

        BeginDeployment();
    }


    private void SetDeploymentWorldLabelsVisible(
        bool visible)
    {
        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null)
                continue;

            foreach (ModelToken model
                in squad.AllLivingModelTokens())
            {
                if (model == null)
                    continue;

                model.SetWoundDisplayVisible(
                    visible &&
                    squad.IsOnBattlefield
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private void BeginDeployment()
    {
        deploymentMode = true;
        SetDeploymentWorldLabelsVisible(false);
        round = 0;
        phase = Phase.Command;
        activeFactionIndex =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                missionAttackerIndex,
                0,
                Mathf.Max(
                    0,
                    factions.Count - 1
                )
            );

        if (factions.Count == 0)
        {
            status = "No factions loaded.";
            return;
        }

        activeFaction =
            factions[activeFactionIndex];

        currentDeploymentSquad =
            FindNextUndeployedForFaction(
                activeFaction
            );

        deploymentRosterScroll =
            Vector2.zero;

        if (currentDeploymentSquad == null)
        {
            AdvanceDeploymentTurn();
            return;
        }

        UpdateDeploymentStatus();
    }

    private List<SquadController>
        UndeployedUnitsForFaction(
            string faction)
    {
        return squads
            .Where(
                squad =>
                    squad.FactionId ==
                        faction &&
                    squad.BattlefieldState ==
                        SquadBattlefieldState.Undeployed &&
                    !squad.IsAttachedLeader
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private void ChooseDeploymentSquad(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        if (!deploymentMode ||
            squad == null ||
            squad.FactionId !=
                activeFaction ||
            squad.BattlefieldState !=
                SquadBattlefieldState.Undeployed)
        {
            return;
        }

        currentDeploymentSquad =
            squad;

        deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex = 0;

        UpdateDeploymentStatus();
    }

    private SquadController FindNextUndeployedForFaction(
        string faction)
    {
        return squads.FirstOrDefault(
            squad =>
                squad.FactionId == faction &&
                squad.BattlefieldState ==
                    SquadBattlefieldState.Undeployed
        );
    }

    private bool AnyUndeployedUnits()
    {
        return squads.Any(
            squad =>
                squad.BattlefieldState ==
                    SquadBattlefieldState.Undeployed
        );
    }

    private void AdvanceDeploymentTurn()
    {
        if (!AnyUndeployedUnits())
        {
            BeginBattle();
            return;
        }

        for (int step = 1;
             step <= factions.Count;
             step++)
        {
            int index =
                (activeFactionIndex + step) %
                factions.Count;

            SquadController next =
                FindNextUndeployedForFaction(
                    factions[index]
                );

            if (next != null)
            {
                activeFactionIndex = index;
                activeFaction =
                    factions[index];

                currentDeploymentSquad =
                    next;

                deploymentRosterScroll =
                    Vector2.zero;

                UpdateDeploymentStatus();
                return;
            }
        }

        BeginBattle();
    }

    private void UpdateDeploymentStatus()
    {
        if (currentDeploymentSquad == null)
        {
            status =
                "No unit waiting for deployment.";
            return;
        }

        string zone =
            activeMissionBattlefield != null
            ? activeMissionBattlefield.DisplayName +
              " " +
              (FactionUsesAttackerZone(
                   activeFaction)
                  ? "ATTACKER deployment zone"
                  : "DEFENDER deployment zone")
            : "deployment zone";

        status =
            "DEPLOYMENT — " +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            ": selected " +
            currentDeploymentSquad.DisplayName +
            ". Choose any undeployed unit from the roster, then place it wholly inside the " +
            zone +
            " or send it to Reserves. " +
            (currentDeploymentSquad.IsLeaderUnit
                ? "LEADER unit: attach it to a compatible bodyguard in the roster, whether or not it has deployed yet, or deploy it separately."
                : (currentDeploymentSquad.HasDeepStrike
                    ? "This unit has Deep Strike."
                    : "This unit will use Strategic Reserves if reserved."));
    }

    private void TryDeployCurrentSquad(
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (!deploymentMode ||
            currentDeploymentSquad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController deploying =
            currentDeploymentSquad;

        SquadController attachedLeader =
            deploying.AttachedLeader;

        destination.y = 0f;

        bool legal;

        if (attachedLeader != null &&
            !attachedLeader.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            legal =
                TryDeployJoinedFormation(
                    deploying,
                    attachedLeader,
                    destination
                );
        }
        else
        {
            Vector3 oldRoot =
                deploying.transform.position;

            deploying.DeployToBattlefield(
                destination
            );

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            legal =
                DeploymentLocationLegal(
                    deploying,
                    activeFaction
                ) &&
                deploying.IsCoherent() &&
                AllModelsInsideBoard(
                    deploying
                ) &&
                AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                    deploying
                );

            if (!legal)
            {
                bool zoneLegal =
                    DeploymentLocationLegal(
                        deploying,
                        activeFaction
                    );

                bool coherent =
                    deploying.IsCoherent();

                bool insideBoard =
                    AllModelsInsideBoard(
                        deploying
                    );

                bool placementClear =
                    AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                        deploying
                    );

                deploying.StageForDeployment();
                deploying.transform.position =
                    oldRoot;

                if (!zoneLegal)
                {
                    status =
                        "Illegal deployment: the unit is not wholly inside its deployment zone (or does not satisfy Infiltrators).";
                }
                else if (!insideBoard)
                {
                    status =
                        "Illegal deployment: part of the unit is outside the battlefield.";
                }
                else if (!coherent)
                {
                    status =
                        "Illegal deployment: the unit is not in Unit Coherency.";
                }
                else if (!placementClear)
                {
                    status =
                        "Illegal deployment: one or more models overlap a deployed model or blocking terrain.";
                }

                return;
            }
        }

        if (!legal)
            return;

        SquadController placed =
            deploying;

        currentDeploymentSquad = null;

        status =
            placed.DisplayName +
            (placed.AttachedLeader != null
                ? " + " +
                  placed.AttachedLeader.DisplayName +
                  " deployed as one Attached unit."
                : " deployed.");

        AdvanceDeploymentTurn();
    }

    private bool TryDeployJoinedFormation(
        SquadController bodyguard,
        SquadController leader,
        Vector3 requestedCentre)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            leader == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        // Deployment happens while both squads are still in the
        // Undeployed state. LivingModelTokens() intentionally returns an
        // empty list for off-battlefield squads, so use the deployment-safe
        // accessor that returns alive model objects regardless of battlefield
        // state.
        List<ModelToken> bodyModels =
            bodyguard.AllLivingModelTokens();

        List<ModelToken> leaderModels =
            leader.AllLivingModelTokens();

        if (bodyModels.Count == 0 ||
            leaderModels.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                "Attached Unit deployment state error: " +
