                bodyguard.DisplayName +
                " has " +
                bodyModels.Count +
                " model(s); " +
                leader.DisplayName +
                " has " +
                leaderModels.Count +
                " model(s).";
            return false;
        }

        Vector3 oldBodyRoot =
            bodyguard.transform.position;

        Vector3 oldLeaderRoot =
            leader.transform.position;

        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3>
            oldLocalPositions =
                new Dictionary<
                    ModelToken,
                    Vector3
                >();

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in bodyModels.Concat(
                leaderModels))
        {
            oldLocalPositions[model] =
                model.transform.localPosition;
        }

        bodyguard.DeployToBattlefield(
            requestedCentre
        );

        leader.DeployToBattlefield(
            requestedCentre
        );

        List<ModelToken> joined =
            new List<ModelToken>();

        joined.AddRange(
            bodyModels
        );

        // Put Leader models last so the attached Character naturally appears
        // on the outside/back of the generated bodyguard formation.
        joined.AddRange(
            leaderModels
        );

        float maxDiameter =
            joined.Max(
                model =>
                    Mathf.Max(
                        0.70f,
                        model.BaseRadiusInches *
                            2f
                    )
            );

        float spacing =
            maxDiameter +
            0.10f;

        int columns =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                Mathf.FloorToInt(
                    Mathf.Sqrt(
                        joined.Count
                    )
                ),
                2,
                Mathf.Max(
                    2,
                    joined.Count
                )
            );

        int rows =
            Mathf.CeilToInt(
                joined.Count /
                (float)columns
            );

        List<Vector3> offsets =
            new List<Vector3>();

        for (int i = 0;
             i < joined.Count;
             i++)
        {
            int row =
                i / columns;

            int column =
                i % columns;

            float x =
                (column -
                 (columns - 1) *
                 0.5f) *
                spacing;

            float z =
                (row -
                 (rows - 1) *
                 0.5f) *
                spacing;

            offsets.Add(
                new Vector3(
                    x,
                    0f,
                    z
                )
            );
        }

        // Account for the actual edge of each model's base when clamping the
        // generated formation into the battlefield/deployment zone.
        float minOffsetX =
            float.MaxValue;

        float maxOffsetX =
            float.MinValue;

        float minOffsetZ =
            float.MaxValue;

        float maxOffsetZ =
            float.MinValue;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < joined.Count;
             i++)
        {
            float radius =
                joined[i]
                    .BaseRadiusInches;

            minOffsetX =
                Mathf.Min(
                    minOffsetX,
                    offsets[i].x -
                    radius
                );

            maxOffsetX =
                Mathf.Max(
                    maxOffsetX,
                    offsets[i].x +
                    radius
                );

            minOffsetZ =
                Mathf.Min(
                    minOffsetZ,
                    offsets[i].z -
                    radius
                );

            maxOffsetZ =
                Mathf.Max(
                    maxOffsetZ,
                    offsets[i].z +
                    radius
                );
        }

        // Centre bounds are now battlefield-only. The active Chapter
        // Approved deployment polygon is validated separately for every base,
        // which allows diagonal and quarter deployment maps.
        float minCentreX =
            -BoardWidth * 0.5f -
            minOffsetX;

        float maxCentreX =
            BoardWidth * 0.5f -
            maxOffsetX;

        float minCentreZ =
            -BoardDepth * 0.5f -
            minOffsetZ;

        float maxCentreZ =
            BoardDepth * 0.5f -
            maxOffsetZ;

        if (minCentreX >
                maxCentreX ||
            minCentreZ >
                maxCentreZ)
        {
            RestoreFailedJoinedDeployment(
                bodyguard,
                leader,
                oldBodyRoot,
                oldLeaderRoot,
                oldLocalPositions
            );

            status =
                "Illegal deployment: this Attached unit physically cannot fit inside the current deployment zone. Choose a larger battlefield/deployment preset.";

            return false;
        }

        Vector3 clampedCentre =
            requestedCentre;

        clampedCentre.x =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                clampedCentre.x,
                minCentreX,
                maxCentreX
            );

        clampedCentre.z =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                clampedCentre.z,
                minCentreZ,
                maxCentreZ
            );

        clampedCentre.y = 0f;

        List<Vector3> centres =
            BuildDeploymentCentreCandidates(
                clampedCentre,
                minCentreX,
                maxCentreX,
                minCentreZ,
                maxCentreZ
            );

        string lastJoinedDeploymentFailure =
            "no legal formation candidate";

        foreach (Vector3 centre
            in centres)
        {
            for (int i = 0;
                 i < joined.Count;
                 i++)
            {
                Vector3 position =
                    centre +
                    offsets[i];

                position.y =
                    joined[i]
                        .transform.position.y;

                joined[i]
                    .transform.position =
                    position;
            }

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            string failureReason;

            if (JoinedDeploymentFormationIsLegal(
                    bodyguard,
                    joined,
                    activeFaction,
                    out failureReason))
            {
                bodyguard.RefreshVisuals();
                leader.RefreshVisuals();

                return true;
            }

            lastJoinedDeploymentFailure =
                failureReason;
        }

        RestoreFailedJoinedDeployment(
            bodyguard,
            leader,
            oldBodyRoot,
            oldLeaderRoot,
            oldLocalPositions
        );

        status =
            "Illegal Attached Unit deployment: " +
            lastJoinedDeploymentFailure +
            ".";

        return false;
    }

    private bool JoinedDeploymentFormationIsLegal(
        SquadController bodyguard,
        List<ModelToken> joined,
        string faction,
        out string failureReason)
    {
        failureReason = "";

        if (bodyguard == null ||
            joined == null ||
            joined.Count == 0)
        {
            failureReason =
                "the joined unit has no models";
            return false;
        }

        // 1) Every complete base must be inside the battlefield.
        foreach (ModelToken model
            in joined)
        {
            if (model == null ||
                !model.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!InsideBoard(
                    model,
                    model.transform.position))
            {
                failureReason =
                    model.RoleName +
                    " is outside the battlefield";
                return false;
            }
        }

        // 2) Every complete base must be wholly within the active
        // mission deployment polygon.
        MissionDeploymentZone zone =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                faction
            );

        if (zone == null)
        {
            failureReason =
                "no mission deployment zone is configured";
            return false;
        }

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in joined)
        {
            if (model == null ||
                !model.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!zone.ContainsBase(
                    model.transform.position,
                    model.BaseRadiusInches))
            {
                failureReason =
                    model.RoleName +
                    " is not wholly inside the " +
                    (activeMissionBattlefield != null
                        ? activeMissionBattlefield
                            .DisplayName
                        : "mission") +
                    " deployment zone";
                return false;
            }
        }

        // 3) Members of the same Attached Unit are checked once against
        // each other. They are NOT sent through CanPlaceModel, because that
        // generic method sees the Bodyguard and Leader as two SquadController
        // objects and can therefore treat members of the same joined unit as
        // battlefield blockers.
        for (int i = 0;
             i < joined.Count;
             i++)
        {
            ModelToken first =
                joined[i];

            if (first == null ||
                !first.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            for (int j = i + 1;
                 j < joined.Count;
                 j++)
            {
                ModelToken second =
                    joined[j];

                if (second == null ||
                    !second.IsAlive)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                float required =
                    MinimumModelSeparation(
                        first,
                        second
                    );

                float actual =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        first.transform.position,
                        second.transform.position
                    );

                if (actual <
                    required - 0.015f)
                {
                    failureReason =
                        "two models in the Attached Unit overlap (" +
                        first.RoleName +
                        " / " +
                        second.RoleName +
                        ")";
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        HashSet<ModelToken> joinedSet =
            new HashSet<ModelToken>(
                joined.Where(
                    model =>
                        model != null
                )
            );

        // 4) Only OTHER deployed units are external blockers.
        foreach (ModelToken model
            in joined)
        {
            if (model == null ||
                !model.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (SquadController squad
                in squads)
            {
                if (squad == null ||
                    !squad.IsOnBattlefield)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (ModelToken other
                    in squad.LivingModelTokens())
                {
                    if (other == null ||
                        !other.IsAlive ||
                        joinedSet.Contains(
                            other
                        ))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (HorizontalDistance(
                            model.transform.position,
                            other.transform.position
                        ) <
                        MinimumModelSeparation(
                            model,
                            other
                        ) -
                        0.015f)
                    {
                        failureReason =
                            model.RoleName +
                            " overlaps deployed unit " +
                            squad.DisplayName;
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (ModelOverlapsBlockingTerrain(
                    model,
                    model.transform.position))
            {
                failureReason =
                    model.RoleName +
                    " overlaps blocking terrain";
                return false;
            }
        }

        // 5) Coherency is evaluated last on the completed joined formation.
        if (!bodyguard.IsCoherent())
        {
            failureReason =
                "the generated Bodyguard + Leader formation is not coherent";
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool ModelOverlapsBlockingTerrain(
        ModelToken model,
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (model == null)
            return false;

        float probeRadius =
            Mathf.Max(
                0.20f,
                model.BaseRadiusInches *
                    0.85f
            );

        Vector3 probe =
            destination +
            Vector3.up * 0.35f;

        Collider[] overlaps =
            Physics.OverlapSphere(
                probe,
                probeRadius
            );

        foreach (Collider col
            in overlaps)
        {
            if (col == null)
                continue;

            TerrainFeature terrain =
                col.GetComponent<
                    TerrainFeature
                >();

            if (terrain == null)
            {
                terrain =
                    col.GetComponentInParent<
                        TerrainFeature
                    >();
            }

            if (terrain != null &&
                terrain.BlocksMovement)
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private List<Vector3>
        BuildDeploymentCentreCandidates(
            Vector3 requested,
            float minX,
            float maxX,
            float minZ,
            float maxZ)
    {
        List<Vector3> result =
            new List<Vector3>();

        result.Add(
            requested
        );

        float[] distances =
        {
            0.75f,
            1.5f,
            2.5f,
            4.0f,
            6.0f,
            8.0f,
            10.0f,
            14.0f
        };

        foreach (float distance
            in distances)
        {
            for (int angle = 0;
                 angle < 360;
                 angle += 15)
            {
                float radians =
                    angle *
                    Mathf.Deg2Rad;

                Vector3 candidate =
                    requested +
                    new Vector3(
                        Mathf.Cos(radians) *
                            distance,
                        0f,
                        Mathf.Sin(radians) *
                            distance
                    );

                candidate.x =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        candidate.x,
                        minX,
                        maxX
                    );

                candidate.z =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        candidate.z,
                        minZ,
                        maxZ
                    );

                bool duplicate =
                    result.Any(
                        existing =>
                            HorizontalDistance(
                                existing,
                                candidate
                            ) <
                            0.05f
                    );

                if (!duplicate)
                {
                    result.Add(
                        candidate
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        // Polygon maps can have legal pockets that are not reachable by a
        // short radial search from the click. Add a coarse whole-battlefield
        // fallback, then keep candidates ordered by distance from the user's
        // requested point.
        for (float x = minX;
             x <= maxX + 0.001f;
             x += 3.0f)
        {
            for (float z = minZ;
                 z <= maxZ + 0.001f;
                 z += 3.0f)
            {
                Vector3 candidate =
                    new Vector3(
                        x,
                        0f,
                        z
                    );

                if (!result.Any(
                    existing =>
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            existing,
                            candidate
                        ) <
                        0.10f))
                {
                    result.Add(
                        candidate
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        return result
            .OrderBy(
                candidate =>
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        requested,
                        candidate
                    )
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private void RestoreFailedJoinedDeployment(
        SquadController bodyguard,
        SquadController leader,
        Vector3 oldBodyRoot,
        Vector3 oldLeaderRoot,
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3>
            oldLocalPositions)
    {
        bodyguard.StageForDeployment();
        leader.StageForDeployment();

        bodyguard.transform.position =
            oldBodyRoot;

        leader.transform.position =
            oldLeaderRoot;

        foreach (
            KeyValuePair<
                ModelToken,
                Vector3
            > pair
            in oldLocalPositions)
        {
            if (pair.Key != null)
            {
                pair.Key
                    .transform.localPosition =
                    pair.Value;
            }
        }

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
    }


    private bool DeploymentLocationLegal(
        SquadController squad,
        string faction)
    {
        if (SquadFitsDeploymentZone(
                squad,
                faction))
        {
            return true;
        }

        bool infiltrators =
            UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                squad,
                "Infiltrators"
            );

        if (squad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            infiltrators =
                infiltrators &&
                UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                    squad.AttachedLeader,
                    "Infiltrators"
                );
        }

        if (!infiltrators)
            return false;

        if (activeMissionBattlefield == null)
            return false;

        string opponentFaction =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                value =>
                    value != faction
            );

        MissionDeploymentZone opponentZone =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                opponentFaction
            );

        if (opponentZone == null)
            return false;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            if (model == null)
                continue;

            float distanceToOpponentZone =
                opponentZone.DistanceToZone(
                    model.transform.position
                ) -
                model.BaseRadiusInches;

            if (distanceToOpponentZone <=
                8.0f + 0.001f)
            {
                return false;
            }

            foreach (SquadController enemy
                in squads)
            {
                if (enemy == null ||
                    enemy.FactionId == faction ||
                    !enemy.IsAlive ||
                    !enemy.IsOnBattlefield)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (ModelToken enemyModel
                    in JoinedModels(enemy))
                {
                    if (HorizontalDistance(
                            model.transform.position,
                            enemyModel.transform.position
                        ) <=
                        8.0f)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool SquadFitsDeploymentZone(
        SquadController squad,
        string faction)
    {
        if (squad == null)
            return false;

        MissionDeploymentZone zone =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                faction
            );

        if (zone == null)
        {
            // Custom/open-war fallback.
            float boardMin =
                -BoardWidth * 0.5f;

            float boardMax =
                BoardWidth * 0.5f;

            float minX;
            float maxX;

            if (factions.Count > 0 &&
                faction == factions[0])
            {
                minX = boardMin;
                maxX =
                    boardMin +
                    DeploymentZoneWidth;
            }
            else
            {
                minX =
                    boardMax -
                    DeploymentZoneWidth;

                maxX = boardMax;
            }

            foreach (ModelToken model
                in JoinedModels(squad))
            {
                float radius =
                    model != null
                    ? model.BaseRadiusInches
                    : 0f;

                if (model.transform.position.x -
                        radius <
                        minX - 0.001f ||
                    model.transform.position.x +
                        radius >
                        maxX + 0.001f)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            if (model == null)
                continue;

            if (!zone.ContainsBase(
                    model.transform.position,
                    model.BaseRadiusInches))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private List<SquadController>
        CompatibleDeploymentBodyguards()
    {
        if (currentDeploymentSquad == null ||
            !currentDeploymentSquad.IsLeaderUnit)
        {
            return new List<SquadController>();
        }

        return squads
            .Where(
                squad =>
                    squad.IsAlive &&
                    squad.BattlefieldState !=
                        SquadBattlefieldState.Reserves &&
                    currentDeploymentSquad
                        .CanAttachTo(squad)
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private SquadController
        CurrentDeploymentBodyguardTarget()
    {
        List<SquadController> candidates =
            CompatibleDeploymentBodyguards();

        if (candidates.Count == 0)
            return null;

        deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex,
                0,
                candidates.Count - 1
            );

        return candidates[
            deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex
        ];
    }

    private void CycleDeploymentBodyguardTarget()
    {
        List<SquadController> candidates =
            CompatibleDeploymentBodyguards();

        if (candidates.Count <= 1)
            return;

        deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex =
            (deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex + 1) %
            candidates.Count;

        SquadController target =
            candidates[
                deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex
            ];

        status =
            "Selected bodyguard for " +
            currentDeploymentSquad.DisplayName +
            ": " +
            target.DisplayName +
            ".";
    }

    private void AttachCurrentLeader()
    {
        List<SquadController> candidates =
            CompatibleDeploymentBodyguards();

        if (currentDeploymentSquad == null ||
            !currentDeploymentSquad.IsLeaderUnit)
        {
            status =
                "The current deployment unit is not a Leader.";
            return;
        }

        if (candidates.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                "No compatible bodyguard exists in this roster/deployment state.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController leader =
            currentDeploymentSquad;

        SquadController bodyguard =
            CurrentDeploymentBodyguardTarget();

        if (bodyguard == null)
        {
            status =
                "No compatible bodyguard exists in this roster/deployment state.";
            return;
        }

        if (!leader.AttachToBodyguard(
            bodyguard))
        {
            status =
                "Leader attachment failed.";
            return;
        }

        if (bodyguard.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            // Re-layout the complete Attached Unit around the Bodyguard's
            // current centre rather than trying to squeeze the Leader into a
            // single leftover gap.
            Vector3 joinedCentre =
                bodyguard.CurrentCentre();

            if (!TryDeployJoinedFormation(
                    bodyguard,
                    leader,
                    joinedCentre))
            {
                leader.DetachFromBodyguard();
                leader.StageForDeployment();

                status =
                    "Leader attachment could not be completed: " +
                    status;
                return;
            }

            currentDeploymentSquad = null;

            status =
                leader.DisplayName +
                " attached to deployed " +
                bodyguard.DisplayName +
                " and the complete Attached Unit was re-formed legally.";

            AdvanceDeploymentTurn();
            return;
        }

        leader.StageForDeployment();

        currentDeploymentSquad =
            bodyguard;

        deploymentRosterScroll =
            Vector2.zero;

        status =
            leader.DisplayName +
            " attached to " +
            bodyguard.DisplayName +
            ". Place the joined unit now; the Leader deploys with its bodyguard.";
    }

    private bool TryPlaceLeaderNextToBodyguard(
        SquadController leader,
        SquadController bodyguard)
    {
        if (leader == null ||
            bodyguard == null ||
            !bodyguard.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            return false;
        }

        List<ModelToken> bodyguardModels =
            bodyguard.IsOnBattlefield
            ? bodyguard.LivingModelTokens()
            : bodyguard.AllLivingModelTokens();

        ModelToken leaderModel =
            (leader.IsOnBattlefield
                ? leader.LivingModelTokens()
                : leader.AllLivingModelTokens())
                .FirstOrDefault();

        if (bodyguardModels.Count == 0 ||
            leaderModel == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        Vector3 originalLeaderRoot =
            leader.transform.position;

        Vector3 bodyCentre =
            bodyguard.CurrentCentre();

        // Build a much denser set of candidate positions than the old
        // eight-direction ring. Large joined units can sit close to a
        // deployment-zone edge, so a valid Leader position may be at an
        // oblique angle between two Bodyguard models.
        List<Vector3> candidates =
            new List<Vector3>();

        foreach (ModelToken anchor
            in bodyguardModels
                .OrderByDescending(
                    model =>
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            model.transform.position,
                            bodyCentre
                        )
                ))
        {
            float contactDistance =
                MinimumModelSeparation(
                    anchor,
                    leaderModel
                );

            float[] radii =
            {
                contactDistance + 0.04f,
                contactDistance + 0.16f,
                Mathf.Min(
                    1.75f,
                    contactDistance + 0.38f
                )
            };

            Vector3 outward =
                anchor.transform.position -
                bodyCentre;

            outward.y = 0f;

            if (outward.sqrMagnitude <
                0.001f)
            {
                outward =
                    Vector3.forward;
            }

            outward.Normalize();

            for (int angle = 0;
                 angle < 360;
                 angle += 15)
            {
                Vector3 direction =
                    Quaternion.Euler(
                        0f,
                        angle,
                        0f
                    ) *
                    outward;

                foreach (float radius
                    in radii)
                {
                    Vector3 candidate =
                        anchor.transform.position +
                        direction *
                        radius;

                    candidate.y = 0f;

                    candidates.Add(
                        candidate
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        // Also sample a ring around the joined unit centre. This catches a
        // legal gap outside a dense formation even when no single anchor's
        // outward vector points directly at it.
        float bodyExtent = 0f;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in bodyguardModels)
        {
            bodyExtent =
                Mathf.Max(
                    bodyExtent,
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        bodyCentre,
                        model.transform.position
                    ) +
                    model.BaseRadiusInches
                );
        }

        float leaderRing =
            bodyExtent +
            leaderModel.BaseRadiusInches +
            0.08f;

        for (int angle = 0;
             angle < 360;
             angle += 10)
        {
            float radians =
                angle *
                Mathf.Deg2Rad;

            candidates.Add(
                new Vector3(
                    bodyCentre.x +
                        Mathf.Cos(radians) *
                        leaderRing,
                    0f,
                    bodyCentre.z +
                        Mathf.Sin(radians) *
                        leaderRing
                )
            );
        }

        // Prefer positions close to the bodyguard while remaining in the
        // active player's deployment zone.
        candidates =
            candidates
                .OrderBy(
                    candidate =>
                        bodyguardModels.Min(
                            model =>
                                HorizontalDistance(
                                    candidate,
                                    model.transform.position
                                )
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        foreach (Vector3 candidate
            in candidates)
        {
            leader.DeployToBattlefield(
                candidate
            );

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            if (!LeaderDeploymentCandidateIsLegal(
                    leader,
                    bodyguard,
                    leaderModel))
            {
                leader.StageForDeployment();
                continue;
            }

            return true;
        }

        leader.StageForDeployment();
        leader.transform.position =
            originalLeaderRoot;

        return false;
    }

    private bool LeaderDeploymentCandidateIsLegal(
        SquadController leader,
        SquadController bodyguard,
        ModelToken leaderModel)
    {
        if (leader == null ||
            bodyguard == null ||
            leaderModel == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!InsideBoard(
                leaderModel.transform.position))
        {
            return false;
        }

        // The joined unit must remain wholly in the correct deployment zone.
        // Use the bodyguard's faction because it is the unit currently being
        // deployed; this avoids any display-faction naming differences on
        // imported Leader datasheets.
        if (!SquadFitsDeploymentZone(
                bodyguard,
                bodyguard.FactionId))
        {
            return false;
        }

        // The Leader itself must not overlap any already deployed model or
        // blocking terrain. CanPlaceModel deliberately ignores off-board
        // undeployed/reserve units as of v26.7.
        if (!CanPlaceModel(
                leaderModel,
                leaderModel.transform.position))
        {
            return false;
        }

        float nearestBodyguard =
            bodyguard.LivingModelTokens()
                .Min(
                    model =>
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            leaderModel.transform.position,
                            model.transform.position
                        )
                );

        if (nearestBodyguard >
            2.0f + 0.001f)
        {
            return false;
        }

        // Validate coherency only after the physical/zone checks. This uses
        // the joined bodyguard+Leader model list because the attachment is
        // already established before deployment.
        if (!bodyguard.IsCoherent())
        {
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void SendCurrentDeploymentToReserves()
    {
        if (!deploymentMode ||
            currentDeploymentSquad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController reserved =
            currentDeploymentSquad;

        reserved.SendToReserves();

        if (reserved.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            reserved.AttachedLeader
                .SendToReserves();
        }

        currentDeploymentSquad = null;

        status =
            reserved.DisplayName +
            " placed in Reserves.";

        AdvanceDeploymentTurn();
    }

    private void StartFactionBattleRoundRules()
    {
        if (factionRules != null)
        {
            factionRules.StartBattleRound(
                round,
                squads
            );
        }

        if (aeldariRules != null)
        {
            foreach (string faction
                in factions)
            {
                aeldariRules.StartBattleRound(
                    faction,
                    squads
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private void BeginBattle()
    {
        deploymentMode = false;
        SetDeploymentWorldLabelsVisible(true);
        currentDeploymentSquad = null;
        reservePlacementSquad = null;
        reserveCycleIndex = -1;

        round = 1;
        turnsCompletedThisRound = 0;

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            traditionalFirstTurnPending =
                true;

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                2
            );

            AppendBattleLog(
                "ROLL-OFF",
                "First turn",
                "Players resolve the first-turn roll-off manually. Warboard only needs to know which player won."
            );

            status =
                "TRADITIONAL FIRST-TURN ROLL-OFF: roll one D6 each, reroll ties yourself, then select the winner.";

            return;
        }

        int p1Roll;
        int p2Roll;

        do
        {
            p1Roll =
                DiceRoller.RollD6(
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        factions[0]
                    ) +
                    " first-turn roll"
                );

            p2Roll =
                DiceRoller.RollD6(
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        factions[1]
                    ) +
                    " first-turn roll"
                );
        }
        while (p1Roll == p2Roll);

        firstTurnFactionIndex =
            p1Roll > p2Roll
            ? 0
            : 1;

        activeFactionIndex =
            firstTurnFactionIndex;

        activeFaction =
            factions[
                activeFactionIndex
            ];

        firstTurnRollSummary =
            DisplayFactionName(
                factions[0]
            ) +
            " " +
            p1Roll +
            " vs " +
            DisplayFactionName(
                factions[1]
            ) +
            " " +
            p2Roll +
            " — " +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " wins the roll-off and takes first turn.";

        StartFactionBattleRoundRules();

        CaptureObjectiveControlAtPhaseStart();
        ClearPhaseDestructionRecords();

        BeginActiveTurn(
            true
        );
    }


    private void CompleteTraditionalFirstTurnRollOff(
        int winnerIndex)
    {
        if (!traditionalFirstTurnPending ||
            winnerIndex < 0 ||
            winnerIndex >= factions.Count)
        {
            return;
        }

        traditionalFirstTurnPending = false;

        firstTurnFactionIndex =
            winnerIndex;

        activeFactionIndex =
            firstTurnFactionIndex;

        activeFaction =
            factions[
                activeFactionIndex
            ];

        firstTurnRollSummary =
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " won the manually resolved first-turn roll-off and takes first turn.";

        AppendBattleLog(
            "ROLL-OFF",
            "First turn",
            firstTurnRollSummary
        );

        StartFactionBattleRoundRules();

        CaptureObjectiveControlAtPhaseStart();
        ClearPhaseDestructionRecords();

        BeginActiveTurn(
            true
        );
    }

    private void BeginActiveTurn(
        bool firstTurn)
    {
        phase = Phase.Command;
        resurrectionOrbUsedThisTurn = false;

        if (factionRules != null)
        {
            factionRules.StartTurn(
                activeFaction
            );
        }

        CaptureObjectiveControlAtPhaseStart();
        ClearPhaseDestructionRecords();
        stratagemsUsedThisPhase.Clear();

        if (missionSystem != null)
        {
            missionSystem.BeginTurn(
                activeFaction,
                round
            );
        }

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad.IsAlive &&
                squad.FactionId ==
                    activeFaction)
            {
                squad.StartTurn();
            }
        }

        // 11e Core CP: both players gain 1 CP at the start of each
        // Command phase.
        foreach (string faction
            in factions)
        {
            GainCommandPoints(
                faction,
                1
            );
        }

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.TurnStarted,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    activeFaction,
                Phase = phase,
                Note =
                    firstTurn
                    ? "First turn"
                    : "New turn"
            }
        );

        RaisePhaseStarted();

        string battleShockSummary =
            ResolveCommandPhaseBattleShock();

        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        status =
            (firstTurn &&
             !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                firstTurnRollSummary)
                ? firstTurnRollSummary +
                  " | "
                : "") +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " Command phase. BOTH players gain +1 CP. " +
            CommandPointText() +
            (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                battleShockSummary)
                ? " | No Battle-shock tests required."
                : " | " +
                  battleShockSummary);
    }

    private int EffectiveLeadership(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return 7;

        int leadership =
            unit.BestLeadership();

        if (unit.HasKeyword(
                "wraith construct") &&
            FriendlyKeywordWithin(
                unit,
                "aeldari",
                "psyker",
                12f))
        {
            leadership =
                Mathf.Min(
                    leadership,
                    6
                );
        }

        return leadership;
    }

    private string ResolveCommandPhaseBattleShock()
    {
        List<string> results =
            new List<string>();

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            traditionalBattleShockQueue.Clear();
            traditionalBattleShockUnit = null;
            traditionalBattleShockPending = false;
            traditionalBattleShockLabel =
                "Command phase Battle-shock";

            foreach (SquadController squad
                in squads)
            {
                if (squad == null ||
                    !squad.IsAlive ||
                    !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
                    squad.IsAttachedLeader ||
                    squad.FactionId !=
                        activeFaction)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                SquadController unit =
                    squad.JoinedActionController();

                bool mustTest =
                    unit.IsBattleShocked ||
                    unit.IsAtOrBelowHalfStrength();

                if (!mustTest)
                    continue;

                traditionalBattleShockQueue.Enqueue(
                    unit
                );
            }

            if (traditionalBattleShockQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                BeginNextTraditionalBattleShock();

                return
                    "Traditional: " +
                    (traditionalBattleShockQueue.Count +
                     (traditionalBattleShockPending
                        ? 1
                        : 0)) +
                    " Battle-shock test(s) require manual tabletop resolution.";
            }

            return "";
        }

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null ||
                !squad.IsAlive ||
                !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
                squad.IsAttachedLeader ||
                squad.FactionId !=
                    activeFaction)
            {
                continue;
            }

            SquadController unit =
                squad.JoinedActionController();

            bool mustTest =
                unit.IsBattleShocked ||
                unit.IsAtOrBelowHalfStrength();

            if (!mustTest)
                continue;

            int leadership =
                EffectiveLeadership(
                    unit
                );

            int roll =
                DiceRoller.Roll2D6(
                    "Battle-shock: " +
                    unit.DisplayName
                );

            bool passed =
                roll >= leadership;

            unit.SetBattleShocked(
                !passed,
                roll
            );

            AppendBattleLog(
                "BATTLE-SHOCK",
                unit.DisplayName,
                "Leadership " +
                leadership +
                "+ • roll " +
                roll +
                " → " +
                (passed
                    ? "PASS"
                    : "FAIL — BATTLE-SHOCKED (OC 0)")
            );

            results.Add(
                unit.DisplayName +
                " " +
                roll +
                " vs Ld " +
                leadership +
                (passed
                    ? "+ PASS"
                    : "+ FAIL → BATTLE-SHOCKED")
            );
        }

        return string.Join(
            " | ",
            results.ToArray()
        );
    }

    private void BeginNextTraditionalBattleShock()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode)
            return;

        while (traditionalBattleShockQueue.Count > 0)
        {
            SquadController unit =
                traditionalBattleShockQueue.Dequeue();

            if (unit == null ||
                !unit.IsAlive ||
                !unit.IsOnBattlefield)
            {
                continue;
            }

            traditionalBattleShockUnit =
                unit.JoinedActionController();

            traditionalBattleShockPending =
                true;

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                2
            );

            status =
                "BATTLE-SHOCK TEST REQUIRED: " +
                traditionalBattleShockUnit.DisplayName +
                ". Roll 2D6 manually, apply any tabletop rules yourself, then mark PASS or FAIL.";

            return;
        }

        traditionalBattleShockPending = false;
        traditionalBattleShockUnit = null;

        status =
            "All required Traditional Battle-shock tests have been marked.";
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalBattleShock(
        bool passed)
    {
        if (!traditionalBattleShockPending ||
            traditionalBattleShockUnit == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            traditionalBattleShockUnit;

        int leadership =
            EffectiveLeadership(
                unit
            );

        unit.SetBattleShocked(
            !passed,
            0
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "BATTLE-SHOCK",
            unit.DisplayName,
            traditionalBattleShockLabel +
            " • Leadership " +
            leadership +
            "+ • player marked " +
            (passed
                ? "PASS"
                : "FAIL — BATTLE-SHOCKED") +
            ". Physical dice were not interpreted by Warboard."
        );

        traditionalBattleShockPending = false;
        traditionalBattleShockUnit = null;

        BeginNextTraditionalBattleShock();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();
    }

    private void HandleLoggedDiceRoll(
        DiceRollRecord record)
    {
        if (record == null)
            return;

        string results =
            record.Results != null &&
            record.Results.Length > 0
            ? "[" +
              string.Join(
                  ", ",
                  record.Results
                      .Select(
                          value =>
                              value.ToString()
                      )
                      .ToArray()
              ) +
              "]"
            : "[]";

        AppendBattleLog(
            "ROLL",
            record.Label,
            results +
            " = " +
            record.Total
        );
    }

    public void AppendBattleLog(
        string category,
        string title,
        string detail)
    {
        WarboardBattleLogEntry entry =
            new WarboardBattleLogEntry
            {
                Sequence =
                    ++battleLogSequence,
                Round = round,
                Faction =
                    activeFaction ?? "",
                Phase =
                    deploymentMode
                    ? "DEPLOYMENT"
                    : phase.ToString()
                        .ToUpper(),
                Category =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        category)
                    ? "EVENT"
                    : category,
                Title =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        title)
                    ? "Event"
                    : title,
                Detail =
                    detail ?? "",
                Time =
                    System.DateTime.Now
            };

        battleLog.Add(
            entry
        );

        while (battleLog.Count > 300)
        {
            battleLog.RemoveAt(0);
        }
    }

    private string ObjectiveLiveState(
        ObjectiveController objective)
    {
        if (objective == null)
            return "Uncontrolled";

        Dictionary<string, int> totals =
            objective.ObjectiveControlTotals(
                squads
            );

        string controller =
            objective.Controller(
                squads
            );

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                controller))
        {
            return controller;
        }

        return totals.Count > 0
            ? "Contested"
            : "Uncontrolled";
    }

    private void RefreshObjectiveDisplays()
    {
        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in objectives)
        {
            if (objective == null)
                continue;

            objective.RefreshStatus(
                squads
            );

            string liveState =
                ObjectiveLiveState(
                    objective
                );

            string previous = "";

            bool known =
                objectiveLiveControl
                    .TryGetValue(
                        objective,
                        out previous
                    );

            objectiveLiveControl[
                objective] =
                liveState;

            if (known &&
                previous != liveState &&
                !deploymentMode &&
                round > 0)
            {
                AppendBattleLog(
                    "OBJECTIVE",
                    objective.name,
                    previous +
                    " → " +
                    liveState
                );
            }
        }

        List<ObjectiveController> stale =
            objectiveLiveControl
                .Keys
                .Where(
                    objective =>
                        objective == null ||
                        !objectives.Contains(
                            objective
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in stale)
        {
            objectiveLiveControl.Remove(
                objective
            );
        }
    }


    private void RaisePhaseStarted()
    {
        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.PhaseStarted,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    activeFaction,
                Phase = phase
            }
        );
    }

    public int GetCommandPoints(
        string faction)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            faction))
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int value;

        return commandPoints.TryGetValue(
            faction,
            out value)
            ? value
            : 0;
    }

    public void GainCommandPoints(
        string faction,
        int amount)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            faction) ||
            amount <= 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (!commandPoints.ContainsKey(
            faction))
        {
            commandPoints[faction] = 0;
        }

        commandPoints[faction] +=
            amount;
    }

    public bool TrySpendCommandPoints(
        string faction,
        int amount)
    {
        if (amount <= 0)
            return true;

        int current =
            GetCommandPoints(
                faction
            );

        if (current < amount)
            return false;

        commandPoints[faction] =
            current -
            amount;

        return true;
    }


    public bool ArmCommandReroll(
        CommandRerollStage stage)
    {
        if (stage ==
            CommandRerollStage.None)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (phase != Phase.Shoot &&
            phase != Phase.Fight)
        {
            status =
                "Hit/Wound Command Re-roll can only be armed during Shoot or Fight.";
            return false;
        }

        if (GetCommandPoints(
            activeFaction) < 1)
        {
            status =
                ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
                " has no CP available.";
            return false;
        }

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            if (!TrySpendCommandPoints(
                    activeFaction,
                    1))
            {
                return false;
            }

            showStratagemMenu = false;
            ClearArmedCommandReroll();

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                1
            );

            AppendBattleLog(
                "STRATAGEM",
                "Command Re-roll",
                ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
                " spent 1 CP for Command Re-roll (" +
                stage.ToString() +
                "). Player performs the reroll manually."
            );

            status =
                "COMMAND RE-ROLL: 1 CP spent. Reroll the appropriate physical die yourself; Warboard will not generate or judge the result.";

            return true;
        }

        armedCommandRerollStage =
            stage;

        armedCommandRerollFaction =
            activeFaction;

        showStratagemMenu = false;

        status =
            "Command Re-roll armed for the next failed " +
            stage.ToString().ToUpper() +
            " roll made by " +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            ". 1 CP is spent only if the reroll is actually triggered.";

        return true;
    }

    public bool TryConsumeArmedCommandReroll(
        string faction,
        CommandRerollStage stage,
        int originalRoll,
        int requiredRoll,
        SquadController source,
        SquadController target,
        out int rerolledValue)
    {
        rerolledValue =
            originalRoll;

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            if (armedCommandRerollStage ==
                    stage &&
                string.Equals(
                    armedCommandRerollFaction,
                    faction,
                    System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                status =
                    "TRADITIONAL COMMAND RE-ROLL: reroll the physical die yourself. Warboard will not generate or validate the replacement result.";

                ClearArmedCommandReroll();
            }

            return false;
        }

        if (stage ==
            CommandRerollStage.None ||
            armedCommandRerollStage !=
                stage ||
            !string.Equals(
                armedCommandRerollFaction,
                faction,
                System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (source != null &&
            source
                .JoinedActionController()
                .IsBattleShocked)
        {
            ClearArmedCommandReroll();

            status =
                source.DisplayName +
                " is Battle-shocked and cannot be targeted by Command Re-roll.";

            return false;
        }

        // Only trigger the armed reroll when the roll actually failed.
        if (originalRoll >=
            requiredRoll)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (source == null ||
            !SpendStratagemCPForUnit(
                source,
                1,
                "Command Re-roll"))
        {
            ClearArmedCommandReroll();

            return false;
        }

        rerolledValue =
            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                "Command Re-roll: " +
                stage.ToString()
            );

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    stage ==
                    CommandRerollStage.Hit
                    ? GameEventType.HitRolled
                    : GameEventType.WoundRolled,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    faction,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = source,
                Target = target,
                RollTotal =
                    rerolledValue,
                PreviousRollTotal =
                    originalRoll,
                IsReroll = true,
                Note =
                    "Command Re-roll"
            }
        );

        status =
            "Command Re-roll: " +
            originalRoll +
            " → " +
            rerolledValue +
            " on a " +
            stage.ToString().ToLowerInvariant() +
            " roll. " +
            GetCommandPoints(
                faction
            ) +
            " CP remaining.";

        ClearArmedCommandReroll();

        return true;
    }

    public void ClearUnusedArmedCommandRerollAfterAttack(
        string faction)
    {
        if (armedCommandRerollStage ==
                CommandRerollStage.None ||
            !string.Equals(
                armedCommandRerollFaction,
                faction,
                System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return;
        }

        // The attack had no failed roll at the armed stage. No CP was spent.
        ClearArmedCommandReroll();
    }

    private void ClearArmedCommandReroll()
    {
        armedCommandRerollStage =
            CommandRerollStage.None;

        armedCommandRerollFaction =
            "";
    }

    private string ArmedCommandRerollText()
    {
        if (armedCommandRerollStage ==
            CommandRerollStage.None)
        {
            return "None";
        }

        return
            "Command Re-roll: next failed " +
            armedCommandRerollStage.ToString();
    }

    private List<SquadController> ActiveFactionReserves()
    {
        return squads
            .Where(
                squad =>
                    squad.FactionId ==
                        activeFaction &&
                    !squad.IsAttachedLeader &&
                    squad.IsInReserves &&
                    squad.IsAlive
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private int EffectiveReserveRound(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        return
            round +
            (aeldariRules != null &&
             aeldariRules
                .TreatReserveRoundAsOneHigher(
                    unit
                )
                ? 1
                : 0);
    }

    private bool ReserveCanArriveThisRound(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return false;

        return
            unit.MustIngressFromVeil ||
            EffectiveReserveRound(
                unit
            ) >= 2;
    }

    private void CycleReservePlacement()
    {
        if (deploymentMode)
            return;

        if (phase != Phase.Move)
        {
            status =
                "Reserves can only arrive in the Move phase.";
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> available =
            ActiveFactionReserves()
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        ReserveCanArriveThisRound(
                            unit
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (available.Count == 0)
        {
            reservePlacementSquad = null;
            status =
                activeFaction +
                " has no units waiting in Reserves.";
            return;
        }

        reserveCycleIndex =
            (reserveCycleIndex + 1) %
            available.Count;

        reservePlacementSquad =
            available[
                reserveCycleIndex
            ];

        string method =
            reservePlacementSquad.HasDeepStrike
            ? "DEEP STRIKE: place anywhere on the board, more than 8 from every enemy model."
            : "STRATEGIC RESERVE: place within 6 of a board edge and more than 8 from every enemy model.";

        status =
            "Reserve selected: " +
            reservePlacementSquad.DisplayName +
            ". " +
            method +
            " Click the board to attempt placement.";
    }

    private bool TryPlaceAttachedLeaderAfterReserveArrival(
        SquadController bodyguard)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null ||
            !bodyguard.AttachedLeader.IsAlive)
        {
            return true;
        }

        SquadController leader =
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader;

        List<ModelToken> bodyModels =
            bodyguard.LivingModelTokens();

        if (bodyModels.Count == 0)
            return false;

        ModelToken leaderModel =
            leader.LivingModelTokens()
                .FirstOrDefault();

        if (leaderModel == null)
            return false;

        foreach (ModelToken anchor
            in bodyModels)
        {
            float leaderSpacing =
                MinimumModelSeparation(
                    anchor,
                    leaderModel
                ) +
                0.06f;

            for (int angle = 0;
                 angle < 360;
                 angle += 30)
            {
                float radians =
                    angle *
                    Mathf.Deg2Rad;

                Vector3 candidate =
                    anchor.transform.position +
                    new Vector3(
                        Mathf.Cos(radians) *
                            leaderSpacing,
                        0f,
                        Mathf.Sin(radians) *
                            leaderSpacing
                    );

                leader.DeployToBattlefield(
                    candidate
                );

                Physics.SyncTransforms();

                bool legal =
                    leader.IsCoherent() &&
                    AllModelsInsideBoard(leader) &&
                    AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(leader) &&
                    leader.DistanceTo(bodyguard) <=
                        2.05f &&
                    ReserveEnemyDistanceIsLegal(
                        leader
                    ) &&
                    ReserveRoundDeploymentZoneIsLegal(
                        leader
                    ) &&
                    (bodyguard.HasDeepStrike ||
                     StrategicReserveEdgeIsLegal(
                        leader
                     ));

                if (legal)
                    return true;

                leader.SendToReserves();
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void TryPlaceReserveSquad(
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (reservePlacementSquad == null ||
            phase != Phase.Move ||
            !ReserveCanArriveThisRound(
                reservePlacementSquad))
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController squad =
            reservePlacementSquad;

        Vector3 oldRoot =
            squad.transform.position;

        destination.y = 0f;

        squad.DeployToBattlefield(
            destination
        );

        Physics.SyncTransforms();

        bool legal =
            AllModelsInsideBoard(squad) &&
            AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(squad) &&
            squad.IsCoherent() &&
            ReserveEnemyDistanceIsLegal(squad) &&
            ReserveRoundDeploymentZoneIsLegal(
                squad
            );

        if (legal &&
            !squad.HasDeepStrike)
        {
            legal =
                StrategicReserveEdgeIsLegal(
                    squad
                );
        }

        if (legal &&
            squad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            legal =
                TryPlaceAttachedLeaderAfterReserveArrival(
                    squad
                );
        }

        if (!legal)
        {
            squad.SendToReserves();

            if (squad.AttachedLeader != null)
                squad.AttachedLeader.SendToReserves();

            squad.transform.position =
                oldRoot;

            status =
                squad.HasDeepStrike
                ? "Illegal Deep Strike position: stay on-board, avoid collisions, and remain more than 8 from enemy models."
                : "Illegal Strategic Reserve position: unit must be within 6 of a board edge and more than 8 from enemy models.";
            return;
        }

        squad.HasMoved = true;
        squad.MarkSetUpThisTurn();
        squad.MustIngressFromVeil = false;
        squad.TemporaryDeepStrike = false;

        if (squad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            squad.AttachedLeader.MustIngressFromVeil = false;
            squad.AttachedLeader.TemporaryDeepStrike = false;
        }

        reservePlacementSquad = null;
        reserveCycleIndex = -1;

        status =
            squad.DisplayName +
            (squad.HasDeepStrike
                ? " arrived by Deep Strike."
                : " arrived from Strategic Reserves.") +
            " It counts as having moved.";
    }

    private bool ReserveRoundDeploymentZoneIsLegal(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        if (squad == null ||
            EffectiveReserveRound(
                squad
            ) >= 3 ||
            squad.HasDeepStrike)
        {
            return true;
        }

        string opponent =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction !=
                        squad.FactionId
            );

        MissionDeploymentZone opponentZone =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                opponent
            );

        if (opponentZone == null)
            return true;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            if (model != null &&
                opponentZone.ContainsBase(
                    model.transform.position,
                    model.BaseRadiusInches))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool ReserveEnemyDistanceIsLegal(
        SquadController arriving)
    {
        foreach (ModelToken model
            in arriving.LivingModelTokens())
        {
            foreach (SquadController enemySquad
                in squads)
            {
                if (!enemySquad.IsOnBattlefield ||
                    enemySquad.FactionId ==
                        arriving.FactionId ||
                    !enemySquad.IsAlive)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (ModelToken enemy
                    in enemySquad.LivingModelTokens())
                {
                    if (HorizontalDistance(
                            model.transform.position,
                            enemy.transform.position
                        ) <=
                        ReserveEnemyExclusionDistance)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool StrategicReserveEdgeIsLegal(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        float halfWidth =
            BoardWidth * 0.5f;

        float halfDepth =
            BoardDepth * 0.5f;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            Vector3 position =
                model.transform.position;

            float radius =
                model != null
                ? model.BaseRadiusInches
                : 0f;

            float xEdgeDistance =
                halfWidth -
                Mathf.Abs(position.x) -
                radius;

            float zEdgeDistance =
                halfDepth -
                Mathf.Abs(position.z) -
                radius;

            float distanceToEdge =
                Mathf.Min(
                    xEdgeDistance,
                    zEdgeDistance
                );

            if (distanceToEdge >
                StrategicReserveEdgeDistance +
                0.001f)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void TryBeginSelectedMissionAction()
    {
        if (missionSystem == null ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            status =
                "Select a unit before starting a mission action.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad
                .JoinedActionController();

        MissionActionDefinition definition =
            missionSystem.PrimaryAction(
                unit.FactionId
            );

        if (definition == null)
        {
            status =
                "This Primary Mission has no unit-started Objective Action.";
            return;
        }

        string reason;

        if (!missionSystem.CanStartMissionAction(
                unit,
                out reason))
        {
            status = reason;
            return;
        }

        if (definition.TargetType ==
            MissionActionTargetType.None)
        {
            string result;

            missionSystem.StartMissionAction(
                unit,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                out result
            );

            status = result;
            RefreshMoveRing();
            return;
        }

        missionActionTargetingUnit =
            unit;

        missionActionTargetingDefinition =
            definition;

        showMissionPanel = false;

        string targetInstruction =
            definition.TargetType ==
                MissionActionTargetType.Objective
            ? "click a legal objective marker"
            : definition.TargetType ==
                MissionActionTargetType.EnemyUnit
                ? "click a legal enemy unit"
                : definition.TargetType ==
                    MissionActionTargetType
                        .OperationMarkerTerrain
                    ? "click terrain containing an opponent operation marker"
                    : "click a legal terrain area";

        status =
            definition.DisplayName +
            ": " +
            targetInstruction +
            ". ESC cancels.";
    }

    private string SelectedMissionActionButtonText()
    {
        if (missionSystem == null ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            return "MISSION ACTION";
        }

        MissionActionDefinition definition =
            missionSystem.PrimaryAction(
                selectedSquad.FactionId
            );

        return
            definition == null
            ? "NO MISSION ACTION"
            : definition.DisplayName
                .ToUpper();
    }

    private void HandleClick(Vector3 mousePosition)
    {
        Ray ray =
            gameCamera.ScreenPointToRay(mousePosition);

        RaycastHit hit;

        if (!Physics.Raycast(ray, out hit, 1000f))
            return;

        ModelToken clickedModel =
            hit.collider
                .GetComponentInParent<
                    ModelToken
                >();

        SquadController clickedSquad =
            clickedModel != null
            ? clickedModel.Squad
            : hit.collider
                .GetComponentInParent<
                    SquadController
                >();

        ObjectiveController clickedObjective =
            hit.collider
                .GetComponentInParent<
                    ObjectiveController
                >();

        TerrainFeature clickedTerrain =
            hit.collider
                .GetComponentInParent<
                    TerrainFeature
                >();

        if (!IsXcomMode &&
            (manualWoundEditMode ||
             manualRestoreEditMode) &&
            clickedModel != null)
        {
            SelectModelForAction(
                clickedModel.Squad
                    .JoinedActionController(),
                clickedModel
            );

            manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

            status =
                manualRestoreEditMode
                ? "MANUAL RESTORE: " +
                  clickedModel.Squad
                      .JoinedActionController()
                      .DisplayName +
                  " selected. Use RESTORE controls to return destroyed models."
                : "MANUAL WOUNDS: " +
                  clickedModel.RoleName +
                  " has " +
                  clickedModel.CurrentWounds +
                  "/" +
                  clickedModel.MaxWounds +
                  " wounds. Use the wound controls above the board.";

            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack != null &&
            interactiveAttack
                .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            if (clickedModel != null)
            {
                TryChooseAttackCasualty(
                    clickedModel
                );
            }
            else
            {
                status =
                    "CASUALTY SELECTION: click a valid model in the damaged unit, or use KEEP AUTOMATIC in the casualty window.";
            }

            return;
        }

        if (missionActionTargetingDefinition !=
                null &&
            missionActionTargetingUnit != null)
        {
            SquadController enemyTarget =
                clickedSquad != null
                ? clickedSquad
                    .JoinedActionController()
                : null;

            string actionResult =
                "Mission action system is unavailable.";

            bool started = false;

            if (missionSystem != null)
            {
                started =
                    missionSystem.StartMissionAction(
                        missionActionTargetingUnit,
                        clickedObjective,
                        clickedTerrain,
                        enemyTarget,
                        out actionResult
                    );
            }

            status = actionResult;

            if (started)
            {
                missionActionTargetingUnit = null;
                missionActionTargetingDefinition =
                    null;

                RefreshMoveRing();
            }

            return;
        }

        if (specialMoveSquad != null)
        {
            if (hit.collider.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
            {
                TryCompleteSpecialMove(
                    hit.point
                );
            }

            return;
        }

        if (specialShootUnit != null)
        {
            if (clickedSquad != null)
            {
                SquadController target =
                    clickedSquad.JoinedActionController();

                if (target.FactionId !=
                    specialShootUnit.FactionId)
                {
                    TryCompleteSpecialShoot(
                        target
                    );
                }
            }

            return;
        }

        if (deploymentMode)
        {
            if (hit.collider.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
                TryDeployCurrentSquad(hit.point);

            return;
        }

        if (reservePlacementSquad != null &&
            phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            if (hit.collider.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
                TryPlaceReserveSquad(hit.point);

            return;
        }

        if (clickedSquad != null)
        {
            SquadController actionSquad =
                clickedSquad.JoinedActionController();

            string selectableFaction =
                phase == Phase.Fight &&
                fightSequenceActive
                ? fightSelectionFaction
                : activeFaction;

            if (actionSquad.FactionId == selectableFaction)
            {
                ModelToken actionModel =
                    clickedModel;

                HandleFriendlyClick(
                    actionSquad,
                    actionModel
                );

                return;
            }

            if (selectedSquad == null)
                return;

            if (phase == Phase.Shoot)
                TryShoot(
                    selectedSquad,
                    actionSquad
                );
            else if (phase == Phase.Charge)
                TryCharge(
                    selectedSquad,
                    actionSquad
                );
            else if (phase == Phase.Fight)
                TryFight(
                    selectedSquad,
                    actionSquad
                );

            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move &&
            selectedSquad != null &&
            hit.collider.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
        {
            if (wholeSquadMoveMode)
                TryMoveWholeSquad(hit.point);
            else if (selectedModel != null)
                TryMoveSelectedModel(hit.point);

            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
            fightActivationUnit != null &&
            selectedModel != null &&
            (fightActivationStage ==
                FightActivationStage.PileIn ||
             fightActivationStage ==
                FightActivationStage.Consolidate) &&
            hit.collider.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
        {
            TryFightStageMoveSelectedModel(
                hit.point
            );

            return;
        }
    }

    private void HandleFriendlyClick(
        SquadController squad,
        ModelToken model)
    {
        bool isDoubleClick =
            phase == Phase.Move &&
            model != null &&
            lastFriendlyClickedSquad == squad &&
            Time.time - lastFriendlyClickTime <= DoubleClickWindow;

        lastFriendlyClickTime = Time.time;
        lastFriendlyClickedSquad = squad;

        if (isDoubleClick)
        {
            SelectWholeSquadForMovement(squad);
            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move &&
            model != null)
        {
            SelectSingleModel(
                squad,
                model
            );
        }
        else if ((phase == Phase.Shoot ||
                  phase == Phase.Charge ||
                  phase == Phase.Fight) &&
                 model != null)
        {
            if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
                fightActivationStage !=
                    FightActivationStage.None &&
                fightActivationUnit != null &&
                squad.JoinedActionController() !=
                    fightActivationUnit)
            {
                status =
                    "Finish the current fight activation before selecting another unit.";
                return;
            }

            SelectModelForAction(
                squad,
                model
            );

            if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.Attacks)
            {
                PrepareFightModelWeaponSelection(
                    model
                );
            }
        }
        else
        {
            SelectSquadForAction(
                squad
            );
        }
    }

    private void SelectModelForAction(
        SquadController squad,
