        ModelToken model)
    {
        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = squad;
        selectedModel = model;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = false;

        selectedSquad.SetSelected(true);

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader
                .SetSelected(true);
        }

        if (model != null &&
            model.Squad != null)
        {
            if (model.Squad ==
                selectedSquad)
            {
                selectedSquad.FocusModel(
                    model
                );
            }
            else
            {
                selectedSquad.FocusModel(
                    null
                );

                model.Squad.FocusModel(
                    model
                );
            }
        }

        UpdateSelectionStatus();
        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void SelectSingleModel(
        SquadController squad,
        ModelToken model)
    {
        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = squad;
        selectedModel = model;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = false;

        selectedSquad.SetSelected(true);

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.SetSelected(true);

        if (model.Squad == selectedSquad)
        {
            selectedSquad.FocusModel(model);
        }
        else
        {
            selectedSquad.FocusModel(null);
            model.Squad.FocusModel(model);
        }

        UpdateSelectionStatus();
        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void SelectWholeSquadForMovement(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = squad;
        selectedModel = null;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = true;

        selectedSquad.SetSelected(true);
        selectedSquad.HighlightWholeSquad();

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.SetSelected(true);
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.HighlightWholeSquad();
        }

        status =
            "WHOLE SQUAD MOVE: " +
            selectedSquad.DisplayName +
            ". Click the board to translate the formation. " +
            "Every model must remain within its own move allowance.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void SelectSquadForAction(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = squad;
        selectedModel = null;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = false;

        selectedSquad.SetSelected(true);

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.SetSelected(true);

        UpdateSelectionStatus();
        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void UpdateSelectionStatus()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null)
        {
            status = "No squad selected.";
            return;
        }

        string coherence =
            selectedSquad.IsCoherent()
            ? "COHERENT"
            : "OUT OF COHERENCY";

        status =
            selectedSquad.DisplayName +
            (selectedSquad.HasFallenBack
                ? " | FALL BACK"
                : "") +
            (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced
                ? " | ADVANCED +" +
                  selectedSquad.AdvanceBonus
                : "") +
            " | " +
            selectedSquad.LivingModels +
            "/" +
            selectedSquad.StartingModels +
            " models | " +
            coherence +
            " | " +
            (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null
                ? "ATTACHED UNIT — " +
                  selectedSquad.LeaderSummary()
                : selectedSquad.LeaderSummary()) +
            " | Loadouts: " +
            selectedSquad.LoadoutSummary();

        if (missionSystem != null &&
            missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                selectedSquad))
        {
            string actionName =
                missionSystem.ActiveActionName(
                    selectedSquad
                );

            status +=
                " | MISSION ACTION: " +
                (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    actionName)
                    ? "started/completed"
                    : actionName);
        }

        if (selectedModel != null &&
            phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            float remaining =
                SelectedModelRemainingMove();

            status +=
                " | selected model remaining move " +
                remaining.ToString("0.0") +
                " — blue ring";
        }
        else if (selectedModel != null &&
                 phase == Phase.Shoot)
        {
            float range =
                SelectedModelMaximumRangedRange();

            status +=
                range > 0.01f
                ? " | selected model max usable weapon range " +
                  range.ToString("0.#") +
                  " — amber ring"
                : " | selected model has no currently usable ranged weapon";
        }
        else if (selectedModel != null &&
                 phase == Phase.Charge)
        {
            status +=
                SelectedUnitCanCharge()
                ? " | maximum pre-roll charge reach 12 — red ring"
                : " | this unit cannot currently declare a charge";
        }
        else if (selectedModel != null &&
                 phase == Phase.Fight &&
                 fightActivationUnit != null &&
                 ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                     selectedModel))
        {
            if (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn ||
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.Consolidate)
            {
                Vector3 start;

                if (!fightStageStartPositions
                    .TryGetValue(
                        selectedModel,
                        out start))
                {
                    start =
                        selectedModel
                            .transform.position;
                }

                float used =
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        start,
                        selectedModel
                            .transform.position
                    );

                status +=
                    " | " +
                    (fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.PileIn
                        ? "pile-in"
                        : "consolidation") +
                    " " +
                    used.ToString("0.0") +
                    "/" +
                    FightStageMoveLimit()
                        .ToString("0.0") +
                    "″ • hold ALT to measure";
            }
            else if (fightActivationStage ==
                     FightActivationStage.Attacks)
            {
                status +=
                    fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                        .Contains(
                            selectedModel)
                    ? " | this model has already resolved its melee attacks"
                    : " | click an enemy target to choose this model's melee weapon";
            }
        }
    }

    private void ClearSelectionVisualOnly()
    {
        if (selectedSquad != null)
        {
            selectedSquad.SetSelected(false);

            if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
                selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.SetSelected(false);
        }

        if (moveRing != null)
            moveRing.enabled = false;

        HideMovementCursorMeasure();
    }

    private void ClearSelection()
    {
        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = null;
        selectedModel = null;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = false;

        if (reservePlacementSquad != null)
        {
            reservePlacementSquad = null;
            reserveCycleIndex = -1;
            status = "Reserve placement cancelled.";
        }
        else
        {
            status = "No squad selected.";
        }
    }

    private void SyncAttachedLeaderAfterBodyguardMove(
        SquadController bodyguard)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null ||
            !bodyguard.AttachedLeader.IsAlive ||
            !bodyguard.AttachedLeader.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController leader =
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader;

        if (leader.DistanceTo(bodyguard) <=
            2.0f + 0.05f)
        {
            return;
        }

        ModelToken leaderModel =
            leader.LivingModelTokens()
                .FirstOrDefault();

        if (leaderModel == null)
            return;

        ModelToken nearestBodyguard =
            bodyguard.ClosestLivingModelTo(
                leaderModel.transform.position
            );

        if (nearestBodyguard == null)
            return;

        Vector3 direction =
            leaderModel.transform.position -
            nearestBodyguard.transform.position;

        direction.y = 0f;

        if (direction.sqrMagnitude <
            0.001f)
        {
            direction = Vector3.back;
        }

        direction.Normalize();

        float[] angleOffsets =
        {
            0f, 30f, -30f,
            60f, -60f,
            90f, -90f,
            180f
        };

        foreach (float angle
            in angleOffsets)
        {
            Vector3 radial =
                Quaternion.Euler(
                    0f,
                    angle,
                    0f
                ) *
                direction;

            float leaderSpacing =
                MinimumModelSeparation(
                    nearestBodyguard,
                    leaderModel
                ) +
                0.06f;

            Vector3 candidate =
                nearestBodyguard.transform.position +
                radial *
                leaderSpacing;

            candidate.y =
                leaderModel.transform.position.y;

            if (!InsideBoard(candidate))
                continue;

            if (!CanPlaceModel(
                leaderModel,
                candidate))
            {
                continue;
            }

            leaderModel.transform.position =
                candidate;

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            if (leader.DistanceTo(bodyguard) <=
                2.0f + 0.05f)
            {
                leader.RefreshVisuals();
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private void TranslateAttachedLeader(
        SquadController bodyguard,
        Vector3 delta)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null ||
            !bodyguard.AttachedLeader.IsAlive ||
            !bodyguard.AttachedLeader.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            return;
        }

        bodyguard.AttachedLeader
            .TranslateLivingModels(delta);

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
    }

    private Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3>
        CaptureAttachedLeaderPositions(
            SquadController bodyguard)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return bodyguard.AttachedLeader
            .CaptureLivingPositions();
    }

    private void RestoreAttachedLeaderPositions(
        SquadController bodyguard,
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> positions)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null ||
            positions == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        bodyguard.AttachedLeader
            .RestorePositions(positions);

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
    }

    private List<ModelToken> JoinedModels(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        if (squad == null)
            return new List<ModelToken>();

        SquadController actionUnit =
            squad.JoinedActionController();

        return actionUnit
            .JoinedLivingModelTokens();
    }

    private Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3>
        CaptureJoinedPositions(
            SquadController squad)
    {
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> result =
            new Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3>();

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            result[model] =
                model.transform.position;
        }

        return result;
    }

    private void RestoreJoinedPositions(
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> positions)
    {
        if (positions == null)
            return;

        foreach (
            KeyValuePair<ModelToken, Vector3> pair
            in positions)
        {
            if (pair.Key != null &&
                pair.Key.IsAlive)
            {
                pair.Key.transform.position =
                    pair.Value;
            }
        }

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
    }

    private void TranslateJoinedModels(
        SquadController squad,
        Vector3 delta)
    {
        delta.y = 0f;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            model.transform.position +=
                delta;
        }

        squad.RefreshVisuals();

        if (squad.AttachedLeader != null)
            squad.AttachedLeader.RefreshVisuals();

        Physics.SyncTransforms();
    }

    private void HandleBodyguardDestroyed(
        SquadController bodyguard)
    {
        if (bodyguard == null ||
            bodyguard.IsAlive ||
            bodyguard.AttachedLeader == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController leader =
            bodyguard.DetachAttachedLeader();

        if (leader != null)
        {
            leader.HasMoved =
                bodyguard.HasMoved;

            leader.HasShot =
                bodyguard.HasShot;

            leader.HasCharged =
                bodyguard.HasCharged;

            leader.HasFought =
                bodyguard.HasFought;

            status +=
                " " +
                leader.DisplayName +
                " survives as a separate unit.";
        }
    }

    private void TryOpenCommandAbilities()
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Command ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            status =
                "Select one of your units in the Command phase first.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        if (FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                unit,
                "Word of the Phoenix") &&
            unit.AttachedLeader != null &&
            unit.DestroyedModelTokens().Count > 0)
        {
            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Word of the Phoenix",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            SelectSquadForAction(
                                unit
                            );

                            manualRestoreEditMode = true;
                            manualWoundEditMode = false;
                            manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

                            QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                                "WORD OF THE PHOENIX",
                                unit.DisplayName +
                                ": resolve the initial D6 and any follow-up D3+1 entirely yourself. If models return, use RESTORE EDIT to choose and return exactly the models your tabletop result permits.",
                                1
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int test =
                            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                "Word of the Phoenix: " +
                                unit.DisplayName
                            );

                        if (test < 2)
                        {
                            status =
                                "WORD OF THE PHOENIX: rolled 1 — no models return.";
                            return;
                        }

                        int count =
                            RollTabletopD3(
                                "Word of the Phoenix returned models"
                            ) + 1;

                        int returned =
                            ReanimateFullModels(
                                unit,
                                count,
                                true
                            );

                        status =
                            "WORD OF THE PHOENIX: returned " +
                            returned +
                            " Bodyguard model(s).";
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        bool hasTears =
            FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                unit,
                "Tears of Isha"
            );

        if (hasTears)
        {
            List<SquadController> wraiths =
                squads
                    .Where(
                        target =>
                            target != null &&
                            !target.IsAttachedLeader &&
                            target.IsAlive &&
                            target.IsOnBattlefield &&
                            target.FactionId ==
                                unit.FactionId &&
                            target.HasKeyword(
                                "wraith construct") &&
                            !target.TearsOfIshaUsedThisTurn &&
                            JoinedDistance(
                                unit,
                                target
                            ) <= 6.001f
                    )
                    .ToList();

            foreach (SquadController wraith
                in wraiths)
            {
                SquadController captured = wraith;

                options.Add(
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Tears of Isha: " +
                        captured.DisplayName,
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            captured.TearsOfIshaUsedThisTurn =
                                true;

                            if (!IsXcomMode)
                            {
                                SelectSquadForAction(
                                    captured
                                );

                                if (captured.DestroyedModelTokens().Count > 0)
                                {
                                    manualRestoreEditMode = true;
                                    manualWoundEditMode = false;
                                    manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

                                    QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                                        "TEARS OF ISHA",
                                        captured.DisplayName +
                                        ": this use returns a destroyed model. Choose the correct model yourself with RESTORE EDIT and return it at the state required by the tabletop rule.",
                                        0
                                    );
                                }
                                else
                                {
                                    manualWoundEditMode = true;
                                    manualRestoreEditMode = false;

                                    QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                                        "TEARS OF ISHA",
                                        captured.DisplayName +
                                        ": resolve the D3 healing roll yourself, then apply the exact number of restored wounds with WOUND EDIT.",
                                        1
                                    );
                                }

                                return;
                            }

                            if (captured.DestroyedModelTokens().Count > 0)
                            {
                                int returned =
                                    ReanimateFullModels(
                                        captured,
                                        1
                                    );

                                status =
                                    "TEARS OF ISHA: returned " +
                                    returned +
                                    " destroyed model.";
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                int amount =
                                    RollTabletopD3(
                                        "Tears of Isha: " +
                                        captured.DisplayName
                                    );

                                int healed =
                                    captured.HealWounds(
                                        amount
                                    );

                                status =
                                    "TEARS OF ISHA: healed " +
                                    healed +
                                    " wound(s).";
                            }
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        if (options.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                "No currently usable Command abilities were found on the selected unit.";
            return;
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Close",
                CloseRuleChoice
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "COMMAND ABILITIES",
            unit.DisplayName +
            " — choose an available Command-phase ability.",
            options
        );
    }

    private int ReanimateFullModels(
        SquadController unit,
        int count,
        bool excludeSupportWeapons = false)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            count <= 0 ||
            !unit.IsOnBattlefield ||
            unit.LivingModels <= 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int returned = 0;

        while (returned < count)
        {
            ModelToken dead =
                unit.DestroyedModelTokens()
                    .FirstOrDefault(
                        model =>
                            !excludeSupportWeapons ||
                            (model.RoleName ?? "").IndexOf(
                                "Support Weapon",
                                System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            ) < 0
                    );

            if (dead == null)
                break;

            Vector3 position;

            if (!TryFindReanimationPosition(
                    unit,
                    dead,
                    out position))
            {
                break;
            }

            dead.Revive(
                dead.MaxWounds,
                position
            );

            returned++;
        }

        unit.RefreshVisuals();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        return returned;
    }

    private bool OfferSpiritMarkAfterMove(
        SquadController movingUnit,
        System.Action after)
    {
        if (movingUnit == null ||
            !movingUnit.IsAlive ||
            !movingUnit.IsOnBattlefield ||
            !FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                movingUnit,
                "Spirit Mark"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController spiritseer =
            movingUnit.AttachedLeader != null &&
            UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                movingUnit.AttachedLeader,
                "Spirit Mark")
            ? movingUnit.AttachedLeader
            : movingUnit;

        if (spiritseer.SpiritMarkUsedThisTurn)
            return false;

        List<SquadController> wraiths =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.FactionId ==
                            movingUnit.FactionId &&
                        unit.HasKeyword(
                            "wraith construct") &&
                        JoinedDistance(
                            movingUnit,
                            unit
                        ) <= 6.001f
                )
                .ToList();

        List<SquadController> enemies =
            squads
                .Where(
                    enemy =>
                        enemy != null &&
                        !enemy.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        enemy.IsAlive &&
                        enemy.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        enemy.FactionId !=
                            movingUnit.FactionId &&
                        JoinedModels(movingUnit).Any(
                            model =>
                                ModelHasLineOfSight(
                                    model,
                                    enemy
                                )
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (wraiths.Count == 0 ||
            enemies.Count == 0)
        {
            return false;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController wraith
            in wraiths)
        {
            foreach (SquadController enemy
                in enemies)
            {
                SquadController capturedWraith =
                    wraith;

                SquadController capturedEnemy =
                    enemy;

                options.Add(
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        capturedWraith.DisplayName +
                        " → " +
                        capturedEnemy.DisplayName,
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            capturedWraith.SpiritMarkTarget =
                                capturedEnemy;

                            spiritseer.SpiritMarkUsedThisTurn =
                                true;

                            status =
                                "SPIRIT MARK: " +
                                capturedWraith.DisplayName +
                                " gains Sustained Hits 1 against " +
                                capturedEnemy.DisplayName +
                                " until the start of your next Movement phase.";

                            if (after != null)
                                after();
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip Spirit Mark",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    spiritseer.SpiritMarkUsedThisTurn = true;

                    if (after != null)
                        after();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "SPIRIT MARK",
            "The Spiritseer ended a move. Select one nearby Wraith Construct and one visible enemy unit.",
            options
        );

        return true;
    }

    private int TotalBattleFocusTokens(
        string faction)
    {
        int baseTokens =
            factionRules != null
            ? factionRules
                .GetBattleFocusTokens(
                    faction
                )
            : 0;

        int bonusTokens =
            aeldariRules != null
            ? aeldariRules
                .BonusBattleFocus(
                    faction
                )
            : 0;

        return
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                baseTokens +
                bonusTokens
            );
    }

    private bool UnitHasBattleFocusRules(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return false;

        bool native =
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.UnitHasBattleFocus(
                unit,
                squads
            );

        bool spiritGuides =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules
                .GrantsBattleFocusFromSpiritGuides(
                    unit,
                    squads
                );

        return native || spiritGuides;
    }

    private bool CanUseBattleFocusManoeuvre(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            unit.AgileManoeuvreUsedThisPhase ||
            TotalBattleFocusTokens(
                unit.FactionId
            ) <= 0)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return UnitHasBattleFocusRules(
            unit
        );
    }

    private bool SpendBattleFocusFor(
        SquadController unit,
        string manoeuvre)
    {
        if (!CanUseBattleFocusManoeuvre(
                unit))
        {
            return false;
        }

        bool spent = false;

        if (factionRules.GetBattleFocusTokens(
                unit.FactionId) > 0)
        {
            spent =
                factionRules.SpendBattleFocus(
                    unit.FactionId,
                    1
                );
        }

        if (!spent &&
            aeldariRules != null)
        {
            spent =
                aeldariRules
                    .SpendBonusBattleFocus(
                        unit.FactionId
                    );
        }

        if (!spent)
            return false;

        unit.AgileManoeuvreUsedThisPhase = true;

        status =
            manoeuvre +
            " — " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            ". Battle Focus: " +
            TotalBattleFocusTokens(
                unit.FactionId
            );

        return true;
    }

    private void TryUseSwiftAsTheWind()
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Move ||
            selectedSquad == null ||
            selectedSquad.HasMoved)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                unit,
                "SWIFT AS THE WIND"))
        {
            status =
                "Swift as the Wind is not currently available.";
            return;
        }

        float swiftBonus =
            2f +
            (aeldariRules != null
                ? aeldariRules
                    .SwiftAsTheWindExtraMove(
                        unit.FactionId
                    )
                : 0f);

        unit.BattleFocusMoveBonus +=
            swiftBonus;

        RefreshMoveRing();

        status +=
            " +" +
            swiftBonus.ToString("0.#") +
            " Move until end of phase.";
    }

    private void TryUseFlittingShadows()
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Move ||
            selectedSquad == null ||
            selectedSquad.HasMoved)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                unit,
                "FLITTING SHADOWS"))
        {
            status =
                "Flitting Shadows is not currently available.";
            return;
        }

        unit.FlittingShadowsActive = true;

        status +=
            " Fire Overwatch cannot target this unit until end of turn.";
    }

    private void TryUseStarEngines()
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Move ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!unit.HasKeyword("vehicle") ||
            !unit.HasAdvanced)
        {
            status =
                "Star Engines requires a Battle Focus Vehicle that has declared an Advance.";
            return;
        }

        if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                unit,
                "STAR ENGINES"))
        {
            status =
                "Star Engines is not currently available.";
            return;
        }

        unit.StarEnginesActive = true;

        status +=
            " Ranged weapons have Assault until end of turn.";
    }

    private void OfferOpportunitySeized(
        SquadController fallingBackUnit,
        List<SquadController> enemiesEngagedBefore)
    {
        if (fallingBackUnit == null ||
            enemiesEngagedBefore == null ||
            enemiesEngagedBefore.Count == 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            enemiesEngagedBefore
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        !unit.HasKeyword("titanic") &&
                        CanUseBattleFocusManoeuvre(
                            unit
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
            return;

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Opportunity Seized: " +
                    captured.DisplayName +
                    " (1 BF)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                                captured,
                                "OPPORTUNITY SEIZED"))
                        {
                            return;
                        }

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            OpenTraditionalNumericPrompt(
                                "OPPORTUNITY SEIZED",
                                captured.DisplayName +
                                " makes a D6+1 Normal move. Roll it yourself and enter the final movement distance.",
                                2 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            captured.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                7 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            captured.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                2 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            captured.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                1,
                                value =>
                                    BeginSpecialMove(
                                        captured,
                                        value,
                                        "Opportunity Seized",
                                        null
                                    )
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int move =
                            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                "Opportunity Seized: " +
                                captured.DisplayName
                            ) +
                            1 +
                            (aeldariRules != null
                                ? aeldariRules
                                    .AgileD6Bonus(
                                        captured.FactionId
                                    )
                                : 0);

                        BeginSpecialMove(
                            captured,
                            move,
                            "Opportunity Seized",
                            null
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                CloseRuleChoice
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "BATTLE FOCUS — OPPORTUNITY SEIZED",
            fallingBackUnit.DisplayName +
            " ended a Fall Back move. An eligible unit that started the phase engaged with it can spend 1 Battle Focus token to make a D6+1 Normal move.",
            options
        );
    }

    private bool OfferFadeBackAfterAttack(
        InteractiveAttackController attack,
        System.Action after)
    {
        if (attack == null ||
            attack.Mode != AttackMode.Ranged ||
            attack.TotalHits <= 0 ||
            attack.Target == null ||
            !attack.Target.IsAlive ||
            attack.Target.HasKeyword("titanic") ||
            !CanUseBattleFocusManoeuvre(
                attack.Target))
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            attack.Target.JoinedActionController();

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Fade Back (1 BF)",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();

                    if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                            unit,
                            "FADE BACK"))
                    {
                        if (after != null)
                            after();
                        return;
                    }

                    if (!IsXcomMode)
                    {
                        OpenTraditionalNumericPrompt(
                            "FADE BACK",
                            unit.DisplayName +
                            " makes a D6+1 Normal move. Resolve the roll/rerolls yourself and enter the final distance.",
                            2 +
                                (aeldariRules != null
                                    ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                        unit.FactionId)
                                    : 0),
                            7 +
                                (aeldariRules != null
                                    ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                        unit.FactionId)
                                    : 0),
                            2 +
                                (aeldariRules != null
                                    ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                        unit.FactionId)
                                    : 0),
                            1,
                            value =>
                                BeginSpecialMove(
                                    unit,
                                    value,
                                    "Fade Back",
                                    after
                                ),
                            after
                        );

                        return;
                    }

                    int move =
                        DiceRoller.RollD6(
                            "Fade Back: " +
                            unit.DisplayName
                        ) +
                        1 +
                        (aeldariRules != null
                            ? aeldariRules
                                .AgileD6Bonus(
                                    unit.FactionId
                                )
                            : 0);

                    BeginSpecialMove(
                        unit,
                        move,
                        "Fade Back",
                        after
                    );
                }
            )
        );

        if (UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                unit.FactionId) &&
            unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
            factionRules.CanUseLethalSurge(
                unit.FactionId))
        {
            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Lethal Surge via Fade Back (1 BF)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendBattleFocusFor(
                                unit,
                                "FADE BACK — LETHAL SURGE"))
                        {
                            if (after != null)
                                after();
                            return;
                        }

                        factionRules.MarkLethalSurgeUsed(
                            unit.FactionId
                        );

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            OpenTraditionalNumericPrompt(
                                "LETHAL SURGE",
                                unit.DisplayName +
                                " makes a D6+1 Surge move. Resolve the roll yourself and enter the final distance.",
                                2 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            unit.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                7 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            unit.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                2 +
                                    (aeldariRules != null
                                        ? aeldariRules.AgileD6Bonus(
                                            unit.FactionId)
                                        : 0),
                                1,
                                value =>
                                    BeginSurgeMove(
                                        unit,
                                        value,
                                        "Lethal Surge",
                                        after
                                    ),
                                after
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int move =
                            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                "Lethal Surge: " +
                                unit.DisplayName
                            ) +
                            1 +
                            (aeldariRules != null
                                ? aeldariRules
                                    .AgileD6Bonus(
                                        unit.FactionId
                                    )
                                : 0);

                        BeginSurgeMove(
                            unit,
                            move,
                            "Lethal Surge",
                            after
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Do not Fade Back",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    if (after != null)
                        after();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "BATTLE FOCUS — FADE BACK",
            unit.DisplayName +
            " was hit by ranged attacks. Spend 1 Battle Focus token to make a D6+1 Normal move.",
            options
        );

        return true;
    }

    private void TryDeclareAdvance()
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Move ||
            selectedSquad == null ||
            !selectedSquad.IsAlive ||
            selectedSquad.FactionId !=
                activeFaction)
        {
            status =
                "Select one of your units in the Move phase first.";
            return;
        }

        if (IsEngaged(selectedSquad))
        {
            status =
                "Engaged units cannot Advance.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedSquad.HasFallenBack)
        {
            status =
                "A unit that is Falling Back cannot Advance.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedSquad.HasMoved)
        {
            status =
                "Declare the Advance before moving the unit.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced)
        {
            status =
                selectedSquad.DisplayName +
                " already has an Advance roll of +" +
                selectedSquad.AdvanceBonus +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            traditionalAdvancePending = true;
            traditionalAdvanceUnit = unit;
            traditionalAdvanceResult = 1;

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                1
            );

            AppendBattleLog(
                "ADVANCE",
                unit.DisplayName,
                "Advance declared. Player resolves the D6 manually and enters the final movement bonus."
            );

            status =
                "TRADITIONAL ADVANCE: roll your D6 in the free tray, apply any tabletop rerolls yourself, then enter the final result.";

            return;
        }

        int roll =
            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                "Advance: " +
                unit.DisplayName
            );

        bool suddenStormReroll =
            unit.FactionSuddenStormActive &&
            unit.AttachedLeader != null &&
            unit.AttachedLeader.IsAlive &&
            unit.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword(
                "necrons") &&
            unit.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword(
                "character");

        bool eldritchRaidersReroll =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.CanRerollAdvance(
                unit
            );

        if (suddenStormReroll ||
            eldritchRaidersReroll)
        {
            int capturedRoll = roll;

            string rerollSource =
                suddenStormReroll
                ? "PROTOCOL OF THE SUDDEN STORM"
                : "YRIEL'S OWN";

            OpenRuleChoice(
                rerollSource +
                " — ADVANCE",
                unit.DisplayName +
                " rolled " +
                capturedRoll +
                ". " +
                (suddenStormReroll
                    ? "Because it is led by a Necron Character, this Advance roll can be re-rolled."
                    : "Eldritch Raiders allows this Anhrathe/Rangers/Shroud Runners unit to re-roll its Advance roll."),
                new[]
                {
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Keep " + capturedRoll,
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();
                            ApplyAdvanceRoll(
                                unit,
                                capturedRoll
                            );
                        }
                    ),
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Re-roll Advance",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            int reroll =
                                DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                    rerollSource +
                                    " Advance re-roll: " +
                                    unit.DisplayName
                                );

                            ApplyAdvanceRoll(
                                unit,
                                reroll
                            );
                        }
                    )
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        ApplyAdvanceRoll(
            unit,
            roll
        );
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalAdvance()
    {
        if (!traditionalAdvancePending ||
            traditionalAdvanceUnit == null)
        {
            traditionalAdvancePending = false;
            traditionalAdvanceUnit = null;
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            traditionalAdvanceUnit;

        int result =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                traditionalAdvanceResult,
                1,
                6
            );

        traditionalAdvancePending = false;
        traditionalAdvanceUnit = null;

        ApplyAdvanceRoll(
            unit,
            result
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "ADVANCE",
            unit.DisplayName,
            "Player-entered final Advance result: +" +
            result +
            ". Warboard did not interpret or validate the physical dice."
        );

        status =
            unit.DisplayName +
            " Advance set to +" +
            result +
            ".";
    }

    private void CancelTraditionalAdvance()
    {
        traditionalAdvancePending = false;
        traditionalAdvanceUnit = null;

        status =
            "Advance declaration cancelled.";
    }

    private void ApplyAdvanceRoll(
        SquadController unit,
        int roll)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return;

        unit.DeclareAdvance(
            roll
        );

        status =
            unit.DisplayName +
            " ADVANCES: +" +
            roll +
            " movement. It cannot charge this turn; in Shooting it can only use Assault weapons.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void TryMoveSelectedModel(Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            selectedModel == null ||
            !selectedModel.IsAlive)
        {
            return;
        }

        bool wasEngagedBeforeMove =
            IsEngaged(selectedSquad);

        List<SquadController> enemiesEngagedBeforeMove =
            wasEngagedBeforeMove
            ? EngagedEnemies(selectedSquad)
            : new List<SquadController>();

        if (wasEngagedBeforeMove)
            selectedSquad.HasFallenBack = true;

        destination.y =
            selectedModel.transform.position.y;

        float displacement =
            selectedModel.DistanceMovedFromTurnStart(destination);

        float maxMove =
            selectedSquad
                .GetMovementAllowanceFor(
                    selectedModel
                );

        if (displacement > maxMove + 0.001f)
        {
            status =
                "Model move too far: " +
                displacement.ToString("0.0") +
                " / " +
                maxMove.ToString("0.0");
            return;
        }

        if (!InsideBoard(destination))
        {
            status = "Destination is off the board.";
            return;
        }

        if (!CanPlaceModel(selectedModel, destination))
        {
            status = "Cannot place that model there.";
            return;
        }

        selectedModel.transform.position = destination;

        selectedSquad.HasMoved = true;
        selectedSquad.RefreshVisuals();

        string coherence =
            selectedSquad.IsCoherent()
            ? "coherent"
            : "OUT OF COHERENCY";

        status =
            (selectedSquad.HasFallenBack
                ? "FALL BACK: "
                : (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced
                    ? "ADVANCE: "
                    : "")) +
            "Moved model " +
            displacement.ToString("0.0") +
            " from its start; squad is " +
            coherence +
            ".";

        RefreshMoveRing();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        System.Action afterMove =
            () =>
            {
                if (wasEngagedBeforeMove &&
                    !IsEngaged(selectedSquad))
                {
                    OfferOpportunitySeized(
                        selectedSquad,
                        enemiesEngagedBeforeMove
                    );
                }
            };

        if (!OfferSpiritMarkAfterMove(
                selectedSquad,
                afterMove))
        {
            afterMove();
        }
    }

    private void TryMoveWholeSquad(Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            !wholeSquadMoveMode ||
            !selectedSquad.IsAlive)
        {
            return;
        }

        bool wasEngagedBeforeMove =
            IsEngaged(selectedSquad);

        List<SquadController> enemiesEngagedBeforeMove =
            wasEngagedBeforeMove
            ? EngagedEnemies(selectedSquad)
            : new List<SquadController>();

        if (wasEngagedBeforeMove)
        {
            selectedSquad.HasFallenBack = true;

            if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
                selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.HasFallenBack = true;
        }

        List<ModelToken> joined =
            JoinedModels(selectedSquad);

        if (joined.Count == 0)
            return;

        Vector3 currentCentre =
            Vector3.zero;

        foreach (ModelToken model in joined)
            currentCentre += model.transform.position;

        currentCentre /= joined.Count;

        destination.y =
            currentCentre.y;

        Vector3 delta =
            destination -
            currentCentre;

        delta.y = 0f;

        if (!selectedSquad
            .CanTranslateWithinNormalMove(delta))
        {
            status =
                "Whole Attached-unit move rejected: at least one model (including the Leader) would exceed its own move allowance.";
            return;
        }

        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original =
            CaptureJoinedPositions(
                selectedSquad
            );

        TranslateJoinedModels(
            selectedSquad,
            delta
        );

        bool legal =
            AllModelsInsideBoard(
                selectedSquad
            ) &&
            AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                selectedSquad
            ) &&
            selectedSquad.IsCoherent();

        if (!legal)
        {
            RestoreJoinedPositions(
                original
            );

            status =
                "Whole Attached-unit move rejected: collision, board edge, or joined-unit coherency problem.";
            return;
        }

        selectedSquad.HasMoved = true;

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.HasMoved = true;

        status =
            (selectedSquad.HasFallenBack
                ? "FALL BACK: "
                : (selectedSquad.HasAdvanced
                    ? "ADVANCE: "
                    : "")) +
            "Moved " +
            (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null
                ? "Attached unit"
                : "whole squad") +
            " by " +
            HorizontalMagnitude(delta)
                .ToString("0.0") +
            ". Formation preserved.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        System.Action afterMove =
            () =>
            {
                if (wasEngagedBeforeMove &&
                    !IsEngaged(selectedSquad))
                {
                    OfferOpportunitySeized(
                        selectedSquad,
                        enemiesEngagedBeforeMove
                    );
                }
            };

        if (!OfferSpiritMarkAfterMove(
                selectedSquad,
                afterMove))
        {
            afterMove();
        }
    }

    private float MinimumModelSeparation(
        ModelToken first,
        ModelToken second)
    {
        float firstRadius =
            first != null
            ? first.BaseRadiusInches
            : MinimumModelCentreDistance *
              0.5f;

        float secondRadius =
            second != null
            ? second.BaseRadiusInches
            : MinimumModelCentreDistance *
              0.5f;

        return
            Mathf.Max(
                0.20f,
                firstRadius +
                secondRadius
            );
    }

    private bool CanPlaceModel(
        ModelToken movingModel,
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        // Only units actually on the battlefield physically occupy table
        // space. Undeployed and reserve units retain temporary hidden world
        // positions for bookkeeping, but those positions must never block
        // deployment, movement, charges or reanimation.
        foreach (SquadController squad in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null ||
                !squad.IsOnBattlefield)
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (ModelToken otherModel in squad.LivingModelTokens())
            {
                if (otherModel == null ||
                    !otherModel.IsAlive ||
                    otherModel == movingModel)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (HorizontalDistance(
                        destination,
                        otherModel.transform.position
                    ) <
                    MinimumModelSeparation(
                        movingModel,
                        otherModel
                    ))
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        // Destination may not sit inside terrain.
        Vector3 testPoint =
            destination +
            Vector3.up * 0.4f;

        Collider[] overlaps =
            Physics.OverlapSphere(
                testPoint,
                0.31f
            );

        foreach (Collider col in overlaps)
        {
            if (col == null)
                continue;

            if (col.gameObject ==
                movingModel.gameObject)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (col.GetComponent<BoardSurface>() != null)
                continue;

            TerrainFeature terrain =
                col.GetComponent<TerrainFeature>();

            if (terrain != null &&
                terrain.BlocksMovement)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool CombatMovePathIsClear(
        ModelToken movingModel,
        Vector3 start,
        Vector3 destination,
        bool ignoreEnemyModels = false)
    {
        Vector3 flat =
            destination -
            start;

        flat.y = 0f;

        float distance =
            HorizontalMagnitude(flat);

        if (distance <= 0.001f)
            return true;

        int steps =
            Mathf.Max(
                2,
                Mathf.CeilToInt(
                    distance /
                    CombatPathSampleStep
                )
            );

        // Enemy models and terrain are solid during charge / pile-in /
        // consolidation. This prevents a model from "teleporting" through
        // the enemy unit to a legal-looking point on the far side.
        for (int i = 1; i <= steps; i++)
        {
            float t =
                i /
                (float)steps;

            Vector3 sample =
                Vector3.Lerp(
                    start,
                    destination,
                    t
                );

            if (!ignoreEnemyModels)
            {
                foreach (SquadController squad in squads)
                {
                    if (squad == null ||
                        !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
                        squad.FactionId ==
                            movingModel.Squad.FactionId)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    foreach (ModelToken enemy
                        in squad.LivingModelTokens())
                    {
                        if (HorizontalDistance(
                                sample,
                                enemy.transform.position
                            ) <
                            MinimumModelSeparation(
                                movingModel,
                                enemy
                            ))
                        {
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Vector3 terrainProbe =
                sample +
                Vector3.up * 0.4f;

            Collider[] overlaps =
                Physics.OverlapSphere(
                    terrainProbe,
                    0.28f
                );

            foreach (Collider col in overlaps)
            {
                if (col != null)
                {
                    TerrainFeature terrain =
                        col.GetComponent<TerrainFeature>();

                    if (terrain != null &&
                        terrain.BlocksMovement)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(SquadController squad)
    {
        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            if (!CanPlaceModel(
                model,
                model.transform.position))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool AllModelsInsideBoard(SquadController squad)
    {
        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            if (!InsideBoard(
                model,
                model.transform.position))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool InsideBoard(Vector3 point)
    {
        return
            Mathf.Abs(point.x) <=
                BoardWidth * 0.5f &&
            Mathf.Abs(point.z) <=
                BoardDepth * 0.5f;
    }

    private bool InsideBoard(
        ModelToken model,
        Vector3 point)
    {
        float radius =
            model != null
            ? model.BaseRadiusInches
            : 0f;

        return
            point.x - radius >=
                -BoardWidth * 0.5f &&
            point.x + radius <=
                BoardWidth * 0.5f &&
            point.z - radius >=
                -BoardDepth * 0.5f &&
            point.z + radius <=
                BoardDepth * 0.5f;
    }

    public bool MissionUnitIsEngaged(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        return
            unit != null &&
            IsEngaged(
                unit.JoinedActionController()
            );
    }

    public bool MissionUnitsHaveLineOfSight(
        SquadController source,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (source == null ||
            target == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            JoinedModels(source)
                .Any(
                    model =>
                        model != null &&
                        ModelHasLineOfSight(
                            model,
                            target
                        )
                );
    }

    public bool UnitWithinTerrainArea(
        SquadController unit,
        TerrainFeature terrain)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            terrain == null ||
            !unit.IsAlive ||
            !unit.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            return false;
        }

        Collider collider =
            terrain.GetComponent<
                Collider
            >();

        if (collider == null)
            return false;

        Bounds bounds =
            collider.bounds;

        return
            unit
                .JoinedLivingModelTokens()
                .Any(
                    model =>
                    {
                        Vector3 p =
                            model.transform.position;

                        return
                            p.x >=
                                bounds.min.x -
                                model.BaseRadiusInches &&
                            p.x <=
                                bounds.max.x +
                                model.BaseRadiusInches &&
                            p.z >=
                                bounds.min.z -
                                model.BaseRadiusInches &&
                            p.z <=
                                bounds.max.z +
                                model.BaseRadiusInches;
                    }
                );
    }

    public bool MissionPositionInEitherDeploymentZone(
        Vector3 position)
    {
        if (activeMissionBattlefield == null)
            return false;

        Vector2 point =
            new Vector2(
                position.x,
                position.z
            );

        return
            (activeMissionBattlefield
                 .AttackerZone != null &&
             activeMissionBattlefield
                 .AttackerZone
                 .ContainsPoint(point)) ||
            (activeMissionBattlefield
                 .DefenderZone != null &&
             activeMissionBattlefield
                 .DefenderZone
                 .ContainsPoint(point));
    }

    public bool IsPositionInOpponentTerritory(
        string faction,
        Vector3 position)
    {
        if (activeMissionBattlefield == null ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return false;
        }

        MissionDeploymentZone own =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                faction
            );

        string opponent =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                value =>
                    value != faction
            );

        MissionDeploymentZone enemy =
            DeploymentZoneForFaction(
                opponent
            );

        if (own == null ||
            enemy == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        float ownDistance =
            own.DistanceToZone(
                position
            );

        float enemyDistance =
            enemy.DistanceToZone(
                position
            );

        return
            enemyDistance <
            ownDistance;
    }

    public bool IsHomeObjectiveForFaction(
        ObjectiveController objective,
        string faction)
    {
        if (objective == null ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            faction == factions[0]
            ? objective.MissionRole ==
                MissionObjectiveRole
                    .PlayerOneHome
            : objective.MissionRole ==
                MissionObjectiveRole
                    .PlayerTwoHome;
    }

    public bool IsOpponentHomeObjectiveForFaction(
        ObjectiveController objective,
        string faction)
    {
        if (objective == null ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            faction == factions[0]
            ? objective.MissionRole ==
                MissionObjectiveRole
                    .PlayerTwoHome
            : objective.MissionRole ==
                MissionObjectiveRole
                    .PlayerOneHome;
    }

    public void ApplyFactionAttackRules(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        WeaponData weapon,
        AttackMode attackMode,
        UniversalAttackRuleState state)
    {
        if (factionRules != null)
        {
            factionRules.ApplyAttackModifiers(
                attacker,
                target,
                weapon,
                attackMode,
                state
            );
        }

        if (aeldariRules != null)
        {
            aeldariRules.ApplyAttackModifiers(
                attacker,
                target,
                weapon,
                attackMode,
                state
            );
        }
    }

                weapon,
                attackMode
              )
            : 0;
    }

    public int RollTabletopD3(
        string label)
    {
        int raw =
            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                label +
                " (D3)"
            );

        return Mathf.CeilToInt(
            raw / 2f
        );
    }

    public int ReanimateUnit(
        SquadController squad,
        int wounds)
    {
        if (squad == null ||
            wounds <= 0 ||
            !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
            squad.LivingModels <= 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int remaining = wounds;
        int restored = 0;

        int healed =
            squad.HealWounds(
                remaining
            );

        restored += healed;
        remaining -= healed;

        while (remaining > 0)
        {
            ModelToken dead =
                squad
                    .DestroyedModelTokens()
                    .FirstOrDefault();

            if (dead == null)
                break;

            Vector3 position;

            if (!TryFindReanimationPosition(
                squad,
                dead,
                out position))
            {
                break;
            }

            dead.Revive(
                1,
                position
            );

            restored++;
            remaining--;

            if (remaining > 0)
            {
                int extra =
                    dead.Heal(
                        remaining
                    );

                restored += extra;
                remaining -= extra;
            }
        }

        squad.RefreshVisuals();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        return restored;
    }

    private bool TryFindReanimationPosition(
        SquadController squad,
        ModelToken returningModel,
        out Vector3 result)
    {
        result =
            returningModel.transform.position;

        List<ModelToken> living =
            squad.LivingModelTokens();

        if (living.Count == 0)
            return false;

        float[] radii =
        {
            1.0f,
            1.4f,
            1.8f
        };

        foreach (ModelToken anchor
            in living)
        {
            foreach (float radius
                in radii)
            {
                for (int angle = 0;
                     angle < 360;
                     angle += 30)
                {
                    float radians =
                        angle *
                        Mathf.Deg2Rad;

                    Vector3 candidate =
                        anchor.transform.position +
                        new Vector3(
                            Mathf.Cos(radians) *
                                radius,
                            0f,
                            Mathf.Sin(radians) *
                                radius
                        );

                    if (!InsideBoard(
                            candidate))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (!CanPlaceModel(
                            returningModel,
                            candidate))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    result = candidate;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private bool UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
        string faction)
    {
        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                faction))
        {
            return
                aeldariRules.DetachmentIs(
                    faction,
                    AeldariDetachment.DevotedOfYnnead
                );
        }

        return
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.IsYnnari(
                faction
            );
    }

    public bool CanUseMacabreResilience(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (factionRules == null ||
            target == null ||
            attacker == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        bool validUnit =
            UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                unit.FactionId) &&
            unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
            (unit.HasKeyword("infantry") ||
             unit.HasKeyword("mounted")) &&
            !unit.HasKeyword(
                "wraith construct");

        return
            validUnit &&
            !unit.FactionMacabreResilienceActive &&
            !unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase &&
            !unit.IsBattleShocked &&
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) >= 1;
    }

    public bool UseMacabreResilience(
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (target == null)
            return false;

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Macabre Resilience"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.FactionMacabreResilienceActive =
            true;

        status =
            "MACABRE RESILIENCE: " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            " is -1 to Wound until the end of the phase.";

        return true;
    }

    public bool CanUsePartingTheVeil(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (phase != Phase.Fight ||
            factionRules == null ||
            target == null ||
            attacker == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        return
            UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                unit.FactionId) &&
            unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
            !unit.PartingTheVeilActive &&
            !unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase &&
            !unit.IsBattleShocked &&
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) >= 2;
    }

    public bool UsePartingTheVeil(
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (target == null)
            return false;

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                2,
                "Parting the Veil"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.PartingTheVeilActive = true;

        status =
            "PARTING THE VEIL: destroyed models that have not fought can fight after the attacking unit finishes.";

        return true;
    }

    private bool TryUseSoulsight()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            phase != Phase.Shoot)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                unit.FactionId) ||
            !unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") ||
            unit.HasShot ||
            unit.IsBattleShocked ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Soulsight"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.FactionSoulsightActive =
            true;

        status =
            "SOULSIGHT: " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            " ranged weapons gain Lethal Hits and Ignores Cover until end of phase.";

        showStratagemMenu = false;
        return true;
    }

    private bool TryUseEmissariesOfYnnead()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            phase != Phase.Fight)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                unit.FactionId) ||
            !unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") ||
            !unit.HasKeyword("infantry") ||
            unit.HasFought ||
            unit.IsBattleShocked ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Emissaries of Ynnead"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (unit.IsBelowStartingStrength())
        {
            unit.FactionEmissariesRerollAll =
                true;

            status =
                "EMISSARIES OF YNNEAD: " +
                unit.DisplayName +
                " re-rolls failed Hit rolls in melee this phase.";
        }
        else
        {
            unit.FactionEmissariesRerollOnes =
                true;

            status =
                "EMISSARIES OF YNNEAD: " +
                unit.DisplayName +
                " re-rolls Hit rolls of 1 in melee this phase.";
        }

        showStratagemMenu = false;
        return true;
    }

    private bool TryUseHungryVoid()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            phase != Phase.Fight)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!factionRules.IsNecrons(
                unit.FactionId) ||
            unit.HasFought ||
            unit.IsBattleShocked ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Protocol of the Hungry Void"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.FactionHungryVoidActive =
            true;

        status =
            "PROTOCOL OF THE HUNGRY VOID: " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            " gets +1 melee Strength" +
            (unit.AttachedLeader != null
                ? " and +1 AP while led by a Necron Character."
                : ".");

        showStratagemMenu = false;
        return true;
    }

    private bool TryUseSuddenStorm()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            phase != Phase.Move)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!factionRules.IsNecrons(
                unit.FactionId) ||
            unit.IsBattleShocked ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Protocol of the Sudden Storm"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.FactionSuddenStormActive =
            true;

        status =
            "PROTOCOL OF THE SUDDEN STORM: " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            " ranged weapons count as Assault until end of turn.";
