        showStratagemMenu = false;
        return true;
    }

    private void OpenRuleChoice(
        string title,
        string description,
        IEnumerable<RuleChoiceOption> options)
    {
        ruleChoiceTitle =
            title ??
            "RULE CHOICE";

        ruleChoiceDescription =
            description ??
            "";

        ruleChoiceOptions.Clear();
        ruleChoiceScroll = Vector2.zero;

        if (options != null)
        {
            ruleChoiceOptions.AddRange(
                options.Where(
                    option =>
                        option != null
                )
            );
        }

        showRuleChoiceWindow =
            ruleChoiceOptions.Count > 0;
    }

    private void CloseRuleChoice()
    {
        showRuleChoiceWindow = false;
        ruleChoiceTitle = "";
        ruleChoiceDescription = "";
        ruleChoiceOptions.Clear();
    }

    private void BeginSpecialMove(
        SquadController squad,
        float maximumDistance,
        string label,
        System.Action onComplete)
    {
        if (squad == null ||
            !squad.IsAlive ||
            !squad.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            if (onComplete != null)
                onComplete();

            return;
        }

        specialMoveSquad =
            squad.JoinedActionController();

        specialMoveMaxDistance =
            Mathf.Max(
                0f,
                maximumDistance
            );

        specialMoveLabel =
            label ??
            "Special Move";

        specialMoveCompleted =
            onComplete;

        specialMoveIsSurge = false;
        specialMoveSurgeTarget = null;
        specialMoveSurgeStartDistance = 0f;

        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();
        selectedSquad = specialMoveSquad;
        selectedModel = null;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = true;
        specialMoveSquad.SetSelected(true);

        status =
            specialMoveLabel +
            ": click the board to move " +
            specialMoveSquad.DisplayName +
            " up to " +
            specialMoveMaxDistance.ToString("0.#") +
            ".";
    }

    private void BeginSurgeMove(
        SquadController squad,
        float maximumDistance,
        string label,
        System.Action onComplete)
    {
        if (squad == null ||
            !squad.IsAlive ||
            !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
            squad.IsBattleShocked ||
            IsEngaged(squad))
        {
            if (onComplete != null)
                onComplete();

            return;
        }

        SquadController target =
            FindNearestEnemy(
                squad
            );

        if (target == null)
        {
            if (onComplete != null)
                onComplete();

            return;
        }

        BeginSpecialMove(
            squad,
            maximumDistance,
            label,
            onComplete
        );

        specialMoveIsSurge = true;
        specialMoveSurgeTarget =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        specialMoveSurgeStartDistance =
            JoinedDistance(
                specialMoveSquad,
                specialMoveSurgeTarget
            );

        status =
            specialMoveLabel +
            ": SURGE toward closest enemy " +
            specialMoveSurgeTarget.DisplayName +
            " up to " +
            specialMoveMaxDistance.ToString("0.#") +
            ". End closer, engage it if reachable, and do not engage another enemy.";
    }

    private void TryCompleteSpecialMove(
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        SquadController squad =
            specialMoveSquad;

        if (squad == null)
            return;

        List<ModelToken> joined =
            JoinedModels(squad);

        if (joined.Count == 0)
        {
            FinishSpecialMove();
            return;
        }

        Vector3 centre =
            Vector3.zero;

        foreach (ModelToken model in joined)
            centre += model.transform.position;

        centre /= joined.Count;

        destination.y = centre.y;

        Vector3 delta =
            destination - centre;

        delta.y = 0f;

        float distance =
            HorizontalMagnitude(delta);

        if (distance >
            specialMoveMaxDistance +
            0.001f)
        {
            status =
                specialMoveLabel +
                " move too far: " +
                distance.ToString("0.0") +
                " / " +
                specialMoveMaxDistance.ToString("0.0") +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original =
            CaptureJoinedPositions(
                squad
            );

        TranslateJoinedModels(
            squad,
            delta
        );

        bool legal =
            AllModelsInsideBoard(
                squad
            ) &&
            AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                squad
            ) &&
            squad.IsCoherent();

        if (specialMoveIsSurge)
        {
            float endDistance =
                specialMoveSurgeTarget != null
                ? JoinedDistance(
                    squad,
                    specialMoveSurgeTarget
                  )
                : 999f;

            bool closer =
                endDistance <
                    specialMoveSurgeStartDistance -
                    0.001f;

            bool targetReachable =
                specialMoveSurgeStartDistance <=
                    specialMoveMaxDistance +
                    EngagementRange +
                    0.05f;

            bool engagedTarget =
                specialMoveSurgeTarget != null &&
                UnitsAreEngaged(
                    squad,
                    specialMoveSurgeTarget
                );

            bool engagedWrongEnemy =
                squads.Any(
                    enemy =>
                        enemy != null &&
                        enemy.IsAlive &&
                        enemy.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        enemy.FactionId !=
                            squad.FactionId &&
                        enemy.JoinedActionController() !=
                            specialMoveSurgeTarget &&
                        UnitsAreEngaged(
                            squad,
                            enemy
                        )
                );

            legal =
                legal &&
                closer &&
                !engagedWrongEnemy &&
                (!targetReachable ||
                 engagedTarget);
        }
        else
        {
            legal =
                legal &&
                !IsEngaged(
                    squad
                );
        }

        if (!legal)
        {
            RestoreJoinedPositions(
                original
            );

            status =
                specialMoveLabel +
                " rejected: collision, board edge, engagement or coherency problem.";
            return;
        }

        squad.HasMoved = true;

        if (squad.AttachedLeader != null)
            squad.AttachedLeader.HasMoved = true;

        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        status =
            specialMoveLabel +
            ": moved " +
            squad.DisplayName +
            " " +
            distance.ToString("0.0") +
            ".";

        FinishSpecialMove();
    }

    private void FinishSpecialMove()
    {
        System.Action callback =
            specialMoveCompleted;

        specialMoveSquad = null;
        specialMoveMaxDistance = 0f;
        specialMoveLabel = "";
        specialMoveCompleted = null;
        specialMoveIsSurge = false;
        specialMoveSurgeTarget = null;
        specialMoveSurgeStartDistance = 0f;

        ClearSelection();

        if (callback != null)
            callback();
    }

    private void BeginSpecialShoot(
        SquadController shooter,
        SquadController forcedTarget,
        string label,
        System.Action onComplete)
    {
        if (shooter == null ||
            !shooter.IsAlive ||
            !shooter.IsOnBattlefield)
        {
            if (onComplete != null)
                onComplete();

            return;
        }

        specialShootUnit =
            shooter.JoinedActionController();

        specialShootForcedTarget =
            forcedTarget != null
            ? forcedTarget.JoinedActionController()
            : null;

        specialShootLabel =
            label ??
            "Special Shooting";

        specialShootCompleted =
            onComplete;

        if (specialShootForcedTarget != null)
        {
            TryCompleteSpecialShoot(
                specialShootForcedTarget
            );
            return;
        }

        status =
            specialShootLabel +
            ": click an eligible enemy target for " +
            specialShootUnit.DisplayName +
            ".";
    }

    private void TryCompleteSpecialShoot(
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (specialShootUnit == null ||
            target == null ||
            !target.IsAlive ||
            !target.IsOnBattlefield ||
            target.FactionId ==
                specialShootUnit.FactionId)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (specialShootForcedTarget != null &&
            target.JoinedActionController() !=
                specialShootForcedTarget)
        {
            status =
                specialShootLabel +
                " must target " +
                specialShootForcedTarget.DisplayName +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        SquadController shooter =
            specialShootUnit;

        bool engaged =
            IsEngaged(shooter);

        List<WeaponAttackSelection> weapons =
            GetEligibleRangedWeapons(
                shooter,
                target,
                engaged
            );

        if (weapons.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                specialShootLabel +
                ": no eligible ranged weapons can target " +
                target.DisplayName +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        System.Action callback =
            specialShootCompleted;

        specialShootUnit = null;
        specialShootForcedTarget = null;
        specialShootLabel = "";
        specialShootCompleted = null;

        interactiveAttackCompletionCallback =
            callback;

        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction =
            false;
        interactiveAttackSuppressesPostReactions =
            true;

        BeginInteractiveAttack(
            shooter,
            target,
            weapons,
            AttackMode.Ranged,
            engaged
        );
    }

    public float JoinedDistancePublic(
        SquadController a,
        SquadController b)
    {
        return JoinedDistance(a, b);
    }

    public bool FriendlyKeywordWithin(
        SquadController unit,
        string keywordA,
        string keywordB,
        float distance)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return false;

        return squads.Any(
            other =>
                other != null &&
                other.IsAlive &&
                other.IsOnBattlefield &&
                other.FactionId ==
                    unit.FactionId &&
                other != unit &&
                other.HasKeyword(
                    keywordA) &&
                other.HasKeyword(
                    keywordB) &&
                JoinedDistance(
                    other,
                    unit
                ) <=
                    distance + 0.001f
        );
    }

    public bool FriendlyEnhancementAuraWithin(
        SquadController unit,
        string ruleName,
        float distance,
        bool excludeCharacters)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return false;

        return squads.Any(
            other =>
                other != null &&
                other.IsAlive &&
                other.IsOnBattlefield &&
                other.FactionId ==
                    unit.FactionId &&
                JoinedDistance(
                    other,
                    unit
                ) <=
                    distance + 0.001f &&
                (!excludeCharacters ||
                 !unit.HasKeyword(
                    "character")) &&
                FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                    other,
                    ruleName
                )
        );
    }

    public bool UnitWithinAnyObjective(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        return objectives.Any(
            objective =>
                objective != null &&
                objective.UnitWithinRange(
                    unit
                )
        );
    }

    private List<SquadController> EngagedEnemies(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return new List<SquadController>();

        return squads
            .Where(
                other =>
                    other != null &&
                    other.IsAlive &&
                    other.IsOnBattlefield &&
                    !other.IsAttachedLeader &&
                    other.FactionId !=
                        unit.FactionId &&
                    JoinedDistance(
                        unit,
                        other
                    ) <=
                        EngagementRange +
                        0.05f
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private PhaseSnapshot CurrentPhaseSnapshot()
    {
        switch (phase)
        {
            case Phase.Command:
                return PhaseSnapshot.Command;
            case Phase.Move:
                return PhaseSnapshot.Move;
            case Phase.Shoot:
                return PhaseSnapshot.Shoot;
            case Phase.Charge:
                return PhaseSnapshot.Charge;
            case Phase.Fight:
                return PhaseSnapshot.Fight;
            default:
                return PhaseSnapshot.End;
        }
    }

    public void RecordModelDestroyed(
        ModelToken model,
        SquadController attacker)
    {
        if (model == null ||
            model.Squad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        DestroyedModelRecord record =
            new DestroyedModelRecord
            {
                Model = model,
                Unit = model.Squad,
                Attacker = attacker,
                Position = model.transform.position,
                Phase = CurrentPhaseSnapshot(),
                HadFought = model.Squad.HasFought
            };

        destroyedModelsThisPhase.Add(
            record
        );

        if (missionSystem != null)
        {
            missionSystem.RecordModelDestroyed(
                model
            );
        }

        SquadController unit =
            model.Squad;

        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            attacker != null &&
            attacker.FactionId !=
                unit.FactionId &&
            aeldariRules.DetachmentIs(
                unit.FactionId,
                AeldariDetachment.SpiritConclave
            ) &&
            unit.HasKeyword("asuryani") &&
            unit.HasKeyword("psyker"))
        {
            SquadController marked =
                attacker.JoinedActionController();

            marked.AeldariVengefulDeadTokens++;

            AppendBattleLog(
                "AELDARI",
                "Vengeful Dead",
                marked.DisplayName +
                " gained a Vengeful Dead token after destroying an Asuryani Psyker model."
            );
        }

        if (unit.LivingModels == 0 &&
            !destroyedUnitsRecordedThisPhase.Contains(
                unit))
        {
            destroyedUnitsRecordedThisPhase.Add(
                unit
            );

            destroyedUnitsThisPhase.Add(
                new DestroyedUnitRecord
                {
                    Unit = unit,
                    Attacker = attacker,
                    LastPosition =
                        model.transform.position,
                    FactionId = unit.FactionId,
                    Phase = CurrentPhaseSnapshot()
                }
            );

            if (missionSystem != null)
            {
                missionSystem.RecordUnitDestroyed(
                    unit,
                    attacker
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private List<DestroyedModelRecord>
        DestroyedModelsForUnitThisPhase(
            SquadController unit)
    {
        return destroyedModelsThisPhase
            .Where(
                record =>
                    record != null &&
                    record.Unit == unit
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    public bool SpendStratagemCPForUnit(
        SquadController unit,
        int cost,
        string label)
    {
        return SpendFactionStratagemCP(
            unit,
            cost,
            label
        );
    }

    private bool SpendFactionStratagemCP(
        SquadController target,
        int baseCost,
        string label)
    {
        if (target == null ||
            target.IsBattleShocked)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        if (unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase)
        {
            status =
                label +
                ": that unit has already been targeted by a Stratagem this phase.";
            return false;
        }

        string usageKey =
            unit.FactionId +
            "|" +
            (label ?? "Stratagem");

        if (stratagemsUsedThisPhase.Contains(
                usageKey))
        {
            status =
                label +
                ": that Stratagem has already been used by this player this phase.";
            return false;
        }

        int cost =
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                baseCost
            );

        SquadController overlord = null;

        if (FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                unit,
                "My Will Be Done"))
        {
            if (UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                    unit,
                    "My Will Be Done"))
            {
                overlord = unit;
            }
            else if (unit.AttachedLeader != null &&
                     UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                        unit.AttachedLeader,
                        "My Will Be Done"))
            {
                overlord = unit.AttachedLeader;
            }

            if (overlord != null &&
                overlord.MyWillBeDoneUsedRound !=
                    round)
            {
                cost =
                    Mathf.Max(
                        0,
                        cost - 1
                    );
            }
        }

        if (!TrySpendCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId,
                cost))
        {
            status =
                label +
                ": not enough CP.";
            return false;
        }

        stratagemsUsedThisPhase.Add(
            usageKey
        );

        unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase = true;

        if (unit.AttachedLeader != null)
            unit.AttachedLeader.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase = true;

        if (overlord != null &&
            cost < baseCost)
        {
            overlord.MyWillBeDoneUsedRound =
                round;

            status =
                "My Will Be Done reduced " +
                label +
                " by 1CP. ";
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool TryUseConqueringTyrant()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            phase != Phase.Shoot)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad.JoinedActionController();

        if (!factionRules.IsNecrons(
                unit.FactionId) ||
            unit.HasShot ||
            unit.IsBattleShocked ||
            unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                unit.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            status =
                "Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant is not available for the selected unit.";
            return false;
        }

        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                unit,
                1,
                "Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant"))
        {
            return false;
        }

        unit.FactionConqueringTyrantActive = true;

        status =
            "PROTOCOL OF THE CONQUERING TYRANT: at half range, " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            (unit.AttachedLeader != null &&
             unit.AttachedLeader.IsAlive &&
             unit.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword("necrons") &&
             unit.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword("character")
                ? " re-rolls failed Hit rolls."
                : " re-rolls Hit rolls of 1.");

        showStratagemMenu = false;
        return true;
    }

    private void BeginInteractiveAttack(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> selections,
        AttackMode attackMode,
        bool engagedShooting)
    {
        if (interactiveAttack != null)
        {
            status =
                "Another attack is already being resolved.";
            return;
        }

        ClearArmedCommandReroll();

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.AttackStarted,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    attacker.FactionId,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = attacker,
                Target = target,
                AttackMode =
                    attackMode
            }
        );

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            BeginTraditionalAttackDeclaration(
                attacker,
                target,
                selections,
                attackMode,
                engagedShooting
            );

            return;
        }

        interactiveAttack =
            new InteractiveAttackController(
                this,
                attacker,
                target,
                selections,
                attackMode
            );

        status =
            attacker.DisplayName +
            " attacks " +
            target.DisplayName +
            (IsXcomMode
                ? ". XCOM resolution running."
                : ". Resolve the dice popup stage by stage.") +
            (engagedShooting
                ? " PISTOLS ONLY."
                : "");

        if (IsXcomMode)
        {
            ResumeXcomAttack();
        }
    }

    private string TraditionalAttackSelectionSummary(
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> selections)
    {
        if (selections == null ||
            selections.Count == 0)
        {
            return "No weapon profile";
        }

        return string.Join(
            " | ",
            selections
                .Where(
                    selection =>
                        selection != null &&
                        selection.weapon != null
                )
                .Select(
                    selection =>
                    {
                        WeaponData weapon =
                            selection.weapon;

                        string attacks =
                            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                weapon.attacksExpression)
                            ? weapon.attacksPerModel
                                .ToString()
                            : weapon.attacksExpression;

                        string damage =
                            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                weapon.damageExpression)
                            ? weapon.damage.ToString()
                            : weapon.damageExpression;

                        return
                            (selection.model != null
                                ? selection.model.RoleName +
                                  ": "
                                : "") +
                            weapon.displayName +
                            " • A " +
                            attacks +
                            " • S " +
                            weapon.strength +
                            " • AP " +
                            weapon.ap +
                            " • D " +
                            damage;
                    }
                )
                .ToArray()
        );
    }

    private void BeginTraditionalAttackDeclaration(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> selections,
        AttackMode attackMode,
        bool engagedShooting)
    {
        if (traditionalAttackPending)
        {
            status =
                "Finish the currently declared Traditional attack first.";
            return;
        }

        traditionalAttackPending = true;
        traditionalAttackAttacker = attacker;
        traditionalAttackTarget = target;
        traditionalAttackSelections =
            selections != null
            ? new List<WeaponAttackSelection>(
                selections
              )
            : new List<WeaponAttackSelection>();
        traditionalAttackMode = attackMode;
        traditionalAttackConsumesAction =
            interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction;
        traditionalAttackModelLevel =
            interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting;
        traditionalAttackCompletionCallback =
            interactiveAttackCompletionCallback;

        if (attackMode ==
            AttackMode.Ranged)
        {
            foreach (WeaponAttackSelection selection
                in traditionalAttackSelections)
            {
                if (selection == null ||
                    selection.model == null ||
                    selection.weapon == null)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                selection.model.MarkWeaponUsed(
                    selection.weapon
                );

                if (traditionalAttackModelLevel)
                {
                    selection.model
                        .MarkRangedWeaponFiredThisTurn(
                            selection.weapon
                        );
                }
            }
        }

        showDiceTray = true;
        manualWoundEditMode = true;
        EnsureTraditionalDiceTray();

        string weaponSummary =
            TraditionalAttackSelectionSummary(
                traditionalAttackSelections
            );

        AppendBattleLog(
            attackMode == AttackMode.Ranged
            ? "SHOOTING DECLARATION"
            : "FIGHT DECLARATION",
            attacker.DisplayName +
            " → " +
            target.DisplayName,
            weaponSummary +
            ". Traditional mode: dice and damage are entirely player-resolved."
        );

        status =
            "TRADITIONAL: " +
            attacker.DisplayName +
            " → " +
            target.DisplayName +
            " • " +
            weaponSummary +
            ". Use the free 3D dice tray and manual wound controls, then click ATTACK RESOLVED." +
            (engagedShooting
                ? " PISTOLS/CLOSE-QUARTERS ONLY."
                : "");
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalAttackDeclaration()
    {
        if (!traditionalAttackPending)
            return;

        SquadController attacker =
            traditionalAttackAttacker;
        SquadController target =
            traditionalAttackTarget;
        AttackMode attackMode =
            traditionalAttackMode;
        bool consumeAction =
            traditionalAttackConsumesAction;
        bool modelLevel =
            traditionalAttackModelLevel;
        System.Action completion =
            traditionalAttackCompletionCallback;

        List<string> hazardousWeapons =
            traditionalAttackSelections
                .Where(
                    selection =>
                        selection != null &&
                        selection.weapon != null &&
                        WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                            selection.weapon,
                            "hazardous"
                        )
                )
                .Select(
                    selection =>
                        selection.weapon.displayName
                )
                .Distinct()
                .ToList();

        traditionalAttackPending = false;
        traditionalAttackAttacker = null;
        traditionalAttackTarget = null;
        traditionalAttackSelections.Clear();
        traditionalAttackConsumesAction = true;
        traditionalAttackModelLevel = false;
        traditionalAttackCompletionCallback = null;

        interactiveAttackCompletionCallback = null;
        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction = true;
        interactiveAttackSuppressesPostReactions = false;
        interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting = false;

        if (attacker != null)
        {
            if (attackMode ==
                AttackMode.Ranged)
            {
                if (modelLevel)
                {
                    RefreshModelLevelShootingState(
                        attacker
                    );
                }
                else if (consumeAction)
                {
                    attacker.HasShot = true;

                    if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
                    {
                        attacker.AttachedLeader.HasShot = true;
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (consumeAction)
            {
                attacker.HasFought = true;

                if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
                {
                    attacker.AttachedLeader.HasFought = true;
                }

                if (attacker.IsAlive)
                {
                    SquadController consolidationTarget =
                        FindNearestEnemy(attacker);

                    if (consolidationTarget != null)
                    {
                        AutoMoveSquadToward(
                            attacker,
                            consolidationTarget,
                            attacker.SuddenStrikeActive
                                ? 6f
                                : ConsolidateDistance,
                            0.86f
                        );
                    }
                }

                attacker.KatahSustainedActive = false;
                attacker.KatahLethalActive = false;

                AdvanceFightPriority(
                    attacker
                );
            }
        }

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.AttackResolved,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    attacker != null
                    ? attacker.FactionId
                    : activeFaction,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = attacker,
                Target = target,
                AttackMode = attackMode,
                Amount = 0
            }
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            attackMode == AttackMode.Ranged
            ? "SHOOTING"
            : "MELEE",
            "Traditional attack resolved",
            "Dice and damage were resolved manually by the players. Warboard changed no wounds automatically."
        );

        if (hazardousWeapons.Count > 0)
        {
            QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                "HAZARDOUS",
                "The resolved attack used: " +
                string.Join(
                    ", ",
                    hazardousWeapons.ToArray()
                ) +
                ". Resolve all required Hazardous rolls yourself and manually apply any wounds/models.",
                Mathf.Max(
                    1,
                    hazardousWeapons.Count
                )
            );
        }

        HandleBodyguardDestroyed(target);
        HandleBodyguardDestroyed(attacker);
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        manualWoundEditMode =
            traditionalRuleAlertPending;
        pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate = null;

        if (completion != null)
        {
            completion();
        }
        else
        {
            status =
                "Traditional attack marked RESOLVED. No dice or damage were interpreted by Warboard.";
        }
    }

    private List<ModelToken>
        TraditionalRestoreCandidates()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null)
        {
            return new List<ModelToken>();
        }

        SquadController unit =
            selectedSquad
                .JoinedActionController();

        List<ModelToken> dead =
            unit.DestroyedModelTokens()
                .Where(
                    model =>
                        model != null
                )
                .ToList();

        if (unit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            dead.AddRange(
                unit.AttachedLeader
                    .DestroyedModelTokens()
                    .Where(
                        model =>
                            model != null
                    )
            );
        }

        return dead;
    }

    private void CycleTraditionalRestoreCandidate(
        int direction)
    {
        List<ModelToken> dead =
            TraditionalRestoreCandidates();

        if (dead.Count == 0)
        {
            manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;
            return;
        }

        manualRestoreDeadIndex =
            (manualRestoreDeadIndex +
             direction) %
            dead.Count;

        if (manualRestoreDeadIndex < 0)
        {
            manualRestoreDeadIndex +=
                dead.Count;
        }
    }

    private void TraditionalRestoreCurrentModel(
        bool fullWounds)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            selectedSquad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        List<ModelToken> dead =
            TraditionalRestoreCandidates();

        if (dead.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                selectedSquad.DisplayName +
                " has no destroyed models to restore.";
            return;
        }

        manualRestoreDeadIndex =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                manualRestoreDeadIndex,
                0,
                dead.Count - 1
            );

        ModelToken returning =
            dead[
                manualRestoreDeadIndex
            ];

        SquadController owner =
            returning.Squad;

        if (owner == null)
            return;

        Vector3 position;

        if (!TryFindReanimationPosition(
                owner,
                returning,
                out position))
        {
            // For an Attached Leader with no other living Leader model,
            // use the joined Bodyguard as the placement anchor.
            SquadController joined =
                selectedSquad
                    .JoinedActionController();

            if (!TryFindReanimationPosition(
                    joined,
                    returning,
                    out position))
            {
                status =
                    "No legal nearby placement was found for " +
                    returning.RoleName +
                    ".";
                return;
            }
        }

        int wounds =
            fullWounds
            ? returning.MaxWounds
            : 1;

        returning.Revive(
            wounds,
            position
        );

        owner.RefreshVisuals();

        SquadController joinedUnit =
            selectedSquad
                .JoinedActionController();

        joinedUnit.RefreshVisuals();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "RESTORE",
            joinedUnit.DisplayName,
            returning.RoleName +
            " manually returned at " +
            wounds +
            "/" +
            returning.MaxWounds +
            " wounds. Warboard did not determine whether a rule permitted this."
        );

        manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

        status =
            returning.RoleName +
            " manually returned at " +
            wounds +
            " wound(s).";
    }

    private void TraditionalAdjustSelectedWounds(
        int delta)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            selectedModel == null ||
            !selectedModel.IsAlive ||
            delta == 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (delta < 0 &&
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds <=
                -delta)
        {
            TryTraditionalRemoveSelectedModel();
            return;
        }

        int before =
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds;

        if (delta < 0)
        {
            selectedModel.ApplyDamage(
                -delta
            );
        }
        else
        {
            selectedModel.Heal(
                delta
            );
        }

        int after =
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds;

        selectedModel.RefreshWoundDisplay();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "WOUNDS",
            selectedModel.RoleName,
            before +
            " → " +
            after +
            " wounds (manual Traditional adjustment)."
        );

        status =
            selectedModel.RoleName +
            ": " +
            after +
            "/" +
            selectedModel.MaxWounds +
            " wounds.";
    }

    private void TryTraditionalRemoveSelectedModel()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            selectedModel == null ||
            !selectedModel.IsAlive)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController actionUnit =
            selectedModel.Squad != null
            ? selectedModel.Squad
                .JoinedActionController()
            : null;

        if (actionUnit != null &&
            !actionUnit
                .WouldRemainCoherentAfterRemoving(
                    selectedModel
                ))
        {
            pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate =
                selectedModel;

            status =
                "COHERENCY WARNING: removing " +
                selectedModel.RoleName +
                " will leave the unit out of coherency. Choose another model or click REMOVE ANYWAY.";

            return;
        }

        ConfirmTraditionalModelRemoval(
            selectedModel
        );
    }

    private void ConfirmTraditionalModelRemoval(
        ModelToken model)
    {
        if (model == null ||
            !model.IsAlive)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            model.Squad != null
            ? model.Squad
                .JoinedActionController()
            : null;

        string role = model.RoleName;
        string squadName =
            model.Squad != null
            ? model.Squad.DisplayName
            : "Unit";

        model.ApplyDamage(
            model.CurrentWounds
        );

        RecordModelDestroyed(
            model,
            traditionalAttackPending
            ? traditionalAttackAttacker
            : null
        );

        QueueTraditionalDeathTrigger(
            model
        );

        int coherencyLosses =
            ResolveTraditionalForcedCoherencyLosses(
                unit,
                traditionalAttackPending
                ? traditionalAttackAttacker
                : null
            );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "CASUALTY",
            squadName,
            role +
            " removed manually." +
            (coherencyLosses > 0
                ? " " +
                  coherencyLosses +
                  " additional model(s) removed for Unit Coherency."
                : "")
        );

        pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate = null;
        selectedModel = null;

        HandleBodyguardDestroyed(unit);
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        status =
            role +
            " removed manually." +
            (coherencyLosses > 0
                ? " " +
                  coherencyLosses +
                  " additional model(s) removed to restore coherency."
                : " Resolve any death-triggered rules manually.");
    }

    private int ResolveTraditionalForcedCoherencyLosses(
        SquadController unit,
        SquadController attacker)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return 0;

        SquadController actionUnit =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        int removed = 0;
        int guard = 0;

        while (actionUnit.IsAlive &&
               !actionUnit.IsCoherent() &&
               guard < 64)
        {
            guard++;

            ModelToken casualty =
                actionUnit
                    .IncoherentModels()
                    .Where(
                        model =>
                            model != null &&
                            model.IsAlive
                    )
                    .OrderByDescending(
                        model =>
                            HorizontalDistance(
                                model.transform.position,
                                actionUnit.CurrentCentre()
                            )
                    )
                    .FirstOrDefault();

            if (casualty == null)
                break;

            casualty.ApplyDamage(
                casualty.CurrentWounds
            );

            RecordModelDestroyed(
                casualty,
                attacker
            );

            QueueTraditionalDeathTrigger(
                casualty
            );

            removed++;
        }

        actionUnit.RefreshVisuals();

        if (actionUnit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            actionUnit.AttachedLeader
                .RefreshVisuals();
        }

        return removed;
    }

    private void QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
        string title,
        string text,
        int suggestedDice,
        System.Action completion = null)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode)
            return;

        if (completion != null)
        {
            System.Action previous =
                traditionalRuleAlertCompletion;

            traditionalRuleAlertCompletion =
                previous == null
                ? completion
                : () =>
                {
                    previous();
                    completion();
                };
        }

        if (traditionalRuleAlertPending)
        {
            traditionalRuleAlertText +=
                "\n\n" +
                title +
                ": " +
                text;

            traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice =
                Mathf.Max(
                    traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice,
                    suggestedDice
                );
        }
        else
        {
            traditionalRuleAlertPending = true;
            traditionalRuleAlertTitle =
                title ?? "TRIGGERED RULE";
            traditionalRuleAlertText =
                text ?? "";
            traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice =
                Mathf.Max(
                    0,
                    suggestedDice
                );
        }

        if (traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice > 0)
        {
            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice
            );
        }

        AppendBattleLog(
            "TRIGGER",
            traditionalRuleAlertTitle,
            text +
            " Resolve this rule manually in Traditional mode."
        );
    }

    private void AcknowledgeTraditionalRuleAlert()
    {
        traditionalRuleAlertPending = false;
        traditionalRuleAlertTitle = "";
        traditionalRuleAlertText = "";
        traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice = 1;

        System.Action completion =
            traditionalRuleAlertCompletion;

        traditionalRuleAlertCompletion = null;

        status =
            "Triggered-rule reminder acknowledged. Apply any resulting wounds/models manually.";

        if (completion != null)
        {
            completion();
        }
        else if (endPhaseFlowRunning)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
        }
    }

    private void QueueTraditionalDeathTrigger(
        ModelToken destroyedModel)
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            destroyedModel == null ||
            destroyedModel.Squad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        string expression =
            UniversalRuleRegistry
                .GetDeadlyDemiseExpression(
                    destroyedModel.Squad
                );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                expression))
        {
            return;
        }

        QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
            "DEADLY DEMISE",
            destroyedModel.Squad.DisplayName +
            " has Deadly Demise " +
            expression +
            ". Roll the trigger and any resulting mortal wounds yourself, then use WOUND EDIT / REMOVE to apply the outcome.",
            1
        );
    }

    private void ResumeXcomAttack()
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null)
            return;

        bool completed =
            interactiveAttack
                .FastResolveUntilDecision();

        if (completed)
        {
            FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            return;
        }

        status =
            "XCOM RESOLUTION PAUSED — decision required: " +
            interactiveAttack.StageTitle +
            ".";
    }

    private void StartPostAttackFlow(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack,
        System.Action finalizer)
    {
        postAttackFlowQueue.Clear();
        postAttackFinalizer = finalizer;

        if (resolvedAttack == null)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        if (resolvedAttack.Mode ==
            AttackMode.Melee)
        {
            postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                () =>
                    OfferFightOnDeath(
                        resolvedAttack
                    )
            );
        }

        postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            () =>
                OfferUndyingLegions(
                    resolvedAttack
                )
        );

        postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            () =>
                OfferVengefulStars(
                    resolvedAttack
                )
        );

        postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            () =>
                OfferPallOfDread(
                    resolvedAttack
                )
        );

        postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            () =>
                ResolveStormOfWhispers(
                    resolvedAttack
                )
        );

        if (resolvedAttack.Mode ==
            AttackMode.Ranged)
        {
            postAttackFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                () =>
                {
                    if (!OfferFadeBackAfterAttack(
                            resolvedAttack,
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow))
                    {
                        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                    }
                }
            );
        }

        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
    }

    private void ContinuePostAttackFlow()
    {
        if (showRuleChoiceWindow ||
            specialMoveSquad != null ||
            specialShootUnit != null ||
            interactiveAttack != null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (postAttackFlowQueue.Count > 0)
        {
            System.Action next =
                postAttackFlowQueue.Dequeue();

            next();
            return;
        }

        System.Action finalizer =
            postAttackFinalizer;

        postAttackFinalizer = null;

        if (finalizer != null)
            finalizer();
    }

    private void OfferFightOnDeath(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack)
    {
        SquadController target =
            resolvedAttack.Target != null
            ? resolvedAttack.Target.JoinedActionController()
            : null;

        if (target == null)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<ModelToken> candidates =
            new List<ModelToken>();

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in resolvedAttack.DestroyedModels)
        {
            if (model == null ||
                model.Squad == null)
            {
                continue;
            }

            DestroyedModelRecord record =
                destroyedModelsThisPhase
                    .LastOrDefault(
                        item =>
                            item != null &&
                            item.Model == model
                    );

            if (record != null &&
                record.HadFought)
            {
                continue;
            }

            bool parting =
                target.PartingTheVeilActive &&
                target.HasKeyword("ynnari");

            bool malevolentSouls =
                UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                    model.Squad,
                    "Malevolent Souls"
                );

            if (parting)
            {
                candidates.Add(model);
                continue;
            }

            if (malevolentSouls)
            {
                int roll =
                    DiceRoller.RollD6(
                        "Malevolent Souls: " +
                        target.DisplayName
                    );

                if (roll >= 3)
                    candidates.Add(model);
            }
        }

        if (candidates.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<WeaponAttackSelection> selections =
            BuildFightOnDeathSelections(
                candidates
            );

        if (selections.Count == 0 ||
            resolvedAttack.Attacker == null ||
            !resolvedAttack.Attacker.IsAlive)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "FIGHT ON DEATH",
            candidates.Count +
            " destroyed model(s) can fight after the attacking unit finishes.",
            new[]
            {
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Resolve fight-on-death attacks",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        interactiveAttackCompletionCallback =
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow;

                        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction =
                            false;
                        interactiveAttackSuppressesPostReactions =
                            true;

                        BeginInteractiveAttack(
                            target,
                            resolvedAttack.Attacker,
                            selections,
                            AttackMode.Melee,
                            false
                        );
                    }
                ),
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Skip fight-on-death attacks",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();
                        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                    }
                )
            }
        );
    }

    private List<WeaponAttackSelection>
        BuildFightOnDeathSelections(
            List<ModelToken> models)
    {
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> result =
            new List<WeaponAttackSelection>();

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in models)
        {
            if (model == null)
                continue;

            List<WeaponData> weapons =
                model.MeleeWeapons
                    .Where(
                        weapon =>
                            weapon != null
                    )
                    .ToList();

            WeaponData normal =
                weapons.FirstOrDefault(
                    weapon =>
                        !WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                            weapon,
                            "extra_attacks")
                );

            if (normal != null)
            {
                result.Add(
                    new WeaponAttackSelection(
                        model,
                        normal
                    )
                );
            }

            foreach (WeaponData extra
                in weapons.Where(
                    weapon =>
                        WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                            weapon,
                            "extra_attacks")
                ))
            {
                result.Add(
                    new WeaponAttackSelection(
                        model,
                        extra
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        return result;
    }

    private void OfferUndyingLegions(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack)
    {
        SquadController target =
            resolvedAttack.Target != null
            ? resolvedAttack.Target.JoinedActionController()
            : null;

        int destroyed =
            resolvedAttack.DestroyedModels.Count;

        bool eligible =
            target != null &&
            target.IsAlive &&
            target.IsOnBattlefield &&
            target.LivingModels > 0 &&
            destroyed > 0 &&
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.IsNecrons(
                target.FactionId) &&
            !target.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase &&
            GetCommandPoints(
                target.FactionId
            ) >= 1;

        if (!eligible)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "PROTOCOL OF THE UNDYING LEGIONS",
            target.DisplayName +
            " lost one or more models to this attack. Activate Reanimation Protocols now?",
            new[]
            {
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Reanimate (1 CP)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                target,
                                1,
                                "Protocol of the Undying Legions"))
                        {
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            SelectSquadForAction(
                                target
                            );

                            manualRestoreEditMode = true;
                            manualWoundEditMode = false;
                            manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

                            QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                                "PROTOCOL OF THE UNDYING LEGIONS",
                                target.DisplayName +
                                ": resolve the Reanimation roll and any Character bonus yourself, then use WOUND EDIT / RESTORE EDIT to apply exactly what the tabletop result permits.",
                                1,
                                ContinuePostAttackFlow
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int amount =
                            RollTabletopD3(
                                "Undying Legions: " +
                                target.DisplayName
                            );

                        if (target.AttachedLeader != null &&
                            target.AttachedLeader.IsAlive &&
                            target.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword(
                                "necrons") &&
                            target.AttachedLeader.HasKeyword(
                                "character"))
                        {
                            amount += 1;
                        }

                        int restored =
                            ReanimateUnit(
                                target,
                                amount
                            );

                        status =
                            "UNDYING LEGIONS: restored " +
                            restored +
                            " wound(s).";

                        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                    }
                ),
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Skip",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();
                        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                    }
                )
            }
        );
    }

    private void OfferVengefulStars(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack)
    {
        if (resolvedAttack.Mode !=
                AttackMode.Ranged ||
            resolvedAttack.Target == null ||
            resolvedAttack.Attacker == null)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        DestroyedUnitRecord destroyed =
            destroyedUnitsThisPhase
                .LastOrDefault(
                    record =>
                        record != null &&
                        record.Unit ==
                            resolvedAttack.Target
                                .JoinedActionController()
                );

        if (destroyed == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            !factionRules.IsNecrons(
                destroyed.FactionId))
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.FactionId ==
                            destroyed.FactionId &&
                        unit.HasKeyword("character") &&
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            unit.CurrentCentre(),
                            destroyed.LastPosition
                        ) <= 6.001f &&
                        !unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase &&
                        GetCommandPoints(
                            unit.FactionId
                        ) >= 2
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Vengeful Stars: " +
                    captured.DisplayName +
                    " (2 CP)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                captured,
                                2,
                                "Protocol of the Vengeful Stars"))
                        {
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        BeginSpecialShoot(
                            captured,
                            resolvedAttack.Attacker,
                            "Protocol of the Vengeful Stars",
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "PROTOCOL OF THE VENGEFUL STARS",
            "A Necron unit was destroyed by this Shooting attack. An eligible nearby Necron Character can retaliate against the attacking unit.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void OfferPallOfDread(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack)
    {
        if (resolvedAttack.Target == null)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        SquadController target =
            resolvedAttack.Target.JoinedActionController();

        DestroyedUnitRecord destroyed =
            destroyedUnitsThisPhase
                .LastOrDefault(
                    record =>
                        record != null &&
                        record.Unit == target
                );

        if (destroyed == null ||
            factionRules == null ||
            !UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                target.FactionId) ||
            !target.HasKeyword("ynnari") ||
            target.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase ||
            GetCommandPoints(
                target.FactionId
            ) < 1)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<ObjectiveController> eligible =
            objectives
                .Where(
                    objective =>
                    {
                        string previous;

                        bool controlled =
                            objectiveControlAtPhaseStart.TryGetValue(
                                objective,
                                out previous
                            ) &&
                            previous ==
                                target.FactionId;

                        bool wasInRange =
                            destroyedModelsThisPhase.Any(
                                modelRecord =>
                                    modelRecord != null &&
                                    modelRecord.Unit == target &&
                                    HorizontalDistance(
                                        objective.transform.position,
                                        modelRecord.Position
                                    ) <=
                                        ObjectiveController.ControlRadius +
                                        0.001f
                            );

                        return
                            controlled &&
                            wasInRange;
                    }
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        for (int i = 0;
             i < eligible.Count;
             i++)
        {
            ObjectiveController objective =
                eligible[i];

            int number = i + 1;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Pall of Dread: Objective " +
                    number +
                    " (1 CP)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                target,
                                1,
                                "Pall of Dread"))
                        {
                            ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        objective.SecureFor(
                            target.FactionId
                        );

                        objective.RefreshStatus(
                            squads
                        );

                        status =
                            "PALL OF DREAD: objective remains secured until the opponent's Level of Control exceeds yours at the end of a phase.";

                        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinuePostAttackFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "PALL OF DREAD",
            "The destroyed Ynnari unit was within an objective you controlled at the end of the previous phase.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void ResolveStormOfWhispers(
        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack)
    {
        if (resolvedAttack.Mode ==
                AttackMode.Ranged &&
            resolvedAttack.TotalHits > 0 &&
            resolvedAttack.Attacker != null &&
            resolvedAttack.Target != null &&
            FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                resolvedAttack.Attacker,
                "Storm of Whispers"))
        {
            ResolveImmediateBattleShock(
                resolvedAttack.Target,
                "Storm of Whispers"
            );
        }

        ContinuePostAttackFlow();
    }

    private void ResolveImmediateBattleShock(
        SquadController unit,
        string label)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            !unit.IsAlive)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController target =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            traditionalBattleShockQueue.Clear();
            traditionalBattleShockUnit = target;
            traditionalBattleShockLabel =
                label;
            traditionalBattleShockPending =
                true;

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                2
            );

            status =
                label +
                ": " +
                target.DisplayName +
                " must take a Battle-shock test. Resolve 2D6 manually and mark PASS or FAIL.";

            AppendBattleLog(
                "BATTLE-SHOCK",
                target.DisplayName,
                label +
                " triggered a manual Traditional Battle-shock test."
            );

            return;
        }

        int leadership =
            EffectiveLeadership(
                target
            );

        int roll =
            DiceRoller.Roll2D6(
                label +
                ": " +
                target.DisplayName
            );

        bool passed =
            roll >= leadership;

        target.SetBattleShocked(
            !passed,
            roll
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "BATTLE-SHOCK",
            target.DisplayName,
            label +
            " • Leadership " +
            leadership +
            "+ • roll " +
            roll +
            " → " +
            (passed
                ? "PASS"
                : "FAIL — BATTLE-SHOCKED (OC 0)")
        );

        status =
            label +
            ": " +
            target.DisplayName +
            " rolled " +
            roll +
            " vs Ld " +
            leadership +
            (passed
                ? " — passed."
                : " — BATTLE-SHOCKED.");
    }

    private void FinalizeInteractiveAttack()
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null ||
            !interactiveAttack.IsComplete)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack
            .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            pendingCasualtyCandidate = null;
            pendingCasualtyCoherencyWarning = false;

            InteractiveCasualtyChoice choice =
                interactiveAttack
                    .CurrentPendingCasualty;

            status =
                "CASUALTY SELECTION: " +
                (choice != null
                    ? choice.Label
                    : "model") +
                " was destroyed. Click the model you want to remove, or keep the automatic casualty.";

            return;
        }

        InteractiveAttackController resolvedAttack =
            interactiveAttack;

        SquadController attacker =
            resolvedAttack.Attacker;

        SquadController target =
            resolvedAttack.Target;

        AttackMode attackMode =
            resolvedAttack.Mode;

        int woundsLost =
            resolvedAttack.TotalWoundsLost;

        if (attackMode ==
            AttackMode.Ranged)
        {
            resolvedAttack.MarkOneShotWeaponsUsed();

            if (interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting)
            {
                resolvedAttack
                    .MarkRangedWeaponsFiredThisTurn();
            }
        }

        bool modelLevelShooting =
            interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting;

        bool consumeNormalAction =
            interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction;

        bool suppressPostReactions =
            interactiveAttackSuppressesPostReactions;

        System.Action completionCallback =
            interactiveAttackCompletionCallback;

        interactiveAttack = null;
        interactiveAttackCompletionCallback = null;
        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction = true;
        interactiveAttackSuppressesPostReactions = false;
        interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting = false;

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.AttackResolved,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    attacker != null
                    ? attacker.FactionId
                    : activeFaction,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = attacker,
                Target = target,
                AttackMode =
                    attackMode,
                Amount =
                    woundsLost
            }
        );

        if (consumeNormalAction &&
            attacker != null)
        {
            if (attackMode ==
                AttackMode.Ranged)
            {
                attacker.HasShot = true;

                if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
                    attacker.AttachedLeader.HasShot = true;
            }
            else
            {
                attacker.HasFought = true;

                if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
                    attacker.AttachedLeader.HasFought = true;
            }
        }

        if (modelLevelShooting &&
            attacker != null)
        {
            RefreshModelLevelShootingState(
                attacker
            );
        }

        HandleBodyguardDestroyed(target);
        HandleBodyguardDestroyed(attacker);
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        if (target != null &&
            !target.IsAlive &&
            (target.AttachedLeader == null ||
             !target.AttachedLeader.IsAlive))
        {
            GameEventBus.Raise(
                new GameEventContext
                {
                    Type =
                        GameEventType.UnitDestroyed,
                    Game = this,
                    ActingFaction =
                        attacker != null
                        ? attacker.FactionId
                        : activeFaction,
                    Phase = phase,
                    Source = attacker,
                    Target = target,
                    AttackMode =
                        attackMode
                }
            );
        }

        System.Action finalizer =
            () =>
            {
                if (attackMode ==
                        AttackMode.Melee &&
                    attacker != null &&
                    attacker.IsAlive &&
                    consumeNormalAction)
                {
                    SquadController consolidationTarget =
                        FindNearestEnemy(attacker);

                    if (consolidationTarget != null)
                    {
                        AutoMoveSquadToward(
                            attacker,
                            consolidationTarget,
                            attacker.SuddenStrikeActive
                                ? 6f
                                : ConsolidateDistance,
                            0.86f
                        );
                    }

                    attacker.KatahSustainedActive = false;
                    attacker.KatahLethalActive = false;

                    AdvanceFightPriority(
                        attacker
                    );
                }

                AppendBattleLog(
                    attackMode ==
                        AttackMode.Ranged
                    ? "SHOOTING"
                    : "MELEE",
                    resolvedAttack.SummaryText(),
                    resolvedAttack.AuditText()
                );

                status =
                    resolvedAttack.SummaryText() +
                    (attackMode ==
                        AttackMode.Melee &&
                     consumeNormalAction
                        ? " Consolidation resolved."
                        : "");

                if (completionCallback != null)
                    completionCallback();
            };

        if (suppressPostReactions)
        {
            finalizer();
        }
        else
        {
            StartPostAttackFlow(
                resolvedAttack,
                finalizer
            );
        }
    }

    private bool CasualtyCandidateIsValid(
        ModelToken candidate)
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null ||
            candidate == null ||
            !candidate.IsAlive)
        {
            return false;
        }

        InteractiveCasualtyChoice choice =
            interactiveAttack
                .CurrentPendingCasualty;

        if (choice == null ||
            choice.automaticModel == null ||
            choice.automaticModel.Squad == null ||
            candidate.Squad == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            candidate.Squad ==
            choice.automaticModel.Squad;
    }

    private void TryChooseAttackCasualty(
        ModelToken candidate)
    {
        if (!CasualtyCandidateIsValid(
                candidate))
        {
            status =
                "That model is not a legal replacement casualty for this destroyed model.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController actionUnit =
            candidate.Squad
                .JoinedActionController();

        bool coherent =
            actionUnit
                .WouldRemainCoherentAfterRemoving(
                    candidate
                );

        if (!coherent)
        {
            pendingCasualtyCandidate =
                candidate;

            pendingCasualtyCoherencyWarning =
                true;

            status =
                "COHERENCY WARNING: removing " +
                candidate.RoleName +
                " will leave the unit out of coherency. Confirm REMOVE ANYWAY or choose another model.";

            return;
        }

        ConfirmAttackCasualty(
            candidate
        );
    }

    private void ConfirmAttackCasualty(
        ModelToken candidate)
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null ||
            !interactiveAttack
                .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            return;
        }

        ModelToken casualty =
            interactiveAttack
                .ConfirmNextCasualty(
                    candidate
                );

        if (casualty != null)
        {
            AppendBattleLog(
                "CASUALTY",
                casualty.Squad != null
                ? casualty.Squad.DisplayName
                : "Unit",
                casualty.RoleName +
                " removed by player casualty selection."
            );
        }

        pendingCasualtyCandidate = null;
        pendingCasualtyCoherencyWarning = false;

        if (interactiveAttack
            .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            InteractiveCasualtyChoice next =
                interactiveAttack
                    .CurrentPendingCasualty;

            status =
                "CASUALTY SELECTED: " +
                (casualty != null
                    ? casualty.RoleName
                    : "model") +
                ". Select the next casualty (" +
                interactiveAttack
                    .PendingCasualtyCount +
                " remaining)." +
                (next != null
                    ? " Automatic suggestion: " +
                      next.Label +
                      "."
                    : "");

            return;
        }

        int coherencyLosses =
            ResolveForcedCoherencyLosses(
                interactiveAttack.Target,
                interactiveAttack.Attacker
            );

        if (coherencyLosses > 0)
        {
            status =
                coherencyLosses +
                " additional model(s) were removed because the unit remained out of coherency.";
        }

        FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
    }

    private int ResolveForcedCoherencyLosses(
        SquadController unit,
        SquadController attacker)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return 0;

        SquadController actionUnit =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        int removed = 0;
        int guard = 0;

        while (actionUnit.IsAlive &&
               !actionUnit.IsCoherent() &&
               guard < 64)
        {
            guard++;

            List<ModelToken> invalid =
                actionUnit
                    .IncoherentModels();

            ModelToken casualty =
                invalid
                    .Where(
                        model =>
                            model != null &&
                            model.IsAlive
                    )
                    .OrderByDescending(
                        model =>
                            HorizontalDistance(
                                model.transform.position,
                                actionUnit.CurrentCentre()
                            )
                    )
                    .FirstOrDefault();

            if (casualty == null)
                break;

            casualty.ApplyDamage(
                casualty.CurrentWounds
            );

            RecordModelDestroyed(
                casualty,
                attacker
            );

            ResolveDeadlyDemise(
                casualty
            );

            removed++;
        }

        actionUnit.RefreshVisuals();

        if (actionUnit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            actionUnit.AttachedLeader
                .RefreshVisuals();
        }

        return removed;
    }

    private bool TargetHasLoneOperativeProtection(
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (target == null)
            return false;

        SquadController unit =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        if (FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                unit,
                "Lone Operative"))
        {
            return true;
        }

        bool spiritseer =
            UniversalRuleRegistry.UnitHasRule(
                unit,
                "Spiritseer"
            ) ||
            unit.DisplayName.IndexOf(
                "Spiritseer",
                System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
            ) >= 0;

        if (!spiritseer)
            return false;

        return squads.Any(
            wraith =>
                wraith != null &&
                wraith.IsAlive &&
                wraith.IsOnBattlefield &&
                wraith.FactionId ==
