                    unit.FactionId &&
                wraith.HasKeyword(
                    "wraith construct") &&
                JoinedDistance(
                    unit,
                    wraith
                ) <= 3.001f
        );
    }

    private void TryShoot(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (missionSystem != null &&
            missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                attacker) &&
            !attacker.HasKeyword(
                "titanic"))
        {
            status =
                "That unit started a mission action this turn and cannot shoot.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.HasShot)
        {
            status =
                "That squad has already shot.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.HasFallenBack &&
            !(aeldariRules != null &&
              aeldariRules.CanShootAfterFallBack(
                  attacker
              )))
        {
            status =
                "That squad Fell Back this turn and cannot shoot.";
            return;
        }

        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.HasRange18Protection(
                target) &&
            JoinedDistance(
                attacker,
                target
            ) > 18.001f)
        {
            status =
                target.DisplayName +
                " cannot be selected as a ranged target from more than 18 inches away.";
            return;
        }

        if (TargetHasLoneOperativeProtection(
                target) &&
            JoinedDistance(
                attacker,
                target
            ) > 12.001f)
        {
            status =
                target.DisplayName +
                " has Lone Operative protection and cannot be targeted from more than 12 inches away.";
            return;
        }

        bool engaged =
            IsEngaged(attacker);

        bool attackerBig =
            attacker.HasKeyword("monster") ||
            attacker.HasKeyword("vehicle");

        bool targetEngaged =
            IsEngaged(target);

        bool targetBig =
            target.HasKeyword("monster") ||
            target.HasKeyword("vehicle");

        bool engagedTogether =
            UnitsAreEngaged(
                attacker,
                target
            );

        if (engaged &&
            !attackerBig &&
            !engagedTogether)
        {
            status =
                "An engaged non-Monster/Vehicle unit can only use Close-Quarters/Pistol weapons against an enemy it is engaged with.";
            return;
        }

        if (targetEngaged &&
            !targetBig &&
            !engagedTogether)
        {
            status =
                "That target is engaged and is not a Monster/Vehicle, so it cannot be selected for this ranged attack.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedModel == null ||
            selectedModel.Squad == null ||
            selectedModel.Squad
                .JoinedActionController() !=
                attacker.JoinedActionController())
        {
            status =
                "SHOOTING: click the specific model that will fire, then click its target.";
            return;
        }

        List<WeaponAttackSelection> eligibleWeapons =
            GetEligibleRangedWeapons(
                attacker,
                target,
                engaged
            )
            .Where(
                selection =>
                    selection != null &&
                    selection.model ==
                        selectedModel
            )
            .ToList();

        if (eligibleWeapons.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                selectedModel.RoleName +
                " has no unused ranged weapon with legal range/line of sight to " +
                target.DisplayName +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        OpenModelWeaponChoice(
            attacker,
            selectedModel,
            target,
            eligibleWeapons,
            engaged
        );
    }

    private void OpenModelWeaponChoice(
        SquadController attacker,
        ModelToken firingModel,
        SquadController target,
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> choices,
        bool engaged)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            firingModel == null ||
            target == null ||
            choices == null ||
            choices.Count == 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        List<WeaponAttackSelection> distinct =
            choices
                .GroupBy(
                    selection =>
                        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            selection.weapon.id)
                        ? selection.weapon.id
                        : selection.weapon.displayName
                )
                .Select(
                    group =>
                        group.First()
                )
                .ToList();

        if (distinct.Count == 1)
        {
            StartModelWeaponAttack(
                attacker,
                firingModel,
                target,
                distinct[0],
                engaged
            );

            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (WeaponAttackSelection choice
            in distinct)
        {
            WeaponAttackSelection captured =
                choice;

            WeaponData weapon =
                captured.weapon;

            string profile =
                weapon.displayName +
                "  •  " +
                weapon.range.ToString("0.#") +
                "\"  A " +
                (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    weapon.attacksExpression)
                    ? weapon.attacksPerModel
                        .ToString()
                    : weapon.attacksExpression) +
                "  S " +
                weapon.strength +
                "  AP " +
                weapon.ap;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    profile,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        StartModelWeaponAttack(
                            attacker,
                            firingModel,
                            target,
                            captured,
                            engaged
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "SELECT WEAPON — " +
            firingModel.RoleName,
            "Choose which weapon this model will fire at " +
            target.DisplayName +
            ". After it resolves, select the model again to fire another unused compatible weapon, or mark the model/unit done shooting.",
            options.ToArray()
        );
    }

    private bool OfferAeldariPathOfWarrior(
        SquadController unit,
        System.Action after)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            aeldariRules == null ||
            !aeldariRules.IsPathOfWarriorUnit(
                unit))
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController actionUnit =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        if (actionUnit
            .AeldariPathChoiceMadeThisPhase)
        {
            return false;
        }

        actionUnit
            .AeldariPathChoiceMadeThisPhase =
            true;

        if (FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                actionUnit,
                "Mantle of Wisdom"))
        {
            actionUnit.AeldariRerollHitOnes =
                true;

            actionUnit.AeldariRerollWoundOnes =
                true;

            AppendBattleLog(
                "AELDARI",
                "Path of the Warrior",
                actionUnit.DisplayName +
                " gains both re-roll Hit rolls of 1 and re-roll Wound rolls of 1 from Mantle of Wisdom."
            );

            if (after != null)
                after();

            return true;
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "PATH OF THE WARRIOR — " +
            actionUnit.DisplayName,
            IsXcomMode
            ? "Choose the Aspect Host attack discipline Warboard should apply until end of phase."
            : "Choose the attack discipline being used on the tabletop. Warboard records the state but does not interpret your Traditional dice.",
            new[]
            {
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Re-roll Hit rolls of 1",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        actionUnit
                            .AeldariRerollHitOnes =
                            true;

                        AppendBattleLog(
                            "AELDARI",
                            "Path of the Warrior",
                            actionUnit.DisplayName +
                            " selected re-roll Hit rolls of 1."
                        );

                        if (after != null)
                            after();
                    }
                ),
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Re-roll Wound rolls of 1",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        actionUnit
                            .AeldariRerollWoundOnes =
                            true;

                        AppendBattleLog(
                            "AELDARI",
                            "Path of the Warrior",
                            actionUnit.DisplayName +
                            " selected re-roll Wound rolls of 1."
                        );

                        if (after != null)
                            after();
                    }
                )
            }
        );

        return true;
    }

    private void StartModelWeaponAttack(
        SquadController attacker,
        ModelToken firingModel,
        SquadController target,
        WeaponAttackSelection choice,
        bool engaged)
    {
        if (choice == null ||
            choice.weapon == null ||
            choice.model !=
                firingModel)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (OfferAeldariPathOfWarrior(
                attacker,
                () =>
                    StartModelWeaponAttack(
                        attacker,
                        firingModel,
                        target,
                        choice,
                        engaged
                    )))
        {
            return;
        }

        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction = false;
        interactiveAttackModelLevelShooting = true;

        BeginInteractiveAttack(
            attacker,
            target,
            new List<WeaponAttackSelection>
            {
                choice
            },
            AttackMode.Ranged,
            engaged
        );
    }

    private void RefreshModelLevelShootingState(
        SquadController attacker)
    {
        if (attacker == null)
            return;

        SquadController actionUnit =
            attacker.JoinedActionController();

        List<ModelToken> firingModels =
            JoinedModels(
                actionUnit
            )
            .Where(
                model =>
                    model != null &&
                    model.IsAlive &&
                    model.RangedWeapons.Count > 0
            )
            .ToList();

        bool finished =
            firingModels.Count == 0 ||
            firingModels.All(
                model =>
                    model
                        .HasCompletedShootingThisTurn
            );

        actionUnit.HasShot =
            finished;

        if (actionUnit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            actionUnit.AttachedLeader.HasShot =
                finished;
        }

        if (finished)
        {
            status =
                actionUnit.DisplayName +
                " has finished shooting.";
        }
    }

    private void CompleteSelectedModelShooting()
    {
        if (selectedModel == null)
            return;

        selectedModel
            .CompleteShootingThisTurn();

        RefreshModelLevelShootingState(
            selectedSquad
        );

        status =
            selectedModel.RoleName +
            " marked DONE shooting.";
    }

    private void CompleteSelectedUnitShooting()
    {
        if (selectedSquad == null)
            return;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(
                selectedSquad))
        {
            if (model != null &&
                model.IsAlive)
            {
                model
                    .CompleteShootingThisTurn();
            }
        }

        selectedSquad.HasShot = true;

        if (selectedSquad.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            selectedSquad.AttachedLeader.HasShot = true;
        }

        status =
            selectedSquad.DisplayName +
            " marked DONE shooting.";
    }


    private List<WeaponAttackSelection>
        GetEligibleRangedWeapons(
            SquadController attacker,
            SquadController target,
            bool attackerEngaged)
    {
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> result =
            new List<WeaponAttackSelection>();

        if (missionSystem != null &&
            missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                attacker) &&
            !attacker.HasKeyword(
                "titanic"))
        {
            return result;
        }

        bool attackerBig =
            attacker.HasKeyword("monster") ||
            attacker.HasKeyword("vehicle");

        bool targetEngaged =
            IsEngaged(target);

        bool targetBig =
            target.HasKeyword("monster") ||
            target.HasKeyword("vehicle");

        bool engagedTogether =
            UnitsAreEngaged(
                attacker,
                target
            );

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(attacker))
        {
            List<WeaponData> eligible =
                new List<WeaponData>();

            foreach (WeaponData weapon
                in model.RangedWeapons)
            {
                if (weapon == null ||
                    !model.CanUseWeapon(
                        weapon) ||
                    !model.CanFireRangedWeaponThisTurn(
                        weapon))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                bool closeQuarters =
                    RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
                        weapon,
                        "pistol"
                    ) ||
                    WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                        weapon,
                        "close_quarters"
                    );

                bool indirect =
                    WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                        weapon,
                        "indirect_fire"
                    );

                bool blast =
                    WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                        weapon,
                        "blast"
                    );

                if (attacker.HasAdvanced &&
                    !attacker
                        .JoinedActionController()
                        .SnapShootingActive &&
                    !WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                        weapon,
                        "assault") &&
                    !attacker
                        .JoinedActionController()
                        .FactionSuddenStormActive &&
                    !attacker
                        .JoinedActionController()
                        .StarEnginesActive &&
                    !(aeldariRules != null &&
                      aeldariRules
                        .VehicleRangedHasAssault(
                            attacker
                        )))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (attackerEngaged &&
                    !attackerBig)
                {
                    if (!closeQuarters ||
                        !engagedTogether)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                if (attackerBig &&
                    attackerEngaged &&
                    blast &&
                    targetEngaged)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (targetEngaged &&
                    !targetBig &&
                    !engagedTogether)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                float distance =
                    ModelDistanceToSquad(
                        model,
                        target
                    );

                float effectiveRange =
                    weapon.range +
                    AeldariRangedRangeModifier(
                        attacker,
                        weapon
                    );

                if (distance >
                    effectiveRange +
                    0.001f)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                bool visible =
                    ModelHasLineOfSight(
                        model,
                        target
                    );

                if (!visible)
                {
                    bool legalIndirect =
                        indirect &&
                        !attackerEngaged &&
                        !attacker.HasAdvanced &&
                        !targetEngaged;

                    if (!legalIndirect)
                        continue;
                }

                eligible.Add(weapon);
            }

            if (eligible.Count == 0)
                continue;

            if (attackerEngaged &&
                !attackerBig)
            {
                foreach (WeaponData weapon
                    in eligible)
                {
                    result.Add(
                        new WeaponAttackSelection(
                            model,
                            weapon
                        )
                    );
                }

                continue;
            }

            bool hasNonCloseQuarters =
                eligible.Any(
                    weapon =>
                        !RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
                            weapon,
                            "pistol"
                        ) &&
                        !WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                            weapon,
                            "close_quarters"
                        )
                );

            foreach (WeaponData weapon
                in eligible)
            {
                bool closeQuarters =
                    RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
                        weapon,
                        "pistol"
                    ) ||
                    WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                        weapon,
                        "close_quarters"
                    );

                // Outside engagement, do not mix Close-Quarters/Pistol
                // weapons with other ranged weapons.
                if (!attackerEngaged &&
                    hasNonCloseQuarters &&
                    closeQuarters)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                result.Add(
                    new WeaponAttackSelection(
                        model,
                        weapon
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        return result;
    }


    private void TryCharge(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (missionSystem != null &&
            missionSystem.UnitHasStartedAction(
                attacker))
        {
            status =
                "That unit started a mission action this turn and cannot declare a charge.";
            return;
        }

        if (showStratagemReaction)
        {
            status =
                "Resolve the current reaction window first.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.HasCharged)
        {
            status =
                "That squad already attempted a charge.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.HasFallenBack &&
            !(aeldariRules != null &&
              aeldariRules.CanChargeAfterFallBack(
                  attacker
              )))
        {
            status =
                "That squad Fell Back this turn and cannot charge.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.HasAdvanced &&
            !(aeldariRules != null &&
              aeldariRules.CanChargeAfterAdvance(
                  attacker
              )))
        {
            status =
                "That squad Advanced this turn and cannot declare a charge.";
            return;
        }

        if (IsEngaged(attacker))
        {
            status =
                "That squad is already engaged.";
            return;
        }

        attacker.HasCharged = true;

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            traditionalChargePending = true;
            traditionalChargeAttacker =
                attacker;
            traditionalChargeTarget =
                target;
            traditionalChargeResult = 2;

            OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                2
            );

            AppendBattleLog(
                "CHARGE",
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " → " +
                target.DisplayName,
                "Charge declared. Player resolves 2D6 and any tabletop rerolls manually, then enters the final total."
            );

            status =
                "TRADITIONAL CHARGE: roll 2D6 manually, handle any rerolls yourself, then enter the final charge total.";

            return;
        }

        int roll =
            RollChargeDice();

        BeginChargeRollDecision(
            attacker,
            target,
            roll
        );
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalCharge()
    {
        if (!traditionalChargePending ||
            traditionalChargeAttacker == null ||
            traditionalChargeTarget == null)
        {
            traditionalChargePending = false;
            traditionalChargeAttacker = null;
            traditionalChargeTarget = null;
            return;
        }

        SquadController attacker =
            traditionalChargeAttacker;

        SquadController target =
            traditionalChargeTarget;

        int result =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                traditionalChargeResult,
                2,
                12
            );

        traditionalChargePending = false;
        traditionalChargeAttacker = null;
        traditionalChargeTarget = null;

        AppendBattleLog(
            "CHARGE",
            attacker.DisplayName +
            " → " +
            target.DisplayName,
            "Player-entered final Charge total: " +
            result +
            ". Warboard did not interpret the physical dice or offer rerolls."
        );

        ResolveChargeRoll(
            attacker,
            target,
            result,
            false,
            0
        );
    }

    private void CancelTraditionalCharge()
    {
        if (traditionalChargeAttacker != null)
        {
            // The declaration itself counts as the attempted charge in the
            // normal phase flow. Cancel is therefore a UI escape before the
            // final result is submitted, not a second chance to charge.
            status =
                traditionalChargeAttacker.DisplayName +
                " charge declaration remains attempted; no result was entered.";
        }

        traditionalChargePending = false;
        traditionalChargeAttacker = null;
        traditionalChargeTarget = null;
    }

    private int RollChargeDice()
    {
        return
            DiceRoller.Roll2D6(
                "Charge"
            );
    }

    private void BeginChargeRollDecision(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        int roll)
    {
        GameEventContext context =
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.ChargeRolled,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    activeFaction,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = attacker,
                Target = target,
                RollTotal = roll,
                PreviousRollTotal = 0,
                IsReroll = false
            };

        GameEventBus.Raise(context);

        IStratagem commandReroll =
            StratagemRegistry.Get(
                "command_reroll"
            );

        if (attacker
            .JoinedActionController()
            .IsBattleShocked)
        {
            ResolveChargeRoll(
                attacker,
                target,
                roll,
                false,
                0
            );

            return;
        }

        if (commandReroll != null &&
            commandReroll.CanUse(
                this,
                activeFaction,
                context))
        {
            pendingChargeAttacker =
                attacker;

            pendingChargeTarget =
                target;

            pendingChargeRoll =
                roll;

            pendingOriginalChargeRoll =
                roll;

            pendingReactionContext =
                context;

            showStratagemReaction = true;

            status =
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " rolled " +
                roll +
                " to charge " +
                target.DisplayName +
                ". Keep the roll or spend 1 CP on Command Re-roll.";

            return;
        }

        ResolveChargeRoll(
            attacker,
            target,
            roll,
            false,
            0
        );
    }

    private void KeepPendingChargeRoll()
    {
        if (!showStratagemReaction ||
            pendingChargeAttacker == null ||
            pendingChargeTarget == null)
        {
            CloseStratagemReaction();
            return;
        }

        SquadController attacker =
            pendingChargeAttacker;

        SquadController target =
            pendingChargeTarget;

        int roll =
            pendingChargeRoll;

        CloseStratagemReaction();

        ResolveChargeRoll(
            attacker,
            target,
            roll,
            false,
            0
        );
    }

    private void UseCommandRerollOnPendingCharge()
    {
        if (!showStratagemReaction ||
            pendingReactionContext == null ||
            pendingChargeAttacker == null ||
            pendingChargeTarget == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        IStratagem commandReroll =
            StratagemRegistry.Get(
                "command_reroll"
            );

        if (commandReroll == null ||
            !commandReroll.Use(
                this,
                activeFaction,
                pendingReactionContext))
        {
            status =
                "Command Re-roll is not available.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController attacker =
            pendingChargeAttacker;

        SquadController target =
            pendingChargeTarget;

        int previous =
            pendingOriginalChargeRoll;

        int reroll =
            RollChargeDice();

        GameEventBus.Raise(
            new GameEventContext
            {
                Type =
                    GameEventType.ChargeRolled,
                Game = this,
                ActingFaction =
                    activeFaction,
                Phase = phase,
                Source = attacker,
                Target = target,
                RollTotal = reroll,
                PreviousRollTotal =
                    previous,
                IsReroll = true,
                Note =
                    "Command Re-roll"
            }
        );

        CloseStratagemReaction();

        ResolveChargeRoll(
            attacker,
            target,
            reroll,
            true,
            previous
        );
    }

    private void CloseStratagemReaction()
    {
        showStratagemReaction = false;
        pendingReactionContext = null;

        pendingChargeAttacker = null;
        pendingChargeTarget = null;

        pendingChargeRoll = 0;
        pendingOriginalChargeRoll = 0;
    }

    private void ResolveChargeRoll(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        int roll,
        bool wasRerolled,
        int previousRoll)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            target == null ||
            !attacker.IsAlive ||
            !target.IsAlive)
        {
            status =
                "Charge could not be resolved because a unit is no longer available.";
            return;
        }

        float targetDistance =
            JoinedDistance(
                attacker,
                target
            );

        string rollText =
            wasRerolled
            ? "Command Re-roll " +
              previousRoll +
              " → " +
              roll
            : "rolled " +
              roll;

        if (targetDistance >
            roll + 0.001f)
        {
            status =
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " " +
                rollText +
                ". " +
                target.DisplayName +
                " is " +
                targetDistance.ToString("0.0") +
                " away, so the charge fails.";
            return;
        }

        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original =
            CaptureJoinedPositions(
                attacker
            );

        AutoMoveSquadToward(
            attacker,
            target,
            roll,
            0.90f
        );

        RefineChargePlacementToCurrentRules(
            attacker,
            target,
            roll,
            original
        );

        bool successfulEnd =
            ChargeEndStateIsLegal(
                attacker,
                target,
                roll,
                original
            );

        if (!successfulEnd)
        {
            RestoreJoinedPositions(
                original
            );

            Vector3 legalDelta;

            if (TryFindFormationChargeTranslation(
                attacker,
                target,
                roll,
                out legalDelta))
            {
                TranslateJoinedModels(
                    attacker,
                    legalDelta
                );

                RefineChargePlacementToCurrentRules(
                    attacker,
                    target,
                    roll,
                    original
                );

                successfulEnd =
                    ChargeEndStateIsLegal(
                        attacker,
                        target,
                        roll,
                        original
                    );
            }
        }

        if (!successfulEnd)
        {
            RestoreJoinedPositions(
                original
            );

            status =
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " " +
                rollText +
                ", but the charge solver could not find a legal route/formation into " +
                target.DisplayName +
                ". No models move.";

            return;
        }

        attacker.HasMoved = true;
        attacker.HasCharged = true;
        attacker.MarkMadeChargeMove();

        if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            attacker.AttachedLeader.HasMoved = true;
            attacker.AttachedLeader.HasCharged = true;
        }

        status =
            attacker.DisplayName +
            " " +
            rollText +
            " and charged " +
            target.DisplayName +
            ". Each charger was placed using the current Charge Move priorities: closer; within 1 if possible; engaged if possible.";
    }

    private bool ChargeEndStateIsLegal(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        float chargeDistance,
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            target == null ||
            original == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (JoinedDistance(
                attacker,
                target
            ) >
            EngagementRange + 0.05f)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!attacker.IsCoherent() ||
            !AllModelsInsideBoard(attacker) ||
            !AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(attacker) ||
            IsEngagedWithNonTargetEnemy(
                attacker,
                target
            ))
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(attacker))
        {
            Vector3 start;

            if (!original.TryGetValue(
                    model,
                    out start))
            {
                return false;
            }

            Vector3 end =
                model.transform.position;

            if (HorizontalDistance(
                    start,
                    end
                ) >
                chargeDistance + 0.01f)
            {
                return false;
            }

            float before =
                DistancePointToSquad(
                    start,
                    target
                );

            float after =
                DistancePointToSquad(
                    end,
                    target
                );

            // 11e Charge Move: every model must finish closer to one or more
            // selected charge targets.
            if (after >=
                before - 0.001f)
            {
                return false;
            }

            // If this model can legally finish within 1" of a charge target,
            // it must do so.
            if (after >
                    1.0f + 0.05f &&
                ChargingModelCanReachDistance(
                    attacker,
                    model,
                    target,
                    start,
                    chargeDistance,
                    1.0f))
            {
                return false;
            }

            // Otherwise, if it can legally finish engaged with a charge
            // target, it must do so.
            if (after >
                    EngagementRange + 0.05f &&
                ChargingModelCanReachDistance(
                    attacker,
                    model,
                    target,
                    start,
                    chargeDistance,
                    EngagementRange))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void RefineChargePlacementToCurrentRules(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        float chargeDistance,
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            target == null ||
            original == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        // Several passes are useful because moving one charger into an open
        // slot can expose a different legal slot to another model.
        for (int pass = 0;
             pass < 4;
             pass++)
        {
            bool changed = false;

            List<ModelToken> chargers =
                JoinedModels(attacker)
                    .OrderBy(
                        model =>
                            DistancePointToSquad(
                                model.transform.position,
                                target
                            )
                    )
                    .ToList();

            foreach (ModelToken model
                in chargers)
            {
                Vector3 start;

                if (!original.TryGetValue(
                        model,
                        out start))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                Vector3 candidate;

                if (!TryFindBestChargeDestination(
                        attacker,
                        model,
                        target,
                        start,
                        chargeDistance,
                        out candidate))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (HorizontalDistance(
                        candidate,
                        model.transform.position
                    ) <= 0.01f)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                Vector3 current =
                    model.transform.position;

                model.transform.position =
                    candidate;

                Physics.SyncTransforms();

                bool wholeUnitLegal =
                    attacker.IsCoherent() &&
                    AllModelsInsideBoard(
                        attacker
                    ) &&
                    AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                        attacker
                    ) &&
                    !IsEngagedWithNonTargetEnemy(
                        attacker,
                        target
                    );

                if (!wholeUnitLegal)
                {
                    model.transform.position =
                        current;

                    Physics.SyncTransforms();
                    continue;
                }

                changed = true;
            }

            if (!changed)
                break;
        }

        attacker.RefreshVisuals();

        if (attacker.AttachedLeader != null)
            attacker.AttachedLeader.RefreshVisuals();
    }

    private bool TryFindBestChargeDestination(
        SquadController attacker,
        ModelToken movingModel,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 originalStart,
        float chargeDistance,
        out Vector3 result)
    {
        result =
            movingModel.transform.position;

        float startingDistance =
            DistancePointToSquad(
                originalStart,
                target
            );

        Vector3 current =
            movingModel.transform.position;

        float currentDistance =
            DistancePointToSquad(
                current,
                target
            );

        List<Vector3> candidates =
            BuildChargeDestinationCandidates(
                movingModel,
                target,
                originalStart,
                chargeDistance
            );

        bool found = false;

        int bestTier =
            ChargeDistanceTier(
                currentDistance
            );

        float bestTargetDistance =
            currentDistance;

        float bestTravel =
            HorizontalDistance(
                originalStart,
                current
            );

        foreach (Vector3 candidate
            in candidates)
        {
            if (!ChargeDestinationIsLocallyLegal(
                    attacker,
                    movingModel,
                    target,
                    originalStart,
                    candidate,
                    chargeDistance,
                    startingDistance))
            {
                continue;
            }

            Vector3 previous =
                movingModel.transform.position;

            movingModel.transform.position =
                candidate;

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            bool coherent =
                attacker.IsCoherent();

            movingModel.transform.position =
                previous;

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            if (!coherent)
                continue;

            float targetDistance =
                DistancePointToSquad(
                    candidate,
                    target
                );

            int tier =
                ChargeDistanceTier(
                    targetDistance
                );

            float travel =
                HorizontalDistance(
                    originalStart,
                    candidate
                );

            bool better =
                tier < bestTier ||
                (tier == bestTier &&
                 targetDistance <
                    bestTargetDistance - 0.01f) ||
                (tier == bestTier &&
                 Mathf.Abs(
                    targetDistance -
                    bestTargetDistance
                 ) <= 0.01f &&
                 travel < bestTravel);

            if (!better)
                continue;

            found = true;
            bestTier = tier;
            bestTargetDistance =
                targetDistance;
            bestTravel = travel;
            result = candidate;
        }

        return found;
    }

    private List<Vector3> BuildChargeDestinationCandidates(
        ModelToken movingModel,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 originalStart,
        float chargeDistance)
    {
        List<Vector3> candidates =
            new List<Vector3>();

        // Current position is included so the refinement pass only moves a
        // model when it can genuinely improve its rule-priority tier.
        candidates.Add(
            movingModel.transform.position
        );

        foreach (ModelToken enemy
            in JoinedModels(target)
                .OrderBy(
                    model =>
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            originalStart,
                            model.transform.position
                        )
                ))
        {
            float contactDistance =
                MinimumModelSeparation(
                    movingModel,
                    enemy
                );

            float[] desiredRadii =
            {
                contactDistance +
                    0.03f,
                Mathf.Max(
                    contactDistance +
                        0.05f,
                    0.90f
                ),
                Mathf.Max(
                    contactDistance +
                        0.05f,
                    1.00f
                ),
                Mathf.Max(
                    contactDistance +
                        0.05f,
                    1.35f
                ),
                Mathf.Max(
                    contactDistance +
                        0.05f,
                    1.70f
                ),
                Mathf.Max(
                    contactDistance +
                        0.05f,
                    EngagementRange -
                        0.03f
                )
            };

            Vector3 fromEnemy =
                originalStart -
                enemy.transform.position;

            fromEnemy.y = 0f;

            if (fromEnemy.sqrMagnitude <=
                0.0001f)
            {
                fromEnemy =
                    Vector3.back;
            }

            fromEnemy.Normalize();

            for (int angle = -165;
                 angle <= 180;
                 angle += 15)
            {
                Vector3 direction =
                    Quaternion.Euler(
                        0f,
                        angle,
                        0f
                    ) *
                    fromEnemy;

                foreach (float radius
                    in desiredRadii)
                {
                    Vector3 candidate =
                        enemy.transform.position +
                        direction *
                        Mathf.Max(
                            radius,
                            contactDistance +
                                0.02f
                        );

                    candidate.y =
                        originalStart.y;

                    if (HorizontalDistance(
                            originalStart,
                            candidate
                        ) <=
                        chargeDistance + 0.01f)
                    {
                        candidates.Add(
                            candidate
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Also sample the actual route toward the target. This allows models
        // that cannot engage to still use their legal charge distance and end
        // closer while maintaining coherency.
        ModelToken nearest =
            ClosestJoinedModelTo(
                target,
                originalStart
            );

        if (nearest != null)
        {
            Vector3 delta =
                nearest.transform.position -
                originalStart;

            delta.y = 0f;

            if (delta.sqrMagnitude >
                0.0001f)
            {
                Vector3 direction =
                    delta.normalized;

                for (float travel = 0.20f;
                     travel <=
                        chargeDistance + 0.001f;
                     travel += 0.20f)
                {
                    Vector3 candidate =
                        originalStart +
                        direction *
                        Mathf.Min(
                            travel,
                            chargeDistance
                        );

                    candidate.y =
                        originalStart.y;

                    candidates.Add(
                        candidate
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        return candidates;
    }

    private bool ChargeDestinationIsLocallyLegal(
        SquadController attacker,
        ModelToken movingModel,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 originalStart,
        Vector3 destination,
        float chargeDistance,
        float startingDistance)
    {
        if (!InsideBoard(
                destination))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (HorizontalDistance(
                originalStart,
                destination
            ) >
            chargeDistance + 0.01f)
        {
            return false;
        }

        float endingDistance =
            DistancePointToSquad(
                destination,
                target
            );

        if (endingDistance >=
            startingDistance - 0.001f)
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!CanPlaceModel(
                movingModel,
                destination))
        {
            return false;
        }

        bool canPassEnemies =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules
                .CanMoveThroughEnemyModelsWhenCharging(
                    attacker
                );

        if (!CombatMovePathIsClear(
                movingModel,
                originalStart,
                destination,
                canPassEnemies))
        {
            return false;
        }

        // A Charge Move cannot finish engaged with an enemy unit that was not
        // selected as a charge target.
        foreach (SquadController other
            in squads)
        {
            if (other == null ||
                other == target ||
                other.IsAttachedLeader ||
                other.FactionId ==
                    attacker.FactionId ||
                !other.IsAlive ||
                !other.IsOnBattlefield)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (DistancePointToSquad(
                    destination,
                    other
                ) <=
                EngagementRange + 0.05f)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool ChargingModelCanReachDistance(
        SquadController attacker,
        ModelToken movingModel,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 originalStart,
        float chargeDistance,
        float requiredDistance)
    {
        Vector3 current =
            movingModel.transform.position;

        float startingDistance =
            DistancePointToSquad(
                originalStart,
                target
            );

        List<Vector3> candidates =
            BuildChargeDestinationCandidates(
                movingModel,
                target,
                originalStart,
                chargeDistance
            );

        foreach (Vector3 candidate
            in candidates)
        {
            if (DistancePointToSquad(
                    candidate,
                    target
                ) >
                requiredDistance + 0.05f)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!ChargeDestinationIsLocallyLegal(
                    attacker,
                    movingModel,
                    target,
                    originalStart,
                    candidate,
                    chargeDistance,
                    startingDistance))
            {
                continue;
            }

            movingModel.transform.position =
                candidate;

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            bool valid =
                attacker.IsCoherent() &&
                AllModelsInsideBoard(
                    attacker
                ) &&
                AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                    attacker
                ) &&
                !IsEngagedWithNonTargetEnemy(
                    attacker,
                    target
                );

            movingModel.transform.position =
                current;

            Physics.SyncTransforms();

            if (valid)
                return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private int ChargeDistanceTier(
        float targetDistance)
    {
        if (targetDistance <=
            1.0f + 0.05f)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        if (targetDistance <=
            EngagementRange + 0.05f)
        {
            return 1;
        }

        return 2;
    }

    private bool TryFindFormationChargeTranslation(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        float roll,
        out Vector3 result)
    {
        result = Vector3.zero;

        Vector3 attackerCentre =
            attacker.CurrentCentre();

        Vector3 targetCentre =
            target.CurrentCentre();

        Vector3 baseDirection =
            targetCentre -
            attackerCentre;

        baseDirection.y = 0f;

        if (baseDirection.sqrMagnitude <=
            0.0001f)
        {
            baseDirection =
                attacker.DirectionToward(
                    target
                );
        }

        baseDirection.Normalize();

        // Search straight ahead first, then progressively wider arcs.
        float[] angleOffsets =
        {
            0f,
            5f, -5f,
            10f, -10f,
            15f, -15f,
            20f, -20f,
            25f, -25f,
            30f, -30f,
            40f, -40f,
            50f, -50f,
            60f, -60f,
            75f, -75f,
            90f, -90f
        };

        // Small increments are intentional. A rigid formation can have a very
        // narrow legal "just engaged but not overlapping" position.
        const float distanceStep = 0.20f;

        Vector3 bestDelta =
            Vector3.zero;

        float bestTravel =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (float angle
            in angleOffsets)
        {
            Vector3 direction =
                Quaternion.Euler(
                    0f,
                    angle,
                    0f
                ) *
                baseDirection;

            for (float travel =
                    distanceStep;
                 travel <=
                    roll + 0.001f;
                 travel +=
                    distanceStep)
            {
                Vector3 delta =
                    direction *
                    Mathf.Min(
                        travel,
                        roll
                    );

                if (!FormationTranslationCandidateIsLegal(
                    attacker,
                    target,
                    delta))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                float finalDistance =
                    DistanceSquadToTargetAfterTranslation(
                        attacker,
                        target,
                        delta
                    );

                if (finalDistance >
                    EngagementRange + 0.05f)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (travel <
                    bestTravel)
                {
                    bestTravel =
                        travel;

                    bestDelta =
                        delta;
                }

                // For this angle, later samples only move farther than needed.
                break;
            }
        }

        if (bestTravel ==
            float.MaxValue)
        {
            return false;
        }

        result =
            bestDelta;

        return true;
    }

    private bool FormationTranslationCandidateIsLegal(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 delta)
    {
        if (HorizontalMagnitude(delta) >
            0.001f)
        {
            foreach (ModelToken model
                in JoinedModels(attacker))
            {
                Vector3 start =
                    model.transform.position;

                Vector3 destination =
                    start +
                    delta;

                if (!InsideBoard(destination))
                    return false;

                // Every charging model must end closer to a charge target.
                float before =
                    DistancePointToSquad(
                        start,
                        target
                    );

                float after =
                    DistancePointToSquad(
                        destination,
                        target
                    );

                if (after >=
                    before - 0.001f)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                // Do not cross terrain/enemy model footprints.
                bool canPassEnemies =
                    aeldariRules != null &&
                    aeldariRules
                        .CanMoveThroughEnemyModelsWhenCharging(
                            attacker
                        );

                if (!CombatMovePathIsClear(
                    model,
                    start,
                    destination,
                    canPassEnemies
                ))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                // Final position may not overlap an enemy model.
                foreach (ModelToken enemy
                    in JoinedModels(target))
                {
                    if (HorizontalDistance(
                            destination,
                            enemy.transform.position
                        ) <
                        MinimumModelSeparation(
                            model,
                            enemy
                        ))
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                }

                // Nor may it overlap models in any other enemy unit.
                foreach (SquadController other
                    in squads)
                {
                    if (other == target ||
                        other == null ||
                        !other.IsOnBattlefield ||
                        other.FactionId ==
                            attacker.FactionId ||
                        !other.IsAlive)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    foreach (ModelToken enemy
                        in other.LivingModelTokens())
                    {
                        if (HorizontalDistance(
                                destination,
                                enemy.transform.position
                            ) <
                            MinimumModelSeparation(
                                model,
                                enemy
                            ))
                        {
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // A rigid translation preserves the attacker's internal distances, so
        // coherency remains exactly what it was before the charge. The current
        // unit should already be coherent when selected to charge.
        if (!attacker.IsCoherent())
            return false;

        float translatedDistance =
            DistanceSquadToTargetAfterTranslation(
                attacker,
                target,
                delta
            );

        if (translatedDistance >
            EngagementRange + 0.05f)
        {
            return false;
        }

        // Check that this translation does not bring the charging unit into
        // Engagement Range of an enemy that was not selected as a target.
        foreach (SquadController other
            in squads)
        {
            if (other == target ||
                other.IsAttachedLeader ||
                other.FactionId ==
                    attacker.FactionId ||
                !other.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (DistanceSquadToTargetAfterTranslation(
                    attacker,
                    other,
                    delta
                ) <=
                EngagementRange + 0.05f)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private float DistanceSquadToTargetAfterTranslation(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target,
        Vector3 delta)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ModelToken attackerModel
            in JoinedModels(attacker))
        {
            Vector3 translated =
                attackerModel.transform.position +
                delta;

            foreach (ModelToken targetModel
                in JoinedModels(target))
            {
                best =
                    Mathf.Min(
                        best,
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            translated,
                            targetModel.transform.position
                        )
                    );
            }
        }

        return best ==
            float.MaxValue
            ? 999f
            : best;
    }

    private bool IsEngagedWithNonTargetEnemy(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        foreach (SquadController other
            in squads)
        {
            if (other == target ||
                other.IsAttachedLeader ||
                other.FactionId ==
                    attacker.FactionId ||
                !other.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (JoinedDistance(
                    attacker,
                    other
                ) <=
                EngagementRange + 0.05f)
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void BeginFightPhaseFlow()
    {
        fightEligibleAtStart.Clear();
        heraldOfYnneadTarget = null;
        heraldOfYnneadFaction = "";

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in squads)
        {
            if (unit == null ||
                unit.IsAttachedLeader ||
                !unit.IsAlive ||
                !unit.IsOnBattlefield)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (IsEngaged(unit))
            {
                fightEligibleAtStart.Add(
                    unit.JoinedActionController()
                );
            }
        }

        if (OfferHeraldOfYnnead())
            return;

        OfferLethalReprisal();
    }

    private bool OfferHeraldOfYnnead()
    {
        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController source
            in squads)
        {
            if (source == null ||
                source.IsAttachedLeader ||
                !source.IsAlive ||
                !source.IsOnBattlefield ||
                !FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                    source,
                    "Herald of Ynnead"))
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (SquadController enemy
                in EngagedEnemies(source))
            {
                SquadController capturedSource =
                    source;

                SquadController capturedEnemy =
                    enemy;

                options.Add(
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        capturedSource.DisplayName +
                        " marks " +
                        capturedEnemy.DisplayName,
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            heraldOfYnneadTarget =
                                capturedEnemy
                                    .JoinedActionController();

                            heraldOfYnneadFaction =
                                capturedSource.FactionId;

                            status =
                                "HERALD OF YNNEAD: friendly Aeldari re-roll Wound rolls of 1 against " +
                                capturedEnemy.DisplayName +
                                " this Fight phase.";

                            OfferLethalReprisal();
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        if (options.Count == 0)
            return false;

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip Herald of Ynnead",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    OfferLethalReprisal();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "HERALD OF YNNEAD",
            "At the start of the Fight phase, select one enemy unit within Engagement Range of the Herald.",
            options
        );

        return true;
    }

    public bool HasHeraldOfYnneadReroll(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            target == null ||
            heraldOfYnneadTarget == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            attacker.FactionId ==
                heraldOfYnneadFaction &&
            attacker.HasOwnKeywordValue(
                "aeldari") &&
            target.JoinedActionController() ==
                heraldOfYnneadTarget;
    }

    private void OfferLethalReprisal()
    {
        List<SquadController> reprisalEligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        factionRules != null &&
                        UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                            unit.FactionId) &&
                        unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
                        !unit.HasKeyword("titanic") &&
                        unit.IsBelowStartingStrength()
                )
                .ToList();

        if (reprisalEligible.Count == 0)
        {
            BeginFightSequence();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in reprisalEligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Lethal Reprisal: " +
                    captured.DisplayName,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();
                        captured.TemporaryFightsFirst = true;

                        status =
                            "LETHAL REPRISAL: " +
                            captured.DisplayName +
                            " has Fights First until end of phase.";

                        BeginFightSequence();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip Lethal Reprisal",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    BeginFightSequence();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "STRENGTH FROM DEATH — LETHAL REPRISAL",
            "At the start of the Fight phase, select one below-Starting-Strength Ynnari unit to gain Fights First.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void BeginFightSequence()
    {
        ClearFightActivationState();

        fightSequenceActive = true;
        fightPriorityStep =
            FightPriorityStep.FightsFirst;
        fightSelectionFaction =
            activeFaction;

        ResolveFightSelector(
            activeFaction
        );

        status +=
            " | " +
            FightPriorityText();
    }

    private bool UnitHasFightsFirst(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null)
            return false;

        SquadController actionUnit =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        return
            actionUnit.MadeChargeMove ||
            actionUnit.TemporaryFightsFirst ||
            FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                actionUnit,
                "Fights First"
            );
    }

    private bool IsEligibleToFightNow(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (unit == null ||
            unit.IsAttachedLeader ||
            !unit.IsAlive ||
            !unit.IsOnBattlefield ||
            unit.HasFought)
        {
            return false;
        }

        SquadController actionUnit =
            unit.JoinedActionController();

        bool rulesEligible =
            IsEngaged(actionUnit) ||
            actionUnit.MadeChargeMove ||
            fightEligibleAtStart.Contains(
                actionUnit
            );

        if (!rulesEligible)
            return false;

        return squads.Any(
            enemy =>
                enemy != null &&
                enemy.IsAlive &&
                enemy.IsOnBattlefield &&
                !enemy.IsAttachedLeader &&
                enemy.FactionId !=
                    actionUnit.FactionId &&
                JoinedDistance(
                    actionUnit,
                    enemy
                ) <=
                    EngagementRange +
                    6.05f
        );
    }

    private List<SquadController> EligibleFightUnits(
        string faction,
        bool fightsFirstOnly)
    {
        return squads
            .Where(
                unit =>
                    unit != null &&
                    !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                    unit.FactionId == faction &&
                    IsEligibleToFightNow(
                        unit
                    ) &&
                    (!fightsFirstOnly ||
                     UnitHasFightsFirst(
                        unit
                     ))
            )
            .ToList();
    }

    private bool AnyEligibleFightUnits()
    {
        return squads.Any(
            unit =>
                unit != null &&
                !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                IsEligibleToFightNow(
                    unit
                )
        );
    }

    private string OtherFaction(
        string faction)
    {
        return factions.FirstOrDefault(
            value =>
                value != faction
        ) ?? faction;
    }

    private void ResolveFightSelector(
        string preferredFaction)
    {
        if (!fightSequenceActive)
            return;

        if (fightPriorityStep ==
            FightPriorityStep.FightsFirst)
        {
            List<SquadController> allFirst =
                squads
                    .Where(
                        unit =>
                            unit != null &&
                            !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                            IsEligibleToFightNow(
                                unit
                            ) &&
                            UnitHasFightsFirst(
                                unit
                            )
                    )
                    .ToList();

            if (allFirst.Count > 0)
            {
                if (EligibleFightUnits(
                        preferredFaction,
                        true
                    ).Count > 0)
                {
                    fightSelectionFaction =
                        preferredFaction;
                    return;
                }

                string other =
                    OtherFaction(
                        preferredFaction
                    );

                if (EligibleFightUnits(
                        other,
                        true
                    ).Count > 0)
                {
                    fightSelectionFaction = other;
                    return;
                }
            }

            fightPriorityStep =
                FightPriorityStep.Remaining;
        }

        if (!AnyEligibleFightUnits())
        {
            fightSequenceActive = false;
            fightPriorityStep =
                FightPriorityStep.None;
            fightSelectionFaction = "";
            return;
        }

        if (EligibleFightUnits(
                preferredFaction,
                false
            ).Count > 0)
        {
            fightSelectionFaction =
                preferredFaction;
            return;
        }

        string fallback =
            OtherFaction(
                preferredFaction
            );

        if (EligibleFightUnits(
                fallback,
                false
            ).Count > 0)
        {
            fightSelectionFaction =
                fallback;
            return;
        }

        fightSequenceActive = false;
        fightPriorityStep =
            FightPriorityStep.None;
        fightSelectionFaction = "";
    }

    private void AdvanceFightPriority(
        SquadController unitThatFought)
    {
        if (!fightSequenceActive ||
            unitThatFought == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        string next =
            OtherFaction(
                unitThatFought.FactionId
            );

        ResolveFightSelector(
            next
        );

        status +=
            " | " +
            FightPriorityText();
    }

    private string FightPriorityText()
    {
        if (!fightSequenceActive)
            return "Fight sequence complete";

        return
            "Fight priority: " +
            (fightPriorityStep ==
                FightPriorityStep.FightsFirst
                ? "FIGHTS FIRST"
                : "REMAINING") +
            " — " +
            DisplayFactionName(
                fightSelectionFaction
            ) +
            " selects";
    }

    private void ExecuteFight(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        BeginFightActivation(
            attacker,
            target
        );
    }

    private bool ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
        ModelToken model)
    {
        return
            model != null &&
            model.IsAlive &&
            model.Squad != null &&
            fightActivationUnit != null &&
            model.Squad
                .JoinedActionController() ==
                fightActivationUnit;
    }

    private float FightStageMoveLimit()
    {
        if (fightActivationUnit == null)
            return 0f;

        return
            fightActivationUnit
                .SuddenStrikeActive
            ? 6f
            : (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn
                ? PileInDistance
                : ConsolidateDistance);
    }

    private void CaptureFightStageStartPositions()
    {
        fightStageStartPositions.Clear();
        fightStageMovedModels.Clear();

        if (fightActivationUnit == null)
            return;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(
                fightActivationUnit))
        {
            if (model == null ||
                !model.IsAlive)
            {
                continue;
            }

            fightStageStartPositions[
                model] =
                model.transform.position;
        }
    }

    private float DistancePointToClosestEnemyModel(
        Vector3 point,
        string faction)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in squads)
        {
            if (unit == null ||
                !unit.IsAlive ||
                !unit.IsOnBattlefield ||
                unit.IsAttachedLeader ||
                unit.FactionId ==
                    faction)
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (ModelToken model
                in JoinedModels(unit))
            {
                if (model == null ||
                    !model.IsAlive)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                best =
                    Mathf.Min(
                        best,
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            point,
                            model.transform.position
                        )
                    );
            }
        }

        return best;
    }

    private float DistancePointToClosestObjective(
        Vector3 point)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in objectives)
        {
            if (objective == null)
                continue;

            best =
                Mathf.Min(
                    best,
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        point,
                        objective.transform.position
                    )
                );
        }

        return best;
    }

    private bool FightStageDestinationLegal(
        ModelToken model,
        Vector3 destination,
        out string reason)
    {
        reason = "";

        if (!ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                model) ||
            (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.PileIn &&
             fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Consolidate))
        {
            reason =
                "No pile-in/consolidation move is active for that model.";
            return false;
        }

        Vector3 start;

        if (!fightStageStartPositions
            .TryGetValue(
                model,
                out start))
        {
            start =
                model.transform.position;
        }

        destination.y =
            model.transform.position.y;

        float moveDistance =
            HorizontalDistance(
                start,
                destination
            );

        float limit =
            FightStageMoveLimit();

        if (moveDistance >
            limit + 0.001f)
        {
            reason =
                "That move exceeds the " +
                limit.ToString("0.#") +
                "″ " +
                (fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn
                    ? "pile-in"
                    : "consolidation") +
                " allowance.";
            return false;
        }

        if (!InsideBoard(destination))
        {
            reason =
                "That model would leave the battlefield.";
            return false;
        }

        if (!CanPlaceModel(
                model,
                destination))
        {
            reason =
                "That model cannot legally occupy that position.";
            return false;
        }

        if (!CombatMovePathIsClear(
                model,
                model.transform.position,
                destination))
        {
            reason =
                "The combat move path is blocked.";
            return false;
        }

        float startEnemy =
            DistancePointToClosestEnemyModel(
                start,
                fightActivationUnit.FactionId
            );

        float endEnemy =
            DistancePointToClosestEnemyModel(
                destination,
                fightActivationUnit.FactionId
            );

        if (startEnemy <
                float.MaxValue &&
            endEnemy >=
                startEnemy - 0.001f)
        {
            reason =
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn
                ? "A pile-in model must finish closer to the nearest enemy model."
                : "A consolidating model must finish closer to the nearest enemy model.";

            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void TryFightStageMoveSelectedModel(
        Vector3 destination)
    {
        if (selectedModel == null ||
            !ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                selectedModel))
        {
            status =
                "Select a model from the active fighting unit first.";
            return;
        }

        string reason;

        if (!FightStageDestinationLegal(
                selectedModel,
                destination,
                out reason))
        {
            status = reason;
            return;
        }

        selectedModel.transform.position =
            new Vector3(
                destination.x,
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position.y,
                destination.z
            );

        Physics.SyncTransforms();

        fightStageMovedModels.Add(
            selectedModel
        );

        fightActivationUnit.RefreshVisuals();

        if (fightActivationUnit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            fightActivationUnit
                .AttachedLeader
                .RefreshVisuals();
        }

        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();
        RefreshMoveRing();

        Vector3 start =
            fightStageStartPositions
                .ContainsKey(
                    selectedModel)
            ? fightStageStartPositions[
                selectedModel]
            : selectedModel.transform.position;

        float distance =
            HorizontalDistance(
                start,
                selectedModel
                    .transform.position
            );

        status =
            selectedModel.RoleName +
            " " +
            (fightActivationStage ==
                FightActivationStage.PileIn
                ? "piled in "
                : "consolidated ") +
            distance.ToString("0.0") +
            "″.";
    }

    private void BeginFightActivation(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (attacker == null ||
            target == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        fightActivationUnit =
            attacker.JoinedActionController();

        fightActivationInitialTarget =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        fightActivationStage =
            FightActivationStage.PileIn;

        fightModelsResolvedThisActivation.Clear();

        CaptureFightStageStartPositions();

        selectedSquad =
