            fightActivationUnit;

        if (!ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                selectedModel))
        {
            selectedModel =
                JoinedModels(
                    fightActivationUnit)
                    .FirstOrDefault(
                        model =>
                            model != null &&
                            model.IsAlive
                    );
        }

        if (selectedModel != null)
        {
            SelectModelForAction(
                fightActivationUnit,
                selectedModel
            );
        }

        AppendBattleLog(
            "FIGHT",
            fightActivationUnit.DisplayName +
            " activation",
            "PILE-IN started against " +
            fightActivationInitialTarget.DisplayName +
            ". Player moves models individually before resolving model-level melee attacks."
        );

        status =
            "PILE-IN: " +
            fightActivationUnit.DisplayName +
            ". Select each model and click its destination. Hold ALT for the live distance ruler. Click DONE PILE-IN when the unit is coherent.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void ClearFightModelFocus()
    {
        if (fightActivationUnit == null)
            return;

        fightActivationUnit.FocusModel(
            null
        );

        if (fightActivationUnit.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            fightActivationUnit
                .AttachedLeader
                .FocusModel(
                    null
                );
        }
    }

    private void CompleteFightPileIn()
    {
        if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.PileIn ||
            fightActivationUnit == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (!fightActivationUnit.IsCoherent())
        {
            status =
                "Cannot finish pile-in: the unit is out of coherency.";
            return;
        }

        if (!AllModelsInsideBoard(
                fightActivationUnit) ||
            !AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                fightActivationUnit))
        {
            status =
                "Cannot finish pile-in: one or more models are in an illegal position.";
            return;
        }

        fightActivationStage =
            FightActivationStage.Attacks;

        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;

        fightStageStartPositions.Clear();
        fightStageMovedModels.Clear();

        selectedModel = null;
        ClearFightModelFocus();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "FIGHT",
            fightActivationUnit.DisplayName,
            "Pile-in complete. Model-level melee attack selection started."
        );

        status =
            "MELEE ATTACKS: click the exact fighting model, choose its melee weapon/profile, then click an enemy target. Extra Attacks profiles are added automatically.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private List<ModelToken>
        FightPotentialAttackModels()
    {
        if (fightActivationUnit == null)
        {
            return new List<ModelToken>();
        }

        return
            JoinedModels(
                fightActivationUnit)
                .Where(
                    model =>
                        model != null &&
                        model.IsAlive &&
                        model.MeleeWeapons.Count > 0
                )
                .ToList();
    }

    private List<WeaponData>
        DistinctMeleeWeapons(
            ModelToken model,
            bool extraAttacks)
    {
        if (model == null)
        {
            return new List<WeaponData>();
        }

        return
            model.MeleeWeapons
                .Where(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon != null &&
                        model.CanUseWeapon(
                            weapon) &&
                        WeaponRuleParser.Has(
                            weapon,
                            "extra_attacks") ==
                        extraAttacks
                )
                .GroupBy(
                    weapon =>
                        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            weapon.id)
                        ? weapon.id
                        : weapon.displayName
                )
                .Select(
                    group =>
                        group.First()
                )
                .ToList();
    }

    private List<WeaponAttackSelection>
        BuildFightModelSelections(
            ModelToken model,
            WeaponData selectedNormal)
    {
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> result =
            new List<WeaponAttackSelection>();

        if (model == null)
            return result;

        if (selectedNormal != null)
        {
            result.Add(
                new WeaponAttackSelection(
                    model,
                    selectedNormal
                )
            );
        }

        foreach (WeaponData extra
            in DistinctMeleeWeapons(
                model,
                true))
        {
            result.Add(
                new WeaponAttackSelection(
                    model,
                    extra
                )
            );
        }

        return result;
    }

    private string MeleeWeaponProfileText(
        WeaponData weapon)
    {
        if (weapon == null)
            return "Unknown melee weapon";

        string attacks =
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                weapon.attacksExpression)
            ? weapon.attacksPerModel
                .ToString()
            : weapon.attacksExpression;

        string damage =
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                weapon.damageExpression)
            ? weapon.damage.ToString()
            : weapon.damageExpression;

        return
            weapon.displayName +
            "  •  A " +
            attacks +
            "  •  WS " +
            weapon.skill +
            "+  •  S " +
            weapon.strength +
            "  •  AP " +
            weapon.ap +
            "  •  D " +
            damage;
    }

    private void PrepareFightModelWeaponSelection(
        ModelToken model)
    {
        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;

        if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Attacks ||
            !ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                model))
        {
            return;
        }

        if (fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
            .Contains(
                model))
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " has already resolved its melee attacks this activation.";
            return;
        }

        List<WeaponData> normal =
            DistinctMeleeWeapons(
                model,
                false
            );

        List<WeaponData> extras =
            DistinctMeleeWeapons(
                model,
                true
            );

        if (normal.Count == 0 &&
            extras.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " has no usable melee weapon.";
            return;
        }

        fightPreparedAttackModel =
            model;

        if (normal.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " has only [EXTRA ATTACKS] profiles. Click an enemy target.";

            RefreshMoveRing();
            return;
        }

        if (normal.Count == 1)
        {
            fightPreparedMeleeWeapon =
                normal[0];

            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " → " +
                normal[0].displayName +
                ". Click an enemy target.";

            RefreshMoveRing();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (WeaponData weapon
            in normal)
        {
            WeaponData captured =
                weapon;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    MeleeWeaponProfileText(
                        captured
                    ),
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        fightPreparedAttackModel =
                            model;

                        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon =
                            captured;

                        status =
                            model.RoleName +
                            " → " +
                            captured.displayName +
                            ". Click an enemy target.";

                        RefreshMoveRing();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "SELECT MELEE WEAPON — " +
            model.RoleName,
            "Choose this model's normal melee profile. Any [EXTRA ATTACKS] profiles are added automatically. Then click the enemy target.",
            options.ToArray()
        );
    }

    private void TryFightModelAttack(
        ModelToken model,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Attacks ||
            fightActivationUnit == null ||
            !ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                model))
        {
            status =
                "No model-level melee attack is currently available.";
            return;
        }

        if (fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
            .Contains(
                model))
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " has already resolved or skipped its attacks this activation.";
            return;
        }

        if (target == null ||
            !target.IsAlive ||
            !target.IsOnBattlefield ||
            target.FactionId ==
                fightActivationUnit.FactionId)
        {
            status =
                "Select an enemy unit as this model's melee target.";
            return;
        }

        float distance =
            ModelDistanceToSquad(
                model,
                target
            );

        if (distance >
            EngagementRange +
            0.05f)
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " is " +
                distance.ToString("0.0") +
                "″ from that unit and is not in melee range.";
            return;
        }

        if (fightPreparedAttackModel !=
            model)
        {
            PrepareFightModelWeaponSelection(
                model
            );

            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " needs a melee weapon/profile selected before choosing a target.";
            return;
        }

        StartFightModelWeaponAttack(
            model,
            target,
            fightPreparedMeleeWeapon
        );
    }

    private void StartFightModelWeaponAttack(
        ModelToken model,
        SquadController target,
        WeaponData selectedNormal)
    {
        if (!ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                model) ||
            target == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (OfferAeldariPathOfWarrior(
                fightActivationUnit,
                () =>
                    StartFightModelWeaponAttack(
                        model,
                        target,
                        selectedNormal
                    )))
        {
            return;
        }

        List<WeaponAttackSelection> selections =
            BuildFightModelSelections(
                model,
                selectedNormal
            );

        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;

        if (selections.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                model.RoleName +
                " has no melee attack profiles to resolve.";
            return;
        }

        interactiveAttackConsumesNormalAction =
            false;

        interactiveAttackCompletionCallback =
            () =>
                CompleteFightModelAttack(
                    model
                );

        BeginInteractiveAttack(
            fightActivationUnit,
            target,
            selections,
            AttackMode.Melee,
            false
        );
    }

    private void CompleteFightModelAttack(
        ModelToken model)
    {
        if (model != null)
        {
            fightModelsResolvedThisActivation.Add(
                model
            );
        }

        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;

        if (fightActivationStage !=
            FightActivationStage.Attacks)
        {
            return;
        }

        ClearFightModelFocus();

        selectedModel = null;

        int remaining =
            FightPotentialAttackModels()
                .Count(
                    candidate =>
                        !fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                            .Contains(
                                candidate)
                );

        if (remaining <= 0)
        {
            BeginFightConsolidation();
            return;
        }

        status =
            "MELEE: model resolved. " +
            remaining +
            " fighting model(s) remain. Select the next model, or click DONE ATTACKS.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void SkipSelectedFightModel()
    {
        if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Attacks ||
            !ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                selectedModel))
        {
            return;
        }

        string role =
            selectedModel.RoleName;

        fightModelsResolvedThisActivation.Add(
            selectedModel
        );

        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;
        selectedModel = null;

        ClearFightModelFocus();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "FIGHT",
            fightActivationUnit != null
                ? fightActivationUnit.DisplayName
                : "Unit",
            role +
            " marked DONE without resolving a melee attack."
        );

        int remaining =
            FightPotentialAttackModels()
                .Count(
                    candidate =>
                        !fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                            .Contains(
                                candidate)
                );

        if (remaining <= 0)
        {
            BeginFightConsolidation();
            return;
        }

        status =
            role +
            " marked DONE. " +
            remaining +
            " model(s) remain.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void CompleteFightAttacks()
    {
        if (fightActivationStage !=
            FightActivationStage.Attacks)
        {
            return;
        }

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in FightPotentialAttackModels())
        {
            fightModelsResolvedThisActivation.Add(
                model
            );
        }

        BeginFightConsolidation();
    }

    private void BeginFightConsolidation()
    {
        if (fightActivationUnit == null)
        {
            ClearFightActivationState();
            return;
        }

        fightActivationStage =
            FightActivationStage.Consolidate;

        CaptureFightStageStartPositions();

        selectedModel =
            JoinedModels(
                fightActivationUnit)
                .FirstOrDefault(
                    model =>
                        model != null &&
                        model.IsAlive
                );

        if (selectedModel != null)
        {
            SelectModelForAction(
                fightActivationUnit,
                selectedModel
            );
        }

        AppendBattleLog(
            "FIGHT",
            fightActivationUnit.DisplayName,
            "Model-level attacks complete. CONSOLIDATION started."
        );

        status =
            "CONSOLIDATE: move models individually up to " +
            FightStageMoveLimit()
                .ToString("0.#") +
            "″. Hold ALT for the live ruler, then click DONE CONSOLIDATE.";

        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void CompleteFightConsolidation()
    {
        if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Consolidate ||
            fightActivationUnit == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (fightActivationUnit.IsAlive &&
            !fightActivationUnit.IsCoherent())
        {
            status =
                "Cannot finish consolidation: the unit is out of coherency.";
            return;
        }

        if (fightActivationUnit.IsAlive &&
            (!AllModelsInsideBoard(
                fightActivationUnit) ||
             !AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(
                fightActivationUnit)))
        {
            status =
                "Cannot finish consolidation: one or more models are in an illegal position.";
            return;
        }

        SquadController completed =
            fightActivationUnit;

        completed.HasFought = true;

        if (completed.AttachedLeader != null)
        {
            completed.AttachedLeader.HasFought =
                true;
        }

        completed.KatahSustainedActive = false;
        completed.KatahLethalActive = false;

        AppendBattleLog(
            "FIGHT",
            completed.DisplayName,
            "Consolidation complete. Fight activation finished."
        );

        ClearFightActivationState();

        status =
            completed.DisplayName +
            " completed its fight activation.";

        AdvanceFightPriority(
            completed
        );
    }

    private void ClearFightActivationState()
    {
        fightActivationStage =
            FightActivationStage.None;

        fightActivationUnit = null;
        fightActivationInitialTarget = null;
        fightPreparedAttackModel = null;
        fightPreparedMeleeWeapon = null;

        fightModelsResolvedThisActivation.Clear();
        fightStageMovedModels.Clear();
        fightStageStartPositions.Clear();

        ClearSelectionVisualOnly();

        selectedSquad = null;
        selectedModel = null;
        wholeSquadMoveMode = false;

        HideMovementCursorMeasure();
        RefreshMoveRing();
    }

    private void TryFight(
        SquadController attacker,
        SquadController target)
    {
        attacker =
            attacker.JoinedActionController();

        target =
            target.JoinedActionController();

        if (fightActivationStage !=
            FightActivationStage.None)
        {
            if (fightActivationUnit == null ||
                attacker !=
                    fightActivationUnit)
            {
                status =
                    "Finish the current fight activation before selecting another unit.";
                return;
            }

            if (fightActivationStage !=
                FightActivationStage.Attacks)
            {
                status =
                    fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.PileIn
                    ? "Finish the model-by-model pile-in before resolving melee attacks."
                    : "Finish consolidation before starting another fight.";
                return;
            }

            if (selectedModel == null ||
                !ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                    selectedModel))
            {
                status =
                    "MELEE: click the exact model that will fight, then click its enemy target.";
                return;
            }

            TryFightModelAttack(
                selectedModel,
                target
            );

            return;
        }

        if (!fightSequenceActive)
        {
            status =
                "The Fight sequence is not active yet.";
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.FactionId !=
            fightSelectionFaction)
        {
            status =
                "It is " +
                DisplayFactionName(
                    fightSelectionFaction
                ) +
                "'s fight activation. " +
                FightPriorityText();
            return;
        }

        if (!IsEligibleToFightNow(
                attacker))
        {
            status =
                "That unit is not eligible to fight now.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedModel == null ||
            selectedModel.Squad == null ||
            selectedModel.Squad
                .JoinedActionController() !=
                attacker)
        {
            status =
                "FIGHT: click the exact model in the unit you want to activate, then click an enemy unit.";
            return;
        }

        if (fightPriorityStep ==
                FightPriorityStep.FightsFirst &&
            !UnitHasFightsFirst(
                attacker))
        {
            status =
                "Fights First units must be resolved before remaining combats.";
            return;
        }

        float allowedPile =
            attacker.SuddenStrikeActive
            ? 6f
            : PileInDistance;

        if (attacker.DistanceTo(target) >
            EngagementRange +
            allowedPile +
            0.1f)
        {
            status =
                "Target is too far away to pile in.";
            return;
        }

        if (FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                attacker,
                "Martial Ka'tah") &&
            !attacker.KatahChoiceMadeThisFight)
        {
            OpenRuleChoice(
                "MARTIAL KA'TAH",
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " is selected to fight. Choose one Ka'tah Stance for these attacks.",
                new[]
                {
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Dacatarai — Sustained Hits 1",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();
                            attacker.KatahChoiceMadeThisFight = true;
                            attacker.KatahSustainedActive = true;
                            attacker.KatahLethalActive = false;
                            ExecuteFight(attacker, target);
                        }
                    ),
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Rendax — Lethal Hits",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();
                            attacker.KatahChoiceMadeThisFight = true;
                            attacker.KatahSustainedActive = false;
                            attacker.KatahLethalActive = true;
                            ExecuteFight(attacker, target);
                        }
                    )
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        if (CanUseBattleFocusManoeuvre(
                attacker))
        {
            OpenRuleChoice(
                "BATTLE FOCUS — SUDDEN STRIKE",
                attacker.DisplayName +
                " is selected to fight. Spend 1 Battle Focus token to increase pile-in and consolidation moves to 6 inches this phase?",
                new[]
                {
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Use Sudden Strike (1 BF)",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            if (SpendBattleFocusFor(
                                    attacker,
                                    "SUDDEN STRIKE"))
                            {
                                attacker.SuddenStrikeActive = true;
                            }

                            ExecuteFight(
                                attacker,
                                target
                            );
                        }
                    ),
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        "Fight normally",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();
                            ExecuteFight(
                                attacker,
                                target
                            );
                        }
                    )
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        ExecuteFight(
            attacker,
            target
        );
    }


    private List<WeaponAttackSelection>
        GetEligibleMeleeWeapons(
            SquadController attacker,
            SquadController target)
    {
        List<WeaponAttackSelection> result =
            new List<WeaponAttackSelection>();

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(attacker))
        {
            float distance =
                ModelDistanceToSquad(
                    model,
                    target
                );

            if (distance >
                EngagementRange +
                0.05f)
            {
                continue;
            }

            List<WeaponData> weapons =
                model.MeleeWeapons
                    .Where(
                        weapon =>
                            weapon != null
                    )
                    .ToList();

            if (weapons.Count == 0)
                continue;

            // Extra Attacks profiles are made in addition to the one normal
            // melee weapon the model selects.
            List<WeaponData> extraAttacks =
                weapons
                    .Where(
                        weapon =>
                            RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
                                weapon,
                                "extra_attacks"
                            )
                    )
                    .GroupBy(
                        weapon =>
                            weapon.id ??
                            weapon.displayName
                    )
                    .Select(
                        group =>
                            group.First()
                    )
                    .ToList();

            List<WeaponData> normalWeapons =
                weapons
                    .Where(
                        weapon =>
                            !RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
                                weapon,
                                "extra_attacks"
                            )
                    )
                    .GroupBy(
                        weapon =>
                            weapon.id ??
                            weapon.displayName
                    )
                    .Select(
                        group =>
                            group.First()
                    )
                    .ToList();

            WeaponData selectedNormal =
                normalWeapons
                    .OrderByDescending(
                        weapon =>
                            EstimateMeleeWeaponValue(
                                model,
                                target,
                                weapon
                            )
                    )
                    .FirstOrDefault();

            if (selectedNormal != null)
            {
                result.Add(
                    new WeaponAttackSelection(
                        model,
                        selectedNormal
                    )
                );
            }

            foreach (WeaponData weapon
                in extraAttacks)
            {
                result.Add(
                    new WeaponAttackSelection(
                        model,
                        weapon
                    )
                );
            }

            // Edge case: a data source presents only an Extra Attacks profile.
            if (selectedNormal == null &&
                extraAttacks.Count == 0)
            {
                WeaponData fallback =
                    weapons.FirstOrDefault();

                if (fallback != null)
                {
                    result.Add(
                        new WeaponAttackSelection(
                            model,
                            fallback
                        )
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        return result;
    }

    private float EstimateMeleeWeaponValue(
        ModelToken model,
        SquadController target,
        WeaponData weapon)
    {
        if (weapon == null)
            return 0f;

        SquadController attackOwner =
            model.Squad != null
            ? model.Squad
            : selectedSquad;

        int skill =
            attackOwner != null
            ? attackOwner.GetMeleeSkill(
                target,
                weapon
              )
            : Mathf.Clamp(
                weapon.skill,
                2,
                6
              );

        float hitChance =
            Mathf.Clamp01(
                (7f - skill) /
                6f
            );

        int woundNeeded =
            RulesEngine.WoundRollNeeded(
                weapon.strength,
                target.Toughness
            );

        float woundChance =
            Mathf.Clamp01(
                (7f - woundNeeded) /
                6f
            );

        int saveNeeded =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                target.GetSave(
                    attackOwner
                ) -
                weapon.ap,
                2,
                7
            );

        float failedSaveChance =
            saveNeeded >= 7
            ? 1f
            : Mathf.Clamp01(
                (saveNeeded - 1f) /
                6f
              );

        float averageAttacks =
            RulesEngine.AverageCharacteristic(
                weapon.attacksExpression,
                weapon.attacksPerModel
            );

        float averageDamage =
            RulesEngine.AverageCharacteristic(
                weapon.damageExpression,
                weapon.damage
            );

        if (RulesEngine.HasKeyword(
            weapon,
            "torrent"))
        {
            hitChance = 1f;
        }

        return
            averageAttacks *
            hitChance *
            woundChance *
            failedSaveChance *
            averageDamage;
    }

    private SquadController FindNearestEnemy(SquadController squad)
    {
        SquadController best = null;
        float bestDistance = float.MaxValue;

        foreach (SquadController other in squads)
        {
            if (!other.IsAlive ||
                other.IsAttachedLeader ||
                other.FactionId == squad.FactionId)
            {
                continue;
            }

            float d =
                JoinedDistance(
                    squad,
                    other
                );

            if (d < bestDistance)
            {
                bestDistance = d;
                best = other;
            }
        }

        return best;
    }

    private void AutoMoveSquadToward(
        SquadController mover,
        SquadController target,
        float maxDistance,
        float stopDistance)
    {
        Dictionary<ModelToken, Vector3> original =
            CaptureJoinedPositions(
                mover
            );

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(mover))
        {
            Vector3 candidate;

            if (TryFindLegalCombatMove(
                model,
                target,
                maxDistance,
                stopDistance,
                out candidate))
            {
                model.transform.position =
                    candidate;

                Physics.SyncTransforms();
            }
        }

        if (!mover.IsCoherent() ||
            !AllModelsInsideBoard(mover) ||
            !AllModelsHaveLegalPlacement(mover))
        {
            RestoreJoinedPositions(
                original
            );
        }
        else
        {
            mover.RefreshVisuals();

            if (mover.AttachedLeader != null)
                mover.AttachedLeader.RefreshVisuals();
        }
    }

    private bool TryFindLegalCombatMove(
        ModelToken movingModel,
        SquadController target,
        float maxDistance,
        float stopDistance,
        out Vector3 result)
    {
        Vector3 from =
            movingModel.transform.position;

        float startingDistance =
            ModelDistanceToSquad(
                movingModel,
                target
            );

        List<ModelToken> targetModels =
            JoinedModels(target)
                .OrderBy(
                    model =>
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            from,
                            model.transform.position
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (targetModels.Count == 0)
        {
            result = from;
            return false;
        }

        if (startingDistance <=
            stopDistance + 0.001f)
        {
            result = from;
            return true;
        }

        float[] angleOffsets =
        {
            0f,
            12f, -12f,
            24f, -24f,
            36f, -36f,
            48f, -48f,
            60f, -60f,
            72f, -72f,
            84f, -84f,
            96f, -96f,
            108f, -108f,
            120f, -120f,
            135f, -135f,
            150f, -150f,
            165f, -165f,
            180f
        };

        Vector3 best =
            from;

        float bestDistance =
            startingDistance;

        bool found = false;

        foreach (ModelToken targetModel
            in targetModels)
        {
            float desiredRadius =
                Mathf.Max(
                    stopDistance,
                    MinimumModelSeparation(
                        movingModel,
                        targetModel
                    ) +
                    0.08f
                );

            Vector3 targetPoint =
                targetModel.transform.position;

            Vector3 outward =
                from -
                targetPoint;

            outward.y = 0f;

            if (outward.sqrMagnitude <=
                0.0001f)
            {
                outward =
                    Vector3.back;
            }

            outward.Normalize();

            foreach (float angle
                in angleOffsets)
            {
                Vector3 radial =
                    Quaternion.Euler(
                        0f,
                        angle,
                        0f
                    ) *
                    outward;

                Vector3 desired =
                    targetPoint +
                    radial *
                    desiredRadius;

                desired.y = from.y;

                Vector3 delta =
                    desired -
                    from;

                delta.y = 0f;

                float requiredMove =
                    HorizontalMagnitude(
                        delta
                    );

                Vector3 candidate;

                if (requiredMove <=
                    maxDistance + 0.001f)
                {
                    candidate = desired;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (delta.sqrMagnitude <=
                        0.0001f)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    candidate =
                        from +
                        delta.normalized *
                        maxDistance;

                    candidate.y = from.y;
                }

                if (!InsideBoard(candidate))
                    continue;

                if (!CanPlaceModel(
                        movingModel,
                        candidate))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!CombatMovePathIsClear(
                        movingModel,
                        from,
                        candidate))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                float endingDistance =
                    DistancePointToSquad(
                        candidate,
                        target
                    );

                if (endingDistance >=
                    startingDistance - 0.001f)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!found ||
                    endingDistance <
                        bestDistance)
                {
                    found = true;
                    bestDistance =
                        endingDistance;
                    best = candidate;
                }
            }
        }

        result =
            found
            ? best
            : from;

        return found;
    }

    private float DistancePointToSquad(
        Vector3 point,
        SquadController target)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in target.LivingModelTokens())
        {
            best =
                Mathf.Min(
                    best,
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        point,
                        model.transform.position
                    )
                );
        }

        return best ==
            float.MaxValue
            ? 999f
            : best;
    }

    private float JoinedDistance(
        SquadController a,
        SquadController b)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ModelToken modelA
            in JoinedModels(a))
        {
            foreach (ModelToken modelB
                in JoinedModels(b))
            {
                best =
                    Mathf.Min(
                        best,
                        HorizontalDistance(
                            modelA.transform.position,
                            modelB.transform.position
                        )
                    );
            }
        }

        return best ==
            float.MaxValue
            ? 999f
            : best;
    }

    private bool IsEngaged(SquadController squad)
    {
        return squads.Any(
            other =>
                other != squad &&
                other.IsAlive &&
                other.FactionId != squad.FactionId &&
                JoinedDistance(squad, other) <= EngagementRange
        );
    }

    public bool IsUnitEngagedPublic(
        SquadController squad)
    {
        return
            squad != null &&
            IsEngaged(squad);
    }

    public bool UnitsAreEngaged(
        SquadController a,
        SquadController b)
    {
        if (a == null ||
            b == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return
            JoinedDistance(
                a,
                b
            ) <=
            EngagementRange +
            0.001f;
    }

    public bool ModelCanSeeUnit(
        ModelToken shooter,
        SquadController target)
    {
        return ModelHasLineOfSight(
            shooter,
            target
        );
    }

    public bool FriendlyUnitCanSeeTarget(
        string faction,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (target == null)
            return false;

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null ||
                !squad.IsAlive ||
                !squad.IsOnBattlefield ||
                squad.FactionId !=
                    faction)
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (ModelToken model
                in JoinedModels(squad))
            {
                if (ModelHasLineOfSight(
                    model,
                    target))
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    public bool TargetUnitHasCoverFromShooter(
        ModelToken shooter,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (shooter == null ||
            target == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        List<ModelToken> targetModels =
            JoinedModels(target);

        if (targetModels.Count == 0)
            return false;

        return targetModels.All(
            model =>
                TargetModelHasCoverFromShooter(
                    shooter,
                    model
                )
        );
    }

    private bool ModelHasLineOfSight(
        ModelToken shooter,
        SquadController target)
    {
        if (shooter == null ||
            !shooter.IsAlive)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (ModelToken targetModel
            in JoinedModels(target))
        {
            if (ModelHasLineOfSightToModel(
                shooter,
                targetModel))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    public bool ModelCanSeeModel(
        ModelToken shooter,
        ModelToken targetModel)
    {
        return ModelHasLineOfSightToModel(
            shooter,
            targetModel
        );
    }

    private bool ModelHasLineOfSightToModel(
        ModelToken shooter,
        ModelToken targetModel)
    {
        Vector3 origin =
            shooter.transform.position +
            Vector3.up * 0.5f;

        Vector3 destination =
            targetModel.transform.position +
            Vector3.up * 0.5f;

        Vector3 direction =
            destination -
            origin;

        float distance =
            direction.magnitude;

        RaycastHit[] hits =
            Physics.RaycastAll(
                origin,
                direction.normalized,
                distance
            );

        foreach (RaycastHit hit
            in hits.OrderBy(h => h.distance))
        {
            ModelToken hitModel =
                hit.collider.GetComponent<ModelToken>();

            if (hitModel == shooter ||
                hitModel == targetModel)
            {
                continue;
            }

            TerrainFeature terrain =
                hit.collider.GetComponent<TerrainFeature>();

            if (terrain != null &&
                terrain.BlocksLineOfSight)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    public bool TargetModelHasCoverFromShooter(
        ModelToken shooter,
        ModelToken targetModel)
    {
        if (shooter == null ||
            targetModel == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        Vector3 origin =
            shooter.transform.position +
            Vector3.up * 0.5f;

        Vector3 destination =
            targetModel.transform.position +
            Vector3.up * 0.5f;

        Vector3 direction =
            destination -
            origin;

        float distance =
            direction.magnitude;

        RaycastHit[] hits =
            Physics.RaycastAll(
                origin,
                direction.normalized,
                distance
            );

        foreach (RaycastHit hit
            in hits.OrderBy(h => h.distance))
        {
            ModelToken hitModel =
                hit.collider.GetComponent<ModelToken>();

            if (hitModel == shooter ||
                hitModel == targetModel)
            {
                continue;
            }

            TerrainFeature terrain =
                hit.collider.GetComponent<TerrainFeature>();

            if (terrain != null &&
                terrain.GrantsCover)
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private ModelToken ClosestJoinedModelTo(
        SquadController squad,
        Vector3 point)
    {
        ModelToken bestModel = null;
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ModelToken model
            in JoinedModels(squad))
        {
            float distance =
                HorizontalDistance(
                    point,
                    model.transform.position
                );

            if (distance < best)
            {
                best = distance;
                bestModel = model;
            }
        }

        return bestModel;
    }

    private float ModelDistanceToSquad(
        ModelToken model,
        SquadController target)
    {
        float best =
            float.MaxValue;

        foreach (ModelToken targetModel
            in JoinedModels(target))
        {
            best =
                Mathf.Min(
                    best,
                    HorizontalDistance(
                        model.transform.position,
                        targetModel.transform.position
                    )
                );
        }

        return best ==
            float.MaxValue
            ? 999f
            : best;
    }

    private bool ActiveFactionIsCoherent()
    {
        return squads
            .Where(
                squad =>
                    squad.IsAlive &&
                    squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                    squad.FactionId == activeFaction
            )
            .All(
                squad =>
                    squad.IsCoherent()
            );
    }

    private SquadController FirstIncoherentSquad()
    {
        return squads.FirstOrDefault(
            squad =>
                squad.IsAlive &&
                squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                squad.FactionId == activeFaction &&
                !squad.IsCoherent()
        );
    }

    private void CaptureObjectiveControlAtPhaseStart()
    {
        objectiveControlAtPhaseStart.Clear();

        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in objectives)
        {
            if (objective == null)
                continue;

            objectiveControlAtPhaseStart[objective] =
                objective.Controller(
                    squads
                );
        }
    }

    private void ClearPhaseDestructionRecords()
    {
        destroyedModelsThisPhase.Clear();
        destroyedUnitsThisPhase.Clear();
        destroyedUnitsRecordedThisPhase.Clear();
    }

    private void BuildEndPhaseFlow()
    {
        endPhaseFlowQueue.Clear();
        endPhaseFlowRunning = true;

        if (phase == Phase.Command &&
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.IsNecrons(
                activeFaction))
        {
            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferNormalReanimationProtocols
            );
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferFireOverwatch
            );
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Shoot)
        {
            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferDeathAnswersDeath
            );

            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferLethalIntent
            );
        }

        endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            OfferEternalRevenant
        );

        endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
            OfferResurrectionOrb
        );

        if (phase == Phase.End)
        {
            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferWindriderHostExtraction
            );

            endPhaseFlowQueue.Enqueue(
                OfferVeilOfDarkness
            );
        }
    }

    private void ContinueEndPhaseFlow()
    {
        if (!endPhaseFlowRunning)
            return;

        if (showRuleChoiceWindow ||
            specialMoveSquad != null ||
            specialShootUnit != null ||
            interactiveAttack != null ||
            TraditionalStateResolutionPending())
        {
            return;
        }

        if (endPhaseFlowQueue.Count > 0)
        {
            System.Action next =
                endPhaseFlowQueue.Dequeue();

            next();
            return;
        }

        endPhaseFlowRunning = false;
        CompletePhaseTransition();
    }

    private void OfferNormalReanimationProtocols()
    {
        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.FactionId ==
                            activeFaction &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.LivingModels > 0 &&
                        unit.HasAnyLostWoundsOrModels() &&
                        FactionRuleSystem
                            .UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                                unit,
                                "Reanimation Protocols"
                            )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                "Reanimation Protocols: no eligible damaged units.";

            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        if (IsXcomMode)
        {
            ResolveNextXcomReanimation(
                eligible
            );

            return;
        }

        BeginTraditionalReanimation(
            eligible
        );
    }

    private void BeginTraditionalReanimation(
        List<SquadController> eligible)
    {
        traditionalReanimationUnits.Clear();

        if (eligible != null)
        {
            traditionalReanimationUnits.AddRange(
                eligible.Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.HasAnyLostWoundsOrModels()
                )
            );
        }

        if (traditionalReanimationUnits.Count == 0)
        {
            traditionalReanimationPending = false;
            traditionalReanimationUnit = null;
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        traditionalReanimationPending = true;
        traditionalReanimationUnit =
            traditionalReanimationUnits[0];
        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;

        manualWoundEditMode = true;

        OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
            1
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "REANIMATION",
            "Reanimation Protocols",
            "Traditional Reanimation window opened. Player resolves all Reanimation dice manually and directly edits returned/healed models."
        );

        status =
            "REANIMATION PROTOCOLS: resolve the tabletop roll yourself. Select a damaged unit, then manually heal or return models.";
    }

    private void SelectTraditionalReanimationUnit(
        SquadController unit)
    {
        if (!traditionalReanimationPending ||
            unit == null ||
            !traditionalReanimationUnits
                .Contains(
                    unit))
        {
            return;
        }

        traditionalReanimationUnit =
            unit;
        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;

        status =
            "REANIMATION: " +
            unit.DisplayName +
            " selected. Apply only the wounds/models your tabletop roll allows.";
    }

    private void TraditionalHealSelectedReanimationWound()
    {
        if (!traditionalReanimationPending ||
            traditionalReanimationUnit == null ||
            selectedModel == null ||
            !selectedModel.IsAlive ||
            selectedModel.Squad == null ||
            selectedModel.Squad
                .JoinedActionController() !=
                traditionalReanimationUnit
                    .JoinedActionController())
        {
            status =
                "Select a living model in the current Reanimation unit first.";
            return;
        }

        if (selectedModel.CurrentWounds >=
            selectedModel.MaxWounds)
        {
            status =
                selectedModel.RoleName +
                " is already at full wounds.";
            return;
        }

        int before =
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds;

        selectedModel.Heal(1);
        selectedModel.RefreshWoundDisplay();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "REANIMATION",
            traditionalReanimationUnit.DisplayName,
            selectedModel.RoleName +
            " manually restored 1 wound (" +
            before +
            " → " +
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds +
            ")."
        );

        status =
            selectedModel.RoleName +
            " restored to " +
            selectedModel.CurrentWounds +
            "/" +
            selectedModel.MaxWounds +
            " wounds.";
    }

    private List<ModelToken>
        TraditionalReanimationDestroyedModels()
    {
        if (traditionalReanimationUnit == null)
        {
            return new List<ModelToken>();
        }

        return
            traditionalReanimationUnit
                .DestroyedModelTokens()
                .Where(
                    model =>
                        model != null
                )
                .ToList();
    }

    private void CycleTraditionalReanimationDeadModel(
        int direction)
    {
        List<ModelToken> dead =
            TraditionalReanimationDestroyedModels();

        if (dead.Count == 0)
        {
            traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;
            return;
        }

        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex =
            (traditionalReanimationDeadIndex +
             direction) %
            dead.Count;

        if (traditionalReanimationDeadIndex < 0)
        {
            traditionalReanimationDeadIndex +=
                dead.Count;
        }
    }

    private void TraditionalReturnSelectedReanimationModel()
    {
        if (!traditionalReanimationPending ||
            traditionalReanimationUnit == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        List<ModelToken> dead =
            TraditionalReanimationDestroyedModels();

        if (dead.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                traditionalReanimationUnit.DisplayName +
                " has no destroyed models to return.";
            return;
        }

        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                traditionalReanimationDeadIndex,
                0,
                dead.Count - 1
            );

        ModelToken returning =
            dead[
                traditionalReanimationDeadIndex
            ];

        Vector3 position;

        if (!TryFindReanimationPosition(
                traditionalReanimationUnit,
                returning,
                out position))
        {
            status =
                "No legal automatic placement position was found for " +
                returning.RoleName +
                ".";
            return;
        }

        returning.Revive(
            1,
            position
        );

        traditionalReanimationUnit.RefreshVisuals();
        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        AppendBattleLog(
            "REANIMATION",
            traditionalReanimationUnit.DisplayName,
            returning.RoleName +
            " manually returned at 1 wound. Warboard did not determine whether the tabletop roll allowed this."
        );

        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;

        status =
            returning.RoleName +
            " returned at 1 wound.";
    }

    private void CompleteTraditionalReanimationUnit()
    {
        if (!traditionalReanimationPending ||
            traditionalReanimationUnit == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController completed =
            traditionalReanimationUnit;

        traditionalReanimationUnits.Remove(
            completed
        );

        AppendBattleLog(
            "REANIMATION",
            completed.DisplayName,
            "Player marked this unit's Reanimation Protocols resolution complete."
        );

        traditionalReanimationUnit =
            traditionalReanimationUnits
                .FirstOrDefault();

        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;

        if (traditionalReanimationUnit == null)
        {
            FinishTraditionalReanimation();
            return;
        }

        status =
            "REANIMATION: choose/resolve the next damaged unit.";
    }

    private void FinishTraditionalReanimation()
    {
        traditionalReanimationPending = false;
        traditionalReanimationUnit = null;
        traditionalReanimationUnits.Clear();
        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex = 0;

        manualWoundEditMode = false;

        AppendBattleLog(
            "REANIMATION",
            "Reanimation Protocols",
            "Player marked Traditional Reanimation resolution complete."
        );

        status =
            "Traditional Reanimation Protocols complete.";

        ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
    }

    private void ResolveNextXcomReanimation(
        List<SquadController> remaining)
    {
        if (remaining == null)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        remaining =
            remaining
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.HasAnyLostWoundsOrModels()
                )
                .ToList();

        if (remaining.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in remaining)
        {
            SquadController captured =
                unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    captured.DisplayName +
                    " — " +
                    Mathf.Max(
                        0,
                        captured.StartingModels -
                        captured.LivingModels
                    ) +
                    " model(s) lost",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        List<SquadController> next =
                            remaining
                                .Where(
                                    value =>
                                        value != captured
                                )
                                .ToList();

                        ResolveXcomReanimationForUnit(
                            captured,
                            next
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "REANIMATION PROTOCOLS AVAILABLE",
            "Select the Necron unit to resolve next. Warboard makes the Reanimation roll automatically; placement and genuine optional choices remain yours.",
            options.ToArray()
        );
    }

    private void ResolveXcomReanimationForUnit(
        SquadController unit,
        List<SquadController> remaining)
    {
        if (unit == null)
        {
            ResolveNextXcomReanimation(
                remaining
            );
            return;
        }

        int rolled =
            RollTabletopD3(
                "Reanimation Protocols: " +
                unit.DisplayName
            );

        System.Action<int> resolve =
            value =>
            {
                int restored =
                    ReanimateUnit(
                        unit,
                        value
                    );

                AppendBattleLog(
                    "REANIMATION",
                    unit.DisplayName,
                    "D3 result " +
                    value +
                    " → " +
                    restored +
                    " wound(s) restored."
                );

                status =
                    "REANIMATION PROTOCOLS: " +
                    unit.DisplayName +
                    " restored " +
                    restored +
                    " wound(s).";

                ResolveNextXcomReanimation(
                    remaining
                );
            };

        bool canReroll =
            FactionRuleSystem
                .UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                    unit,
                    "Their Number Is Legion"
                );

        if (!canReroll)
        {
            resolve(rolled);
            return;
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "THEIR NUMBER IS LEGION",
            unit.DisplayName +
            " generated " +
            rolled +
            " Reanimation point(s). Keep it or use the optional re-roll?",
            new[]
            {
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Keep " +
                    rolled,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();
                        resolve(rolled);
                    }
                ),
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Re-roll automatically",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        int rerolled =
                            RollTabletopD3(
                                "Their Number Is Legion: " +
                                unit.DisplayName
                            );

                        resolve(rerolled);
                    }
                )
            }
        );
    }

    private void ResolveNextNormalReanimation(
        List<SquadController> eligible,
        int index)
    {
        if (eligible == null ||
            index >= eligible.Count)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        SquadController unit =
            eligible[index];

        if (unit == null ||
            !unit.IsAlive ||
            !unit.IsOnBattlefield ||
            unit.LivingModels <= 0 ||
            !unit.HasAnyLostWoundsOrModels())
        {
            ResolveNextNormalReanimation(
                eligible,
                index + 1
            );

            return;
        }

        int rolled =
            RollTabletopD3(
                "Reanimation Protocols: " +
                unit.DisplayName
            );

        bool canReroll =
            FactionRuleSystem
                .UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                    unit,
                    "Their Number Is Legion"
                );

        System.Action<int> resolve =
            value =>
            {
                int restored =
                    ReanimateUnit(
                        unit,
                        value
                    );

                status =
                    "REANIMATION PROTOCOLS: " +
                    unit.DisplayName +
                    " restored " +
                    restored +
                    " wound(s) from D3=" +
                    value +
                    ".";

                ResolveNextNormalReanimation(
                    eligible,
                    index + 1
                );
            };

        if (!canReroll)
        {
            resolve(rolled);
            return;
        }

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "THEIR NUMBER IS LEGION",
            unit.DisplayName +
            " rolled " +
            rolled +
            " for Reanimation Protocols. Re-roll that D3?",
            new[]
            {
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Keep " +
                    rolled,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();
                        resolve(rolled);
                    }
                ),
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Re-roll D3",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        int rerolled =
                            RollTabletopD3(
                                "Their Number Is Legion: " +
                                unit.DisplayName
                            );

                        resolve(rerolled);
                    }
                )
            }
        );
    }

    private void OfferFireOverwatch()
    {
        string defender =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction != activeFaction
            );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                defender) ||
            GetCommandPoints(defender) < 1)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController shooter
            in squads.Where(
                unit =>
                    unit != null &&
                    !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                    unit.FactionId == defender &&
                    unit.IsAlive &&
                    unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                    !unit.HasKeyword("titanic") &&
                    !IsEngaged(unit) &&
                    !unit.TargetedByFactionStratagemThisPhase))
        {
            foreach (SquadController enemy
                in squads.Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.FactionId == activeFaction &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        !unit.FlittingShadowsActive &&
                        JoinedDistance(
                            shooter,
                            unit
                        ) <= 24.001f))
            {
                List<WeaponAttackSelection> legal =
                    GetEligibleRangedWeapons(
                        shooter,
                        enemy,
                        false
                    );

                if (legal.Count == 0)
                    continue;

                SquadController capturedShooter =
                    shooter;

                SquadController capturedEnemy =
                    enemy;

                options.Add(
                    new RuleChoiceOption(
                        capturedShooter.DisplayName +
                        " overwatches " +
                        capturedEnemy.DisplayName +
                        " (1 CP)",
                        () =>
                        {
                            CloseRuleChoice();

                            if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                    capturedShooter,
                                    1,
                                    "Fire Overwatch"))
                            {
                                ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                                return;
                            }

                            capturedShooter.SnapShootingActive =
                                true;

                            BeginSpecialShoot(
                                capturedShooter,
                                capturedEnemy,
                                "Fire Overwatch",
                                () =>
                                {
                                    capturedShooter.SnapShootingActive =
                                        false;

                                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                                }
                            );
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        if (options.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip Fire Overwatch",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "FIRE OVERWATCH",
            "End of the opponent's Movement phase. Select an eligible unengaged unit and one visible enemy within 24 inches. Snap Shooting only hits on unmodified 6s and cannot re-roll Hit rolls. Units protected by Flitting Shadows are excluded.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void OfferDeathAnswersDeath()
    {
        string ynnariFaction =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction != activeFaction &&
                    factionRules != null &&
                    UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                        faction
                    )
            );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                ynnariFaction))
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            destroyedModelsThisPhase
                .Where(
                    record =>
                        record != null &&
                        record.Unit != null &&
                        record.Unit.FactionId ==
                            ynnariFaction
                )
                .Select(
                    record =>
                        record.Unit.JoinedActionController()
                )
                .Distinct()
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
                        !unit.HasKeyword(
                            "wraith construct") &&
                        GetCommandPoints(
                            unit.FactionId
                        ) >= 1
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Death Answers Death: " +
                    captured.DisplayName +
                    " (1 CP)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                captured,
                                1,
                                "Death Answers Death"))
                        {
                            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        BeginSpecialShoot(
                            captured,
                            null,
                            "Death Answers Death",
                            ContinueEndPhaseFlow
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "DEATH ANSWERS DEATH",
            "One eligible Ynnari unit that lost models this Shooting phase can shoot as if it were your Shooting phase.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void OfferLethalIntent()
    {
        string ynnariFaction =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction != activeFaction &&
                    factionRules != null &&
                    UsesDevotedOfYnneadRules(
                        faction
                    )
            );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                ynnariFaction))
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<DestroyedUnitRecord> destroyed =
            destroyedUnitsThisPhase
                .Where(
                    record =>
                        record != null &&
                        record.FactionId ==
                            ynnariFaction &&
                        record.Unit != null &&
                        record.Unit.HasKeyword(
                            "ynnari")
                )
                .ToList();

        if (destroyed.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.FactionId ==
                            ynnariFaction &&
                        unit.HasKeyword("ynnari") &&
                        (unit.HasKeyword("infantry") ||
                         unit.HasKeyword("mounted")) &&
                        destroyed.Any(
                            record =>
                                JoinedModels(unit).Any(
                                    model =>
                                        HorizontalDistance(
                                            model.transform.position,
                                            record.LastPosition
                                        ) <= 6.001f
                                )
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Lethal Intent: " +
                    captured.DisplayName,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            OpenTraditionalNumericPrompt(
                                "LETHAL INTENT",
                                captured.DisplayName +
                                " makes a D6+1 move. Resolve the roll yourself and enter the final movement distance.",
                                2,
                                7,
                                2,
                                1,
                                value =>
                                    BeginSpecialMove(
                                        captured,
                                        value,
                                        "Lethal Intent",
                                        ContinueEndPhaseFlow
                                    ),
                                ContinueEndPhaseFlow
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int move =
                            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                "Lethal Intent: " +
                                captured.DisplayName
                            ) + 1;

                        BeginSpecialMove(
                            captured,
                            move,
                            "Lethal Intent",
                            ContinueEndPhaseFlow
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "STRENGTH FROM DEATH — LETHAL INTENT",
            "A Ynnari unit was destroyed this Shooting phase. Select one eligible Ynnari Infantry/Mounted unit that was within 6 inches of a destroyed unit.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void OfferEternalRevenant()
    {
        List<DestroyedModelRecord> eligible =
            destroyedModelsThisPhase
                .Where(
                    record =>
                        record != null &&
                        record.Model != null &&
                        record.Unit != null &&
                        factionRules != null &&
                        factionRules.IsNecrons(
                            record.Unit.FactionId) &&
                        record.Unit.HasKeyword(
                            "infantry") &&
                        record.Unit.HasKeyword(
                            "character") &&
                        !record.Unit.EternalRevenantUsed &&
                        !record.Model.IsAlive &&
                        GetCommandPoints(
                            record.Unit.FactionId
                        ) >= 1
                )
                .GroupBy(
                    record => record.Model
                )
                .Select(group => group.First())
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (DestroyedModelRecord record
            in eligible)
        {
            DestroyedModelRecord captured = record;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Eternal Revenant: " +
                    captured.Unit.DisplayName +
                    " (1 CP)",
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!SpendFactionStratagemCP(
                                captured.Unit,
                                1,
                                "Protocol of the Eternal Revenant"))
                        {
                            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        Vector3 position;

                        if (!TryFindSafeRevivePosition(
                                captured.Model,
                                captured.Position,
                                captured.Unit.FactionId,
                                out position))
                        {
                            status =
                                "Eternal Revenant could not find a legal unengaged setup position.";
                            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                            return;
                        }

                        captured.Unit.DetachFromBodyguard();
                        captured.Unit.EternalRevenantUsed = true;

                        captured.Model.Revive(
                            Mathf.CeilToInt(
                                captured.Model.MaxWounds /
                                2f
                            ),
                            position
                        );

                        captured.Unit.RefreshVisuals();
                        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

                        status =
                            "PROTOCOL OF THE ETERNAL REVENANT: " +
                            captured.Unit.DisplayName +
                            " returned with half wounds.";

                        ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
