                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "PROTOCOL OF THE ETERNAL REVENANT",
            "An eligible Necron Infantry Character was destroyed this phase. It can return at the end of the phase.",
            options
        );
    }

    private void OfferResurrectionOrb()
    {
        if (resurrectionOrbUsedThisTurn)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        unit.HasAnyLostWoundsOrModels() &&
                        FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                            unit,
                            "Resurrection Orb") &&
                        !unit.ResurrectionOrbUsed &&
                        (unit.AttachedLeader == null ||
                         !unit.AttachedLeader.ResurrectionOrbUsed)
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Resurrection Orb: " +
                    captured.DisplayName,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        if (!IsXcomMode)
                        {
                            resurrectionOrbUsedThisTurn = true;

                            if (captured.AttachedLeader != null &&
                                FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                                    captured.AttachedLeader,
                                    "Resurrection Orb"))
                            {
                                captured.AttachedLeader.ResurrectionOrbUsed = true;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                captured.ResurrectionOrbUsed = true;
                            }

                            SelectSquadForAction(
                                captured
                            );

                            manualRestoreEditMode = true;
                            manualWoundEditMode = false;
                            manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;

                            QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                                "RESURRECTION ORB",
                                captured.DisplayName +
                                ": roll the D6 yourself and resolve Reanimation manually. Use WOUND EDIT for healing and RESTORE EDIT for returned models. Warboard will not count the D6 result for you.",
                                1
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        int roll =
                            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                                "Resurrection Orb: " +
                                captured.DisplayName
                            );

                        int restored =
                            ReanimateUnit(
                                captured,
                                roll
                            );

                        resurrectionOrbUsedThisTurn = true;

                        if (captured.AttachedLeader != null &&
                            FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                                captured.AttachedLeader,
                                "Resurrection Orb"))
                        {
                            captured.AttachedLeader.ResurrectionOrbUsed = true;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            captured.ResurrectionOrbUsed = true;
                        }

                        status =
                            "RESURRECTION ORB: D6=" +
                            roll +
                            ", restored " +
                            restored +
                            " wound(s).";

                        ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "RESURRECTION ORB",
            "At the end of the phase, one eligible unit can activate Reanimation Protocols using D6 instead of D3.",
            options
        );
    }

    private int WindriderExtractionLimit()
    {
        if (battlePoints <= 1000)
            return 1;

        if (battlePoints <= 2000)
            return 2;

        return 3;
    }

    private bool IsWindriderExtractionUnit(
        SquadController unit,
        string faction)
    {
        return
            unit != null &&
            !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
            unit.FactionId == faction &&
            unit.IsAlive &&
            unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
            !IsEngaged(unit) &&
            unit.HasKeyword("asuryani") &&
            (unit.HasKeyword("mounted") ||
             unit.HasKeyword("vyper") ||
             unit.DisplayName.IndexOf(
                 "Vyper",
                 System.StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
              ) >= 0);
    }

    private void OfferWindriderHostExtraction()
    {
        if (aeldariRules == null)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        string windriderFaction =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction != activeFaction &&
                    aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                        faction
                    ) &&
                    aeldariRules.DetachmentIs(
                        faction,
                        AeldariDetachment.WindriderHost
                    )
            );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                windriderFaction))
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        IsWindriderExtractionUnit(
                            unit,
                            windriderFaction
                        )
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        ContinueWindriderHostExtraction(
            windriderFaction,
            eligible,
            WindriderExtractionLimit()
        );
    }

    private void ContinueWindriderHostExtraction(
        string faction,
        List<SquadController> eligible,
        int remaining)
    {
        if (remaining <= 0 ||
            eligible == null ||
            eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            status =
                "RIDE THE WIND extraction complete.";

            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible.ToList())
        {
            SquadController captured =
                unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Extract " +
                    captured.DisplayName,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        captured.SendToReserves(
                            true
                        );

                        if (captured.AttachedLeader != null)
                        {
                            captured.AttachedLeader
                                .SendToReserves(
                                    true
                                );
                        }

                        AppendBattleLog(
                            "AELDARI",
                            "Ride the Wind",
                            captured.DisplayName +
                            " was removed at the end of the opponent's turn and placed into Strategic Reserves."
                        );

                        eligible.Remove(
                            captured
                        );

                        ContinueWindriderHostExtraction(
                            faction,
                            eligible,
                            remaining - 1
                        );
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Done — extract no more units",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "WINDRIDER HOST — RIDE THE WIND",
            DisplayFactionName(
                faction
            ) +
            " may place up to " +
            remaining +
            " more eligible Asuryani Mounted/Vyper unit(s) into Strategic Reserves.",
            options.ToArray()
        );
    }

    private void OfferVeilOfDarkness()
    {
        string opponent =
            factions.FirstOrDefault(
                faction =>
                    faction != activeFaction &&
                    factionRules != null &&
                    factionRules.IsNecrons(
                        faction
                    )
            );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                opponent))
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<SquadController> eligible =
            squads
                .Where(
                    unit =>
                        unit != null &&
                        !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        unit.FactionId == opponent &&
                        unit.IsAlive &&
                        unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        !IsEngaged(unit) &&
                        FactionRuleSystem.UnitOrLeaderHasRule(
                            unit,
                            "Veil of Darkness") &&
                        !unit.VeilOfDarknessUsed
                )
                .ToList();

        if (eligible.Count == 0)
        {
            ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
            return;
        }

        List<RuleChoiceOption> options =
            new List<RuleChoiceOption>();

        foreach (SquadController unit
            in eligible)
        {
            SquadController captured = unit;

            options.Add(
                new RuleChoiceOption(
                    "Veil of Darkness: " +
                    captured.DisplayName,
                    () =>
                    {
                        CloseRuleChoice();

                        captured.VeilOfDarknessUsed = true;
                        captured.TemporaryDeepStrike = true;
                        captured.MustIngressFromVeil = true;
                        captured.SendToReserves(true);

                        if (captured.AttachedLeader != null)
                        {
                            captured.AttachedLeader.VeilOfDarknessUsed = true;
                            captured.AttachedLeader.TemporaryDeepStrike = true;
                            captured.AttachedLeader.MustIngressFromVeil = true;
                            captured.AttachedLeader.SendToReserves(true);
                        }

                        status =
                            "VEIL OF DARKNESS: " +
                            captured.DisplayName +
                            " entered Strategic Reserves and must ingress next Movement phase.";

                        ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                    }
                )
            );
        }

        options.Add(
            new RuleChoiceOption(
                "Skip",
                () =>
                {
                    CloseRuleChoice();
                    ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
                }
            )
        );

        OpenRuleChoice(
            "VEIL OF DARKNESS",
            "End of the opponent's turn: an eligible unengaged bearer unit can enter Strategic Reserves.",
            options
        );
    }

    private bool TryFindSafeRevivePosition(
        ModelToken model,
        Vector3 origin,
        string faction,
        out Vector3 result)
    {
        result = origin;

        float[] radii =
        {
            0f,
            0.9f,
            1.4f,
            2.0f,
            2.8f
        };

        foreach (float radius
            in radii)
        {
            for (int angle = 0;
                 angle < 360;
                 angle += 30)
            {
                float radians =
                    angle *
                    Mathf.Deg2Rad;

                Vector3 candidate =
                    origin +
                    new Vector3(
                        Mathf.Cos(radians) *
                            radius,
                        0f,
                        Mathf.Sin(radians) *
                            radius
                    );

                candidate.y =
                    model.transform.position.y;

                if (!InsideBoard(candidate))
                    continue;

                bool enemyTooClose =
                    squads.Any(
                        enemy =>
                            enemy != null &&
                            enemy.IsAlive &&
                            enemy.IsOnBattlefield &&
                            enemy.FactionId !=
                                faction &&
                            JoinedModels(enemy).Any(
                                enemyModel =>
                                    HorizontalDistance(
                                        candidate,
                                        enemyModel.transform.position
                                    ) <=
                                        EngagementRange +
                                        0.05f
                            )
                    );

                if (enemyTooClose)
                    continue;

                bool occupied =
                    squads.Any(
                        squad =>
                            squad != null &&
                            squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                            JoinedModels(squad).Any(
                                other =>
                                    other != model &&
                                    HorizontalDistance(
                                        candidate,
                                        other.transform.position
                                    ) <
                                        MinimumModelSeparation(
                                            model,
                                            other
                                        )
                            )
                    );

                if (occupied)
                    continue;

                result = candidate;
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void NextPhase()
    {
        if (traditionalAttackPending)
        {
            status =
                "Finish the declared Traditional attack and click ATTACK RESOLVED before changing phase.";
            return;
        }

        if (TraditionalStateResolutionPending())
        {
            status =
                "Finish the current Traditional tabletop state prompt before changing phase.";
            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack != null)
        {
            status =
                "Finish the current dice sequence before changing phase.";
            return;
        }

        if (fightActivationStage !=
            FightActivationStage.None)
        {
            status =
                "Finish the current pile-in / melee / consolidation activation before changing phase.";
            return;
        }

        if (showRuleChoiceWindow ||
            specialMoveSquad != null ||
            specialShootUnit != null ||
            endPhaseFlowRunning)
        {
            status =
                "Resolve the current rules/reaction flow first.";
            return;
        }

        if (showStratagemReaction)
        {
            status =
                "Resolve the stratagem reaction window first.";
            return;
        }

        if (showStratagemMenu)
            showStratagemMenu = false;

        if (phase == Phase.Fight &&
            fightSequenceActive &&
            AnyEligibleFightUnits())
        {
            status =
                "Cannot leave the Fight phase: eligible units still have to fight. " +
                FightPriorityText();
            return;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            SquadController veilUnit =
                squads.FirstOrDefault(
                    squad =>
                        squad != null &&
                        squad.FactionId ==
                            activeFaction &&
                        squad.IsInReserves &&
                        squad.MustIngressFromVeil
                );

            if (veilUnit != null)
            {
                status =
                    "Cannot leave Move phase: " +
                    veilUnit.DisplayName +
                    " used Veil of Darkness and must make its ingress move now.";
                return;
            }

            SquadController badFallback =
                squads.FirstOrDefault(
                    squad =>
                        squad.IsAlive &&
                        squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        squad.FactionId ==
                            activeFaction &&
                        squad.HasFallenBack &&
                        IsEngaged(squad)
                );

            if (badFallback != null)
            {
                status =
                    "Cannot leave Move phase: " +
                    badFallback.DisplayName +
                    " started a Fall Back but is still in engagement range.";
                return;
            }

            if (!ActiveFactionIsCoherent())
            {
                SquadController bad =
                    FirstIncoherentSquad();

                status =
                    "Cannot leave Move phase: " +
                    bad.DisplayName +
                    " is out of coherency (" +
                    bad.IncoherentModelCount() +
                    " model(s) invalid).";
                return;
            }
        }

        BuildEndPhaseFlow();
        ContinueEndPhaseFlow();
    }

    private void ApplyAeldariEndOfPhaseObjectiveRules()
    {
        if (aeldariRules == null)
            return;

        foreach (string faction
            in factions)
        {
            if (!aeldariRules.DetachmentIs(
                    faction,
                    AeldariDetachment.CorsairCoterie))
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (ObjectiveController objective
                in objectives)
            {
                if (objective == null ||
                    objective.Controller(
                        squads
                    ) != faction)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                bool voidThiefPresent =
                    squads.Any(
                        unit =>
                            unit != null &&
                            !unit.IsAttachedLeader &&
                            unit.FactionId ==
                                faction &&
                            unit.IsAlive &&
                            unit.IsOnBattlefield &&
                            unit.HasKeyword(
                                "anhrathe") &&
                            objective.UnitWithinRange(
                                unit
                            )
                    );

                if (!voidThiefPresent)
                    continue;

                objective.SecureFor(
                    faction
                );

                AppendBattleLog(
                    "AELDARI",
                    "Void Thieves",
                    objective.name +
                    " remains secured by " +
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        faction
                    ) +
                    " until the opponent exceeds its Level of Control at the end of a phase."
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private void CompletePhaseTransition()
    {
        string factionPhaseSummary = "";

        ApplyAeldariEndOfPhaseObjectiveRules();

        foreach (ObjectiveController objective
            in objectives)
        {
            if (objective != null)
            {
                objective.ResolveSecuredControlAtEndOfPhase(
                    squads
                );
            }
        }

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad != null)
                squad.ClearFactionPhaseEffects();
        }

        ClearSelection();
        ClearArmedCommandReroll();
        showStratagemMenu = false;

        Phase leavingPhase =
            phase;

        if (phase == Phase.Command &&
            missionSystem != null)
        {
            factionPhaseSummary =
                missionSystem
                    .ResolveCommandScoring(
                        activeFaction,
                        round
                    );
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Command)
            phase = Phase.Move;
        else if (phase == Phase.Move)
            phase = Phase.Shoot;
        else if (phase == Phase.Shoot)
            phase = Phase.Charge;
        else if (phase == Phase.Charge)
            phase = Phase.Fight;
        else if (phase == Phase.Fight)
            phase = Phase.End;
        else
        {
            EndTurn();
            return;
        }

        if (leavingPhase == Phase.Fight)
        {
            heraldOfYnneadTarget = null;
            heraldOfYnneadFaction = "";
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            foreach (SquadController unit
                in squads)
            {
                if (unit != null &&
                    unit.FactionId ==
                        activeFaction)
                {
                    unit.SpiritMarkTarget = null;
                }
            }
        }

        CaptureObjectiveControlAtPhaseStart();
        ClearPhaseDestructionRecords();
        stratagemsUsedThisPhase.Clear();

        RaisePhaseStarted();

        status =
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " " +
            phase.ToString() +
            " phase.";

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                factionPhaseSummary))
        {
            status +=
                " | " +
                factionPhaseSummary;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Fight)
        {
            BeginFightPhaseFlow();
        }
    }

    public void ResolveDeadlyDemise(
        ModelToken destroyedModel)
    {
        if (destroyedModel == null ||
            destroyedModel.Squad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        string expression =
            UniversalRuleRegistry
                .GetDeadlyDemiseExpression(
                    destroyedModel.Squad
                );

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            expression))
        {
            return;
        }

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            QueueTraditionalRuleAlert(
                "DEADLY DEMISE",
                destroyedModel.Squad.DisplayName +
                " has Deadly Demise " +
                expression +
                ". Resolve the trigger and resulting mortal wounds manually, then apply them with WOUND EDIT / REMOVE.",
                1
            );

            return;
        }

        int trigger =
            DiceRoller.RollD6(
                "Deadly Demise: " +
                destroyedModel.Squad.DisplayName
            );

        if (trigger != 6)
            return;

        Vector3 origin =
            destroyedModel.transform.position;

        List<SquadController> affected =
            squads
                .Where(
                    squad =>
                        squad != null &&
                        squad.IsAlive &&
                        squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                        !squad.IsAttachedLeader &&
                        squad !=
                            destroyedModel.Squad
                )
                .Where(
                    squad =>
                        JoinedModels(squad)
                            .Any(
                                model =>
                                    HorizontalDistance(
                                        origin,
                                        model.transform.position
                                    ) <= 6f
                            )
                )
                .ToList();

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in affected)
        {
            int mortal =
                RollSimpleExpression(
                    expression,
                    "Deadly Demise damage: " +
                    squad.DisplayName
                );

            ApplyMortalWounds(
                squad,
                mortal,
                "Deadly Demise"
            );
        }

        status =
            destroyedModel.Squad.DisplayName +
            " triggered Deadly Demise " +
            expression +
            ".";
    }

    public int ApplyMortalWounds(
        SquadController target,
        int mortalWounds,
        string label)
    {
        if (target == null ||
            mortalWounds <= 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int remaining =
            mortalWounds;

        int lostTotal = 0;

        while (remaining > 0 &&
               target.IsAlive)
        {
            ModelToken model =
                target.GetAutomaticAllocationModel();

            if (model == null &&
                target.AttachedLeader != null)
            {
                model =
                    target.AttachedLeader
                        .GetAutomaticAllocationModel();
            }

            if (model == null)
                break;

            int one =
                UniversalRuleRegistry
                    .ApplyFeelNoPain(
                        model.Squad,
                        1,
                        label
                    );

            if (one > 0)
            {
                bool wasAlive =
                    model.IsAlive;

                int lost =
                    model.ApplyDamage(1);

                lostTotal += lost;

                if (wasAlive &&
                    !model.IsAlive)
                {
                    ResolveDeadlyDemise(
                        model
                    );
                }
            }

            remaining--;
        }

        return lostTotal;
    }

    private int RollSimpleExpression(
        string expression,
        string label)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            expression))
        {
            return 0;
        }

        string value =
            expression
                .Trim()
                .ToUpperInvariant()
                .Replace(" ", "");

        int flat;

        if (int.TryParse(
            value,
            out flat))
        {
            return Mathf.Max(
                0,
                flat
            );
        }

        int dIndex =
            value.IndexOf('D');

        if (dIndex < 0)
            return 0;

        int count = 1;

        if (dIndex > 0)
        {
            int parsedCount;

            if (int.TryParse(
                value.Substring(
                    0,
                    dIndex
                ),
                out parsedCount))
            {
                count =
                    Mathf.Max(
                        1,
                        parsedCount
                    );
            }
        }

        int modifierIndex = -1;

        for (int i = dIndex + 1;
             i < value.Length;
             i++)
        {
            if (value[i] == '+' ||
                value[i] == '-')
            {
                modifierIndex = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        string sidesText =
            modifierIndex >= 0
            ? value.Substring(
                dIndex + 1,
                modifierIndex - dIndex - 1
              )
            : value.Substring(
                dIndex + 1
              );

        int sides;

        if (!int.TryParse(
            sidesText,
            out sides))
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int result =
            DiceRoller.RollDice(
                count,
                sides,
                label
            ).Total;

        if (modifierIndex >= 0)
        {
            int modifier;

            if (int.TryParse(
                value.Substring(
                    modifierIndex
                ),
                out modifier))
            {
                result += modifier;
            }
        }

        return Mathf.Max(
            0,
            result
        );
    }

    public void AddPrimaryScore(
        string faction,
        int amount,
        int scoringRound = -1)
    {
        AddScore(
            faction,
            amount,
            scoringRound,
            true
        );
    }

    public void AddSecondaryScore(
        string faction,
        int amount,
        int scoringRound = -1)
    {
        AddScore(
            faction,
            amount,
            scoringRound,
            false
        );
    }

    public int AddPrimaryScoreCapped(
        string faction,
        int amount,
        int scoringRound)
    {
        if (amount <= 0)
            return 0;

        int roundRemaining =
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                15 -
                RoundScore(
                    faction,
                    scoringRound,
                    true
                )
            );

        int gameRemaining =
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                45 -
                TotalScoreType(
                    faction,
                    true
                )
            );

        int awarded =
            Mathf.Min(
                amount,
                roundRemaining,
                gameRemaining
            );

        AddScore(
            faction,
            awarded,
            scoringRound,
            true
        );

        return awarded;
    }

    public int AddSecondaryScoreCapped(
        string faction,
        int amount,
        int scoringRound,
        string fixedCard)
    {
        if (amount <= 0)
            return 0;

        int roundRemaining =
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                15 -
                RoundScore(
                    faction,
                    scoringRound,
                    false
                )
            );

        int gameRemaining =
            Mathf.Max(
                0,
                45 -
                TotalScoreType(
                    faction,
                    false
                )
            );

        int fixedRemaining =
            amount;

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                fixedCard))
        {
            if (!fixedSecondaryScoreByCard
                .ContainsKey(
                    faction))
            {
                fixedSecondaryScoreByCard[
                    faction] =
                    new Dictionary<
                        string,
                        int
                    >();
            }

            int current = 0;

            fixedSecondaryScoreByCard[
                faction]
                .TryGetValue(
                    fixedCard,
                    out current
                );

            fixedRemaining =
                Mathf.Max(
                    0,
                    20 - current
                );
        }

        int awarded =
            Mathf.Min(
                amount,
                roundRemaining,
                gameRemaining,
                fixedRemaining
            );

        AddScore(
            faction,
            awarded,
            scoringRound,
            false
        );

        if (awarded > 0 &&
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                fixedCard))
        {
            Dictionary<string, int> cards =
                fixedSecondaryScoreByCard[
                    faction];

            if (!cards.ContainsKey(
                fixedCard))
            {
                cards[fixedCard] = 0;
            }

            cards[fixedCard] +=
                awarded;
        }

        return awarded;
    }

    private void AddScore(
        string faction,
        int amount,
        int scoringRound,
        bool primary)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                faction) ||
            amount <= 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (scoringRound < 1)
        {
            scoringRound =
                Mathf.Max(
                    1,
                    round
                );
        }

        Dictionary<
            string,
            Dictionary<int, int>
        > source =
            primary
            ? primaryScoreByRound
            : secondaryScoreByRound;

        if (!source.ContainsKey(
            faction))
        {
            source[faction] =
                new Dictionary<int, int>();
        }

        Dictionary<int, int> rounds =
            source[faction];

        if (!rounds.ContainsKey(
            scoringRound))
        {
            rounds[scoringRound] = 0;
        }

        rounds[scoringRound] +=
            amount;

        if (!score.ContainsKey(
            faction))
        {
            score[faction] = 0;
        }

        score[faction] +=
            amount;
    }

    private int RoundScore(
        string faction,
        int scoringRound,
        bool primary)
    {
        Dictionary<
            string,
            Dictionary<int, int>
        > source =
            primary
            ? primaryScoreByRound
            : secondaryScoreByRound;

        Dictionary<int, int> rounds;

        if (!source.TryGetValue(
                faction,
                out rounds) ||
            rounds == null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        int value;

        return rounds.TryGetValue(
            scoringRound,
            out value)
            ? value
            : 0;
    }

    private int TotalScoreType(
        string faction,
        bool primary)
    {
        Dictionary<
            string,
            Dictionary<int, int>
        > source =
            primary
            ? primaryScoreByRound
            : secondaryScoreByRound;

        Dictionary<int, int> rounds;

        if (!source.TryGetValue(
                faction,
                out rounds) ||
            rounds == null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        return rounds.Values.Sum();
    }

    private void EndTurn()
    {
        string missionScore = "";
        string secondaryScore = "";

        if (missionSystem != null)
        {
            missionScore =
                missionSystem
                    .ResolveEndTurnScoring(
                        activeFaction,
                        round
                    );

            secondaryScore =
                missionSystem
                    .ResolveAutomaticSecondaryScoring(
                        activeFaction,
                        round
                    );

            missionSystem.EndTurnSnapshot();
        }

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad != null &&
                squad.FactionId ==
                    activeFaction)
            {
                squad.ClearFactionTurnEffects();
            }
        }

        RefreshObjectiveDisplays();

        turnsCompletedThisRound++;

        if (turnsCompletedThisRound >=
            factions.Count)
        {
            turnsCompletedThisRound = 0;

            if (round >= 5)
            {
                FinishBattle(
                    missionScore
                );

                return;
            }

            if (round == 3)
            {
                foreach (SquadController reserve
                    in squads)
                {
                    if (reserve != null &&
                        reserve.IsAlive &&
                        reserve.IsInReserves &&
                        !reserve.IsRepositionedReserve)
                    {
                        reserve
                            .DestroyReserveWithoutTriggers();
                    }
                }
            }

            round++;

            activeFactionIndex =
                firstTurnFactionIndex;

            StartFactionBattleRoundRules();
        }
        else
        {
            activeFactionIndex =
                (activeFactionIndex + 1) %
                factions.Count;
        }

        activeFaction =
            factions[
                activeFactionIndex
            ];

        BeginActiveTurn(
            false
        );

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                missionScore))
        {
            status =
                missionScore +
                " | " +
                status;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                secondaryScore))
        {
            status =
                secondaryScore +
                " | " +
                status;
        }
    }

    private void FinishBattle(
        string finalTurnScore)
    {
        List<string> summaries =
            new List<string>();

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                finalTurnScore))
        {
            summaries.Add(
                finalTurnScore
            );
        }

        if (missionSystem != null)
        {
            foreach (string faction
                in factions)
            {
                string endScore =
                    missionSystem
                        .ResolveEndBattleScoring(
                            faction
                        );

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    endScore))
                {
                    summaries.Add(
                        DisplayFactionName(
                            faction
                        ) +
                        ": " +
                        endScore
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        battleOver = true;
        deploymentMode = false;
        showMissionPanel = false;

        string winner = "";

        if (factions.Count >= 2)
        {
            int p1 =
                score.ContainsKey(
                    factions[0])
                ? score[factions[0]]
                : 0;

            int p2 =
                score.ContainsKey(
                    factions[1])
                ? score[factions[1]]
                : 0;

            if (p1 == p2)
            {
                winner =
                    "DRAW — " +
                    p1 +
                    " VP each";
            }
            else
            {
                string winningFaction =
                    p1 > p2
                    ? factions[0]
                    : factions[1];

                winner =
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        winningFaction
                    ) +
                    " wins " +
                    Mathf.Max(p1, p2) +
                    "–" +
                    Mathf.Min(p1, p2) +
                    " VP";
            }
        }

        battleSummary =
            winner +
            "\n\n" +
            string.Join(
                "\n",
                factions.Select(
                    faction =>
                        DisplayFactionName(
                            faction
                        ) +
                        " — Primary " +
                        TotalScoreType(
                            faction,
                            true
                        ) +
                        " / Secondary " +
                        TotalScoreType(
                            faction,
                            false
                        ) +
                        " / Total " +
                        (score.ContainsKey(
                            faction)
                            ? score[faction]
                            : 0)
                ).ToArray()
            ) +
            (summaries.Count > 0
                ? "\n\n" +
                  string.Join(
                      "\n",
                      summaries.ToArray()
                  )
                : "");

        status =
            winner;
    }


    private void HandleCamera()
    {
        if (gameCamera == null)
            return;

        float speed =
            12f *
            Time.deltaTime;

        Vector3 move =
            Vector3.zero;

        if (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.W))
            move += Vector3.forward;

        if (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.S))
            move += Vector3.back;

        if (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.A))
            move += Vector3.left;

        if (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.D))
            move += Vector3.right;

        gameCamera.transform.position +=
            move.normalized *
            speed;

        float scroll =
            Input.mouseScrollDelta.y;

        if (Mathf.Abs(scroll) > 0.01f)
        {
            Vector3 p =
                gameCamera.transform.position;

            p.y =
                Mathf.Clamp(
                    p.y -
                    scroll * 2f,
                    8f,
                    Mathf.Max(
                        30f,
                        BoardDepth *
                            1.10f
                    )
                );

            gameCamera.transform.position = p;
        }
    }

    private string CommandPointText()
    {
        return string.Join(
            "    ",
            factions.Select(
                faction =>
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        faction
                    ) +
                    ": " +
                    GetCommandPoints(
                        faction
                    ) +
                    " CP"
            ).ToArray()
        );
    }

    private string DisplayFactionName(
        string faction)
    {
        if (faction == "Player 1")
            return playerOneRosterLabel;

        if (faction == "Player 2")
            return playerTwoRosterLabel;

        return faction;
    }

    private string ScoreText()
    {
        return string.Join(
            "    ",
            factions.Select(
                faction =>
                    DisplayFactionName(
                        faction
                    ) +
                    ": " +
                    (score.ContainsKey(
                        faction)
                        ? score[faction]
                        : 0) +
                    " VP"
            ).ToArray()
        );
    }

    private string BattleShockText()
    {
        int shocked =
            squads.Count(
                squad =>
                    squad != null &&
                    squad.IsAlive &&
                    squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                    !squad.IsAttachedLeader &&
                    squad.FactionId ==
                        activeFaction &&
                    squad
                        .JoinedActionController()
                        .IsBattleShocked
            );

        return shocked == 0
            ? "Battle-shock: none"
            : "Battle-shock: " +
              shocked +
              " unit(s)";
    }

    private string CoherencyText()
    {
        int bad =
            squads.Count(
                squad =>
                    squad.IsAlive &&
                    squad.IsOnBattlefield &&
                    squad.FactionId == activeFaction &&
                    !squad.IsCoherent()
            );

        return bad == 0
            ? "Coherency: OK"
            : "Coherency: " +
              bad +
              " squad(s) invalid";
    }

    private void OpenDatasheetForSelection()
    {
        if (selectedModel != null &&
            selectedModel.Squad != null)
        {
            datasheetSquad =
                selectedModel.Squad;
            datasheetModel =
                selectedModel;
        }
        else
        {
            datasheetSquad =
                selectedSquad;
            datasheetModel = null;
        }

        if (datasheetSquad == null)
            return;

        showDatasheet = true;
        datasheetScroll = Vector2.zero;
    }

    private void CloseDatasheet()
    {
        showDatasheet = false;
        datasheetSquad = null;
        datasheetModel = null;
        datasheetScroll = Vector2.zero;
    }

    private void DrawFloatingDatasheetButton()
    {
        if (showDatasheet ||
            selectedSquad == null ||
            deploymentMode ||
            armyImportMode ||
            gameCamera == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        ModelToken anchorModel =
            selectedModel;

        if (anchorModel == null ||
            !anchorModel.IsAlive)
        {
            anchorModel =
                JoinedModels(
                    selectedSquad
                )
                .FirstOrDefault();
        }

        if (anchorModel == null)
            return;

        Vector3 world =
            anchorModel.transform.position +
            Vector3.up * 1.75f;

        Vector3 screen =
            gameCamera.WorldToScreenPoint(
                world
            );

        if (screen.z <= 0f)
            return;

        float x =
            screen.x - 55f;

        float y =
            Screen.height -
            screen.y -
            17f;

        Rect buttonRect =
            new Rect(
                x,
                y,
                110f,
                30f
            );

        if (GUI.Button(
            buttonRect,
            "DATASHEET"))
        {
            OpenDatasheetForSelection();
        }
    }

    private void DrawDatasheetPanel()
    {
        if (!showDatasheet ||
            datasheetSquad == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        SquadController squad =
            datasheetSquad;

        UnitData data =
            squad.SourceData;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                620f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                760f,
                Screen.height - 40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                Screen.width -
                    width -
                    20f,
                20f,
                width,
                height
            );

        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle titleStyle =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        titleStyle.fontSize = 22;
        titleStyle.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle headingStyle =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        headingStyle.fontSize = 15;
        headingStyle.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrapStyle =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrapStyle.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                panel.width - 90f,
                30f
            ),
            squad.DisplayName,
            titleStyle
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    55f,
                panel.y + 10f,
                35f,
                30f
            ),
            "X"))
        {
            CloseDatasheet();
            return;
        }

        Rect scrollOuter =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 14f,
                panel.y + 50f,
                panel.width - 28f,
                panel.height - 64f
            );

        Rect scrollInner =
            new Rect(
                0f,
                0f,
                scrollOuter.width - 20f,
                DatasheetContentHeight(
                    squad,
                    data
                )
            );

        datasheetScroll =
            GUI.BeginScrollView(
                scrollOuter,
                datasheetScroll,
                scrollInner
            );

        float y = 0f;

        if (datasheetModel != null)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    scrollInner.width,
                    24f
                ),
                "SELECTED MODEL",
                headingStyle
            );

            y += 24f;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    scrollInner.width,
                    42f
                ),
                datasheetModel.RoleName +
                " | " +
                datasheetModel.CurrentWounds +
                "/" +
                datasheetModel.MaxWounds +
                " W remaining\n" +
                datasheetModel.EquipmentText(),
                wrapStyle
            );

            y += 52f;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            "CHARACTERISTICS",
            headingStyle
        );

        y += 26f;

        string statLine =
            "M " +
            squad.GetMove()
                .ToString("0.#") +
            "\"     T " +
            squad.Toughness +
            "     Sv " +
            squad.BaseSave +
            "+     W " +
            squad.WoundsPerModel +
            "     Ld " +
            squad.BestLeadership() +
            "+     InSv " +
            (squad.BaseInvulnerableSave > 0
                ? squad.BaseInvulnerableSave +
                  "+"
                : "-") +
            "     FNP " +
            (UniversalRuleRegistry
                .GetFeelNoPain(squad) > 0
                ? UniversalRuleRegistry
                    .GetFeelNoPain(squad) +
                  "+"
                : "-") +
            "     OC " +
            (squad
                .JoinedActionController()
                .IsBattleShocked
                ? "-"
                : squad.BaseObjectiveControl
                    .ToString()) +
            "     Models " +
            squad.LivingModels +
            "/" +
            squad.StartingModels;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            statLine
        );

        y += 30f;

        string extras =
            squad.LeaderSummary() +
            " | " +
            (squad.HasDeepStrike
                ? "Deep Strike"
                : "No Deep Strike") +
            " | " +
            (squad
                .JoinedActionController()
                .IsBattleShocked
                ? "BATTLE-SHOCKED"
                : "Steady") +
            (squad.IsAtOrBelowHalfStrength()
                ? " | At/below Half-strength"
                : "");

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            extras,
            wrapStyle
        );

        y += 34f;

        if (data != null &&
            data.keywords != null &&
            data.keywords.Length > 0)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    scrollInner.width,
                    40f
                ),
                "Keywords: " +
                string.Join(
                    ", ",
                    data.keywords
                ),
                wrapStyle
            );

            y += 48f;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            "RANGED WEAPONS",
            headingStyle
        );

        y += 26f;

        y =
            DrawDatasheetWeaponSection(
                squad,
                true,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                wrapStyle
            );

        y += 8f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            "MELEE WEAPONS",
            headingStyle
        );

        y += 26f;

        y =
            DrawDatasheetWeaponSection(
                squad,
                false,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                wrapStyle
            );

        y += 10f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                scrollInner.width,
                24f
            ),
            "ABILITIES / RULES",
            headingStyle
        );

        y += 26f;

        DatasheetRuleData[] rules =
            data != null
            ? data.datasheetRules
            : null;

        if (rules != null &&
            rules.Length > 0)
        {
            foreach (DatasheetRuleData rule
                in rules)
            {
                if (rule == null)
                    continue;

                string name =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        rule.name
                    )
                    ? "Rule"
                    : rule.name;

                string text =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        rule.text
                    )
                    ? "(No description supplied by roster source.)"
                    : rule.text;

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        0f,
                        y,
                        scrollInner.width,
                        22f
                    ),
                    name,
                    headingStyle
                );

                y += 22f;

                float textHeight =
                    Mathf.Max(
                        38f,
                        wrapStyle.CalcHeight(
                            new GUIContent(text),
                            scrollInner.width
                        ) +
                        4f
                    );

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        0f,
                        y,
                        scrollInner.width,
                        textHeight
                    ),
                    text,
                    wrapStyle
                );

                y +=
                    textHeight +
                    8f;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            string fallback =
                squad.AbilityText();

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    scrollInner.width,
                    42f
                ),
                fallback,
                wrapStyle
            );

            y += 48f;
        }

        GUI.EndScrollView();
    }

    private float DrawDatasheetWeaponSection(
        SquadController squad,
        bool ranged,
        float y,
        float width,
        GUIStyle wrapStyle)
    {
        List<WeaponData> weapons =
            JoinedModels(squad)
                .SelectMany(
                    model =>
                        ranged
                        ? model.RangedWeapons
                        : model.MeleeWeapons
                )
                .Where(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon != null
                )
                .GroupBy(
                    weapon =>
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            weapon.id
                        )
                        ? weapon.displayName
                        : weapon.id
                )
                .Select(
                    group =>
                        group.First()
                )
                .ToList();

        if (weapons.Count == 0)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    width,
                    24f
                ),
                "None"
            );

            return y + 28f;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                width,
                22f
            ),
            "Weapon                         Range    A      Skill   S    AP    D"
        );

        y += 22f;

        foreach (WeaponData weapon
            in weapons)
        {
            string rangeText =
                ranged
                ? weapon.range
                    .ToString("0.#") +
                  "\""
                : "Melee";

            string attacks =
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    weapon.attacksExpression
                )
                ? weapon.attacksExpression
                : weapon.attacksPerModel
                    .ToString();

            string damage =
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    weapon.damageExpression
                )
                ? weapon.damageExpression
                : weapon.damage
                    .ToString();

            string row =
                weapon.displayName +
                "    | " +
                rangeText +
                " | " +
                attacks +
                " | " +
                weapon.skill +
                "+ | " +
                weapon.strength +
                " | " +
                weapon.ap +
                " | " +
                damage;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    width,
                    24f
                ),
                row,
                wrapStyle
            );

            y += 24f;

            string keywordText =
                weapon.keywords != null &&
                weapon.keywords.Length > 0
                ? string.Join(
                    ", ",
                    weapon.keywords
                  )
                : "";

            string raw =
                weapon.rawAbilities ?? "";

            string rules =
                string.Join(
                    " | ",
                    new[]
                    {
                        keywordText,
                        raw
                    }
                    .Where(
                        value =>
                            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                value
                            )
                    )
                    .Distinct()
                    .ToArray()
                );

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                rules))
            {
                float ruleHeight =
                    Mathf.Max(
                        22f,
                        wrapStyle.CalcHeight(
                            new GUIContent(rules),
                            width - 12f
                        )
                    );

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        12f,
                        y,
                        width - 12f,
                        ruleHeight
                    ),
                    rules,
                    wrapStyle
                );

                y +=
                    ruleHeight +
                    4f;
            }
        }

        return y;
    }

    private float DatasheetContentHeight(
        SquadController squad,
        UnitData data)
    {
        int rangedCount =
            JoinedModels(squad)
                .SelectMany(
                    model =>
                        model.RangedWeapons
                )
                .Where(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon != null
                )
                .Select(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon.id ??
                        weapon.displayName
                )
                .Distinct()
                .Count();

        int meleeCount =
            JoinedModels(squad)
                .SelectMany(
                    model =>
                        model.MeleeWeapons
                )
                .Where(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon != null
                )
                .Select(
                    weapon =>
                        weapon.id ??
                        weapon.displayName
                )
                .Distinct()
                .Count();

        int rules =
            data != null &&
            data.datasheetRules != null
            ? data.datasheetRules.Length
            : 0;

        return
            430f +
            (rangedCount + meleeCount) *
                58f +
            rules *
                95f;
    }

    public int WorldUiFactionCount
    {
        get { return factions.Count; }
    }

    public string WorldUiFactionId(
        int index)
    {
        if (index < 0 ||
            index >= factions.Count)
        {
            return "";
        }

        return factions[index];
    }

    public string WorldUiFactionName(
        int index)
    {
        string faction =
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            );

        return
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                faction)
            ? "PLAYER " +
              (index + 1)
            : DisplayFactionName(
                faction
              );
    }

    public int WorldUiCommandPoints(
        int index)
    {
        return GetCommandPoints(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            )
        );
    }

    public int WorldUiRoundPrimary(
        int index,
        int scoringRound)
    {
        return RoundScore(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            ),
            scoringRound,
            true
        );
    }

    public int WorldUiRoundSecondary(
        int index,
        int scoringRound)
    {
        return RoundScore(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            ),
            scoringRound,
            false
        );
    }

    public int WorldUiPrimaryTotal(
        int index)
    {
        return TotalScoreType(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            ),
            true
        );
    }

    public int WorldUiSecondaryTotal(
        int index)
    {
        return TotalScoreType(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            ),
            false
        );
    }

    public string[] WorldUiReserveEntries(
        int index)
    {
        return ReserveBoxEntries(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            )
        ).ToArray();
    }

    public string[] WorldUiDeadEntries(
        int index)
    {
        return DeadBoxEntries(
            WorldUiFactionId(
                index
            )
        ).ToArray();
    }

    private List<string> ReserveBoxEntries(
        string faction)
    {
        List<string> entries =
            new List<string>();

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null ||
                squad.FactionId !=
                    faction ||
                !squad.IsAlive ||
                !squad.IsInReserves ||
                squad.IsAttachedLeader)
            {
                continue;
            }

            string entry =
                squad.DisplayName +
                " [" +
                squad.LivingModels +
                "/" +
                squad.StartingModels +
                "]";

            if (squad.AttachedLeader != null &&
                squad.AttachedLeader.IsAlive &&
                squad.AttachedLeader.IsInReserves)
            {
                entry +=
                    " + " +
                    squad.AttachedLeader.DisplayName;
            }

            entries.Add(entry);
        }

        return entries;
    }

    private List<string> DeadBoxEntries(
        string faction)
    {
        List<string> entries =
            new List<string>();

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in squads)
        {
            if (squad == null ||
                squad.FactionId !=
                    faction)
            {
                continue;
            }

            int casualties =
                Mathf.Max(
                    0,
                    squad.StartingModels -
                    squad.LivingModels
                );

            if (casualties <= 0)
                continue;

            entries.Add(
                squad.DisplayName +
                " — " +
                casualties +
                " dead" +
                (!squad.IsAlive
                    ? " [DESTROYED]"
                    : "")
            );
        }

        return entries;
    }

    private void DrawBattlefieldSidePanels(
        float topY)
    {
        if (factions.Count < 2)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                255f,
                Screen.width * 0.19f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                300f,
                Screen.height -
                    topY -
                    20f
            );

        if (height < 120f)
            return;

        DrawTeamStatusPanel(
            factions[0],
            new Rect(
                15f,
                topY,
                width,
                height
            ),
            ref playerOneSidePanelScroll
        );

        DrawTeamStatusPanel(
            factions[1],
            new Rect(
                Screen.width -
                    width -
                    15f,
                topY,
                width,
                height
            ),
            ref playerTwoSidePanelScroll
        );
    }

    private void DrawTeamStatusPanel(
        string faction,
        Rect panel,
        ref Vector2 scroll)
    {
        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 16;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                panel.y + 8f,
                panel.width - 24f,
                24f
            ),
            DisplayFactionName(
                faction
            ),
            title
        );

        List<string> reserves =
            ReserveBoxEntries(
                faction
            );

        List<string> dead =
            DeadBoxEntries(
                faction
            );

        float contentHeight =
            54f +
            Mathf.Max(
                1,
                reserves.Count
            ) *
            23f +
            38f +
            Mathf.Max(
                1,
                dead.Count
            ) *
            23f;

        Rect outer =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f,
                panel.y + 38f,
                panel.width - 20f,
                panel.height - 48f
            );

        scroll =
            GUI.BeginScrollView(
                outer,
                scroll,
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    0f,
                    outer.width - 20f,
                    contentHeight
                )
            );

        float y = 0f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                outer.width - 24f,
                22f
            ),
            "IN RESERVES",
            title
        );

        y += 26f;

        if (reserves.Count == 0)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    8f,
                    y,
                    outer.width - 32f,
                    22f
                ),
                "—"
            );

            y += 23f;
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (string entry
                in reserves)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        8f,
                        y,
                        outer.width - 32f,
                        22f
                    ),
                    entry
                );

                y += 23f;
            }
        }

        y += 8f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                0f,
                y,
                outer.width - 24f,
                22f
            ),
            "DEAD",
            title
        );

        y += 26f;

        if (dead.Count == 0)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    8f,
                    y,
                    outer.width - 32f,
                    22f
                ),
                "—"
            );
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (string entry
                in dead)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        8f,
                        y,
                        outer.width - 32f,
                        22f
                    ),
                    entry
                );

                y += 23f;
            }
        }

        GUI.EndScrollView();
    }

    private void DrawMatchScoreboard()
    {
        if (deploymentMode ||
            armyImportMode ||
            factions.Count < 2)
        {
            return;
        }

        const int roundsShown = 5;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                460f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height = 250f;

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                Screen.width -
                    width -
                    15f,
                15f,
                width,
                height
            );

        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 17;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle centered =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        centered.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;

        centered.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        string p1 =
            factions[0];

        string p2 =
            factions[1];

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                panel.y + 8f,
                panel.width - 24f,
                24f
            ),
            "MATCH SCOREBOARD",
            title
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                panel.y + 34f,
                panel.width - 24f,
                24f
            ),
            DisplayFactionName(p1) +
            ": " +
            GetCommandPoints(p1) +
            " CP     |     " +
            DisplayFactionName(p2) +
            ": " +
            GetCommandPoints(p2) +
            " CP",
            centered
        );

        float tableY =
            panel.y + 64f;

        float roundWidth = 56f;

        float playerWidth =
            (panel.width -
             roundWidth -
             30f) /
            2f;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f,
                tableY,
                roundWidth,
                22f
            ),
            "ROUND",
            centered
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f +
                    roundWidth,
                tableY,
                playerWidth,
                22f
            ),
            DisplayFactionName(p1) +
            "  P / S",
            centered
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f +
                    roundWidth +
                    playerWidth,
                tableY,
                playerWidth,
                22f
            ),
            DisplayFactionName(p2) +
            "  P / S",
            centered
        );

        tableY += 24f;

        for (int scoringRound = 1;
             scoringRound <=
                roundsShown;
             scoringRound++)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 10f,
                    tableY,
                    roundWidth,
                    21f
                ),
                "R" +
                scoringRound,
                centered
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 10f +
                        roundWidth,
                    tableY,
                    playerWidth,
                    21f
                ),
                RoundScore(
                    p1,
                    scoringRound,
                    true
                ) +
                " / " +
                RoundScore(
                    p1,
                    scoringRound,
                    false
                ),
                centered
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 10f +
                        roundWidth +
                        playerWidth,
                    tableY,
                    playerWidth,
                    21f
                ),
                RoundScore(
                    p2,
                    scoringRound,
                    true
                ) +
                " / " +
                RoundScore(
                    p2,
                    scoringRound,
                    false
                ),
                centered
            );

            tableY += 22f;
        }

        int p1Primary =
            TotalScoreType(
                p1,
                true
            );

        int p1Secondary =
            TotalScoreType(
                p1,
                false
            );

        int p2Primary =
            TotalScoreType(
                p2,
                true
            );

        int p2Secondary =
            TotalScoreType(
                p2,
                false
            );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f,
                panel.y + height - 46f,
                panel.width - 20f,
                22f
            ),
            "TOTAL    " +
            DisplayFactionName(p1) +
            ": P " +
            p1Primary +
            " + S " +
            p1Secondary +
            " = " +
            (p1Primary +
             p1Secondary) +
            " VP     |     " +
            DisplayFactionName(p2) +
            ": P " +
            p2Primary +
            " + S " +
            p2Secondary +
            " = " +
            (p2Primary +
             p2Secondary) +
            " VP",
            centered
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 10f,
                panel.y + height - 24f,
                panel.width - 20f,
                18f
            ),
            "P = Primary   |   S = Secondary",
            centered
        );
    }

    private void DrawRuleChoiceWindow()
    {
        if (!showRuleChoiceWindow)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                680f,
                Screen.width - 60f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                560f,
                Screen.height - 60f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                (Screen.height - height) *
                    0.5f,
                width,
                height
            );

        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 21;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrap =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrap.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 16f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                30f
            ),
            ruleChoiceTitle,
            title
        );

        float descHeight =
            Mathf.Max(
                48f,
                wrap.CalcHeight(
                    new GUIContent(
                        ruleChoiceDescription
                    ),
                    panel.width - 40f
                )
            );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 54f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                descHeight
            ),
            ruleChoiceDescription,
            wrap
        );

        float listY =
            panel.y +
            64f +
            descHeight;

        Rect outer =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                listY,
                panel.width - 40f,
                panel.y +
                    panel.height -
                    20f -
                    listY
            );

        float contentHeight =
            Mathf.Max(
                outer.height - 2f,
                ruleChoiceOptions.Count *
                    48f
            );

        ruleChoiceScroll =
            GUI.BeginScrollView(
                outer,
                ruleChoiceScroll,
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    0f,
                    outer.width - 20f,
                    contentHeight
                )
            );

        float y = 0f;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < ruleChoiceOptions.Count;
             i++)
        {
            RuleChoiceOption option =
                ruleChoiceOptions[i];

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    outer.width - 26f,
                    40f
                ),
                option.Label))
            {
                System.Action action =
                    option.Action;

                if (action != null)
                    action();

                break;
            }

            y += 48f;
        }

        GUI.EndScrollView();
    }

    private void DrawCasualtySelectionWindow()
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null ||
            !interactiveAttack
                .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            return;
        }

        InteractiveCasualtyChoice choice =
