            interactiveAttack
                .CurrentPendingCasualty;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                650f,
                Screen.width - 50f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                120f,
                width,
                255f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.042f,
                0.98f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 20;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrap =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrap.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 14f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                28f
            ),
            "SELECT CASUALTY",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 52f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                72f
            ),
            "The attack destroyed a model. Click the physical model you want to remove. Warboard warns before a choice breaks Unit Coherency. Automatic suggestion: " +
            (choice != null
                ? choice.Label
                : "none") +
            ".",
            wrap
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 138f,
                220f,
                38f
            ),
            "KEEP AUTOMATIC CASUALTY"))
        {
            ConfirmAttackCasualty(
                null
            );
        }

        if (pendingCasualtyCoherencyWarning &&
            pendingCasualtyCandidate != null)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 18f,
                    panel.y + 184f,
                    panel.width - 270f,
                    52f
                ),
                "WARNING: removing " +
                pendingCasualtyCandidate.RoleName +
                " breaks coherency. If confirmed, Warboard removes out-of-coherency models after casualty selection.",
                wrap
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x +
                        panel.width -
                        230f,
                    panel.y + 188f,
                    210f,
                    38f
                ),
                "REMOVE ANYWAY"))
            {
                ConfirmAttackCasualty(
                    pendingCasualtyCandidate
                );
            }
        }
        else
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 258f,
                    panel.y + 142f,
                    panel.width - 278f,
                    50f
                ),
                "Click a model on the battlefield to replace the automatic casualty.",
                wrap
            );
        }
    }

    private void DrawXcomAttackDecisionWindow()
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                690f,
                Screen.width - 50f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                120f,
                width,
                280f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.042f,
                0.98f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 20;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 14f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                28f
            ),
            "XCOM RESOLUTION — DECISION REQUIRED",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 52f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                52f
            ),
            interactiveAttack.Attacker.DisplayName +
            " → " +
            interactiveAttack.Target.DisplayName +
            "\n" +
            interactiveAttack.RequirementText
        );

        float x =
            panel.x + 18f;

        if (interactiveAttack
            .CanUsePartingTheVeil)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    panel.y + 126f,
                    210f,
                    40f
                ),
                "USE PARTING THE VEIL"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .UsePartingTheVeil();

                ResumeXcomAttack();
            }

            x += 220f;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack
            .CanUseMacabreResilience)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    panel.y + 126f,
                    220f,
                    40f
                ),
                "USE MACABRE RESILIENCE"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .UseMacabreResilience();

                ResumeXcomAttack();
            }

            x += 230f;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack
            .CanCommandReroll)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 18f,
                    panel.y + 178f,
                    260f,
                    40f
                ),
                "COMMAND RE-ROLL (1 CP)"))
            {
                if (interactiveAttack
                    .UseCommandReroll())
                {
                    interactiveAttack.Continue();
                    ResumeXcomAttack();
                }
            }
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    280f,
                panel.y + 178f,
                260f,
                40f
            ),
            "DECLINE / CONTINUE AUTO"))
        {
            bool complete =
                interactiveAttack
                    .DeclineDecisionAndFastResolve();

            if (complete)
                FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 232f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                30f
            ),
            "Routine dice stay hidden. Warboard only stops for a real player decision."
        );
    }

    private void DrawInteractiveAttackWindow()
    {
        if (interactiveAttack == null)
            return;

        if (IsXcomMode)
        {
            DrawXcomAttackDecisionWindow();
            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack.IsComplete)
        {
            FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            return;
        }

        InteractiveWeaponVolley volley =
            interactiveAttack.CurrentVolley;

        if (volley == null)
        {
            FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            return;
        }

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                760f,
                Screen.width - 60f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                610f,
                Screen.height - 60f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                (Screen.height - height) *
                    0.5f,
                width,
                height
            );

        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 22;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 16;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 16f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                30f
            ),
            interactiveAttack.StageTitle +
            " — " +
            volley.weapon.displayName,
            title
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 52f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                24f
            ),
            interactiveAttack.Attacker.DisplayName +
            " → " +
            interactiveAttack.Target.DisplayName +
            " | Weapon " +
            interactiveAttack.VolleyNumber +
            "/" +
            interactiveAttack.VolleyCount
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + 82f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                42f
            ),
            interactiveAttack.RequirementText,
            heading
        );

        IReadOnlyList<int> dice =
            interactiveAttack.CurrentDice;

        if (dice.Count > 0)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 22f,
                    panel.y + 126f,
                    150f,
                    22f
                ),
                "Rolled dice:"
            );

            int columns = 10;

            for (int i = 0;
                 i < dice.Count &&
                 i < 40;
                 i++)
            {
                int col =
                    i % columns;

                int row =
                    i / columns;

                DrawDieFace(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x +
                            22f +
                            col * 54f,
                        panel.y +
                            154f +
                            row * 54f,
                        46f,
                        46f
                    ),
                    dice[i]
                );
            }

            if (dice.Count > 40)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 22f,
                        panel.y + 376f,
                        240f,
                        22f
                    ),
                    "+" +
                    (dice.Count - 40) +
                    " additional dice"
                );
            }
        }
        else
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 22f,
                    panel.y + 154f,
                    panel.width - 44f,
                    30f
                ),
                "Press the roll button to roll this stage."
            );
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + height - 174f,
                panel.width - 44f,
                44f
            ),
            interactiveAttack.LastActionText
        );

        Rect mainButton =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + height - 118f,
                230f,
                44f
            );

        InteractiveAttackStage stage =
            interactiveAttack.Stage;

        if (stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.RollHits ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.RollWounds ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.RollSaves ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.RollDamage)
        {
            string label =
                stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.RollHits
                ? "ROLL HIT DICE"
                : stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.RollWounds
                ? "ROLL WOUND DICE"
                : stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.RollSaves
                ? "ROLL SAVES"
                : "ROLL DAMAGE";

            if (GUI.Button(
                mainButton,
                label))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .RollCurrentStage();
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 272f,
                    panel.y + height - 118f,
                    200f,
                    44f
                ),
                "ROLL + NEXT"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .RollAndAdvanceIfNoDecision();

                if (interactiveAttack.IsComplete)
                    FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            }
        }
        else if (stage ==
            InteractiveAttackStage.ApplyDamage)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                mainButton,
                "APPLY DAMAGE"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .RollCurrentStage();
            }
        }
        else
        {
            string label =
                stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.WeaponComplete
                ? (interactiveAttack.VolleyNumber <
                    interactiveAttack.VolleyCount
                    ? "NEXT WEAPON"
                    : "FINISH ATTACK")
                : stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewHits
                ? "CONTINUE TO WOUNDS"
                : stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewWounds
                ? "CONTINUE TO SAVES"
                : stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewSaves
                ? "CONTINUE TO DAMAGE"
                : "APPLY RESULTS";

            if (GUI.Button(
                mainButton,
                label))
            {
                interactiveAttack.Continue();

                if (interactiveAttack.IsComplete)
                    FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            }
        }

        bool reviewStage =
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewHits ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewWounds ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewSaves ||
            stage ==
                InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewDamage;

        if (reviewStage)
        {
            GUI.enabled =
                interactiveAttack
                    .CanCommandReroll;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 272f,
                    panel.y + height - 118f,
                    255f,
                    44f
                ),
                interactiveAttack
                    .CommandRerollButtonText))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .UseCommandReroll();
            }

            GUI.enabled = true;

            string rerollFaction =
                stage ==
                    InteractiveAttackStage.ReviewSaves
                ? interactiveAttack
                    .Target
                    .FactionId
                : interactiveAttack
                    .Attacker
                    .FactionId;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 540f,
                    panel.y + height - 113f,
                    panel.width - 562f,
                    36f
                ),
                gameObject != null
                ? GetCommandPoints(
                    rerollFaction
                  ) +
                  " CP"
                : ""
            );
        }

        if (interactiveAttack
            .CanUsePartingTheVeil)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 272f,
                    panel.y + height - 118f,
                    220f,
                    44f
                ),
                "PARTING THE VEIL (2 CP)"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .UsePartingTheVeil();
            }
        }

        if (interactiveAttack
            .CanUseMacabreResilience)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x +
                        (interactiveAttack.CanUsePartingTheVeil
                            ? 500f
                            : 272f),
                    panel.y + height - 118f,
                    interactiveAttack.CanUsePartingTheVeil
                        ? 220f
                        : 255f,
                    44f
                ),
                "MACABRE RESILIENCE (1 CP)"))
            {
                interactiveAttack
                    .UseMacabreResilience();
            }
        }

        bool decisionAvailable =
            interactiveAttack
                .HasMeaningfulDecision;

        string fastLabel =
            (interactiveAttack
                .CanUsePartingTheVeil ||
             interactiveAttack
                .CanUseMacabreResilience)
            ? "DECLINE REACTION + FAST"
            : decisionAvailable
                ? "DECLINE RE-ROLL + FAST"
                : "FAST RESOLVE TO DECISION";

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 22f,
                panel.y + height - 60f,
                300f,
                38f
            ),
            fastLabel))
        {
            bool completed =
                decisionAvailable
                ? interactiveAttack
                    .DeclineDecisionAndFastResolve()
                : interactiveAttack
                    .FastResolveUntilDecision();

            if (completed)
            {
                FinalizeInteractiveAttack();
            }
            else if (interactiveAttack != null &&
                     interactiveAttack
                        .HasMeaningfulDecision)
            {
                status =
                    "Fast Resolve paused for a real decision: " +
                    interactiveAttack.StageTitle +
                    ".";
            }
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 336f,
                panel.y + height - 58f,
                panel.width - 358f,
                38f
            ),
            "Fast Resolve still rolls every die; it stops when a CP re-roll choice exists."
        );
    }

    private void DrawDiceTrayButton()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            armyImportMode ||
            deploymentMode)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                15,
                Screen.height - 52f,
                120f,
                34f
            ),
            showDiceTray
            ? "Hide Dice"
            : "Dice Tray"))
        {
            showDiceTray =
                !showDiceTray;
        }
    }

    private void DrawDiceTray()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            !showDiceTray ||
            armyImportMode ||
            deploymentMode)
        {
            return;
        }

        EnsureTraditionalDiceTray();

        traditionalDiceTray.DrawGUI();
    }


    private void DrawDieFace(
        Rect rect,
        int value)
    {
        GUI.Box(
            rect,
            ""
        );

        GUIStyle dieStyle =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        dieStyle.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;

        dieStyle.fontSize = 24;
        dieStyle.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            rect,
            value.ToString(),
            dieStyle
        );
    }

    private string ShortDiceLabel(
        string label)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            label))
        {
            return "Roll";
        }

        if (label.Length <= 12)
            return label;

        return label.Substring(
            0,
            11
        ) + "…";
    }

    private void DrawStratagemMenu()
    {
        if (!showStratagemMenu)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                720f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                590f,
                Screen.height - 100f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width -
                 width) *
                    0.5f,
                76f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.045f,
                0.98f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 20;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 14f,
                panel.width - 80f,
                30f
            ),
            "STRATAGEMS   •   " +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            "   •   " +
            GetCommandPoints(
                activeFaction
            ) +
            " CP",
            title
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    48f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                32f,
                28f
            ),
            "X"))
        {
            showStratagemMenu = false;
            return;
        }

        GUIStyle section =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        section.fontSize = 15;
        section.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 54f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                24f
            ),
            "CORE",
            section
        );

        DrawStratagemInfoCard(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 82f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                42f
            ),
            "Command Re-roll — 1 CP",
            StratagemTooltip(
                "Command Re-roll"
            )
        );

        string detachmentHeading =
            "DETACHMENT";

        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                activeFaction))
        {
            detachmentHeading +=
                " — " +
                aeldariRules.DetachmentName(
                    activeFaction
                ).ToUpper();
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 136f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                24f
            ),
            detachmentHeading,
            section
        );

        float left =
            panel.x + 18f;

        float right =
            panel.x +
            panel.width * 0.5f +
            4f;

        float cardWidth =
            panel.width * 0.5f -
            30f;

        float y =
            panel.y + 166f;

        bool isAeldari =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                activeFaction
            );

        bool isDevotedOfYnnead =
            isAeldari &&
            aeldariRules.DetachmentIs(
                activeFaction,
                AeldariDetachment.DevotedOfYnnead
            );

        bool isYnnari =
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.IsYnnari(
                activeFaction
            );

        bool isNecrons =
            factionRules != null &&
            factionRules.IsNecrons(
                activeFaction
            );

        if (isDevotedOfYnnead ||
            (isYnnari && !isAeldari))
        {
            if (DrawStratagemActionButton(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Soulsight — 1 CP",
                "Soulsight"))
            {
                TryUiStratagem(
                    TryUseSoulsight,
                    "Soulsight"
                );
            }

            if (DrawStratagemActionButton(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Emissaries of Ynnead — 1 CP",
                "Emissaries of Ynnead"))
            {
                TryUiStratagem(
                    TryUseEmissariesOfYnnead,
                    "Emissaries of Ynnead"
                );
            }

            y += 54f;

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Macabre Resilience — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Macabre Resilience"
                )
            );

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Parting the Veil — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Parting the Veil"
                )
            );

            y += 54f;

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Pall of Dread — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Pall of Dread"
                )
            );

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Death Answers Death — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Death Answers Death"
                )
            );
        }
        else if (isAeldari)
        {
            AeldariStratagemDefinition[] rules =
                aeldariRules.Stratagems(
                    activeFaction
                );

            for (int i = 0;
                 i < rules.Length;
                 i++)
            {
                AeldariStratagemDefinition rule =
                    rules[i];

                bool rightColumn =
                    i % 2 == 1;

                int row =
                    i / 2;

                Rect card =
                    new Rect(
                        rightColumn
                        ? right
                        : left,
                        y +
                            row *
                            54f,
                        cardWidth,
                        42f
                    );

                DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                    card,
                    rule.Name +
                    " — " +
                    rule.Cost +
                    " CP",
                    rule.Summary
                );
            }
        }
        else if (isNecrons)
        {
            if (DrawStratagemActionButton(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Hungry Void — 1 CP",
                "Protocol of the Hungry Void"))
            {
                TryUiStratagem(
                    TryUseHungryVoid,
                    "Protocol of the Hungry Void"
                );
            }

            if (DrawStratagemActionButton(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Sudden Storm — 1 CP",
                "Protocol of the Sudden Storm"))
            {
                TryUiStratagem(
                    TryUseSuddenStorm,
                    "Protocol of the Sudden Storm"
                );
            }

            y += 54f;

            if (DrawStratagemActionButton(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Conquering Tyrant — 1 CP",
                "Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant"))
            {
                TryUiStratagem(
                    TryUseConqueringTyrant,
                    "Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant"
                );
            }

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Undying Legions — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Protocol of the Undying Legions"
                )
            );

            y += 54f;

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Vengeful Stars — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Protocol of the Vengeful Stars"
                )
            );

            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    y,
                    cardWidth,
                    42f
                ),
                "Eternal Revenant — reactive",
                StratagemTooltip(
                    "Protocol of the Eternal Revenant"
                )
            );
        }
        else
        {
            DrawStratagemInfoCard(
                new Rect(
                    left,
                    y,
                    panel.width - 36f,
                    42f
                ),
                "No faction-specific stratagems are wired for this detachment yet.",
                "Core Stratagems remain available. Faction-specific buttons only appear when Warboard has a rules module for the loaded detachment."
            );
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + height - 94f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                24f
            ),
            "HOVER A STRATAGEM FOR ITS RULE",
            section
        );

        DrawCurrentTooltip(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + height - 66f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                52f
            )
        );
    }

    private bool DrawStratagemActionButton(
        Rect rect,
        string label,
        string ruleName)
    {
        return GUI.Button(
            rect,
            new GUIContent(
                label,
                StratagemTooltip(
                    ruleName
                )
            )
        );
    }

    private void DrawStratagemInfoCard(
        Rect rect,
        string label,
        string tooltip)
    {
        GUI.Box(
            rect,
            new GUIContent(
                label,
                tooltip
            )
        );
    }

    private void TryUiStratagem(
        System.Func<bool> action,
        string displayName)
    {
        bool used =
            action != null &&
            action();

        if (!used)
        {
            status =
                displayName +
                " is not available for the selected unit in the current phase / trigger.";
        }
    }

    private void DrawCurrentTooltip(
        Rect rect)
    {
        string tooltip =
            GUI.tooltip;

        DrawTintedBox(
            rect,
            new Color(
                0.055f,
                0.06f,
                0.075f,
                0.98f
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 10f,
                rect.y + 6f,
                rect.width - 20f,
                rect.height - 12f
            ),
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                tooltip)
            ? "Hover a stratagem to see its trigger and effect."
            : tooltip
        );
    }

    private string StratagemTooltip(
        string name)
    {
        switch (name)
        {
            case "Command Re-roll":
                return "1 CP • After an eligible roll. Re-roll one die. Charge and staged attack windows offer this automatically when legal.";

            case "Soulsight":
                return "1 CP • Shooting phase • Select an eligible YNNARI unit that has not shot. Its ranged weapons gain Lethal Hits and Ignores Cover until end of phase.";

            case "Emissaries of Ynnead":
                return "1 CP • Fight phase • YNNARI Infantry. Re-roll Hit rolls of 1; if below Starting Strength, re-roll failed Hit rolls instead.";

            case "Macabre Resilience":
                return "1 CP • Reactive when an eligible YNNARI Infantry/Mounted unit is targeted. Attacks are -1 to Wound for the phase. Warboard opens the reaction automatically.";

            case "Parting the Veil":
                return "2 CP • Reactive Fight-phase defence. Destroyed eligible models that have not fought can fight after the attacking unit finishes.";

            case "Pall of Dread":
                return "1 CP • Reactive when an eligible YNNARI unit is destroyed on an objective you previously controlled. Secure that objective until the opponent exceeds your Level of Control.";

            case "Death Answers Death":
                return "1 CP • Reactive at the end of the opponent Shooting phase. One eligible YNNARI unit that lost models can shoot as if it were your Shooting phase.";

            case "Protocol of the Hungry Void":
                return "1 CP • Fight phase • Selected Necron unit gets +1 melee Strength; while led by a Necron Character, improve melee AP by 1.";

            case "Protocol of the Sudden Storm":
                return "1 CP • Movement phase • Selected Necron unit's ranged weapons count as Assault until end of turn.";

            case "Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant":
                return "1 CP • Shooting phase • At half range, selected Necron unit re-rolls Hit rolls of 1; if led by a Necron Character it re-rolls failed Hit rolls.";

            case "Protocol of the Undying Legions":
                return "1 CP • Reactive after a Necron unit loses models to an attack. Activate Reanimation Protocols immediately; led units receive the implemented bonus.";

            case "Protocol of the Vengeful Stars":
                return "1 CP • Reactive after a Necron unit is destroyed by a Shooting attack. An eligible nearby Necron Character can retaliate against the attacker.";

            case "Protocol of the Eternal Revenant":
                return "1 CP • Reactive end-of-phase resurrection for an eligible destroyed Necron Infantry Character, returning with half wounds.";

            default:
                return name;
        }
    }

    private void DrawStratagemReactionWindow()
    {
        if (!showStratagemReaction ||
            pendingReactionContext == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        float width = 520f;
        float height = 250f;

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                (Screen.height - height) *
                    0.5f,
                width,
                height
            );

        GUI.Box(panel, "");

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 20;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 18f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                30f
            ),
            "STRATAGEM REACTION",
            title
        );

        string source =
            pendingChargeAttacker != null
            ? pendingChargeAttacker.DisplayName
            : "Unit";

        string target =
            pendingChargeTarget != null
            ? pendingChargeTarget.DisplayName
            : "target";

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 58f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                72f
            ),
            source +
            " rolled " +
            pendingChargeRoll +
            " to charge " +
            target +
            ".\n" +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " has " +
            GetCommandPoints(
                activeFaction
            ) +
            " CP."
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 24f,
                panel.y + 155f,
                220f,
                46f
            ),
            "Keep roll (" +
            pendingChargeRoll +
            ")"))
        {
            KeepPendingChargeRoll();
        }

        IStratagem commandReroll =
            StratagemRegistry.Get(
                "command_reroll"
            );

        bool canReroll =
            commandReroll != null &&
            commandReroll.CanUse(
                this,
                activeFaction,
                pendingReactionContext
            );

        GUI.enabled = canReroll;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 276f,
                panel.y + 155f,
                220f,
                46f
            ),
            "Command Re-roll (1 CP)"))
        {
            UseCommandRerollOnPendingCharge();
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 212f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                25f
            ),
            "Esc keeps the current roll."
        );
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (battleSetupMode)
        {
            DrawBattleSetupPanel();
            return;
        }

        if (missionSetupMode)
        {
            DrawMissionSetupPanel();
            return;
        }

        if (battleOver)
        {
            DrawBattleOverPanel();
            return;
        }

        if (armyImportMode)
        {
            GUI.Box(
                new Rect(
                    15,
                    15,
                    820,
                    360
                ),
                ""
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    28,
                    760,
                    25
                ),
                "WARBOARD v32 | " +
                (IsXcomMode ? "XCOM" : "TRADITIONAL") +
                " • " +
                battleSizeName.ToUpper() +
                " • " +
                battlePoints +
                " PTS • NEW RECRUIT / YELLOWSCRIBE"
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    58,
                    760,
                    42
                ),
                "New Recruit: Export → Integrations → YellowScribe (TTS). Paste each 8-character code below. YellowScribe codes expire quickly."
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    108,
                    150,
                    22
                ),
                "Player 1 code:"
            );

            yellowCodePlayerOne =
                GUI.TextField(
                    new Rect(
                        155,
                        104,
                        170,
                        28
                    ),
                    yellowCodePlayerOne,
                    8
                );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    340,
                    103,
                    160,
                    30
                ),
                loadingYellowScribe
                ? "Loading..."
                : "Load Player 1"))
            {
                BeginYellowScribeLoad(0);
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    515,
                    108,
                    270,
                    22
                ),
                playerOneLoaded
                ? "Loaded: " +
                  playerOneRosterLabel
                : "Not loaded"
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    150,
                    150,
                    22
                ),
                "Player 2 code:"
            );

            yellowCodePlayerTwo =
                GUI.TextField(
                    new Rect(
                        155,
                        146,
                        170,
                        28
                    ),
                    yellowCodePlayerTwo,
                    8
                );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    340,
                    145,
                    160,
                    30
                ),
                loadingYellowScribe
                ? "Loading..."
                : "Load Player 2"))
            {
                BeginYellowScribeLoad(1);
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    515,
                    150,
                    270,
                    22
                ),
                playerTwoLoaded
                ? "Loaded: " +
                  playerTwoRosterLabel
                : "Not loaded"
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    192,
                    760,
                    65
                ),
                status
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    270,
                    245,
                    36
                ),
                "Use Built-In Test Armies"))
            {
                LoadBuiltInTestArmies();
            }

            GUI.enabled =
                playerOneLoaded &&
                playerTwoLoaded &&
                !loadingYellowScribe;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    290,
                    270,
                    245,
                    36
                ),
                "Mission Setup"))
            {
                OpenMissionSetup();
            }

            GUI.enabled = true;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    30,
                    320,
                    760,
                    40
                ),
                missionPresetName + " • " +
                BoardWidth.ToString("0.#") + " x " +
                BoardDepth.ToString("0.#") +
                " battlefield • " +
                DeploymentZoneWidth.ToString("0.#") +
                " deployment zones. Load both rosters, then start deployment."
            );

            return;
        }

        DrawTopCommandBar();
        DrawMovementCursorMeasureLabel();

        if (showBattleLog)
            DrawBattleLogPanel();

        if (showMissionPanel)
            DrawMissionPanel();

        if (showWarboardPanel)
            DrawWarboardPanel();

        if (showBasicCommandsPanel)
            DrawBasicCommandsPanel();

        if (showRuleChoiceWindow)
        {
            DrawRuleChoiceWindow();
            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack != null &&
            interactiveAttack
                .HasPendingCasualtyChoices)
        {
            DrawCasualtySelectionWindow();
            return;
        }

        if (interactiveAttack != null)
        {
            DrawInteractiveAttackWindow();
            return;
        }

        if (showStratagemReaction)
        {
            DrawStratagemReactionWindow();
            return;
        }

        if (showStratagemMenu)
        {
            DrawStratagemMenu();
        }

        if (showDatasheet)
        {
            DrawDatasheetPanel();
            return;
        }

        if (deploymentMode)
        {
            DrawDeploymentPanel();
            return;
        }

        if (!IsXcomMode &&
            traditionalAttackPending)
        {
            DrawTraditionalAttackPanel();
        }

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            DrawTraditionalStateControlPanel();
            DrawTraditionalRuleAlertPanel();
        }

        DrawContextActionBar();
        DrawStatusToast();
        DrawDiceTray();
    }

    private ForceDisposition NextDisposition(
        ForceDisposition value)
    {
        ForceDisposition[] all =
            MissionRegistry.AllDispositions;

        int index =
            System.Array.IndexOf(
                all,
                value
            );

        index =
            (index + 1) %
            all.Length;

        return all[index];
    }

    private MissionSecondaryMode
        NextSecondaryMode(
            MissionSecondaryMode value)
    {
        if (value ==
            MissionSecondaryMode.Tactical)
        {
            return
                MissionSecondaryMode.Fixed;
        }

        if (value ==
            MissionSecondaryMode.Fixed)
        {
            return
                MissionSecondaryMode.Manual;
        }

        return
            MissionSecondaryMode.Tactical;
    }

    private void DrawMissionSetupPanel()
    {
        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                940f,
                Screen.width - 30f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                620f,
                Screen.height - 30f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                (Screen.height - height) *
                    0.5f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.042f,
                1f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 21;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrap =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrap.wordWrap = true;
        wrap.fontSize = 12;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 16f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                30f
            ),
            "CHAPTER APPROVED 2026–27 — MISSION SETUP",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 50f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                42f
            ),
            "Each player's Force Disposition resolves a different Primary Mission through the 11th-edition mission matrix. Secondary mode and terrain-layout slot are selected independently.",
            wrap
        );

        float columnWidth =
            (panel.width - 60f) *
            0.5f;

        Rect p1 =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 105f,
                columnWidth,
                280f
            );

        Rect p2 =
            new Rect(
                p1.x + columnWidth + 20f,
                panel.y + 105f,
                columnWidth,
                280f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            p1,
            new Color(
                0.05f,
                0.055f,
                0.07f,
                1f
            )
        );

        DrawTintedBox(
            p2,
            new Color(
                0.05f,
                0.055f,
                0.07f,
                1f
            )
        );

        DrawMissionSetupPlayer(
            p1,
            0,
            missionDispositionPlayerOne,
            missionDispositionPlayerTwo,
            missionSecondaryPlayerOne
        );

        DrawMissionSetupPlayer(
            p2,
            1,
            missionDispositionPlayerTwo,
            missionDispositionPlayerOne,
            missionSecondaryPlayerTwo
        );

        string p1Faction =
            factions.Count > 0
            ? factions[0]
            : "";

        string p2Faction =
            factions.Count > 1
            ? factions[1]
            : "";

        bool p1Aeldari =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                p1Faction
            );

        bool p2Aeldari =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                p2Faction
            );

        float p1Half =
            (p1.width - 34f) *
            0.5f;

        float p2Half =
            (p2.width - 34f) *
            0.5f;

        if (p1Aeldari)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p1.x + 14f,
                    p1.y + 208f,
                    p1Half,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT AELDARI DETACHMENT"))
            {
                aeldariRules.NextDetachment(
                    p1Faction
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p1.x + 20f + p1Half,
                    p1.y + 208f,
                    p1Half,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT FORCE DISPOSITION"))
            {
                missionDispositionPlayerOne =
                    NextDisposition(
                        missionDispositionPlayerOne
                    );
            }
        }
        else
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p1.x + 14f,
                    p1.y + 208f,
                    p1.width - 28f,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT FORCE DISPOSITION"))
            {
                missionDispositionPlayerOne =
                    NextDisposition(
                        missionDispositionPlayerOne
                    );
            }
        }

        if (p2Aeldari)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p2.x + 14f,
                    p2.y + 208f,
                    p2Half,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT AELDARI DETACHMENT"))
            {
                aeldariRules.NextDetachment(
                    p2Faction
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p2.x + 20f + p2Half,
                    p2.y + 208f,
                    p2Half,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT FORCE DISPOSITION"))
            {
                missionDispositionPlayerTwo =
                    NextDisposition(
                        missionDispositionPlayerTwo
                    );
            }
        }
        else
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    p2.x + 14f,
                    p2.y + 208f,
                    p2.width - 28f,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT FORCE DISPOSITION"))
            {
                missionDispositionPlayerTwo =
                    NextDisposition(
                        missionDispositionPlayerTwo
                    );
            }
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                p1.x + 14f,
                p1.y + 244f,
                p1.width - 28f,
                28f
            ),
            "SECONDARIES: " +
            missionSecondaryPlayerOne
                .ToString()
                .ToUpper()))
        {
            missionSecondaryPlayerOne =
                NextSecondaryMode(
                    missionSecondaryPlayerOne
                );
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                p2.x + 14f,
                p2.y + 244f,
                p2.width - 28f,
                28f
            ),
            "SECONDARIES: " +
            missionSecondaryPlayerTwo
                .ToString()
                .ToUpper()))
        {
            missionSecondaryPlayerTwo =
                NextSecondaryMode(
                    missionSecondaryPlayerTwo
                );
        }

        MissionBattlefieldDefinition previewBattlefield =
            MissionBattlefieldRegistry.Build(
                missionDispositionPlayerOne,
                missionDispositionPlayerTwo,
                missionLayoutIndex,
                BoardWidth,
                BoardDepth
            );

        Rect settings =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 20f,
                panel.y + 395f,
                panel.width - 40f,
                150f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            settings,
            new Color(
                0.045f,
                0.05f,
                0.064f,
                1f
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                settings.x + 14f,
                settings.y + 12f,
                settings.width - 28f,
                24f
            ),
            "BATTLEFIELD / ROLES",
            heading
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                settings.x + 14f,
                settings.y + 48f,
                210f,
                34f
            ),
            "LAYOUT " +
            previewBattlefield.LayoutLabel +
            " • " +
            previewBattlefield.DisplayName
                .ToUpper()))
        {
            missionLayoutIndex++;

            if (missionLayoutIndex > 3)
                missionLayoutIndex = 1;
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                settings.x + 238f,
                settings.y + 48f,
                290f,
                34f
            ),
            missionAttackerIndex == 0
            ? "ATTACKER: " +
              playerOneRosterLabel
            : "ATTACKER: " +
              playerTwoRosterLabel))
        {
            missionAttackerIndex =
                1 - missionAttackerIndex;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                settings.x + 14f,
                settings.y + 92f,
                500f,
                42f
            ),
            "60 × 44 • " +
            previewBattlefield.DisplayName +
            " deployment • six objectives • Layout " +
            previewBattlefield.LayoutLabel +
            " • roll-off winner takes first turn.",
            wrap
        );

        DrawMissionBattlefieldPreview(
            new Rect(
                settings.x +
                    settings.width -
                    250f,
                settings.y + 8f,
                236f,
                132f
            ),
            previewBattlefield
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    240f,
                panel.y +
                    panel.height -
                    58f,
                220f,
                38f
            ),
            "BEGIN DEPLOYMENT"))
        {
            FinishArmyLoading();
        }
    }

    private void DrawMissionBattlefieldPreview(
        Rect rect,
        MissionBattlefieldDefinition definition)
    {
        if (definition == null)
            return;

        DrawTintedBox(
            rect,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.052f,
                1f
            )
        );

        Rect board =
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 8f,
                rect.y + 26f,
                rect.width - 16f,
                rect.height - 34f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            board,
            new Color(
                0.28f,
                0.31f,
                0.30f,
                1f
            )
        );

        System.Func<Vector3, Vector2> toGui =
            world =>
                new Vector2(
                    board.x +
                    (world.x /
                     BoardWidth +
                     0.5f) *
                    board.width,
                    board.y +
                    (0.5f -
                     world.z /
                     BoardDepth) *
                    board.height
                );

        foreach (MissionTerrainSpec terrain
            in definition.Terrain)
        {
            Vector2 centre =
                toGui(
                    terrain.Position
                );

            float w =
                terrain.Size.x /
                BoardWidth *
                board.width;

            float h =
                terrain.Size.z /
                BoardDepth *
                board.height;

            DrawTintedBox(
                new Rect(
                    centre.x - w * 0.5f,
                    centre.y - h * 0.5f,
                    w,
                    h
                ),
                terrain.Trait ==
                    TerrainTrait.Blocking
                ? new Color(
                    0.10f,
                    0.11f,
                    0.12f,
                    0.95f
                  )
                : new Color(
                    0.20f,
                    0.24f,
                    0.20f,
                    0.95f
                  )
            );
        }

        foreach (MissionObjectiveSpec objective
            in definition.Objectives)
        {
            Vector2 centre =
                toGui(
                    objective.Position
                );

            DrawTintedBox(
                new Rect(
                    centre.x - 4f,
                    centre.y - 4f,
                    8f,
                    8f
                ),
                new Color(
                    0.95f,
                    0.80f,
                    0.16f,
                    1f
                )
            );
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 8f,
                rect.y + 4f,
                rect.width - 16f,
                20f
            ),
            definition.DisplayName +
            " • Layout " +
            definition.LayoutLabel
        );
    }

    private void DrawMissionSetupPlayer(
        Rect rect,
        int index,
        ForceDisposition own,
        ForceDisposition opponent,
        MissionSecondaryMode secondaryMode)
    {
        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 17;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle small =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        small.fontSize = 11;
        small.wordWrap = true;

        string player =
            index == 0
            ? playerOneRosterLabel
            : playerTwoRosterLabel;

        string faction =
            factions.Count > index
            ? factions[index]
            : "";

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 14f,
                rect.y + 10f,
                rect.width - 28f,
                24f
            ),
            player,
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 14f,
                rect.y + 40f,
                rect.width - 28f,
                22f
            ),
            "Force Disposition: " +
            MissionRegistry.Display(
                own
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 14f,
                rect.y + 68f,
                rect.width - 28f,
                44f
            ),
            "Primary: " +
            MissionRegistry.ResolvePrimary(
                own,
                opponent
            ),
            heading
        );

        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                faction))
        {
            AeldariDetachmentDefinition definition =
                aeldariRules.GetDefinition(
                    faction
                );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x + 14f,
                    rect.y + 116f,
                    rect.width - 28f,
                    24f
                ),
                "AELDARI: " +
                definition.DisplayName +
                " — " +
                definition.RuleName,
                heading
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x + 14f,
                    rect.y + 142f,
                    rect.width - 28f,
                    56f
                ),
                definition.RuleSummary,
                small
            );
        }
        else
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x + 14f,
                    rect.y + 132f,
                    rect.width - 28f,
                    42f
                ),
                "Faction rules: " +
                (factionRules != null
                    ? factionRules.RuleSummary(
                        faction
                      )
                    : "Generic Core"),
                small
            );
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 14f,
                rect.y + 184f,
                rect.width - 28f,
                20f
            ),
            "Secondaries: " +
            secondaryMode.ToString()
        );
    }
