    private void DrawMissionPanel()
    {
        if (!showMissionPanel ||
            missionSystem == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                820f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                610f,
                Screen.height - 90f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                18f,
                66f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.03f,
                0.042f,
                0.98f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 18;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                28f
            ),
            "MISSION — ROUND " +
            round +
            " • LAYOUT " +
            missionLayoutIndex,
            heading
        );

        if (factions.Count >= 2)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    50f
                ),
                DisplayFactionName(
                    factions[0]
                ) +
                ": " +
                missionSystem
                    .MissionStateSummary(
                        factions[0]
                    ) +
                "\n" +
                DisplayFactionName(
                    factions[1]
                ) +
                ": " +
                missionSystem
                    .MissionStateSummary(
                        factions[1]
                    )
            );
        }

        MissionPlayerState activeState =
            missionSystem.State(
                activeFaction
            );

        if (activeState == null)
            return;

        Rect operations =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 110f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                254f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            operations,
            new Color(
                0.05f,
                0.055f,
                0.07f,
                1f
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                operations.x + 12f,
                operations.y + 10f,
                operations.width - 24f,
                24f
            ),
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " — " +
            activeState.PrimaryMission,
            heading
        );

        float y =
            operations.y + 44f;

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            MissionRegistry.OperationLabel(
                activeState.PrimaryMission
            ),
            activeState.PersistentMarks,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementPersistentMarks(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementPersistentMarks(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            "Operation markers",
            activeState.OperationMarkers,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementOperationMarkers(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementOperationMarkers(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            MissionRegistry.TurnActionLabel(
                activeState.PrimaryMission
            ),
            activeState.TurnActionCompletions,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnActions(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnActions(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            MissionRegistry.TurnBonusLabel(
                activeState.PrimaryMission
            ),
            activeState.TurnBonusCompletions,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnBonus(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnBonus(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            MissionRegistry.SpecialEventLabel(
                activeState.PrimaryMission
            ),
            activeState.TurnSpecialEvents,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnSpecialEvents(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementTurnSpecialEvents(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        DrawMissionCounterRow(
            operations,
            ref y,
            MissionRegistry.BonusLabel(
                activeState.PrimaryMission
            ),
            activeState.PersistentBonusMarks,
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementBonusMarks(
                        activeFaction,
                        -1
                    ),
            () =>
                missionSystem
                    .IncrementBonusMarks(
                        activeFaction,
                        1
                    )
        );

        Rect secondary =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 378f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                panel.height - 394f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            secondary,
            new Color(
                0.05f,
                0.055f,
                0.07f,
                1f
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                secondary.x + 12f,
                secondary.y + 10f,
                secondary.width - 24f,
                24f
            ),
            "SECONDARIES — " +
            activeState.SecondaryMode
                .ToString()
                .ToUpper(),
            heading
        );

        if (activeState.SecondaryMode ==
            MissionSecondaryMode.Fixed)
        {
            DrawFixedSecondaryControls(
                secondary,
                activeState
            );
        }
        else
        {
            DrawTacticalSecondaryControls(
                secondary,
                activeState
            );
        }
    }

    private void DrawMissionCounterRow(
        Rect panel,
        ref float y,
        string label,
        int value,
        System.Action minus,
        System.Action plus)
    {
        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                y,
                panel.width - 150f,
                24f
            ),
            label +
            ": " +
            value
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    126f,
                y - 2f,
                48f,
                26f
            ),
            "-"))
        {
            minus();
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    66f,
                y - 2f,
                48f,
                26f
            ),
            "+"))
        {
            plus();
        }

        y += 32f;
    }

    private void DrawTacticalSecondaryControls(
        Rect rect,
        MissionPlayerState state)
    {
        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 12f,
                rect.y + 76f,
                105f,
                30f
            ),
            "DRAW"))
        {
            status =
                missionSystem.DrawSecondary(
                    activeFaction
                );
        }

        float y =
            rect.y + 116f;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < state.SecondaryHand.Count;
             i++)
        {
            int captured = i;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x + 12f,
                    y,
                    rect.width - 340f,
                    28f
                ),
                state.SecondaryHand[i]
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        314f,
                    y - 2f,
                    72f,
                    28f
                ),
                "+3 VP"))
            {
                int award =
                    missionSystem.ScoreSecondary(
                        activeFaction,
                        captured,
                        3,
                        round
                    );

                status =
                    award > 0
                    ? "Secondary scored +" +
                      award +
                      " VP."
                    : "Secondary score capped or unavailable.";
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        234f,
                    y - 2f,
                    72f,
                    28f
                ),
                "+5 VP"))
            {
                int award =
                    missionSystem.ScoreSecondary(
                        activeFaction,
                        captured,
                        5,
                        round
                    );

                status =
                    award > 0
                    ? "Secondary scored +" +
                      award +
                      " VP."
                    : "Secondary score capped or unavailable.";
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        154f,
                    y - 2f,
                    130f,
                    28f
                ),
                "DISCARD"))
            {
                status =
                    missionSystem
                        .DiscardSecondary(
                            activeFaction,
                            captured
                        );

                break;
            }

            y += 36f;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 12f,
                rect.y +
                    rect.height -
                    34f,
                rect.width - 24f,
                26f
            ),
            "Deck: " +
            state.SecondaryDeck.Count +
            " • Hand: " +
            state.SecondaryHand.Count +
            "/2 • secondary scoring is capped at 15 VP per round / 45 VP total."
        );
    }

    private void DrawFixedSecondaryControls(
        Rect rect,
        MissionPlayerState state)
    {
        float y =
            rect.y + 80f;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < state.FixedSecondaries.Count;
             i++)
        {
            int captured = i;

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x + 12f,
                    y,
                    rect.width - 330f,
                    28f
                ),
                state.FixedSecondaries[i]
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        314f,
                    y - 2f,
                    82f,
                    28f
                ),
                "NEXT"))
            {
                missionSystem
                    .CycleFixedSecondary(
                        activeFaction,
                        captured
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        222f,
                    y - 2f,
                    82f,
                    28f
                ),
                "+3 VP"))
            {
                missionSystem.ScoreSecondary(
                    activeFaction,
                    captured,
                    3,
                    round
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    rect.x +
                        rect.width -
                        130f,
                    y - 2f,
                    106f,
                    28f
                ),
                "+5 VP"))
            {
                missionSystem.ScoreSecondary(
                    activeFaction,
                    captured,
                    5,
                    round
                );
            }

            y += 38f;
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rect.x + 12f,
                rect.y +
                    rect.height -
                    44f,
                rect.width - 24f,
                36f
            ),
            "Each Fixed secondary is capped at 20 VP; total secondary scoring is also capped at 15 VP per round / 45 VP per game."
        );
    }

    private void DrawBattleLogPanel()
    {
        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                820f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                650f,
                Screen.height - 90f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                18f,
                66f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.020f,
                0.025f,
                0.036f,
                0.985f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 19;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle body =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        body.fontSize = 12;
        body.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                panel.width - 160f,
                26f
            ),
            "BATTLE LOG — COMPLETE AUDIT TRAIL",
            heading
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    136f,
                panel.y + 10f,
                120f,
                28f
            ),
            "CLEAR LOG"))
        {
            battleLog.Clear();
            battleLogSequence = 0;
        }

        Rect scrollRect =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                panel.y + 48f,
                panel.width - 24f,
                panel.height - 60f
            );

        float contentHeight =
            Mathf.Max(
                scrollRect.height,
                battleLog.Count *
                    106f
            );

        battleLogScroll =
            GUI.BeginScrollView(
                scrollRect,
                battleLogScroll,
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    0f,
                    scrollRect.width -
                        20f,
                    contentHeight
                )
            );

        float y = 0f;

        foreach (WarboardBattleLogEntry entry
            in battleLog
                .AsEnumerable()
                .Reverse())
        {
            if (entry == null)
                continue;

            Rect entryRect =
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    y,
                    scrollRect.width -
                        30f,
                    98f
                );

            DrawTintedBox(
                entryRect,
                new Color(
                    0.045f,
                    0.05f,
                    0.066f,
                    1f
                )
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    10f,
                    y + 6f,
                    entryRect.width - 20f,
                    20f
                ),
                entry.Header
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    10f,
                    y + 27f,
                    entryRect.width - 20f,
                    22f
                ),
                entry.Title,
                heading
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    10f,
                    y + 51f,
                    entryRect.width - 20f,
                    42f
                ),
                entry.Detail,
                body
            );

            y += 106f;
        }

        GUI.EndScrollView();
    }

    private void DrawMovementCursorMeasureLabel()
    {
        if (!movementCursorMeasureActive ||
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                movementCursorMeasureText))
        {
            return;
        }

        Vector2 mouse =
            Input.mousePosition;

        float x =
            mouse.x + 18f;

        float y =
            Screen.height -
            mouse.y +
            16f;

        float width =
            Mathf.Clamp(
                movementCursorMeasureText.Length *
                    7.2f +
                    24f,
                96f,
                320f
            );

        if (x + width >
            Screen.width - 8f)
        {
            x =
                Screen.width -
                width -
                8f;
        }

        if (y + 30f >
            Screen.height - 8f)
        {
            y =
                Screen.height -
                38f;
        }

        Rect box =
            new Rect(
                x,
                y,
                width,
                30f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            box,
            movementCursorMeasureLegal
            ? new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.12f,
                0.16f,
                0.96f
              )
            : new Color(
                0.22f,
                0.045f,
                0.035f,
                0.97f
              )
        );

        GUIStyle style =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        style.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;

        style.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        style.fontSize = 13;

        style.normal.textColor =
            Color.white;

        GUI.Label(
            box,
            movementCursorMeasureText,
            style
        );
    }

    private void DrawTopCommandBar()
    {
        Rect bar =
            new Rect(
                10f,
                10f,
                Screen.width - 20f,
                46f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            bar,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.055f,
                0.97f
            )
        );

        Color accent =
            FactionColor(
                activeFaction
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            new Rect(
                bar.x,
                bar.y +
                    bar.height -
                    3f,
                bar.width,
                3f
            ),
            accent
        );

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 16;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;
        title.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleLeft;

        string phaseText =
            deploymentMode
            ? "DEPLOYMENT"
            : phase.ToString().ToUpper();

        string roundText =
            deploymentMode
            ? "PRE-GAME"
            : "ROUND " + round;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                bar.x + 14f,
                bar.y + 7f,
                460f,
                30f
            ),
            "WARBOARD v32   •   " +
            (IsXcomMode
                ? "XCOM"
                : "TRADITIONAL") +
            "   •   " +
            battleSizeName.ToUpper() +
            "   •   " +
            roundText +
            "   •   " +
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            "   •   " +
            phaseText,
            title
        );

        float right =
            bar.x +
            bar.width -
            12f;

        if (!deploymentMode)
        {
            right -= 118f;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    bar.y + 7f,
                    110f,
                    30f
                ),
                "NEXT PHASE"))
            {
                NextPhase();
            }
        }

        right -= 84f;

        GUI.enabled =
            !IsXcomMode;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                76f,
                30f
            ),
            IsXcomMode
            ? "AUTO"
            : (showDiceTray
                ? "HIDE 3D"
                : "3D DICE")))
        {
            showDiceTray =
                !showDiceTray;
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        right -= 108f;

        GUI.enabled =
            !deploymentMode;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                100f,
                30f
            ),
            "STRATAGEMS"))
        {
            showStratagemMenu =
                !showStratagemMenu;

            showWarboardPanel = false;
            showBasicCommandsPanel = false;
            showDatasheet = false;
            showMissionPanel = false;
            showBattleLog = false;
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        right -= 106f;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                98f,
                30f
            ),
            "COMMANDS"))
        {
            showBasicCommandsPanel =
                !showBasicCommandsPanel;

            if (showBasicCommandsPanel)
            {
                showWarboardPanel = false;
                showStratagemMenu = false;
                showDatasheet = false;
                showMissionPanel = false;
                showBattleLog = false;
            }
        }

        right -= 72f;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                64f,
                30f
            ),
            "LOG"))
        {
            showBattleLog =
                !showBattleLog;

            if (showBattleLog)
            {
                showWarboardPanel = false;
                showBasicCommandsPanel = false;
                showStratagemMenu = false;
                showDatasheet = false;
                showMissionPanel = false;
            }
        }

        right -= 100f;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                92f,
                30f
            ),
            "MISSION"))
        {
            showMissionPanel =
                !showMissionPanel;

            if (showMissionPanel)
            {
                showWarboardPanel = false;
                showBasicCommandsPanel = false;
                showStratagemMenu = false;
                showDatasheet = false;
                showBattleLog = false;
            }
        }

        right -= 106f;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                right,
                bar.y + 7f,
                98f,
                30f
            ),
            "WARBOARD"))
        {
            showWarboardPanel =
                !showWarboardPanel;

            if (showWarboardPanel)
            {
                showBasicCommandsPanel = false;
                showStratagemMenu = false;
                showDatasheet = false;
                showMissionPanel = false;
                showBattleLog = false;
            }
        }

        if (selectedSquad != null &&
            !deploymentMode)
        {
            right -= 110f;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    right,
                    bar.y + 7f,
                    102f,
                    30f
                ),
                "DATASHEET"))
            {
                showWarboardPanel = false;
                showBasicCommandsPanel = false;
                showStratagemMenu = false;

                OpenDatasheetForSelection();
            }
        }
    }

    private void DrawWarboardPanel()
    {
        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                650f,
                Screen.width -
                    40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                18f,
                66f,
                width,
                320f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.055f,
                0.96f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 18;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                26f
            ),
            "WARBOARD",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 46f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                54f
            ),
            ScoreText() +
            "\n" +
            CommandPointText() +
            "    " +
            CoherencyText() +
            "    " +
            BattleShockText()
        );

        string factionSummary =
            factionRules != null
            ? factionRules.RuleSummary(
                activeFaction
              )
            : "Generic Core";

        string aeldariSummary =
            aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                activeFaction)
            ? aeldariRules.RuleSummary(
                activeFaction
              )
            : "";

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 104f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                72f
            ),
            "FACTION: " +
            factionSummary +
            (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                aeldariSummary)
                ? ""
                : "\nDETACHMENT: " +
                  aeldariSummary)
        );

        if (aeldariRules != null &&
            aeldariRules.IsAeldariFaction(
                activeFaction))
        {
            string[] enhancements =
                aeldariRules.Enhancements(
                    activeFaction
                );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 180f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    54f
                ),
                "Battle Focus: " +
                TotalBattleFocusTokens(
                    activeFaction
                ) +
                " • Enhancements in detachment: " +
                string.Join(
                    ", ",
                    enhancements
                )
            );
        }

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 240f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                64f
            ),
            "Universal: Heavy • Cover • Stealth • Ignores Cover • Indirect • Lance • InSv • FNP • Deadly Demise\n" +
            "Reserves: from Round 2 • >8 from enemies • Strategic Reserves use battlefield edge\n" +
            "Aeldari rules are resolved through the selected detachment module."
        );
    }
