    private void DrawDeploymentPanel()
    {
        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                900f,
                Screen.width -
                    48f
            );

        float height =
            Mathf.Min(
                370f,
                Screen.height -
                    110f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width -
                 width) *
                    0.5f,
                70f,
                width,
                height
            );

        // Fully opaque during deployment so world-space wound labels and
        // miniature highlights cannot visually leak through the menu.
        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.028f,
                0.032f,
                0.043f,
                1.0f
            )
        );

        Color accent =
            FactionColor(
                activeFaction
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            new Rect(
                panel.x,
                panel.y,
                panel.width,
                3f
            ),
            accent
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 18;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 14f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                26f
            ),
            ActiveFactionDisplayName() +
            " DEPLOYMENT",
            heading
        );

        GUIStyle muted =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        muted.fontSize = 12;
        muted.wordWrap = true;

        string instruction =
            "Place the selected unit wholly inside the " +
            (activeMissionBattlefield != null
                ? activeMissionBattlefield
                    .DisplayName +
                  " " +
                  (FactionUsesAttackerZone(
                       activeFaction)
                      ? "ATTACKER"
                      : "DEFENDER") +
                  " deployment zone"
                : "deployment zone") +
            ", or send it to Reserves.";

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y + 43f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                34f
            ),
            instruction,
            muted
        );

        float contentY =
            panel.y + 82f;

        float leftWidth =
            Mathf.Min(
                390f,
                panel.width *
                    0.46f
            );

        float gap = 18f;

        Rect rosterOuter =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                contentY,
                leftWidth,
                205f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            rosterOuter,
            new Color(
                0.045f,
                0.05f,
                0.064f,
                1.0f
            )
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                rosterOuter.x + 10f,
                rosterOuter.y + 8f,
                rosterOuter.width - 20f,
                20f
            ),
            "DEPLOYMENT ORDER"
        );

        List<SquadController> undeployed =
            UndeployedUnitsForFaction(
                activeFaction
            );

        Rect rosterScrollRect =
            new Rect(
                rosterOuter.x + 8f,
                rosterOuter.y + 31f,
                rosterOuter.width - 16f,
                rosterOuter.height - 39f
            );

        float rosterHeight =
            Mathf.Max(
                rosterScrollRect.height - 4f,
                undeployed.Count *
                    34f
            );

        deploymentRosterScroll =
            GUI.BeginScrollView(
                rosterScrollRect,
                deploymentRosterScroll,
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    0f,
                    rosterScrollRect.width -
                        20f,
                    rosterHeight
                )
            );

        float rosterY = 0f;

        foreach (SquadController squad
            in undeployed)
        {
            bool selected =
                squad ==
                currentDeploymentSquad;

            string suffix =
                squad.IsLeaderUnit
                ? "  [LEADER]"
                : (squad.HasDeepStrike
                    ? "  [DEEP STRIKE]"
                    : "");

            string label =
                (selected ? "► " : "") +
                squad.DisplayName +
                suffix;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    0f,
                    rosterY,
                    rosterScrollRect.width -
                        24f,
                    28f
                ),
                label))
            {
                ChooseDeploymentSquad(
                    squad
                );
            }

            rosterY += 34f;
        }

        GUI.EndScrollView();

        Rect detail =
            new Rect(
                rosterOuter.x +
                    rosterOuter.width +
                    gap,
                contentY,
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    18f -
                    (rosterOuter.x +
                     rosterOuter.width +
                     gap),
                205f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            detail,
            new Color(
                0.045f,
                0.05f,
                0.064f,
                1.0f
            )
        );

        string selectedName =
            currentDeploymentSquad != null
            ? currentDeploymentSquad.DisplayName
            : "No unit selected";

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                detail.x + 14f,
                detail.y + 12f,
                detail.width - 28f,
                28f
            ),
            selectedName,
            heading
        );

        if (currentDeploymentSquad != null)
        {
            string reserveType =
                currentDeploymentSquad
                    .HasDeepStrike
                ? "Deep Strike"
                : "Strategic Reserves";

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    detail.x + 14f,
                    detail.y + 47f,
                    detail.width - 28f,
                    58f
                ),
                "Models: " +
                currentDeploymentSquad
                    .StartingModels +
                "\n" +
                currentDeploymentSquad
                    .LeaderSummary() +
                "\nReserve: " +
                reserveType,
                muted
            );
        }

        GUI.enabled =
            currentDeploymentSquad != null;

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                detail.x + 14f,
                detail.y + 116f,
                Mathf.Min(
                    185f,
                    detail.width - 28f
                ),
                32f
            ),
            "SEND TO RESERVES"))
        {
            SendCurrentDeploymentToReserves();
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        if (currentDeploymentSquad != null &&
            currentDeploymentSquad.IsLeaderUnit)
        {
            List<SquadController> attachTargets =
                CompatibleDeploymentBodyguards();

            SquadController chosenBodyguard =
                CurrentDeploymentBodyguardTarget();

            float attachX =
                detail.x + 208f;

            float attachWidth =
                Mathf.Max(
                    120f,
                    detail.x +
                        detail.width -
                        14f -
                        attachX
                );

            GUI.enabled =
                chosenBodyguard != null;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    attachX,
                    detail.y + 116f,
                    attachWidth,
                    32f
                ),
                chosenBodyguard != null
                ? "ATTACH: " +
                  chosenBodyguard.DisplayName
                : "NO BODYGUARD"))
            {
                AttachCurrentLeader();
            }

            GUI.enabled = true;

            if (attachTargets.Count > 1)
            {
                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        attachX,
                        detail.y + 156f,
                        attachWidth,
                        28f
                    ),
                    "NEXT BODYGUARD  " +
                    (deploymentBodyguardChoiceIndex + 1) +
                    "/" +
                    attachTargets.Count))
                {
                    CycleDeploymentBodyguardTarget();
                }
            }
        }

        Rect statusPanel =
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 18f,
                panel.y +
                    panel.height -
                    64f,
                panel.width - 36f,
                46f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            statusPanel,
            new Color(
                0.055f,
                0.06f,
                0.075f,
                1.0f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle statusStyle =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        statusStyle.wordWrap = true;
        statusStyle.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleLeft;
        statusStyle.fontSize = 12;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                statusPanel.x + 12f,
                statusPanel.y + 5f,
                statusPanel.width - 24f,
                statusPanel.height - 10f
            ),
            status,
            statusStyle
        );
    }

    private void DrawTraditionalAttackPanel()
    {
        if (!traditionalAttackPending)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                760f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width - width) *
                    0.5f,
                112f,
                width,
                112f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.055f,
                0.96f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle heading =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        heading.fontSize = 16;
        heading.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 14f,
                panel.y + 10f,
                panel.width - 180f,
                24f
            ),
            "TRADITIONAL — ATTACK DECLARED",
            heading
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 14f,
                panel.y + 36f,
                panel.width - 180f,
                64f
            ),
            (traditionalAttackAttacker != null
                ? traditionalAttackAttacker.DisplayName
                : "Attacker") +
            " → " +
            (traditionalAttackTarget != null
                ? traditionalAttackTarget.DisplayName
                : "Target") +
            "\n" +
            TraditionalAttackSelectionSummary(
                traditionalAttackSelections
            ) +
            "\nResolve dice yourself. Use WOUND EDIT for damage."
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    164f,
                panel.y + 18f,
                148f,
                42f
            ),
            "ATTACK RESOLVED"))
        {
            CompleteTraditionalAttackDeclaration();
        }

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x +
                    panel.width -
                    164f,
                panel.y + 66f,
                148f,
                30f
            ),
            showDiceTray
            ? "HIDE DICE"
            : "SHOW DICE"))
        {
            showDiceTray =
                !showDiceTray;
        }
    }

    private void DrawTraditionalStateControlPanel()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode)
            return;

        bool visible =
            traditionalFirstTurnPending ||
            traditionalNumericPromptPending ||
            traditionalAdvancePending ||
            traditionalChargePending ||
            traditionalBattleShockPending ||
            traditionalReanimationPending;

        if (!visible)
            return;

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                560f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        float height =
            traditionalReanimationPending
            ? 410f
            : 225f;

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                18f,
                116f,
                width,
                height
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.025f,
                0.032f,
                0.046f,
                0.985f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 18;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrap =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrap.wordWrap = true;
        wrap.fontSize = 12;

        if (traditionalFirstTurnPending &&
            factions.Count >= 2)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                "TRADITIONAL — FIRST-TURN ROLL-OFF",
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    58f
                ),
                "Roll one D6 for each player in the free tray. Resolve ties/rerolls yourselves, then tell Warboard only who won.",
                wrap
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 122f,
                    240f,
                    46f
                ),
                DisplayFactionName(
                    factions[0]
                ) +
                " WON"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalFirstTurnRollOff(
                    0
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 272f,
                    panel.y + 122f,
                    240f,
                    46f
                ),
                DisplayFactionName(
                    factions[1]
                ) +
                " WON"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalFirstTurnRollOff(
                    1
                );
            }

            return;
        }

        if (traditionalNumericPromptPending)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                traditionalNumericTitle,
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    62f
                ),
                traditionalNumericText +
                "\nResolve the dice/rerolls yourself. Enter only the final value Warboard needs.",
                wrap
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 116f,
                    150f,
                    28f
                ),
                "FINAL: " +
                traditionalNumericValue,
                title
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 150f,
                    panel.y + 112f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "-1"))
            {
                traditionalNumericValue =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalNumericValue - 1,
                        traditionalNumericMin,
                        traditionalNumericMax
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 210f,
                    panel.y + 112f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "+1"))
            {
                traditionalNumericValue =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalNumericValue + 1,
                        traditionalNumericMin,
                        traditionalNumericMax
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 162f,
                    170f,
                    38f
                ),
                "APPLY FINAL VALUE"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalNumericPrompt();
            }

            if (traditionalNumericCancel != null &&
                GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 198f,
                        panel.y + 162f,
                        120f,
                        38f
                    ),
                    "CANCEL"))
            {
                CancelTraditionalNumericPrompt();
            }

            return;
        }

        if (traditionalAdvancePending &&
            traditionalAdvanceUnit != null)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                "TRADITIONAL — ADVANCE",
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    54f
                ),
                traditionalAdvanceUnit.DisplayName +
                "\nRoll the Advance D6 yourself. Warboard will not inspect the dice or offer rerolls. Enter only the final bonus you are using.",
                wrap
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 112f,
                    120f,
                    28f
                ),
                "FINAL: +" +
                traditionalAdvanceResult,
                title
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 150f,
                    panel.y + 108f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "-1"))
            {
                traditionalAdvanceResult =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalAdvanceResult - 1,
                        1,
                        6
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 210f,
                    panel.y + 108f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "+1"))
            {
                traditionalAdvanceResult =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalAdvanceResult + 1,
                        1,
                        6
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 158f,
                    170f,
                    38f
                ),
                "APPLY RESULT"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalAdvance();
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 196f,
                    panel.y + 158f,
                    120f,
                    38f
                ),
                "CANCEL"))
            {
                CancelTraditionalAdvance();
            }

            return;
        }

        if (traditionalChargePending &&
            traditionalChargeAttacker != null &&
            traditionalChargeTarget != null)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                "TRADITIONAL — CHARGE",
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    58f
                ),
                traditionalChargeAttacker.DisplayName +
                " → " +
                traditionalChargeTarget.DisplayName +
                "\nRoll 2D6 yourself and apply any tabletop rerolls yourself. Enter only the final total.",
                wrap
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 112f,
                    140f,
                    28f
                ),
                "FINAL: " +
                traditionalChargeResult,
                title
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 150f,
                    panel.y + 108f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "-1"))
            {
                traditionalChargeResult =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalChargeResult - 1,
                        2,
                        12
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 210f,
                    panel.y + 108f,
                    54f,
                    32f
                ),
                "+1"))
            {
                traditionalChargeResult =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalChargeResult + 1,
                        2,
                        12
                    );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 158f,
                    190f,
                    38f
                ),
                "APPLY CHARGE TOTAL"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalCharge();
            }

            return;
        }

        if (traditionalBattleShockPending &&
            traditionalBattleShockUnit != null)
        {
            int leadership =
                EffectiveLeadership(
                    traditionalBattleShockUnit
                );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                "BATTLE-SHOCK TEST REQUIRED",
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 46f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    78f
                ),
                traditionalBattleShockUnit.DisplayName +
                " • Leadership " +
                leadership +
                "+\nRoll 2D6 yourself and resolve every tabletop modifier/reroll yourself. Warboard only needs the final state.",
                wrap
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 142f,
                    170f,
                    42f
                ),
                "MARK PASS"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalBattleShock(
                    true
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 198f,
                    panel.y + 142f,
                    170f,
                    42f
                ),
                "MARK FAIL"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalBattleShock(
                    false
                );
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 190f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    24f
                ),
                "FAIL applies the ice-blue Battle-shock state. PASS clears it.",
                wrap
            );

            return;
        }

        if (traditionalReanimationPending)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 12f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    28f
                ),
                "REANIMATION PROTOCOLS — MANUAL",
                title
            );

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 44f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    48f
                ),
                "Roll and resolve Reanimation entirely yourself. Warboard only edits the unit state you explicitly request below.",
                wrap
            );

            if (traditionalReanimationUnits.Count == 0 ||
                traditionalReanimationUnit == null)
            {
                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 16f,
                        panel.y + 104f,
                        180f,
                        38f
                    ),
                    "DONE REANIMATION"))
                {
                    FinishTraditionalReanimation();
                }

                return;
            }

            int currentIndex =
                traditionalReanimationUnits
                    .IndexOf(
                        traditionalReanimationUnit
                    );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 98f,
                    42f,
                    30f
                ),
                "<"))
            {
                int index =
                    (currentIndex - 1 +
                     traditionalReanimationUnits.Count) %
                    traditionalReanimationUnits.Count;

                SelectTraditionalReanimationUnit(
                    traditionalReanimationUnits[
                        index
                    ]
                );
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 66f,
                    panel.y + 101f,
                    panel.width - 132f,
                    28f
                ),
                traditionalReanimationUnit.DisplayName +
                "  (" +
                (currentIndex + 1) +
                "/" +
                traditionalReanimationUnits.Count +
                ")",
                title
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x +
                        panel.width -
                        58f,
                    panel.y + 98f,
                    42f,
                    30f
                ),
                ">"))
            {
                int index =
                    (currentIndex + 1) %
                    traditionalReanimationUnits.Count;

                SelectTraditionalReanimationUnit(
                    traditionalReanimationUnits[
                        index
                    ]
                );
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 140f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    38f
                ),
                "Living " +
                traditionalReanimationUnit.LivingModels +
                "/" +
                traditionalReanimationUnit.StartingModels +
                " • Click a wounded living model on the battlefield to heal it.",
                wrap
            );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 184f,
                    180f,
                    36f
                ),
                "HEAL SELECTED +1"))
            {
                TraditionalHealSelectedReanimationWound();
            }

            List<ModelToken> dead =
                TraditionalReanimationDestroyedModels();

            if (dead.Count > 0)
            {
                traditionalReanimationDeadIndex =
                    Mathf.Clamp(
                        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex,
                        0,
                        dead.Count - 1
                    );

                ModelToken returning =
                    dead[
                        traditionalReanimationDeadIndex
                    ];

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 16f,
                        panel.y + 234f,
                        42f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "<"))
                {
                    CycleTraditionalReanimationDeadModel(
                        -1
                    );
                }

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 66f,
                        panel.y + 238f,
                        panel.width - 252f,
                        26f
                    ),
                    "Return: " +
                    returning.RoleName +
                    " (" +
                    (traditionalReanimationDeadIndex + 1) +
                    "/" +
                    dead.Count +
                    ")"
                );

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x +
                            panel.width -
                            184f,
                        panel.y + 232f,
                        42f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    ">"))
                {
                    CycleTraditionalReanimationDeadModel(
                        1
                    );
                }

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x +
                            panel.width -
                            136f,
                        panel.y + 232f,
                        120f,
                        32f
                    ),
                    "RETURN 1W"))
                {
                    TraditionalReturnSelectedReanimationModel();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        panel.x + 16f,
                        panel.y + 236f,
                        panel.width - 32f,
                        28f
                    ),
                    "No destroyed models remain in this unit."
                );
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 288f,
                    180f,
                    38f
                ),
                "DONE THIS UNIT"))
            {
                CompleteTraditionalReanimationUnit();
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 210f,
                    panel.y + 288f,
                    180f,
                    38f
                ),
                "DONE ALL"))
            {
                FinishTraditionalReanimation();
            }

            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 16f,
                    panel.y + 338f,
                    panel.width - 32f,
                    54f
                ),
                "Warboard does not track Reanimation points in Traditional mode. Only press HEAL / RETURN when your manually resolved tabletop result permits it.",
                wrap
            );
        }
    }

    private void DrawTraditionalRuleAlertPanel()
    {
        if (IsXcomMode ||
            !traditionalRuleAlertPending)
        {
            return;
        }

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                600f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                18f,
                Mathf.Min(
                    540f,
                    Screen.height - 210f
                ),
                width,
                180f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.09f,
                0.045f,
                0.035f,
                0.985f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle title =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        title.fontSize = 18;
        title.fontStyle =
            FontStyle.Bold;

        GUIStyle wrap =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        wrap.wordWrap = true;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 12f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                28f
            ),
            traditionalRuleAlertTitle,
            title
        );

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 46f,
                panel.width - 32f,
                76f
            ),
            traditionalRuleAlertText +
            "\nWarboard will not roll or apply this effect for you.",
            wrap
        );

        if (GUI.Button(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 16f,
                panel.y + 132f,
                180f,
                34f
            ),
            "RESOLVED / ACK"))
        {
            AcknowledgeTraditionalRuleAlert();
        }

        if (traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice > 0)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    panel.x + 210f,
                    panel.y + 132f,
                    150f,
                    34f
                ),
                "SHOW DICE"))
            {
                OpenTraditionalDicePrompt(
                    traditionalRuleAlertSuggestedDice
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private void DrawContextActionBar()
    {
        Rect bar =
            new Rect(
                12f,
                62f,
                Mathf.Min(
                    Screen.width - 24f,
                    1120f
                ),
                42f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            bar,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.055f,
                0.90f
            )
        );

        float x =
            bar.x + 10f;

        if (selectedSquad != null)
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 10f,
                    240f,
                    24f
                ),
                selectedSquad.DisplayName
            );

            x += 245f;
        }
        else
        {
            GUI.Label(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 10f,
                    180f,
                    24f
                ),
                "No unit selected"
            );

            x += 185f;
        }

        if (!IsXcomMode)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    112f,
                    30f
                ),
                manualWoundEditMode
                ? "WOUNDS: ON"
                : "WOUND EDIT"))
            {
                manualWoundEditMode =
                    !manualWoundEditMode;

                if (manualWoundEditMode)
                    manualRestoreEditMode = false;

                pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate = null;

                status =
                    manualWoundEditMode
                    ? "MANUAL WOUNDS: click any model, friendly or enemy, then use -1 / +1 / REMOVE."
                    : "Manual wound editing disabled.";
            }

            x += 119f;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    122f,
                    30f
                ),
                manualRestoreEditMode
                ? "RESTORE: ON"
                : "RESTORE EDIT"))
            {
                manualRestoreEditMode =
                    !manualRestoreEditMode;

                if (manualRestoreEditMode)
                    manualWoundEditMode = false;

                manualRestoreDeadIndex = 0;
                pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate = null;

                status =
                    manualRestoreEditMode
                    ? "MANUAL RESTORE: select a living model/unit, then choose which destroyed model to return."
                    : "Manual restore editing disabled.";
            }

            x += 129f;

            if (manualRestoreEditMode &&
                selectedSquad != null)
            {
                List<ModelToken> restoreCandidates =
                    TraditionalRestoreCandidates();

                if (restoreCandidates.Count > 0)
                {
                    manualRestoreDeadIndex =
                        Mathf.Clamp(
                            manualRestoreDeadIndex,
                            0,
                            restoreCandidates.Count - 1
                        );

                    ModelToken candidate =
                        restoreCandidates[
                            manualRestoreDeadIndex
                        ];

                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            36f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "<"))
                    {
                        CycleTraditionalRestoreCandidate(-1);
                    }

                    x += 40f;

                    GUI.Label(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 10f,
                            140f,
                            24f
                        ),
                        candidate.RoleName +
                        " " +
                        (manualRestoreDeadIndex + 1) +
                        "/" +
                        restoreCandidates.Count
                    );

                    x += 145f;

                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            36f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        ">"))
                    {
                        CycleTraditionalRestoreCandidate(1);
                    }

                    x += 40f;

                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            92f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "RETURN 1W"))
                    {
                        TraditionalRestoreCurrentModel(false);
                    }

                    x += 98f;

                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            104f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "RETURN FULL"))
                    {
                        TraditionalRestoreCurrentModel(true);
                    }

                    x += 110f;
                }
                else
                {
                    GUI.Label(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 10f,
                            130f,
                            24f
                        ),
                        "No dead models"
                    );

                    x += 135f;
                }
            }

            if (manualWoundEditMode &&
                selectedModel != null &&
                selectedModel.IsAlive)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        72f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    selectedModel.CurrentWounds +
                    "/" +
                    selectedModel.MaxWounds +
                    " W"
                );

                x += 76f;

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        48f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "-1"))
                {
                    TraditionalAdjustSelectedWounds(-1);

                    if (selectedModel == null ||
                        !selectedModel.IsAlive)
                    {
                        GUI.enabled = true;
                        return;
                    }
                }

                x += 52f;

                GUI.enabled =
                    selectedModel.CurrentWounds <
                    selectedModel.MaxWounds;

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        48f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "+1"))
                {
                    TraditionalAdjustSelectedWounds(1);
                }

                GUI.enabled = true;
                x += 52f;

                if (pendingTraditionalRemovalCandidate ==
                    selectedModel)
                {
                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            126f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "REMOVE ANYWAY"))
                    {
                        ConfirmTraditionalModelRemoval(
                            selectedModel
                        );

                        GUI.enabled = true;
                        return;
                    }

                    x += 133f;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            82f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "REMOVE"))
                    {
                        TryTraditionalRemoveSelectedModel();

                        GUI.enabled = true;
                        return;
                    }

                    x += 89f;
                }
            }
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move &&
            selectedSquad != null)
        {
            GUI.enabled =
                !selectedSquad.HasMoved &&
                !selectedSquad.HasAdvanced &&
                !IsEngaged(
                    selectedSquad
                );

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    105f,
                    30f
                ),
                "ADVANCE"))
            {
                TryDeclareAdvance();
            }

            GUI.enabled = true;
            x += 112f;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Command &&
            selectedSquad != null)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    150f,
                    30f
                ),
                "COMMAND ABILITIES"))
            {
                TryOpenCommandAbilities();
            }

            x += 158f;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move)
        {
            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    135f,
                    30f
                ),
                ActiveFactionReserves().Count > 0
                ? "RESERVES"
                : "NO RESERVES"))
            {
                CycleReservePlacement();
            }

            x += 142f;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Move &&
            selectedSquad != null &&
            factionRules != null &&
            UnitHasBattleFocusRules(
                selectedSquad
            ))
        {
            int bf =
                TotalBattleFocusTokens(
                    selectedSquad.FactionId
                );

            GUI.enabled =
                !selectedSquad.HasMoved &&
                !selectedSquad.AgileManoeuvreUsedThisPhase &&
                bf > 0;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    128f,
                    30f
                ),
                "SWIFT AS WIND"))
            {
                TryUseSwiftAsTheWind();
            }

            x += 135f;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    128f,
                    30f
                ),
                "FLITTING"))
            {
                TryUseFlittingShadows();
            }

            x += 135f;

            GUI.enabled =
                !selectedSquad.AgileManoeuvreUsedThisPhase &&
                selectedSquad.HasAdvanced &&
                selectedSquad.HasKeyword(
                    "vehicle") &&
                bf > 0;

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    118f,
                    30f
                ),
                "STAR ENGINES"))
            {
                TryUseStarEngines();
            }

            GUI.enabled = true;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Shoot &&
            selectedSquad != null)
        {
            if (selectedModel != null)
            {
                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        120f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "DONE MODEL"))
                {
                    CompleteSelectedModelShooting();
                }

                x += 127f;
            }

            if (GUI.Button(
                new Rect(
                    x,
                    bar.y + 6f,
                    110f,
                    30f
                ),
                "DONE UNIT"))
            {
                CompleteSelectedUnitShooting();
            }

            x += 117f;
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Shoot &&
            selectedSquad != null &&
            missionSystem != null)
        {
            MissionActionDefinition definition =
                missionSystem.PrimaryAction(
                    selectedSquad.FactionId
                );

            if (definition != null)
            {
                string reason;

                GUI.enabled =
                    missionSystem
                        .CanStartMissionAction(
                            selectedSquad,
                            out reason
                        );

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        168f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    SelectedMissionActionButtonText()))
                {
                    TryBeginSelectedMissionAction();
                }

                GUI.enabled = true;
                x += 175f;
            }
        }

        if (phase == Phase.Fight)
        {
            if (fightActivationUnit != null &&
                fightActivationStage ==
                    FightActivationStage.PileIn)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        180f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "PILE-IN  " +
                    fightStageMovedModels.Count +
                    "/" +
                    JoinedModels(
                        fightActivationUnit
                    ).Count
                );

                x += 185f;

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        128f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "DONE PILE-IN"))
                {
                    CompleteFightPileIn();
                }

                x += 135f;

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        bar.width -
                            (x - bar.x) -
                            12f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "Select model → click board • hold ALT to measure"
                );
            }
            else if (fightActivationUnit != null &&
                     fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.Attacks)
            {
                int total =
                    FightPotentialAttackModels()
                        .Count;

                int resolved =
                    fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                        .Count(
                            model =>
                                model != null
                        );

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        155f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "MELEE  " +
                    resolved +
                    "/" +
                    total
                );

                x += 160f;

                if (selectedModel != null &&
                    ModelBelongsToFightActivation(
                        selectedModel) &&
                    !fightModelsResolvedThisActivation
                        .Contains(
                            selectedModel))
                {
                    if (GUI.Button(
                        new Rect(
                            x,
                            bar.y + 6f,
                            108f,
                            30f
                        ),
                        "DONE MODEL"))
                    {
                        SkipSelectedFightModel();
                    }

                    x += 115f;
                }

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        118f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "DONE ATTACKS"))
                {
                    CompleteFightAttacks();
                }

                x += 125f;

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        bar.width -
                            (x - bar.x) -
                            12f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "Model → weapon → enemy"
                );
            }
            else if (fightActivationUnit != null &&
                     fightActivationStage ==
                        FightActivationStage.Consolidate)
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        190f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "CONSOLIDATE  " +
                    fightStageMovedModels.Count +
                    "/" +
                    JoinedModels(
                        fightActivationUnit
                    ).Count
                );

                x += 195f;

                if (GUI.Button(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 6f,
                        158f,
                        30f
                    ),
                    "DONE CONSOLIDATE"))
                {
                    CompleteFightConsolidation();
                }

                x += 165f;

                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        bar.width -
                            (x - bar.x) -
                            12f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    "Select model → click board • hold ALT to measure"
                );
            }
            else
            {
                GUI.Label(
                    new Rect(
                        x,
                        bar.y + 10f,
                        bar.width -
                            (x - bar.x) -
                            12f,
                        24f
                    ),
                    FightPriorityText() +
                    " • select a model, then an enemy unit"
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private void DrawStatusToast()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
            status))
        {
            return;
        }

        float width =
            Mathf.Min(
                760f,
                Screen.width - 40f
            );

        Rect panel =
            new Rect(
                (Screen.width -
                 width) *
                    0.5f,
                Screen.height - 66f,
                width,
                46f
            );

        DrawTintedBox(
            panel,
            new Color(
                0.035f,
                0.04f,
                0.055f,
                0.90f
            )
        );

        GUIStyle style =
            new GUIStyle(
                GUI.skin.label
            );

        style.alignment =
            TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;

        GUI.Label(
            new Rect(
                panel.x + 12f,
                panel.y + 6f,
                panel.width - 24f,
                panel.height - 12f
            ),
            status,
            style
        );
    }

    private void DrawTintedBox(
        Rect rect,
        Color color)
    {
        Color previous =
            GUI.color;

        GUI.color = color;

        GUI.Box(
            rect,
            GUIContent.none
        );

        GUI.color = previous;
    }

    private string ActiveFactionDisplayName()
    {
        return DisplayFactionName(
            activeFaction
        );
    }


    private static float HorizontalDistance(
        Vector3 a,
        Vector3 b)
    {
        return Vector2.Distance(
            new Vector2(a.x, a.z),
            new Vector2(b.x, b.z)
        );
    }

    private static float HorizontalMagnitude(Vector3 vector)
    {
        return new Vector2(vector.x, vector.z).magnitude;
    }

    public static Color FactionColor(string factionId)
    {
        if (factionId == "Aeldari" ||
            factionId == "Player 1")
        {
            return new Color(
                0.65f,
                0.22f,
                0.72f
            );
        }

        if (factionId == "Necrons" ||
            factionId == "Player 2")
        {
            return new Color(
                0.18f,
                0.72f,
                0.40f
            );
        }

        return Color.gray;
    }

    public static void SetObjectColor(GameObject go, Color color)
    {
        Renderer renderer =
            go.GetComponent<Renderer>();

        if (renderer == null)
            return;

        Shader shader =
            Shader.Find("Standard");

        if (shader == null)
            shader =
                Shader.Find(
                    "Universal Render Pipeline/Lit"
                );

        if (shader == null)
            shader =
                Shader.Find(
                    "Sprites/Default"
                );

        if (shader == null)
            return;

        Material material =
            new Material(shader);

        material.color = color;
        renderer.material = material;
    }
}

public class BoardSurface : MonoBehaviour { }
